      ( *** game-vh - live walkable Stardrifter ***

	Clean-source position (0,0,-500), live look controls, and movement
	inside the real VEHICLE + cupola geometry.  Arrow keys look, WASD
	moves, ESC exits.  This began as the direct interactive integration of
	the Wave-10 renderer and now owns the complete ship, flight, landing,
	surface, device, persistence, audio, and GUI state. )

"VHG fpu clean"
	{ DB E3 D9 AF <dFCW mtp bytesperunit> }
	end;

"VHG UTC timestamp"
	( NIV+'s checkpoint epoch: UTC seconds since 1984-01-01, retaining its
	  every-four-years leap rule.  This signed value remains valid through
	  the date range supported by the port. )
	[Timer Command] = READ UTC TIME; isocall;
	=> VHG fpu clean; => SU fp init;
	A = [Year]; A - 1984; [VHGutcdays] = A; A '* 365; [VHGutcsecs] = A;
	A = [VHGutcdays]; A '/ 4; A + [VHGutcsecs]; [VHGutcdays] = A;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 0; A = [Month]; ? A <= 1 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 31; ? A = 2 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 59; ? A = 3 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 90; ? A = 4 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 120; ? A = 5 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 151; ? A = 6 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 181; ? A = 7 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 212; ? A = 8 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 243; ? A = 9 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 273; ? A = 10 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 304; ? A = 11 -> VHG UTC month ready;
	[VHGutcmonthbase] = 334;
    "VHG UTC month ready"
	A = [VHGutcdays]; A + [VHGutcmonthbase]; [VHGutcdays] = A;
	A = [Month]; ? A <= 2 -> VHG UTC leap ready;
	A = [Year]; A '% 4; ? A != 0 -> VHG UTC leap ready; [VHGutcdays]+;
    "VHG UTC leap ready"
	A = [Day]; A - 1; A + [VHGutcdays]; A '* 86400; [VHGutcsecs] = A;
	A = [Hour]; A '* 3600; A + [VHGutcsecs]; [VHGutcsecs] = A;
	A = [Minute]; A '* 60; A + [VHGutcsecs]; A + [Second]; [VHGutcsecs] = A;
	end;

"VHG offline restore"
	=> VHG UTC timestamp;
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A - [VHGsavedsecs]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG offline done;
	[VHGelapsed] = A;

	( unfreeze() resolves a closed-game rescue against helptime. Before arrival
	  the distance-dependent delay simply counts down; more than twenty seconds
	  after arrival ends the event and supplies the source's offline four units. )
	A = [VHGrescueactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG offline collector;
	A = [VHGelapsed]; C = [VHGrescuedelay]; ? C <= 0 -> VHG offline rescue arrived;
	? A > C -> VHG offline rescue crosses arrival;
	C - A; [VHGrescuedelay] = C; -> VHG offline collector;
    "VHG offline rescue crosses arrival"
	A - C; [VHGelapsed] = A; [VHGrescuedelay] = 0;
    "VHG offline rescue arrived"
	[VHGgburst] = 0;
	A = [VHGelapsed]; A + [VHGrescuetick]; ? A <= 20 -> VHG offline rescue store;
    "VHG offline rescue finish"
	[VHGrescueactive] = 0; [VHGrescuetick] = 0; [VHGrescuedelay] = 0; [VHGrescueacc] = 0; [VHGgburst] = 0;
	[MgCharge] = 4; -> VHG offline collector;
    "VHG offline rescue store"
	[VHGrescuetick] = A; [VHGrescueacc] = 0; -> VHG offline collector;

    "VHG offline collector"
	A = [VHGcollector]; ? A = 0 -> VHG offline done;
	A = [VHGelapsed]; A '/ 30; ? A <= 0 -> VHG offline done;
	[VHGofflinegain] = A; A + [MgCharge]; ? A <= 120 -> VHG offline charge ready; A = 120;
    "VHG offline charge ready"
	[MgCharge] = A; ? A >= 120 -> VHG offline collector full;
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; => BrtlSrand; A = 5000; => BrtlRandom; A + 15000; [MgPwr] = A;
	-> VHG offline done;
    "VHG offline collector full"
	[MgPwr] = 20000;
    "VHG offline done"
	end;

"VHG restore window"
	( Version-7 checkpoints retain the player's last valid iGUI dimensions.
	  Resize through iGUI so every chrome/work-area bound is updated together. )
	A = [VHSVok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG restore window done;
	A = [Display Width]; ? A != [VHSVwindoww] -> VHG restore window apply;
	A = [Display Height]; ? A = [VHSVwindowh] -> VHG restore window done;
    "VHG restore window apply"
	[New Display Width] = [VHSVwindoww]; [New Display Height] = [VHSVwindowh];
	=> Resize Display;
    "VHG restore window done"
	end;

"VHG capture clock option"
	( Automated gallery captures may pin the source UTC second with the sole
	  command-line argument clock=<decimal>. Ordinary launches have an empty
	  command line and retain the live UTC clock. )
	A = Command Line; [VHGargptr] = A;
	"VHG capture clock leading space"
	C = [A]; ? C != 32 -> VHG capture clock prefix; A+;
	-> VHG capture clock leading space;
	"VHG capture clock prefix"
	C = [A]; ? C != 99 -> VHG capture clock done; A+;
	C = [A]; ? C != 108 -> VHG capture clock done; A+;
	C = [A]; ? C != 111 -> VHG capture clock done; A+;
	C = [A]; ? C != 99 -> VHG capture clock done; A+;
	C = [A]; ? C != 107 -> VHG capture clock done; A+;
	C = [A]; ? C != 61 -> VHG capture clock done; A+;
	[VHGargvalue] = 0; [VHGargdigits] = 0;
    "VHG capture clock digit"
	C = [A]; ? C = 0 -> VHG capture clock parsed;
	? C = 32 -> VHG capture clock parsed;
	? C < 48 -> VHG capture clock done; ? C > 57 -> VHG capture clock done;
	[VHGargdigits]+; C - 48; E = C; C = [VHGargvalue];
	? C > 214748364 -> VHG capture clock done;
	C '* 10; C + E; [VHGargvalue] = C; A+;
	-> VHG capture clock digit;
    "VHG capture clock parsed"
	A = [VHGargdigits]; ? A = 0 -> VHG capture clock done;
	A = [VHGargvalue]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG capture clock done;
	[VHGNDclocksecs] = A; [VHGNDclockoverride] = 1;
    "VHG capture clock done"
	end;

"VHG run"
	=> Initialize Integrated GUI;
	=> FEnter;
	=> VHA init;
	=> MEM build rtab; => MEM build ztab; => MEM wrap reset;
	=> PAL float init; => VHG palette;
	=> SP build rgt; => SP riga init; => SP gman init; => SP load maps;
	=> SP ncc load; => SP unloadallpv;
	=> PGF constants; => VH view init;
	A = 0; A - 500; [VHGz] = A; [VHGbeta] = 180;
	=> VH cupola build;
	=> VH panels init;
	=> VHCAT load;
	=> VHGDB load;

	[PVh] = 0; [PVmodel] = MDVEH;
	[PVxs] = VHG15F; [PVys] = VHG15F; [PVzs] = VHG15F;
	[PVxm] = 0; [PVym] = 0; [PVzm] = 0; [PVcol] = 0; [PVreq] = 1;
	=> SP loadpv;
	( cache the 720 camera-independent normal mode-2 leaves once )
	[PVh] = 0; [DWmode] = 2; [DWuds] = 0;
	[DWcx] = 0; [DWcy] = 0; [DWcz] = 0;
	[DWcamx] = 0; [DWcamy] = 0; [DWcamz] = 0;
	=> SP drawpv; [VHRiters] = 2; [VHRcacheexpected] = 720; => VH build mode2 cache;
	( Keep the shipped mammal twice, exactly as setup_animals(): handle 2 is
	  the immutable base and handle 3 is reset from it before every animal. )
	[PVh] = 2; [PVmodel] = MDMAM;
	[PVxs] = 3F800000h; [PVys] = 3F800000h; [PVzs] = 3F800000h;
	[PVxm] = 0; [PVym] = 0; [PVzm] = 0; [PVcol] = 64; [PVreq] = 1;
	=> SP loadpv;
	[PVh] = 3; [PVmodel] = MDMAM;
	[PVxs] = 3F800000h; [PVys] = 3F800000h; [PVzs] = 3F800000h;
	[PVxm] = 0; [PVym] = 0; [PVzm] = 0; [PVcol] = 128; [PVreq] = 1;
	=> SP loadpv;
	( The bird renderer uses the same immutable-base/result pair. )
	[PVh] = 4; [PVmodel] = MDBRD;
	[PVxs] = 3F800000h; [PVys] = 3F800000h; [PVzs] = 3F800000h;
	[PVxm] = 0; [PVym] = 0; [PVzm] = 0; [PVcol] = 96; [PVreq] = 1;
	=> SP loadpv;
	[PVh] = 5; [PVmodel] = MDBRD;
	[PVxs] = 3F800000h; [PVys] = 3F800000h; [PVzs] = 3F800000h;
	[PVxm] = 0; [PVym] = 0; [PVzm] = 0; [PVcol] = 128; [PVreq] = 1;
	=> SP loadpv;
	[VHTtx] = 3979984; A = 0; A - 43407; [VHTty] = A;
	A = 0; A - 43984; [VHTtz] = A;
	=> VHG target world;
	=> VHG flight init;
	=> TK seed; => TK start;
	=> VHG fps init;
	=> VHG capture clock option;
	( Resume the versioned port checkpoint automatically when present.  VHSV
	  leaves the initialized new-flight state untouched on a missing, stale,
	  or malformed file, so first launch remains a clean game. )
	=> VHG load checkpoint;
	[VHLpower] = [VHGilight];
	=> VHG restore window;
	( iGUI declares Fold Is Active=YES before its first client loop even
	  though the runtime window opens at full INITIAL HEIGHT.  Make the real
	  host state explicit so rendering starts immediately; remember the
	  restored height for a later genuine fold/unfold cycle. )
	[Previous display height] = [Display Height]; [Fold Is Active] = NO;
	( Missing saves retain VHAwanted's default-on value; valid saves have now
	  supplied their preference.  This is the first point playback may start. )
	=> VHA apply;
	A = [VHSVok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG startup state ready;
	=> VHG load success notice;
    "VHG startup state ready"
	[Always Clear Work Area] = NO;
	[Control Loop Idle Slice] = 0;
	[Small Caption] = VHGcaption;
	[Window Title] = VHGcaption; => Update Title Bar;
	( Update Title Bar covers the title/menu strip, so install and repaint the
	  client menu afterwards; doing this in the opposite order left its live
	  hotspot present but the GAME button visually erased. )
	[Menu To Install] = VHGmenu; => Install Menu;
	[Menu Caption] = VHGmenucaption; => Update Menu Button Appearence;
	[Work Area Manager] = service VHG repaint;
	[Control Loop] = service VHG GUI loop;
	[Sleepy Control Loop] = service VHG GUI sleepy;
	[Client Return Action] = service VHG GUI return;
	[Client Exit Action] = service VHG GUI exit;
	=> TK read wall; [VHGwallstart] = [TKtmp];
	=> Enter Integrated GUI;
	=> VHG capsule checkpoint collapse;
	=> VHSV save;
	=> VHA stop;
	[Display Live Region] = VOID REGION;
	=> FLeave;
	end;

"service VHG repaint"
	( Work Area Manager is re-entrant: Windows can invoke it from an isocall
	  while the normal presenter owns VHGUI's row counters and pointers.  Any
	  resize, compose, layer copy, or present here corrupts that interrupted
	  frame.  The ordinary control loop recomputes geometry and publishes the
	  completed frame continuously, so the only safe callback is state-free. )
	end;

"service VHG GUI loop"
	A = [Fold Is Active]; ? A != NO -> VHG GUI loop done;
	( iGUI already exposes its fullscreen title-bar button.  In exclusive
	  mode, Escape now means "return to the window" and is swallowed until
	  release so the same held key cannot reach the game's quit path. )
	A = [VHGfullescheld]; ? A = 0 -> VHG fullscreen escape ready;
	A = [KEY ESCAPE]; ? A = ON -> VHG GUI loop done;
	[VHGfullescheld] = 0; -> VHG fullscreen escape ready;
    "VHG fullscreen escape ready"
	A = [Display Status]; ? A - EXCLUSIVE -> VHG fullscreen escape done;
	A = [KEY ESCAPE]; ? A = OFF -> VHG fullscreen escape done;
	[VHGfullescheld] = 1; => Now Unfull; -> VHG GUI loop done;
    "VHG fullscreen escape done"
	( Do not advance or publish a client frame while the legacy cooperative
	  display is inactive.  Its iGUI chrome may still repaint, but the complete
	  game backdrop remains the last active frame until focus returns. )
	A = [Display Status]; ? A - ACTIVE -> VHG GUI loop done;
	( Windows converts Alt+F4 into WM_CLOSE before iGUI's enhanced-key
	  session can serve F4, and this runtime discards that message.  Poll the
	  live keyboard map here so the standard chord still takes the same clean
	  save/audio-shutdown path as Esc and the red exit button. )
	A = [KEY F4]; ? A = OFF -> VHG GUI loop frame;
	A = [KEY ALTERNATE]; ? A = OFF -> VHG GUI loop frame;
	[VHGesc] = 1; => VHG capsule checkpoint collapse;
	[Quit Now] = YES; -> VHG GUI loop done;
	"VHG GUI loop frame"
		[VHGloopcalls]+;
		=> VHG one frame;
		[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [VHGprofstart] = [Counts];
		=> VHGUI prepare;
		=> VHG raw snapshot pending;
		=> VHG wide raw pending;
		=> VHG movie capture pending;
		=> VHG fps overlay;
		=> VHG energy overlay;
		=> VHG pod overlay;
		=> VHG surface telemetry overlay;
		=> VHG FCS overlay;
		=> VHG body overlay;
		=> VHG output overlay;
		=> VHG landing overlay;
		=> VHG console overlay;
		=> VHG notice overlay;
		=> VHG info overlay;
		=> VHG FCS menu overlay;
		=> VHG preferences overlay;
		=> VHG device overlay;
		=> VHG movie overlay;
		=> VHG graphics overlay;
		=> VHG help overlay;
		=> VHG about overlay;
		=> VHG wide data pending;
		=> VHG wide advance;
		[VHGsnapshotready] = 1;
		=> VHGUI present;
		A = [VHGUIpublished]; ? A = 0 -> VHG present copied;
		[L2L Region] = vector Work Area; => Update Area Published;
		-> VHG present finished;
	    "VHG present copied"
		( Hand the completed Backdrop page to iGUI through its supported update
		  path.  Calling RETRACE directly from the client control callback races
		  the runtime's cooperative-display activation transition and crashes on
		  an ordinary focus change.  Update Area also preserves iGUI's cursor
		  save/restore ordering. )
		[L2L Region] = vector Work Area; => Update Area Fast;
	    "VHG present finished"
		( Presentation cost includes either the fallback layer copy or fixed-2x
		  dual publication, plus cursor composition and physical retrace. )
		[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [VHGprofnow] = [Counts];
		A = [VHGprofnow]; A - [VHGprofstart]; A + [VHGprofpresent]; [VHGprofpresent] = A;
		=> VHG timing step;
	=> VHG fps tick;
	A = [VHGesc]; ? A = 0 -> VHG GUI loop done;
	=> VHG capsule checkpoint collapse;
	[Quit Now] = YES;
    "VHG GUI loop done"
	end;

"service VHG GUI sleepy"
	end;

"service VHG GUI return"
	A = [VHGreturnhookheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG GUI return done;
	[VHGreturnhookheld] = 1;
	[VHGreturnpending] = 1;
    "VHG GUI return done"
	leave;

"service VHG GUI exit"
	=> VHG capsule checkpoint collapse;
	leave;

"VHG capsule checkpoint collapse"
	( Capsule animation state is deliberately transient and F6 refuses to save
	  it. Collapse a host close/Escape during descent or recovery to the same
	  deterministic settled checkpoint used by startup repair before iGUI begins
	  unwinding the cooperative display. )
	A = [VHGCstate]; A | [VHGcapsulestartpending]; A | [VHGcapsulereturnpending];
	? A = 0 -> VHG capsule checkpoint collapse done;
	[VHGCstate] = 0; [VHGCcapcount] = 0; [VHGCrecover] = 0;
	[VHGcapsulestartpending] = 0;
	[VHGcapsulereturnpending] = 0;
	[VHGmode] = 1; [VHGlanded] = 1;
	[VHGx] = [VHGNDdropx]; [VHGz] = [VHGNDdropz];
	A = [VHGNDdropy]; A - 600; [VHGy] = A;
	[VHGsurfair] = 0; [VHGsurfjet] = 0; [VHGsurfjumping] = 0; [VHGsurfvy] = 0;
	[VHGsurfstep] = 0; [VHGsurfshift] = 0;
    "VHG capsule checkpoint collapse done"
	end;

"service VHG menu controls"
	=> VHG toggle help action;
	end;

"service VHG menu console"
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG menu console open;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGshiponlytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; end;
    "VHG menu console open"
	[VHGabout] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 1; [VHGconsoleview] = 1;
	end;

"service VHG menu save"
	=> VHG save checkpoint action;
	end;

"service VHG menu load"
	=> VHG load checkpoint action;
	end;

"service VHG menu fps"
	=> VHG toggle fps action;
	end;

"service VHG menu fast"
	=> VHG toggle fast action;
	end;

"service VHG menu music"
	=> VHG toggle music action;
	end;

"service VHG menu graphics"
	=> VHG toggle graphics action;
	end;

"service VHG menu fcs"
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG menu fcs open;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGshiponlytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; end;
    "VHG menu fcs open"
	[VHGfcsopen] = 1; [VHGdevaccess] = 1; [VHGdev] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0;
	[VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGabout] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 0;
	end;

"service VHG menu devices"
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG menu devices open;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGshiponlytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; end;
    "VHG menu devices open"
	[VHGdev] = 1; [VHGdevaccess] = 1; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0;
	[VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGabout] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 0;
	end;

"service VHG menu prefs"
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG menu prefs open;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGshiponlytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; end;
    "VHG menu prefs open"
	[VHGprefs] = 1; [VHGdevaccess] = 1; [VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0;
	[VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGabout] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 0;
	end;

"service VHG menu quit"
	( Enter Integrated GUI returns through the normal save/audio cleanup. )
	[VHGesc] = 1; => VHG capsule checkpoint collapse; [Quit Now] = YES;
	end;

"VHG one frame"
	=> VHG cadence;
	=> VHG interpolation advance;
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	=> VHG SL advance; => VHG DL advance; => VHG fpu clean;
	( Automatic walk-in recovery is discovered at the tail of surface input.
	  Start the sealing state here at the next clean frame boundary so terrain
	  traversal never observes a half-transitioned input pose. )
	A = [VHGcapsulestartpending]; ? A = 0 -> VHG capsule start commit done;
	[VHGcapsulestartpending] = 0; => VHGC start return; => VHG fpu clean;
    "VHG capsule start commit done"
	( Commit a landing at a clean frame boundary.  The accepted L event is
	  discovered after the ship and close-approach globe have rendered; doing
	  terrain/sky construction in that same renderer frame can carry stale
	  shared x87/raster scratch into the capsule. )
	A = [VHGlandpending]; ? A = 0 -> VHG landing commit done;
	[VHGlandpending] = 0; => VHG land; => VHG fpu clean;
    "VHG landing commit done"
	A = [VHGcapsulereturnpending]; ? A = 0 -> VHG capsule return commit done;
	[VHGcapsulereturnpending] = 0; => VHG return ship; => VHG fpu clean;
    "VHG capsule return commit done"
	( Checkpoints do not retain VHGCstate.  If a session ended during descent or
	  recovery, loading necessarily yields the otherwise-impossible combination
	  surface mode + airborne + idle capsule.  Settle that orphaned transition
	  before simulation/render/input can observe it.  Active descent/ascent has
	  state 1/2 and is deliberately untouched. )
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG capsule checkpoint ready;
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A != 0 -> VHG capsule checkpoint ready;
	A = [VHGCstate]; ? A != 0 -> VHG capsule checkpoint ready;
	[VHGx] = [VHGNDdropx]; [VHGz] = [VHGNDdropz]; [VHGlanded] = 1;
	=> VHG surface motion reset;
    "VHG capsule checkpoint ready"
		( Capture the last authoritative pose before flight, lift, capsule, and
		  surface simulation advance it.  The old post-render capture could only
		  smooth movement applied later by input, leaving every autonomous pose
		  visibly quantised to 18.206 Hz in the 60-Hz presenter. )
		=> VHG interpolation snapshot;
		A = [VHGdosim]; ? A != 0 -> VHG ship ticks due;
		( Fine approach used to execute all 32 retained movement steps on every
		  third 60-Hz frame.  The average was correct but those periodic bursts
		  made presentation visibly choppy.  Advance the same rational number of
		  steps on every presentation frame while an approach is active. )
		A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG skip ship ticks;
		A = [VHGfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG skip ship ticks;
		A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG skip ship ticks;
		A = [MgIpreaching]; ? A = 0 -> VHG skip ship ticks;
		=> VHG local step; -> VHG skip ship ticks;
	    "VHG ship ticks due"
		A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG skip ship ticks;
	=> VHG flight step;
	=> VHG collector step;
	=> VHG rescue advance;
	=> VHG power reserve;
	=> VHG reset step;
	=> VHG emergency step;
	=> VHG light step;
	=> VHG navigation step;
	=> VHG preference step;
	=> VHG lift tick;
	( p_Forward(step) is clamped before vehicle() in the source loop. )
	=> VHG clamp position;
    "VHG skip ship ticks"
		A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG ticks done;
		A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG ticks done;
		=> VHGC tick;
		"VHG ticks done"
		A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG visor tick done;
		=> VHG UTC timestamp; => VHG visor advance; => VHG surface telemetry update;
		=> VHG info slide advance;
		"VHG visor tick done"
		[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [VHGprofstart] = [Counts];
		=> VHG visor flare mode;
		=> VHG interpolation apply; => VHG render; => VHGND surrounding frame;
		=> VHG source info overlay; => VHG interpolation restore;
		[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [VHGprofnow] = [Counts];
	A = [VHGprofnow]; A - [VHGprofstart]; A + [VHGprofrender]; [VHGprofrender] = A;
		[VHGsentframes]+;
		A = [VHGsent]; ? A != 0 -> VHG no sentinel;
		A = [VHGsentframes]; ? A < 60 -> VHG no sentinel;
		=> VHG write sentinel; [VHGsent] = 1;
    "VHG no sentinel"
		( Surface input samples interpolated terrain height through x87 helpers.
		  Rendering uses the same shared stack, so give input a clean ABI boundary;
		  without it the first walking sample after capsule settlement could return
		  through a corrupted floating-point helper frame. )
		=> VHG fpu clean; => VHG lift postrender; => VHG input;
	( VHGframe is the retained source-loop clock used by the physical orbital
	  console.  Extra F5 presentation frames must not accelerate that display. )
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG frame count done; [VHGframe]+;
    "VHG frame count done"
	end;

"VHG flight init"
	( Live approach from the Wave 9 integrator, aimed at the nearest
	  generated star with a non-empty planetary system. )
	A = [VHTtx]; A + 100; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[MgDzatX0] = [FA0]; [MgDzatX1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHTty]; A + 100; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[MgDzatY0] = [FA0]; [MgDzatY1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHTtz]; A + 100; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[MgDzatZ0] = [FA0]; [MgDzatZ1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHTtx]; => IntToF;
	[MgApX0] = [FA0]; [MgApX1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHTty]; => IntToF;
	[MgApY0] = [FA0]; [MgApY1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHTtz]; => IntToF;
	[MgApZ0] = [FA0]; [MgApZ1] = [FA1];
	[MgInitD0] = VHGINITLO; [MgInitD1] = VHGINITHI;
	[MgCur0] = 0; [MgCur1] = VHGFCH;
	[MgTmp] = [VHTRay]; C = [MgTmp]; => MG widen f32 FA from C;
	[MgT00] = [FA0]; [MgT01] = [FA1];
	[MgAnti] = [VHGantirad]; [MgAptgt] = 1; [MgPwr] = 20000; [MgCharge] = 3;
	[VHGcollector] = 0;
	[MgApreached] = 0; [MgStspeed] = 1; [MgStatus] = 0;
	=> VHG local reset;
	[FI] = 100000; => IntToF; [MgskA] = [FA0]; [MgskB] = [FA1];
	end;

"VHG target world"
	=> VHT extract; => VHT load face; => VHG star palette;
	[nsx] = [VHTtx]; [nsy] = [VHTty]; [nsz] = [VHTtz]; => NsSeedFromXYZ;
	[nsseedin] = A; [nsclass] = [VHTclass]; [nsstarray] = [VHTRay];
	=> NsResetAll; => NsPrepare;
	=> VHCAT count system bodies; => VHCAT refresh;
	[VHSready] = 0;
	end;

"VHG flight retarget"
	( Preserve the live galactic position while arming Vimana for a new star. )
	[FI] = [VHTtx]; => IntToF; [MgApX0] = [FA0]; [MgApX1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHTty]; => IntToF; [MgApY0] = [FA0]; [MgApY1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHTtz]; => IntToF; [MgApZ0] = [FA0]; [MgApZ1] = [FA1];

	[FA0]=[MgDzatX0];[FA1]=[MgDzatX1]; [FB0]=[MgApX0];[FB1]=[MgApX1]; => FSub;
	[MgDxx0]=[FA0];[MgDxx1]=[FA1];
	[FA0]=[MgDzatY0];[FA1]=[MgDzatY1]; [FB0]=[MgApY0];[FB1]=[MgApY1]; => FSub;
	[MgDyy0]=[FA0];[MgDyy1]=[FA1];
	[FA0]=[MgDzatZ0];[FA1]=[MgDzatZ1]; [FB0]=[MgApZ0];[FB1]=[MgApZ1]; => FSub;
	[MgDzz0]=[FA0];[MgDzz1]=[FA1];
	[FA0]=[MgDxx0];[FA1]=[MgDxx1]; [FB0]=[MgDxx0];[FB1]=[MgDxx1]; => FMul;
	[MgAcc0]=[FA0];[MgAcc1]=[FA1];
	[FA0]=[MgDyy0];[FA1]=[MgDyy1]; [FB0]=[MgDyy0];[FB1]=[MgDyy1]; => FMul;
	[FB0]=[MgAcc0];[FB1]=[MgAcc1]; => FAdd; [MgAcc0]=[FA0];[MgAcc1]=[FA1];
	[FA0]=[MgDzz0];[FA1]=[MgDzz1]; [FB0]=[MgDzz0];[FB1]=[MgDzz1]; => FMul;
	[FB0]=[MgAcc0];[FB1]=[MgAcc1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt;
	[MgInitD0]=[FA0];[MgInitD1]=[FA1];

	[MgCur0] = 0; [MgCur1] = VHGFCH;
	[MgTmp] = [VHTRay]; [MgAnti] = [VHGantirad]; [MgAptgt] = 1;
	[MgApreached] = 0; [MgStspeed] = 1; [MgStatus] = 0;
	[VHGcollector] = 0;
	=> VHG local reset;
	end;

"VHG flight step"
	( mgloop's corpus entry seeds MgT0 with ap_target_ray, then uses the same
	  pair as per-axis integration scratch.  A live one-frame caller must
	  restore that boundary input before every continuation frame. )
	A = [MgApreached]; ? A != 0 -> VHG flight local;
	[MgAnti] = [VHGantirad];
	C = [MgTmp]; => MG widen f32 FA from C;
	[MgT00] = [FA0]; [MgT01] = [FA1];
	=> MGD reset; [MgNframes] = 1; => MG vimana loop;
	-> VHG flight telemetry;
    "VHG flight local"
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG flight telemetry;
	A = [MgIpreaching]; ? A = 0 -> VHG flight telemetry; => VHG local step;
"VHG flight telemetry"
	[FA0] = [MgDzatX0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatX1]; => FToIntChop;
	[VHGflightx] = [FI];
	[FA0] = [MgDzatX0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatX1];
	[FB0] = [MgskA]; [FB1] = [MgskB]; => FQuo; => FToIntChop;
	[VHGsecx] = [FI];
	end;

"VHG collector step"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:3394-3426.  The source reseeds Borland random() from the
	  current star identity every frame, then subtracts a distance penalty. )
	A = [VHGcollector]; ? A = 0 -> VHG collector done;
	[FA0] = [MgDzatX0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatX1]; [FI] = [VHTtx]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [MgDzatX0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatX1]; => FSub;
	[MgDxx0] = [FA0]; [MgDxx1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHTty]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [MgDzatY0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatY1]; => FSub; [MgDyy0] = [FA0]; [MgDyy1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHTtz]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatZ1]; => FSub; [MgDzz0] = [FA0]; [MgDzz1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [MgDxx0]; [FA1] = [MgDxx1]; [FB0] = [MgDxx0]; [FB1] = [MgDxx1]; => FMul;
	[MgLdsd0] = [FA0]; [MgLdsd1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [MgDyy0]; [FA1] = [MgDyy1]; [FB0] = [MgDyy0]; [FB1] = [MgDyy1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [MgLdsd0]; [FB1] = [MgLdsd1]; => FAdd; [MgLdsd0] = [FA0]; [MgLdsd1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [MgDzz0]; [FA1] = [MgDzz1]; [FB0] = [MgDzz0]; [FB1] = [MgDzz1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [MgLdsd0]; [FB1] = [MgLdsd1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt;
	[MgLdsd0] = [FA0]; [MgLdsd1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 1; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [MgLdsd0]; [FB1] = [MgLdsd1]; => FAdd;
	[MgLdsd0] = [FA0]; [MgLdsd1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHTid0]; [FA1] = [VHTid1]; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; => BrtlSrand; A = 50; => BrtlRandom; [VHGcollectrate] = A;
	[FI] = 125; A = [VHTclass]; ? A = 5 -> VHG collector penalty ready;
	[FI] = 25;
    "VHG collector penalty ready"
	=> IntToF; [FB0] = [MgLdsd0]; [FB1] = [MgLdsd1]; => FQuo;
	=> FToIntChop; [VHGcollectloss] = [FI];
	A = [VHGcollectrate]; A - [VHGcollectloss]; [VHGcollectrate] = A;
	( Class 5 is always productive: its negative result is raised to one.
	  Class 6 retains the source GET CLOSER failure when its result is not
	  positive. )
	C = [VHTclass]; ? C != 5 -> VHG collector rate ready;
	? A > 0 -> VHG collector rate ready; [VHGcollectrate] = 1; A = 1;
    "VHG collector rate ready"
	? A <= 0 -> VHG collector too far;
	A = [MgPwr]; ? A < 20000 -> VHG collector fill;
	A = [MgCharge]; ? A < 120 -> VHG collector fill;
	[VHGcollector] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGcollectfulltext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 100;
	-> VHG collector done;
    "VHG collector fill"
	A = [MgPwr]; A + [VHGcollectrate]; [MgPwr] = A;
	? A < 20000 -> VHG collector scoping;
	A = [MgCharge]; ? A >= 120 -> VHG collector cap power;
	[MgPwr] = 15001; [MgCharge]+; -> VHG collector scoping;
    "VHG collector cap power"
	[MgPwr] = 20000;
    "VHG collector scoping"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGcollecttext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 25; -> VHG collector done;
    "VHG collector too far"
	[VHGcollector] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGcollectclosertext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 100;
    "VHG collector done"
	end;

"VHG power reserve"
	( The live movement kernels drain MgPwr.  At the +15000 threshold the
	  original ship consumes one stored lithium unit and restores 20000. )
	A = [MgPwr]; ? A > 15000 -> VHG power reserve done;
	A = [MgCharge]; ? A > 0 -> VHG power use lithium;
	? A < 0 -> VHG power omega;
	[MgPwr] = 15000; -> VHG power reserve done;
    "VHG power use lithium"
	[MgCharge]-; [MgPwr] = 20000; -> VHG power reserve done;
    "VHG power omega"
	[MgPwr] = 20000;
"VHG power reserve done"
	end;

"VHG reset step"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:2310-2363.  The onboard reset is a timed 150-step boot
	  sequence, not an instantaneous preference restore.  Preserve the visible
	  emergency-light handoff and the source milestones that affect live systems. )
	A = [VHGresetcount]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG reset step done;
	? A = 150 -> VHG reset stage 150; ? A = 140 -> VHG reset stage 140;
	? A = 130 -> VHG reset stage 130; ? A = 120 -> VHG reset stage 120;
	? A = 115 -> VHG reset stage 115; ? A = 110 -> VHG reset stage 110;
	? A = 101 -> VHG reset stage 101; ? A = 75 -> VHG reset stage 75;
	? A = 10 -> VHG reset stage 10; -> VHG reset decrement;
    "VHG reset stage 150"
	[VHGelight] = 1; [VHGilightlevel] = 0; [VHGilight] = 0; [VHLpower] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGresetdashtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG reset decrement;
    "VHG reset stage 140"
	=> VHG local reset; -> VHG reset decrement;
    "VHG reset stage 130"
	[MgApreached] = 0; [MgStspeed] = 0; -> VHG reset decrement;
    "VHG reset stage 120"
	[VHGgburst] = 0; [VHGsync] = 1; [VHGantirad] = 1; [MgAnti] = 1;
	[VHGfinder] = 0; [VHGamp] = 0; [VHSamp] = 0; [VHSready] = 0; [VHSdrawready] = 0;
	-> VHG reset decrement;
    "VHG reset stage 115"
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; -> VHG reset decrement;
    "VHG reset stage 110"
	[VHGcollector] = 0; [VHGautoscreenoff] = 0; [VHGautoscreentick] = 100;
	-> VHG reset decrement;
    "VHG reset stage 101"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGresetglitchtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG reset decrement;
    "VHG reset stage 75"
	[VHGelight] = 0; [VHGilight] = 1; [VHLpower] = 1; -> VHG reset decrement;
    "VHG reset stage 10"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGresetstandbytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
    "VHG reset decrement"
	[VHGresetcount]-;
    "VHG reset step done"
	end;

"VHG emergency step"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:2370-2393.  Exhausting both flight power and lithium blacks
	  the computers, selects the flickering emergency lamps, and disconnects
	  navigation.  Restored power starts the original 200-step restart path. )
	A = [MgPwr]; ? A > 15000 -> VHG emergency powered;
	A = [MgCharge]; ? A != 0 -> VHG emergency powered;
	A = [VHGelight]; ? A != 0 -> VHG emergency hold;
	[VHGelight] = 1;
	=> VHG local reset;
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0;
    "VHG emergency hold"
	[VHGsync] = 0; [VHGantirad] = 0; [MgAnti] = 0;
	[VHGfinder] = 0; [VHGamp] = 0; [VHSamp] = 0; [VHSready] = 0; [VHSdrawready] = 0;
	[MgApreached] = 0; [MgStspeed] = 0; [VHGcollector] = 0;
	-> VHG emergency step done;
    "VHG emergency powered"
	A = [VHGelight]; ? A = 0 -> VHG emergency step done;
	A = [VHGresetcount]; ? A != 0 -> VHG emergency step done;
	[VHGelight] = 0; [VHGilightlevel] = 0; [VHGilight] = 1; [VHLpower] = 1;
	[VHGresetcount] = 200;
    "VHG emergency step done"
	end;

"VHG light step"
	( The original internal lamp consumes one power unit per 84 seconds.
	  Its separate 0..63 illumination level rises or falls by one on every
	  authentic simulation tick instead of switching the hull instantly. )
	A = [VHGilightlevel]; A + [VHGilight];
	? A >= 0 -> VHG light level nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHG light level nonnegative"
	? A <= 63 -> VHG light level ready; A = 63;
    "VHG light level ready"
	[VHGilightlevel] = A;
	A = [VHGilight]; ? A != 1 -> VHG light step done;
	[VHGlighttick]+; A = [VHGlighttick]; ? A < 1529 -> VHG light step done;
	[VHGlighttick] = 0; [MgPwr]-;
    "VHG light step done"
	end;

"VHG navigation step"
	( Device power costs are advanced only by the 18.206-Hz simulation clock,
	  matching the original wall-time rates rather than presentation frames. )
	A = [VHGamp]; ? A = 0 -> VHG navigation amplifier off;
	[VHGamptick]+; A = [VHGamptick]; ? A < 746 -> VHG navigation amplifier off;
	[VHGamptick] = 0; [MgPwr]-;
    "VHG navigation amplifier off"
	A = [VHGfinder]; ? A = 0 -> VHG navigation finder off;
	[VHGfindertick]+; A = [VHGfindertick]; ? A < 2822 -> VHG navigation finder off;
	[VHGfindertick] = 0; [MgPwr]-;
    "VHG navigation finder off"
	A = [VHGsync]; ? A = 0 -> VHG navigation tracking off;
	[VHGsynctick]+; C = 528;
	? A = 1 -> VHG navigation tracking limit; C = 328;
	? A = 2 -> VHG navigation tracking limit; C = 1056;
	? A = 3 -> VHG navigation tracking limit; C = 127;
	? A = 4 -> VHG navigation tracking limit; C = 601;
    "VHG navigation tracking limit"
	A = [VHGsynctick]; ? A < C -> VHG navigation tracking position;
	[VHGsynctick] = 0; [MgPwr]-;
    "VHG navigation tracking position"
	[VHGtrackphase]+;
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG navigation tracking off;
	A = [MgIpreached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG navigation tracking off;
	=> VHG tracking apply;
    "VHG navigation tracking off"
	end;

"VHG preference step"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:3555-3562 advances the PFS auto-sleep countdown on the
	  authentic game loop. Navigation steering keeps the source's 1.5-degree
	  impulse and 1.1 damping in signed tenths of a degree. )
	A = [VHGautoscreenoff]; ? A = 0 -> VHG preference steering step;
	[VHGautoscreentick]-; A = [VHGautoscreentick]; ? A > 0 -> VHG preference steering step;
	[VHGautoscreentick] = 100; [VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0;
    "VHG preference steering step"
	A = [VHGnavfrac]; A + [VHGnavvel]; C = A; A / 10;
	A + [VHGnavbeta]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHG preference heading ready; A + 360;
    "VHG preference heading ready"
	[VHGnavbeta] = A;
	A = C; A / 10; A '* 10; C - A; [VHGnavfrac] = C;
	A = [VHGnavvel]; A '* 10; A / 11; [VHGnavvel] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG preference steering positive; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG preference steering positive"
	? A >= 5 -> VHG preference step done; [VHGnavvel] = 0;
    "VHG preference step done"
	end;

"VHG tracking apply"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:3167-3206.  Tracking moves the Stardrifter's galactic/local
	  position toward a point around the selected planet; it never moves the
	  player inside the ship.  Keep navigation_beta separate from cockpit look,
	  retain the source axis signs, and chase by 5 percent instead of snapping.
	  The gradual chase is important: a terminal approach can arrive from any
	  direction, and an instantaneous replacement made the globe jump/flicker. )
	A = [VHGplanet]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[VHGtrackray0] = [E plus 0]; [VHGtrackray1] = [E plus 1];
	( Select the factor before invoking the FPU helpers: those services use A,
	  so testing the old A afterwards selected a random tracking mode. )
	A = [VHGsync]; ? A = 1 -> VHG tracking factor fixed;
	? A = 2 -> VHG tracking factor far;
	? A = 3 -> VHG tracking factor synchronous;
	? A = 4 -> VHG tracking factor orbital;
	[FA0] = 33333333h; [FA1] = 3FF33333h;			( near: 1.2 )
	-> VHG tracking factor ready;
    "VHG tracking factor fixed"
	[FA0] = 9999999Ah; [FA1] = 3FFCCCCCh;			( fixed: 1.8 )
	-> VHG tracking factor ready;
    "VHG tracking factor far"
	[FA0] = 9999999Ah; [FA1] = 40159999h;			( far: 5.4 )
	-> VHG tracking factor ready;
    "VHG tracking factor synchronous"
	( Synchronous: hold_z = 1.8 + 0.1 * p_ray, exactly as the source. )
	[FA0] = [VHGtrackray0]; [FA1] = [VHGtrackray1];
	[FB0] = 9999999Ah; [FB1] = 3FB99999h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 9999999Ah; [FB1] = 3FFCCCCCh; => FAdd;
	-> VHG tracking factor ready;
    "VHG tracking factor orbital"
	[FA0] = CCCCCCCDh; [FA1] = 400CCCCCh;			( orbital: 3.6 )
    "VHG tracking factor ready"
	[FB0] = [VHGtrackray0]; [FB1] = [VHGtrackray1]; => FMul;
	[VHGtrackdist0] = [FA0]; [VHGtrackdist1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHGsync]; ? A = 3 -> VHG tracking time angle;
	? A = 4 -> VHG tracking time angle;
	[VHGtrackangle] = [VHGnavbeta]; -> VHG tracking angle ready;
	( Planet rotation and the Vimana orbit both depend on source UTC seconds.
	  Reuse the exact live rotation-period path already used for landings. )
    "VHG tracking time angle"
	[VHGNDplanet] = [VHGplanet]; [VHGNDphaseindex] = [VHGplanet]; [VHGNDmoonmass] = 0;
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGplanet]; A = [E]; [VHGNDowner] = A;
	? A < 0 -> VHG tracking time owner ready;
	E = nsptype; E + A; A = [E]; ? A = 10 -> VHG tracking time companion;
	[VHGNDphaseindex] = [VHGNDowner]; -> VHG tracking time owner ready;
    "VHG tracking time companion"
	[VHGNDmoonmass] = 1;
    "VHG tracking time owner ready"
	=> VHGND UTC seconds; => VHGND orbital phase;
	A = [VHGNDrotation]; [VHGtrackangle] = A;
	A = [VHGsync]; ? A = 3 -> VHG tracking angle ready;
	A = [VHGNDsecs]; A '* 7; A % 360; [VHGtrackangle] = A;
    "VHG tracking angle ready"
	[VHVangle] = [VHGtrackangle];
	=> VHV sincos;
	[FS0] = [VHVsin]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGtrackdist0]; [FA1] = [VHGtrackdist1]; => FMul;
	( desired x is -hold_z*ray*sin(angle) )
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 0; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FB0]; [FA1] = [FB1]; [FB0] = [FC0]; [FB1] = [FC1]; => FSub;
	[VHGtrackwantx0] = [FA0]; [VHGtrackwantx1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHVcos]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGtrackdist0]; [FA1] = [VHGtrackdist1]; => FMul;
	[VHGtrackwantz0] = [FA0]; [VHGtrackwantz1] = [FA1];
	( local += (desired-local)*0.05, including the source's vertical chase. )
	[FA0] = [VHGtrackwantx0]; [FA1] = [VHGtrackwantx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalx1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = 9999999Ah; [FB1] = 3FA99999h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalx1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocalx0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = 0; [FA1] = 0; [FB0] = [VHGlocaly0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaly1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = 9999999Ah; [FB1] = 3FA99999h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaly0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaly1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocaly0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaly1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGtrackwantz0]; [FA1] = [VHGtrackwantz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalz1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = 9999999Ah; [FB1] = 3FA99999h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalz1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocalz0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalz1] = [FA1];
	end;

"VHG rescue advance"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:1875-1883 and 2738-2755: a distance-dependent wait followed
	  by the full 120-second helper fly-by. The rational accumulator turns the
	  authentic 18.206-Hz simulation cadence back into whole source seconds. )
	A = [VHGrescueactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG rescue tick done;
	A = [VHGgburst]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG rescue signal done;
	A - 1; [VHGgburst] = A; ? A != 0 -> VHG rescue signal done;
	[VHGgburst] = 63; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsignaltext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
    "VHG rescue signal done"
	A = [VHGrescueacc]; A + 1000; [VHGrescueacc] = A;
	? A < 18206 -> VHG rescue tick done;
	A - 18206; [VHGrescueacc] = A;
	A = [VHGrescuedelay]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG rescue flyby second;
	A - 1; [VHGrescuedelay] = A; ? A > 0 -> VHG rescue tick done;
	[VHGgburst] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGhelparrivetext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG rescue tick done;
    "VHG rescue flyby second"
	[VHGrescuetick]+; A = [VHGrescuetick];
	? A = 20 -> VHG rescue transfer;
	? A = 100 -> VHG rescue departing;
	? A < 120 -> VHG rescue tick done; -> VHG rescue finished;
    "VHG rescue transfer"
	A = [MgCharge]; ? A >= 3 -> VHG rescue transfer notice; [MgCharge] = 3;
    "VHG rescue transfer notice"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGhelptransfertext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG rescue tick done;
    "VHG rescue departing"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGhelpdeparttext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG rescue tick done;
    "VHG rescue finished"
	[VHGrescueactive] = 0; [VHGrescuetick] = 0; [VHGrescuedelay] = 0; [VHGrescueacc] = 0; [VHGgburst] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGhelpdonetext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
    "VHG rescue tick done"
	end;

"VHG local reset"
	[VHGlocalactive] = 0; [VHGlocaltarget] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGlandpending] = 0;
	[VHGlandingselect] = 0; [VHGlandingview] = 1; [VHGlocalphasetick] = 0;
	[MgIpreaching] = 0; [MgIpreached] = 0;
	end;

"VHG local start"
	( Begin at the selected body's retained orbital radius. The local state
	  stays separate from galactic dzat, so later STAR/NEXT travel remains
	  anchored at the calibrated star. )
	[VHGcollector] = 0;
	[VHGlocalactive] = 1; [VHGlocaltarget] = [VHGplanet]; [VHGlocalacc] = 0;
	[VHGlocalphasetick] = 0;
	[VHGlocalresident1] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGlocalresident2] = 0FFFFFFFFh;
	[VHGlocalpmapbody] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGlocalmmapbody] = 0FFFFFFFFh;
	( Source planets() waits for the 25-radius surface LOD before building a
	  resident map.  Orbital phase setup is still needed from approach start. )
	[VHGNDlon] = [VHGlandinglon]; [VHGNDlat] = [VHGlandinglat];
	=> VHG prepare planet; => VHG fpu clean;
	[VHGNDplanet] = [VHGplanet]; => VHGND general phase setup; => VHG fpu clean;
	( fix_local_target(): planet_xyz, then current Stardrifter minus that
	  absolute target.  Keeping the integrator in a target-relative frame is
	  algebraically identical and avoids disturbing galactic navigation. )
	[VHGNDvecindex] = [VHGplanet]; => VHGND absolute body vector;
	[VHGlocaltx0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [VHGlocaltx1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[VHGlocalty0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [VHGlocalty1] = [VHGNDvecy1];
	[VHGlocaltz0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [VHGlocaltz1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
	[FA0] = [MgDzatX0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatX1]; [FI] = [VHTtx]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [MgDzatX0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatX1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaltx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltx1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocalx0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [MgDzatY0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatY1]; [FI] = [VHTty]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [MgDzatY0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatY1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalty0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalty1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocaly0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaly1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatZ1]; [FI] = [VHTtz]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatZ1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaltz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltz1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocalz0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalz1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalx0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalx1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocalinit0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalinit1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaly0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaly1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaly0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaly1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalinit0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalinit1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocalinit0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalinit1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalz0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalz1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalinit0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalinit1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt;
	[VHGlocalinit0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalinit1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = [MgK1000]; [FB1] = [MgK1001]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocalcur0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalcur1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHGplanet]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1]; => F32Narrow; [MgPray] = [FS0];
	[MgPlX0] = 0; [MgPlX1] = 0; [MgPlY0] = 0; [MgPlY1] = 0; [MgPlZ0] = 0; [MgPlZ1] = 0;
	[MgIpreaching] = 1; [MgIpreached] = 0; [MgStatus] = 5;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGapproachtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;

"VHG local step"
	( Run one exact MG approach frame without replacing galactic position. )
	[VHGgalx0] = [MgDzatX0]; [VHGgalx1] = [MgDzatX1];
	[VHGgaly0] = [MgDzatY0]; [VHGgaly1] = [MgDzatY1];
	[VHGgalz0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [VHGgalz1] = [MgDzatZ1];
	[MgDzatX0] = [VHGlocalx0]; [MgDzatX1] = [VHGlocalx1];
	[MgDzatY0] = [VHGlocaly0]; [MgDzatY1] = [VHGlocaly1];
	[MgDzatZ0] = [VHGlocalz0]; [MgDzatZ1] = [VHGlocalz1];
	[MgInitD0] = [VHGlocalinit0]; [MgInitD1] = [VHGlocalinit1];
	[MgCur0] = [VHGlocalcur0]; [MgCur1] = [VHGlocalcur1];
		[VHGlocalbatch] = 32; A = [VHGfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local batch ready;
		( 32 * 18.206 / 60 = 9.709866... approach steps per fast frame. )
		A = [VHGlocalacc]; A + 582592; [VHGlocalacc] = A;
		A '/ 60000; [VHGlocalbatch] = A; A '* 60000;
		C = [VHGlocalacc]; C - A; [VHGlocalacc] = C;
	    "VHG local batch ready"
	"VHG local substep"
	=> MGD reset; [MgNframes] = 1; => MG approach loop;
	A = [MgIpreached]; ? A != 0 -> VHG local substeps done;
	[VHGlocalbatch]-; A = [VHGlocalbatch]; ? A != 0 -> VHG local substep;
	"VHG local substeps done"
	[VHGlocalx0] = [MgDzatX0]; [VHGlocalx1] = [MgDzatX1];
	[VHGlocaly0] = [MgDzatY0]; [VHGlocaly1] = [MgDzatY1];
	[VHGlocalz0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [VHGlocalz1] = [MgDzatZ1];
	[VHGlocalcur0] = [MgCur0]; [VHGlocalcur1] = [MgCur1];
	[MgDzatX0] = [VHGgalx0]; [MgDzatX1] = [VHGgalx1];
	[MgDzatY0] = [VHGgaly0]; [MgDzatY1] = [VHGgaly1];
	[MgDzatZ0] = [VHGgalz0]; [MgDzatZ1] = [VHGgalz1];
	( secs is integral in the source.  One phase refresh per authentic second
	  keeps the visible orbit and rotation current without redundant UTC calls. )
	[VHGlocalphasetick]+; A = [VHGlocalphasetick]; ? A < 18 -> VHG local step done;
	[VHGlocalphasetick] = 0; [VHGNDplanet] = [VHGplanet]; => VHGND general phase setup;
    "VHG local step done"
	end;

"VHG local render"
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local render done;
	=> VHG local resident scan;
	( planet_xyz(selected) places the approached world in the generated
	  system.  The fine-approach integrator stores the Stardrifter relative
	  to that world, so the primary star is -planet_xyz-ship. )
	[VHGNDvecindex] = [VHGplanet]; => VHGND absolute body vector;
	[VHGlocaltx0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [VHGlocaltx1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[VHGlocalty0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [VHGlocalty1] = [VHGNDvecy1];
	[VHGlocaltz0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [VHGlocaltz1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
	=> VHG local star coords;
	=> VHT visibility; A = [VHTwhiteok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local star premask;
	[PGFt] = [VHTRay]; => PGF ldf32; [VHTtmp0] = [FA0]; [VHTtmp1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 3; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHTtmp0]; [FA1] = [VHTtmp1]; => FMul; => F32Narrow; [WHmag] = [FS0];
	[WHfgm] = 3E99999Ah; [WHshape] = 2; [WHsun] = 0; [WHdstreg] = RGADP; => SP white;
    "VHG local star premask"
	( planets() submits every companion-star corona before mask_pixels(). )
	=> VHG local companion coronas;
	( lens_flares_for's primary-star call receives the near star's own
	  coordinates after the companion loop.  Restore those arguments because
	  every sphere/flare helper shares SFXX/SFYY/SFZZ in this port. )
	=> VHG local star coords; => VHT premask; => VHT mask page;
	A = [VHTglobeok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local star far;
	[GBmag] = [VHTRay]; => SP globe setup; A = [GBok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local planet render;
	( The local-system path draws the same near star at a different translated
	  position.  It must share the source-rate class phase with VHT render. )
	=> VHT phase advance; A = [VHTrenderphase]; [GBstart] = A;
	[GBcmask] = 64; [GBsat] = [VHTglobesat];
	[GBtabreg] = RGGLB; [GBtapreg] = RGSBG; [GBdstreg] = RGADP;
	[GBbytes] = GLBYTES; [GBbubble] = 0; => SP globe raster; => VHT texture cycle;
	-> VHG local planet render;
    "VHG local star far"
	A = [VHTfarok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local planet render; => VHT far pixel;
    "VHG local planet render"
	( planets() traverses every generated body.  The selected body retains
	  the generated surface map below; the rest remain source far-pixels at
	  their live planet_xyz() positions until their own close-resident pass. )
	[VHGlocalbody] = 0;
    "VHG local body loop"
	A = [VHGlocalbody]; ? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG local selected render;
	? A = [VHGplanet] -> VHG local body next;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = A; => VHGND absolute body vector;
	=> VHG local body relative;
	A = [VHGlocalbody]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[VHGlocalray0] = [E plus 0]; [VHGlocalray1] = [E plus 1];
	E = nsptype; E + [VHGlocalbody]; A = [E]; ? A != 10 -> VHG local ordinary body;
	=> VHG local far pixel; -> VHG local body next;
    "VHG local ordinary body"
	( Secondary bodies use the same point/ring/crescent/surface LOD gates and
	  the source's two physical resident maps. )
	[VHGlocalringbody] = [VHGlocalbody]; => VHG local body distance;
	[VHGlocalshowrings] = 0; [VHGlocalshowdisc] = 0; [VHGlocalringsurface] = 0;
	[FI] = 250; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocallim0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocallim1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocallim0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocallim1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local other disc gate;
	A = [VHGlocalbody]; A < 1; E = nspring; E + A;
	A = [E plus 0]; A | [E plus 1]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local other disc gate;
	[VHGlocalshowrings] = 1; [VHGlocalmultmask] = 64;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FQuo;
	[FB0] = 9999999Ah; [FB1] = 3FA99999h; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	[VHGlocalringlayers] = [FI];
    "VHG local other disc gate"
	[FI] = 100; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocallim0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocallim1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocallim0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocallim1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local other point;
	[VHGlocalshowdisc] = 1; => VHG local ring viewpoint;
	[FI] = 25; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocallim0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocallim1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocallim0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocallim1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local other draw back;
	=> VHG local ensure surface; A = [VHGlocalsurfaceok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local other draw back;
	[VHGlocalringsurface] = 1;
    "VHG local other draw back"
	A = [VHGlocalshowrings]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local other crescent;
	[VHGlocalmultmask] = 64; A = [VHGlocalringsurface]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local other back mask ready;
	[VHGlocalmultmask] = [VHGlocalmask];
    "VHG local other back mask ready"
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A + 180; ? A '<= 359 -> VHG local other back angle; A - 360;
    "VHG local other back angle"
	[VHGlocalringstart] = A; => VHG local ring;
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A - 180; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local other front restored; A + 360;
    "VHG local other front restored"
	[VHGlocalringstart] = A;
    "VHG local other crescent"
	[VHGNDplanet] = [VHGlocalbody]; => VHGND exact viewpoint;
	[VHGlocalbodyview] = [VHGNDviewpoint]; [VHGNDplanet] = [VHGplanet];
	A = [VHGlocalringsurface]; ? A != 0 -> VHG local other surface;
	=> VHG local ring center coords;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => F32Narrow; [GLmag] = [FS0];
	=> SP glow setup; A = [GBok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local other foreground;
	A = 124; A - [VHGlocalbodyview]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG local other terminator; A + 360;
    "VHG local other terminator"
	[VHGlocaltermstart] = A;
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A - [VHGlocaltermstart]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG local other glow angle; A + 360;
    "VHG local other glow angle"
	[GLstart] = A; [GLarc] = 130; [GLcol] = 127;
	[GLtabreg] = RGGLB; [GLdstreg] = RGADP; [GLnb] = GLBYTES; => SP glow raster;
	-> VHG local other foreground;
    "VHG local other surface"
	=> VHG local ring center coords;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => F32Narrow; [GBmag] = [FS0];
	=> SP globe setup; A = [GBok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local other foreground;
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A + [VHGNDrotation]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG local other rotation ready; A + 360;
    "VHG local other rotation ready"
	[GBstart] = A; [GBcmask] = [VHGlocalmask]; [GBsat] = 0;
	[GBtabreg] = RGGLB; [GBtapreg] = [VHGlocaltapreg]; [GBdstreg] = RGADP; [GBbytes] = GLBYTES;

    "VHG local other foreground"
	A = [VHGlocalshowrings]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local body next;
	=> VHG local ring; -> VHG local body next;
    "VHG local other point"
	=> VHG local ring center coords; => VHG local far pixel;
	A = [VHGlocalshowrings]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local body next;
	=> VHG local ring viewpoint;
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A + 180; ? A '<= 359 -> VHG local other point back; A - 360;
    "VHG local other point back"
	[VHGlocalringstart] = A; => VHG local ring;
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A - 180; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local other point front; A + 360;
    "VHG local other point front"

	=> VHG local ring center coords; => VHG local far pixel;
    "VHG local body next"
	[VHGlocalbody]+; -> VHG local body loop;

    "VHG local selected render"
	=> VHG local center coords;
	[VHGlocalringbody] = [VHGplanet];
	[VHGNDplanet] = [VHGplanet]; => VHGND exact viewpoint;
	[VHGlocalbodyview] = [VHGNDviewpoint];
	A = [VHGplanet]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[VHGlocalray0] = [E plus 0]; [VHGlocalray1] = [E plus 1];
	E = nsptype; E + [VHGplanet]; A = [E]; ? A != 10 -> VHG local selected planet;
	=> VHG local far pixel; -> VHG local render done;
    "VHG local selected planet"
	=> VHG local body distance; => VHG local ring viewpoint;
	( Source LOD gates: rings at d < 250*ray, disc at d < 100*ray,
	  retained surface at d < 25*ray.  Decide all three before drawing so
	  the back half of a close ring receives the source palette mask. )
	[VHGlocalshowrings] = 0; [VHGlocalshowdisc] = 0; [VHGlocalringsurface] = 0;
	[FI] = 250; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocallim0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocallim1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocallim0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocallim1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local selected disc gate;
	A = [VHGplanet]; A < 1; E = nspring; E + A;
	A = [E plus 0]; A | [E plus 1]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local selected disc gate;
	[VHGlocalshowrings] = 1;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FQuo;
	[FB0] = 9999999Ah; [FB1] = 3FA99999h; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	[VHGlocalringlayers] = [FI];
    "VHG local selected disc gate"
	[FI] = 100; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocallim0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocallim1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocallim0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocallim1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local selected point;
	[VHGlocalshowdisc] = 1;
	[FI] = 25; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocallim0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocallim1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocallim0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocallim1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local selected draw back;
	=> VHG local ensure surface; A = [VHGlocalsurfaceok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local selected draw back;
	[VHGlocalringsurface] = 1;
    "VHG local selected draw back"
	A = [VHGlocalshowrings]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local selected disc;
	[VHGlocalmultmask] = 64; A = [VHGlocalringsurface]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local selected back mask ready;
	[VHGlocalmultmask] = [VHGlocalmask];
    "VHG local selected back mask ready"
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A + 180; ? A '<= 359 -> VHG local selected back angle; A - 360;
    "VHG local selected back angle"
	[VHGlocalringstart] = A; => VHG local ring;
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A - 180; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local selected front angle restored; A + 360;
    "VHG local selected front angle restored"
	[VHGlocalringstart] = A;
    "VHG local selected disc"
	A = [VHGlocalringsurface]; ? A != 0 -> VHG local selected surface;
	=> VHG local center coords;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => F32Narrow; [GLmag] = [FS0];
	=> SP glow setup; A = [GBok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local selected foreground;
	( glowinglobe receives start=viewer plwp and the star-facing
	  terminator_start=(89+35)-cplx_planet_viewpoint. )
	A = 124; A - [VHGlocalbodyview]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG local crescent terminator; A + 360;
    "VHG local crescent terminator"
	[VHGlocaltermstart] = A;
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A - [VHGlocaltermstart]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG local crescent angle; A + 360;
    "VHG local crescent angle"
	[GLstart] = A; [GLarc] = 130; [GLcol] = 127;
	[GLtabreg] = RGGLB; [GLdstreg] = RGADP; [GLnb] = GLBYTES;
	=> SP glow raster; -> VHG local selected foreground;
    "VHG local selected surface"
	=> VHG local center coords;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => F32Narrow; [GBmag] = [FS0];
	=> SP globe setup; A = [GBok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local selected foreground;
	( globe(start) rotates the observer-facing map longitude, not the
	  independently derived star-lighting longitude. )
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A + [VHGNDrotation]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG local rotation ready; A + 360;
    "VHG local rotation ready"
	[GBstart] = A; [GBcmask] = [VHGlocalmask]; [GBsat] = 0;
	[GBtabreg] = RGGLB; [GBtapreg] = [VHGlocaltapreg]; [GBdstreg] = RGADP; [GBbytes] = GLBYTES;
	[GBbubble] = [VHGlocalbubble];
	=> SP globe raster;
    "VHG local selected foreground"
	A = [VHGlocalshowrings]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local render done;
	=> VHG local ring; -> VHG local render done;

    "VHG local selected point"
	=> VHG local center coords; => VHG local far pixel;
	A = [VHGlocalshowrings]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local render done;
	[VHGlocalmultmask] = 64;
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A + 180; ? A '<= 359 -> VHG local selected point back angle; A - 360;
    "VHG local selected point back angle"
	[VHGlocalringstart] = A; => VHG local ring;
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A - 180; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local selected point front angle; A + 360;
    "VHG local selected point front angle"
	[VHGlocalringstart] = A; => VHG local ring;
	=> VHG local center coords; => VHG local far pixel;
"VHG local render done"
	end;

"VHG local companion coronas"
	[VHGlocalbody] = 0; [VHGlocalcompi] = 0FFFFFFFFh;
    "VHG local companion corona loop"
	A = [VHGlocalbody]; ? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG local companion corona done;
	E = nsptype; E + A; A = [E]; ? A != 10 -> VHG local companion corona next;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = [VHGlocalbody]; => VHGND absolute body vector;
	=> VHG local body relative; => VHG local body distance;
	[VHGlocalcompi] = [VHGlocalbody]; [VHGlocalcomptype] = 10;
	[VHGlocalcompx0] = [VHGlocalringcx0]; [VHGlocalcompx1] = [VHGlocalringcx1];
	[VHGlocalcompy0] = [VHGlocalringcy0]; [VHGlocalcompy1] = [VHGlocalringcy1];
	[VHGlocalcompz0] = [VHGlocalringcz0]; [VHGlocalcompz1] = [VHGlocalringcz1];
	[VHGlocalcompdist0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [VHGlocalcompdist1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[VHGlocalcompflareok] = 0; [VHGlocalcompflarecx] = 0; [VHGlocalcompflarecy] = 0;
	( nearstar_p_qsortdist is float in the source, then promoted to p_dsd. )
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1]; => F32Narrow; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGlocaldist0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaldist1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHGlocalbody]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[VHGlocalray0] = [E plus 0]; [VHGlocalray1] = [E plus 1];
	[VHGlocalcompray0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [VHGlocalcompray1] = [VHGlocalray1];
	( Native planets() reseeds each companion from its body index and the
	  system star's signed X coordinate, then passes the narrowed
	  0.15-fast_flandom()*0.3 factor to whiteglobe. )
	A = [VHGlocalbody]; A + [VHTtx]; => SU fast srand;
	=> VHG local fast flandom;
	[FB0] = 33333333h; [FB1] = 3FD33333h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = 33333333h; [FA1] = 3FC33333h; => FSub; => F32Narrow;
	[WHfgm] = [FS0];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1];
	[FI] = 3; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; [WHmag] = [FS0];
	[WHshape] = 2; [WHsun] = 0; [WHdstreg] = RGADP; => SP white;
	( Companion stars use the same condition-0, 60-spoke flare when
	  5*ray < p_dsd < 1000*ray. )
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1];
	[FI] = 5; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG local companion corona next;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1];
	[FI] = 1000; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local companion corona next;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1]; => F32Narrow; [VHFray] = [FS0];
	[VHFspaceray0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [VHFspaceray1] = [VHGlocalray1];
	[VHFdist0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [VHFdist1] = [VHGlocaldist1]; => VH space flare;
	[VHGlocalcompflareok] = [VHFok]; [VHGlocalcompflarecx] = [VHFcx];
	[VHGlocalcompflarecy] = [VHFcy];
    "VHG local companion corona next"
	[VHGlocalbody]+; -> VHG local companion corona loop;
    "VHG local companion corona done"
	end;

"VHG local resident scan"
	( NOCTIS-0.CPP:5290-5317.  Find the two nearest bodies without changing
	  their generated order.  When the nearest is a moon, the second resident
	  is the nearest primary, preserving one useful body for each physical map. )
	[VHGlocalresident1] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGlocalresident2] = 0FFFFFFFFh;
	[VHGlocalbody] = 0;
    "VHG local resident scan body"
	A = [VHGlocalbody]; ? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG local resident pair ready;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = A; => VHGND absolute body vector; => VHG local body relative;
	=> VHG local body distance;
	A = [VHGlocalresident1]; ? A != 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG local resident compare first;
	[VHGlocalresident1] = [VHGlocalbody];
	[VHGlocalnear10] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [VHGlocalnear11] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	-> VHG local resident scan next;
    "VHG local resident compare first"
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalnear10]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalnear11]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A '>= 0 -> VHG local resident compare second;
	[VHGlocalresident2] = [VHGlocalresident1];
	[VHGlocalnear20] = [VHGlocalnear10]; [VHGlocalnear21] = [VHGlocalnear11];
	[VHGlocalresident1] = [VHGlocalbody];
	[VHGlocalnear10] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [VHGlocalnear11] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	-> VHG local resident scan next;
    "VHG local resident compare second"
	A = [VHGlocalresident2]; ? A != 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG local resident compare second distance;
	[VHGlocalresident2] = [VHGlocalbody];
	[VHGlocalnear20] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [VHGlocalnear21] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	-> VHG local resident scan next;
    "VHG local resident compare second distance"
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalnear20]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalnear21]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A '>= 0 -> VHG local resident scan next;
	[VHGlocalresident2] = [VHGlocalbody];
	[VHGlocalnear20] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [VHGlocalnear21] = [VHGlocaldist1];
    "VHG local resident scan next"
	[VHGlocalbody]+; -> VHG local resident scan body;
    "VHG local resident pair ready"
	A = [VHGlocalresident1]; ? A = 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG local resident scan done;
	E = nspowner; E + A; A = [E]; ? A < 0 -> VHG local resident scan done;
	( Closest is a moon: replace the generic runner-up with the nearest primary. )
	[VHGlocalresident2] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGlocalbody] = 0;
    "VHG local resident primary body"
	A = [VHGlocalbody]; ? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG local resident scan done;
	E = nspowner; E + A; C = [E]; ? C >= 0 -> VHG local resident primary next;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = A; => VHGND absolute body vector; => VHG local body relative;
	=> VHG local body distance;
	A = [VHGlocalresident2]; ? A != 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG local resident primary compare;
	[VHGlocalresident2] = [VHGlocalbody];
	[VHGlocalnear20] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [VHGlocalnear21] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	-> VHG local resident primary next;
    "VHG local resident primary compare"
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaldist1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalnear20]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalnear21]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A '>= 0 -> VHG local resident primary next;
	[VHGlocalresident2] = [VHGlocalbody];
	[VHGlocalnear20] = [VHGlocaldist0]; [VHGlocalnear21] = [VHGlocaldist1];
    "VHG local resident primary next"
	[VHGlocalbody]+; -> VHG local resident primary body;
    "VHG local resident scan done"
	end;

"VHG local ensure surface"
	( resident_map1/2 are generation tickets, while p_background and
	  s_background remain the two physical caches. )
	[VHGlocalsurfaceok] = 0;
	A = [VHGlocalringbody]; ? A = [VHGlocalresident1] -> VHG local surface resident;
	? A != [VHGlocalresident2] -> VHG local surface done;
    "VHG local surface resident"
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGlocalringbody]; A = [E]; ? A < 0 -> VHG local surface primary;
	[VHGlocalmask] = 128; [VHGlocaltapreg] = RGSBG;
	A = [VHGlocalmmapbody]; ? A = [VHGlocalringbody] -> VHG local surface style;
	[VHGlocalsavedplanet] = [VHGplanet]; [VHGplanet] = [VHGlocalringbody];
	=> VHG prepare planet; => VHG fpu clean; => VHGND globe surface; => VHG fpu clean;
	[VHGlocalmmapbody] = [VHGlocalringbody]; -> VHG local surface restore;
    "VHG local surface primary"
	[VHGlocalmask] = 192; [VHGlocaltapreg] = RGPBG;
	A = [VHGlocalpmapbody]; ? A = [VHGlocalringbody] -> VHG local surface style;
	[VHGlocalsavedplanet] = [VHGplanet]; [VHGplanet] = [VHGlocalringbody];
	=> VHG prepare planet; => VHG fpu clean; => VHGND globe surface; => VHG fpu clean;
	[VHGlocalpmapbody] = [VHGlocalringbody];
    "VHG local surface restore"
	[VHGplanet] = [VHGlocalsavedplanet]; => VHG prepare planet;
	[VHGNDplanet] = [VHGplanet]; => VHGND general phase setup; => VHG fpu clean;
	"VHG local surface style"
	[VHGlocalbubble] = 0; E = nsptype; E + [VHGlocalringbody]; A = [E];
	? A = 2 -> VHG local surface bubble; ? A = 3 -> VHG local surface bubble;
	? A = 6 -> VHG local surface bubble; ? A = 8 -> VHG local surface bubble;
	? A != 9 -> VHG local surface styled;
    "VHG local surface bubble"
	[VHGlocalbubble] = 1;
    "VHG local surface styled"
	[VHGlocalsurfaceok] = 1;
    "VHG local surface done"
	end;

"VHG local center coords"
	[FA0] = 0; [FA1] = 0; [FB0] = [VHGlocalx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalx1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocalringcx0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringcx1] = [FA1]; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = 0; [FA1] = 0; [FB0] = [VHGlocaly0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaly1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocalringcy0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringcy1] = [FA1]; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = 0; [FA1] = 0; [FB0] = [VHGlocalz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalz1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocalringcz0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringcz1] = [FA1]; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF sa;
	end;

"VHG local ring viewpoint"
	( planet_viewpoint() selects the integral longitude nearest the observer.
	  The retained center is body minus observer, so negate its X/Z pair. )
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringcz0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringcz1]; => FNeg;
	[VHGlocaltmp0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaltmp1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringcx0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringcx1]; => FNeg;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaltmp0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaltmp1]; => FAtan2;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 40668000h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 54442D18h; [FB1] = 400921FBh; => FQuo; => FToIntNear;
	A = [FI]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local ring viewpoint normalized; A + 360;
    "VHG local ring viewpoint normalized"
	C = 359; C - A; C % 360; ? C >= 0 -> VHG local ring viewpoint ready; C + 360;
    "VHG local ring viewpoint ready"
	[VHGlocalringstart] = C;
	end;

"VHG local ring"
	( NOCTIS-0.CPP:5200-5246.  Each call reseeds fast_random from the
	  retained final ring radius, so the two half-rings share the same
	  layered particle structure while straddling the planet raster. )
	A = [VHGlocalringbody]; A < 1; E = nspring; E + A;
	[VHGlocalringray0] = [E plus 0]; [VHGlocalringray1] = [E plus 1];
	A = [E plus 0]; A | [E plus 1]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local ring done;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringray1];
	[FB0] = EB851EB8h; [FB1] = 3F7EB851h; => FMul;
	[VHGlocalringstep0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringstep1] = [FA1];
	=> VHG local ring tilt;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringray1];
	[FB0] = 00000000h; [FB1] = 40C38800h; => FMul;
	[VHGlocaltmp0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaltmp1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGlocalringbody]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [VHGlocaltmp0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltmp1]; => FAdd;
	=> FToIntChop; A = [FI]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 31; => SU fast raw; A = [SUfval]; A + 1; A - [VHGlocalringlayers];
	[VHGlocalringb] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 3; => SU fast raw; A = [SUfval]; A + 1; [VHGlocalringparts] = A;
    "VHG local ring layer"
	A = [VHGlocalringb]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG local ring done;
	[SUfmask] = 7; => SU fast raw; A = [SUfval]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local ring sparse;
	[VHGlocalspreads] = 1; [SUfmask] = 7; => SU fast raw;
	A = [SUfval]; A + 1; [VHGlocalringc] = A; -> VHG local ring cadence ready;
    "VHG local ring sparse"
	[VHGlocalspreads] = 0; [SUfmask] = 3; => SU fast raw;
	A = [SUfval]; A + 1; [VHGlocalringc] = A;
    "VHG local ring cadence ready"
	A = [VHGlocalringstart]; A % [VHGlocalringc];
	C = [VHGlocalringstart]; C - A; [VHGlocalringa] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 1; => SU fast raw; C = [SUfval]; => SU fast raw;
	C + [SUfval]; [VHGlocaleffect] = C;
	[VHGlocalringn] = [VHGlocalringc];
    "VHG local ring arc"
	A = [VHGlocalringn]; A + [VHGlocalringc]; ? A '>= 180 -> VHG local ring layer advance;
	[SUfmask] = 1; => SU fast raw;
	A = [SUfval]; A + [VHGlocalringc]; [VHGlocalringm] = A;
	C = [VHGlocalringn]; C + A; [VHGlocalringn] = C;
	C = [VHGlocalringa]; C + A; ? C '<= 360 -> VHG local ring angle ready; C - 360;
    "VHG local ring angle ready"
	[VHGlocalringa] = C; [VHVangle] = C; => VHV sincos;
	( sy = oy - ringtilt*sin(a). )
	[FS0] = [VHVsin]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringtilt0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringtilt1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocaltmp0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaltmp1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringcy0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringcy1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaltmp0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltmp1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocalringsy0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringsy1] = [FA1];
	( sx = ox + ringray*sin(a). )
	[FS0] = [VHVsin]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringray0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringray1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringcx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringcx1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocalringsx0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringsx1] = [FA1];
	( sz = oz + ringray*cos(a). )
	[FS0] = [VHVcos]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringray0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringray1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringcz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringcz1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocalringsz0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringsz1] = [FA1];
	[VHGlocalringpart] = [VHGlocalringparts];
    "VHG local ring particle"
	A = [VHGlocalringpart]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG local ring arc;
	( sz += step - fast_flandom()*step. )
	=> VHG local fast flandom;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringstep0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringstep1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocaltmp0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaltmp1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringstep0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringstep1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaltmp0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltmp1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringsz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringsz1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocalringsz0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringsz1] = [FA1];
	( sx += step - fast_flandom()*step. )
	=> VHG local fast flandom;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringstep0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringstep1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocaltmp0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaltmp1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringstep0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringstep1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaltmp0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltmp1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringsx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringsx1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocalringsx0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringsx1] = [FA1];
	=> VHG local ring point coords;
	[VHGlocalray0] = 24DD2F1Bh; [VHGlocalray1] = BFA58106h;
	=> VHG local far pixel;
	[VHGlocalringpart]-; -> VHG local ring particle;
    "VHG local ring layer advance"
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringray1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringstep0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringstep1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocalringray0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringray1] = [FA1];
	[SUfmask] = 7; => SU fast raw; A = [SUfval]; ? A != 0 -> VHG local ring radius ready;
	[FI] = 5; => IntToF; [FB0] = [VHGlocalringstep0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringstep1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringray0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringray1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGlocalringray0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringray1] = [FA1];
    "VHG local ring radius ready"
	=> VHG local ring tilt;
	[VHGlocalringb]-; -> VHG local ring layer;
    "VHG local ring done"
	[VHGlocalspreads] = 1; [VHGlocaleffect] = 0;
	A = [VHGlocalringbody]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[VHGlocalray0] = [E plus 0]; [VHGlocalray1] = [E plus 1];
	end;

"VHG local ring tilt"
	[FA0] = 9999999Ah; [FA1] = 3FB99999h;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalringray0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalringray1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocaltmp0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaltmp1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHGlocalringbody]; A < 1; E = nsptilt; E + A;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocaltmp0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocaltmp1];
	[FB0] = [E plus 0]; [FB1] = [E plus 1]; => FMul;
	[VHGlocalringtilt0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringtilt1] = [FA1];
	end;

"VHG local fast flandom"
	[SUfmask] = 7FFFh; => SU fast raw; [FI] = [SUfval]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 7EDA0BE1h; [FB1] = 3F00000Eh; => FMul; => F32Narrow; => FLoadF32;
	end;

"VHG local ring point coords"
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringsx0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringsx1]; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringsy0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringsy1]; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringsz0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringsz1]; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF sa;
	end;

"VHG local ring center coords"
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringcx0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringcx1]; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringcy0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringcy1]; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalringcz0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalringcz1]; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF sa;
	end;

"VHG local body relative"
	( Current VHGND vector is body-from-star.  Convert it into
	  body-from-selected-world-from-Stardrifter for the local camera. )
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaltx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltx1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalx1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocalringcx0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringcx1] = [FA1]; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecy1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalty0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalty1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaly0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaly1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocalringcy0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringcy1] = [FA1]; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaltz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltz1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalz1]; => FSub;
	[VHGlocalringcz0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocalringcz1] = [FA1]; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF sa;
	end;

"VHG local star coords"
	( near star minus selected-world position minus Stardrifter offset. )
	[FA0] = 0; [FA1] = 0; [FB0] = [VHGlocaltx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltx1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalx1]; => FSub; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = 0; [FA1] = 0; [FB0] = [VHGlocalty0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalty1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaly0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaly1]; => FSub; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = 0; [FA1] = 0; [FB0] = [VHGlocaltz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltz1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalz1]; => FSub; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF sa;
	end;

"VHG local body distance"
	[PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF a; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF a; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF add;
	[PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF a; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF add;
	=> FSqrt; [VHGlocaldist0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaldist1] = [FA1];
	end;

"VHG local far pixel"
	( NOCTIS-0.CPP far_pixel_at(): source distance/radius brightness and
	  all four spread rings.  Ring particles also select the original
	  emitting, absorbing, or multicolour single-pixel compositor. )
	=> PG getcoords; A = [GCret]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local far done;
	A = [GCx]; ? A <= 10 -> VHG local far done; ? A >= 310 -> VHG local far done;
	A = [GCy]; ? A <= 10 -> VHG local far done; ? A >= 190 -> VHG local far done;
	[PGFi] = FSRZF; => PGF a; [FB0] = BC6A7EFAh; [FB1] = 3FD89374h; => FMul;
	[VHGlocaltmp0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocaltmp1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGlocalray0]; [FA1] = [VHGlocalray1];
	[FB0] = 33333333h; [FB1] = 40933333h; => FMul;
	[VHGlocallim0] = [FA0]; [VHGlocallim1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 64; => IntToF; [FB0] = [VHGlocaltmp0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaltmp1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [VHGlocallim0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocallim1]; => FAdd; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG local far colour low; A = 0;
    "VHG local far colour low"
	? A <= 63 -> VHG local far colour ready; A = 63;
    "VHG local far colour ready"
	[VHGlocalcentrecolour] = A; [VHGlocalcolour] = A;
	A = [VHGlocalspreads]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local far unspread;
	A = [VHGlocalcentrecolour]; ? A <= 7 -> VHG local far done;
	( edge_color_1: cardinal neighbours. )
	A > 1; [VHGlocalcolour] = A; ? A <= 7 -> VHG local far edge2;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 320; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 320; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 1; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 1; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
    "VHG local far edge2"
	A = [VHGlocalcentrecolour]; A > 2; [VHGlocalcolour] = A; ? A <= 7 -> VHG local far edge3;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 321; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 319; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 321; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 319; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
    "VHG local far edge3"
	A = [VHGlocalcentrecolour]; A > 3; [VHGlocalcolour] = A; ? A <= 7 -> VHG local far edge4;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 640; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 640; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 2; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 2; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
    "VHG local far edge4"
	A = [VHGlocalcentrecolour]; A > 4; [VHGlocalcolour] = A; ? A <= 7 -> VHG local far centre;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 641; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 639; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 641; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 639; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 322; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A - 318; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 322; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; A + 318; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
    "VHG local far centre"
	[VHGlocalcolour] = [VHGlocalcentrecolour];
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
	-> VHG local far done;
    "VHG local far unspread"
	A = [VHGlocalcentrecolour]; ? A = 0 -> VHG local far done;
	A = [GCy]; A '* 320; A + [GCx]; [VHGlocaloff] = A; => VHG local pixel add;
    "VHG local far done"
	end;

"VHG local pixel add"
	A = [VHGlocaloff]; A + 4; [VHGlocaloff] = A;
	? A < 4 -> VHG local pixel done; ? A '>= 64004 -> VHG local pixel done;
	[SPoff] = A; [SPreg] = RGADP; => SP get;
	A = [SPval]; ? A < 64 -> VHG local pixel done;
	C = A; C & 192; [VHGlocalband] = C; A & 63; [VHGlocallow] = A;
	A = [VHGlocaleffect]; ? A = 1 -> VHG local pixel absorbing;
	? A = 2 -> VHG local pixel multicolour;
	A = [VHGlocallow]; A + [VHGlocalcolour]; ? A <= 62 -> VHG local pixel ready; A = 62;
	-> VHG local pixel ready;
    "VHG local pixel absorbing"
	A = [VHGlocallow]; A + [VHGlocalcolour]; A > 1; -> VHG local pixel ready;
    "VHG local pixel multicolour"
	A = [VHGlocallow]; A + [VHGlocalcolour]; A > 1;
	C = [VHGlocalmultmask]; C & 192; [VHGlocalband] = C;
    "VHG local pixel ready"
	A | [VHGlocalband]; [SPval] = A; => SP put;
    "VHG local pixel done"
	end;

"VHG rescue render"
	A = [VHGrescueactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG rescue render done;
	A = [VHGrescuedelay]; ? A > 0 -> VHG rescue render done;
	( Source fly-by distance: a squared approach, a 16000-unit close orbit,
	  and the mirrored squared departure. )
	A = [VHGrescuetick]; ? A < 20 -> VHG rescue distance approach;
	? A > 100 -> VHG rescue distance depart;
	[VHGrescuedist] = 16000; -> VHG rescue distance ready;
    "VHG rescue distance approach"
	C = 20; C - A; -> VHG rescue distance square;
    "VHG rescue distance depart"
	C = A; C - 100;
    "VHG rescue distance square"
	C '* C; C '* 2000; C + 16000; [VHGrescuedist] = C;
    "VHG rescue distance ready"
	( Orbit the helper around the Stardrifter like other_vehicle_at(). )
	A = [VHGrescuetick]; A '* 6; [VHVangle] = A; => VHV sincos;
	[FS0] = [VHVcos]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGrescuedist]; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntChop; [VHGrescuex] = [FI];
	[FS0] = [VHVsin]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGrescuedist]; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntChop; [VHGrescuez] = [FI];
	A = [VHGrescuetick]; A '/ 2; [VHVangle] = A; => VHV sincos;
	[FS0] = [VHVsin]; => FLoadF32; [FI] = 4000; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	[VHGrescuey] = [FI];

	( Exact other_vehicle_at() ordering: the near dome halves, complete external
	  VEHICLE model, then the far dome halves. The y tests split the two full
	  hemispheres around +/-375 exactly as the source does. )
	[VHCroof] = 0;
	A = [VHGx]; A - [VHGrescuex]; [VHVcamxi] = A;
	A = [VHGy]; A - [VHGrescuey]; [VHVcamyi] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A - [VHGrescuez]; A + 3100; [VHVcamzi] = A;
	[VHValpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGbeta]; => VH set view;
	A = [VHGrescuey]; C = 0; C - 375; ? A <= C -> VHG rescue near upper done;
	[VHCyor] = 1; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;
    "VHG rescue near upper done"
	A = [VHGrescuey]; ? A >= 375 -> VHG rescue near lower done;
	A = 0; A - 1; [VHCyor] = A; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;
    "VHG rescue near lower done"

	A = [VHGx]; A - [VHGrescuex]; [VHVcamxi] = A;
	A = [VHGy]; A - [VHGrescuey]; [VHVcamyi] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A - [VHGrescuez]; [VHVcamzi] = A; => VH set view;
	( drawpv(vehicle_handle,0,0,ovhx,ovhy,ovhz,1): submit the helper's
	  unsplit solid-colour polygons through the handle's depth-sort arrays. )
