	[FI] = [VHGrescuex]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [DWcx] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGrescuey]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [DWcy] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGrescuez]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [DWcz] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGx]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [DWcamx] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGy]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [DWcamy] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGz]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [DWcamz] = [FS0];
	[PVh] = 0; [DWmode] = 0; [DWuds] = 1; => SP drawpv;
	[SPcull] = 0; [SPflar] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0; [PGtexf] = 0; => VH join mode0;

	A = [VHGz]; A - [VHGrescuez]; A + 3100; [VHVcamzi] = A; => VH set view;
	A = [VHGrescuey]; ? A <= 375 -> VHG rescue far upper done;
	[VHCyor] = 1; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;
    "VHG rescue far upper done"
	A = [VHGrescuey]; C = 0; C - 375; ? A >= C -> VHG rescue far lower done;
	A = 0; A - 1; [VHCyor] = A; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;
    "VHG rescue far lower done"
	( other_vehicle_at() finishes with the four source-positioned exterior
	  halogens.  Restore the helper-relative camera after the cupola's +3100
	  offset, then share lens_flares_for's projection, occlusion and HUD ghosts. )
	A = [VHGx]; A - [VHGrescuex]; [VHVcamxi] = A;
	A = [VHGy]; A - [VHGrescuey]; [VHVcamyi] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A - [VHGrescuez]; [VHVcamzi] = A;
	[VHValpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGbeta]; => VH set view;
	=> VHG visor flare mode;
	[VHFpx] = 3225; [VHFpy] = 0; [VHFpz] = 0; => VH rescue flare;
	A = 0; A - 3225; [VHFpx] = A; [VHFpz] = 0; => VH rescue flare;
	[VHFpx] = 3225; A = 0; A - 6150; [VHFpz] = A; => VH rescue flare;
	A = 0; A - 3225; [VHFpx] = A; A = 0; A - 6150; [VHFpz] = A;
	=> VH rescue flare;
    "VHG rescue render done"
	end;

"VHG palette"
	( The game's opening vehicle palette: cobalt-blue hull band. )
	=> PAL zero; => PAL range;
	[PVsrc] = range8088; [PVself] = 0;
	[PVfirst] = 0; [PVn] = 64;
	[PVfr] = 16; [PVfg] = 32; [PVfb] = 63; => PAL tavola;

	( Bootstrap bands used before the first system resolves.  VHG star palette
	  replaces 64..127, while VHGND globe surface replaces the resident moon
	  and planet bands 128..191 and 192..255 with surface()'s generated colours. )
	[PVsrc] = range8088; [PVself] = 0;
	[PVfirst] = 64; [PVn] = 64;
	[PVfr] = 32; [PVfg] = 48; [PVfb] = 63; => PAL tavola;
	[PVsrc] = range8088; [PVself] = 0;
	[PVfirst] = 128; [PVn] = 64;
	[PVfr] = 63; [PVfg] = 56; [PVfb] = 48; => PAL tavola;
	[PVsrc] = range8088; [PVself] = 0;
	[PVfirst] = 192; [PVn] = 64;
	[PVfr] = 40; [PVfg] = 52; [PVfb] = 63; => PAL tavola;
	end;

"VHG star palette"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:3666-3777.  A close star owns band 64..127, but that
	  band is three joined ramps: black to its class-specific convective
	  colour, through the stellar class colour, then into white saturation. )
	[VHGstarclass] = [VHTclass]; [VHGstarr] = [VHTred];
	[VHGstarg] = [VHTgreen]; [VHGstarb] = [VHTblue];
	A = [VHTclass]; ? A != 8 -> VHG star palette class ready;
	( Class 8 borrows a deterministic stellar class for its visible colour. )
	[FA0] = [VHTid0]; [FA1] = [VHTid1]; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; => SU fast srand; [SUfmask] = 7FFFh; => SU fast raw;
	A = [SUfval]; => BrtlSrand; A = 12; => BrtlRandom; [VHGstarclass] = A;
	A '* 3; E = VHTclassrgb; E + A;
	[VHGstarr] = [E plus 0]; [VHGstarg] = [E plus 1]; [VHGstarb] = [E plus 2];
    "VHG star palette class ready"
	A = [VHGstarclass]; ? A = 0 -> VHG star palette inner0;
	? A = 1 -> VHG star palette inner1; ? A = 2 -> VHG star palette inner2;
	? A = 3 -> VHG star palette inner3; ? A = 4 -> VHG star palette inner4;
	? A = 5 -> VHG star palette inner5; ? A = 6 -> VHG star palette inner6;
	? A = 7 -> VHG star palette inner7; ? A = 8 -> VHG star palette inner8;
	? A = 9 -> VHG star palette inner9; ? A = 10 -> VHG star palette inner10;
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 36; [VHGstarinnerG] = 48; [VHGstarinnerB] = 64;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner0"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 64; [VHGstarinnerG] = 54; [VHGstarinnerB] = 28;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner1"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 36; [VHGstarinnerG] = 50; [VHGstarinnerB] = 64;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner2"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 24; [VHGstarinnerG] = 32; [VHGstarinnerB] = 48;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner3"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 64; [VHGstarinnerG] = 24; [VHGstarinnerB] = 12;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner4"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 64; [VHGstarinnerG] = 40; [VHGstarinnerB] = 32;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner5"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 28; [VHGstarinnerG] = 20; [VHGstarinnerB] = 12;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner6"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 32; [VHGstarinnerG] = 32; [VHGstarinnerB] = 32;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner7"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 32; [VHGstarinnerG] = 44; [VHGstarinnerB] = 64;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner8"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 64; [VHGstarinnerG] = 60; [VHGstarinnerB] = 32;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner9"
	( Class 9's inner colour is drawn directly from the identity-seeded
	  fast generator, exactly like the source palette branch. )
	[FA0] = [VHTid0]; [FA1] = [VHTid1]; => FToIntChop; A = [FI]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 31; => SU fast raw; A = [SUfval]; A + 32; [VHGstarinnerR] = A;
	=> SU fast raw; A = [SUfval]; A + 32; [VHGstarinnerG] = A;
	=> SU fast raw; A = [SUfval]; A + 16; [VHGstarinnerB] = A;
	-> VHG star palette ready;
    "VHG star palette inner10"
	[VHGstarinnerR] = 32; [VHGstarinnerG] = 26; [VHGstarinnerB] = 22;
    "VHG star palette ready"
	[VHGstarpaletteok] = 0;
	end;

"VHG star palette update"
	( The source recomputes distance-raised colour targets every simulation
	  tick, then eases all six live components toward them by exactly one. )
	A = [VHTpalfar]; ? A = 0 -> VHG star palette close targets;
	[VHGstartargetR] = 48; [VHGstartargetG] = 56; [VHGstartargetB] = 64;
	[VHGstartargetinnerR] = 24; [VHGstartargetinnerG] = 32;
	[VHGstartargetinnerB] = 40; -> VHG star palette compare;

    "VHG star palette close targets"
	[VHGstartargetR] = [VHGstarr]; A = [VHGstarr];
	? A >= [VHTpalsat] -> VHG star palette target g;
	[VHGstartargetR] = [VHTpalsat];
    "VHG star palette target g"
	[VHGstartargetG] = [VHGstarg]; A = [VHGstarg];
	? A >= [VHTpalsat] -> VHG star palette target b;
	[VHGstartargetG] = [VHTpalsat];
    "VHG star palette target b"
	[VHGstartargetB] = [VHGstarb]; A = [VHGstarb];
	? A >= [VHTpalsat] -> VHG star palette target inner r;
	[VHGstartargetB] = [VHTpalsat];
    "VHG star palette target inner r"
	[VHGstartargetinnerR] = [VHGstarinnerR]; A = [VHGstarinnerR];
	? A >= [VHTpalsat] -> VHG star palette target inner g;
	[VHGstartargetinnerR] = [VHTpalsat];
    "VHG star palette target inner g"
	[VHGstartargetinnerG] = [VHGstarinnerG]; A = [VHGstarinnerG];
	? A >= [VHTpalsat] -> VHG star palette target inner b;
	[VHGstartargetinnerG] = [VHTpalsat];
    "VHG star palette target inner b"
	[VHGstartargetinnerB] = [VHGstarinnerB]; A = [VHGstarinnerB];
	? A >= [VHTpalsat] -> VHG star palette compare;
	[VHGstartargetinnerB] = [VHTpalsat];

    "VHG star palette compare"
	A = [VHGstarcurrentR]; ? A != [VHGstartargetR] -> VHG star palette changed;
	A = [VHGstarcurrentG]; ? A != [VHGstartargetG] -> VHG star palette changed;
	A = [VHGstarcurrentB]; ? A != [VHGstartargetB] -> VHG star palette changed;
	A = [VHGstarcurrentinnerR]; ? A != [VHGstartargetinnerR] -> VHG star palette changed;
	A = [VHGstarcurrentinnerG]; ? A != [VHGstartargetinnerG] -> VHG star palette changed;
	A = [VHGstarcurrentinnerB]; ? A != [VHGstartargetinnerB] -> VHG star palette changed;
	A = [VHGstarpaletteok]; ? A != 0 -> VHG star palette update done;
	[VHGstarpaletteok] = 1; -> VHG star palette step r;
    "VHG star palette changed"
	[VHGstarpaletteok] = 0;

    "VHG star palette step r"
	A = [VHGstarcurrentR]; ? A < [VHGstartargetR] -> VHG star palette inc r;
	? A > [VHGstartargetR] -> VHG star palette dec r; -> VHG star palette step g;
    "VHG star palette inc r" [VHGstarcurrentR]+; -> VHG star palette step g;
    "VHG star palette dec r" [VHGstarcurrentR]-;
    "VHG star palette step g"
	A = [VHGstarcurrentG]; ? A < [VHGstartargetG] -> VHG star palette inc g;
	? A > [VHGstartargetG] -> VHG star palette dec g; -> VHG star palette step b;
    "VHG star palette inc g" [VHGstarcurrentG]+; -> VHG star palette step b;
    "VHG star palette dec g" [VHGstarcurrentG]-;
    "VHG star palette step b"
	A = [VHGstarcurrentB]; ? A < [VHGstartargetB] -> VHG star palette inc b;
	? A > [VHGstartargetB] -> VHG star palette dec b; -> VHG star palette step inner r;
    "VHG star palette inc b" [VHGstarcurrentB]+; -> VHG star palette step inner r;
    "VHG star palette dec b" [VHGstarcurrentB]-;
    "VHG star palette step inner r"
	A = [VHGstarcurrentinnerR]; ? A < [VHGstartargetinnerR] -> VHG star palette inc inner r;
	? A > [VHGstartargetinnerR] -> VHG star palette dec inner r; -> VHG star palette step inner g;
    "VHG star palette inc inner r" [VHGstarcurrentinnerR]+; -> VHG star palette step inner g;
    "VHG star palette dec inner r" [VHGstarcurrentinnerR]-;
    "VHG star palette step inner g"
	A = [VHGstarcurrentinnerG]; ? A < [VHGstartargetinnerG] -> VHG star palette inc inner g;
	? A > [VHGstartargetinnerG] -> VHG star palette dec inner g; -> VHG star palette step inner b;
    "VHG star palette inc inner g" [VHGstarcurrentinnerG]+; -> VHG star palette step inner b;
    "VHG star palette dec inner g" [VHGstarcurrentinnerG]-;
    "VHG star palette step inner b"
	A = [VHGstarcurrentinnerB]; ? A < [VHGstartargetinnerB] -> VHG star palette inc inner b;
	? A > [VHGstartargetinnerB] -> VHG star palette dec inner b; -> VHG star palette shades;
    "VHG star palette inc inner b" [VHGstarcurrentinnerB]+; -> VHG star palette shades;
    "VHG star palette dec inner b" [VHGstarcurrentinnerB]-;

    "VHG star palette shades"
	A = pal6; [SHdstb] = A;
	[FBSHfirst] = 64; [FBSHn] = 24;
	[PFnum] = 0; [PFden] = 1; => PAL f32 make;
	[SHsr] = [PFbits]; [SHsg] = [PFbits]; [SHsb] = [PFbits];
	[PFnum] = [VHGstarcurrentinnerR]; => PAL f32 make; [SHer] = [PFbits];
	[PFnum] = [VHGstarcurrentinnerG]; => PAL f32 make; [SHeg] = [PFbits];
	[PFnum] = [VHGstarcurrentinnerB]; => PAL f32 make; [SHeb] = [PFbits]; => PAL shade;

	[FBSHfirst] = 88; [FBSHn] = 16;
	[SHsr] = [SHer]; [SHsg] = [SHeg]; [SHsb] = [SHeb];
	[PFden] = 1;
	[PFnum] = [VHGstarcurrentR]; => PAL f32 make; [SHer] = [PFbits];
	[PFnum] = [VHGstarcurrentG]; => PAL f32 make; [SHeg] = [PFbits];
	[PFnum] = [VHGstarcurrentB]; => PAL f32 make; [SHeb] = [PFbits]; => PAL shade;

	[FBSHfirst] = 104; [FBSHn] = 24;
	[SHsr] = [SHer]; [SHsg] = [SHeg]; [SHsb] = [SHeb];
	[PFden] = 1;
	[PFnum] = 64; => PAL f32 make; [SHer] = [PFbits];
	[PFnum] = 70; => PAL f32 make; [SHeg] = [PFbits];
	[PFnum] = 76; => PAL f32 make; [SHeb] = [PFbits]; => PAL shade;

	( tavola_colori(tmppal + 3*64, 64, 64, 63,63,63): self-copy
	  semantics upload the completed stellar band without replacing it. )
	[PVself] = 1; [PVfirst] = 64; [PVn] = 64;
	[PVfr] = 63; [PVfg] = 63; [PVfb] = 63; => PAL tavola;
    "VHG star palette update done"
	end;

"VHG ship palette update"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:3428-3527.  Band 0 follows the selected star, eclipsing
	  bodies, navigation heading, the retained internal-lamp fade, emergency
	  flicker and the rescue transmitter's four-frame white burst. )
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG ship palette update done;
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG ship palette update done;
	=> VHG info environment geometry;

	( stz = clamp(dzz*cos(nav)-dxx*sin(nav)-abs(dyy)/2,0,dsd)/1.25. )
	( Source navigation_beta is 180 degrees opposite the port's external
	  navigation convention.  The camera path accounts for that when it forms
	  the exterior view; apply the same conversion to the source stz lighting
	  term or planet-orbit views saturate the hull and halogen beams. )
	A = [VHGnavbeta]; A + 180; A % 360; [VHVangle] = A; => VHV sincos;
	[PGFt] = [VHVcos]; => PGF ldf32;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipz0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipz1]; => FMul;
	[VHGshipstz0] = [FA0]; [VHGshipstz1] = [FA1];
	[PGFt] = [VHVsin]; => PGF ldf32;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipx0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipx1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGshipstz0]; [FA1] = [VHGshipstz1]; => FSub;
	[VHGshipstz0] = [FA0]; [VHGshipstz1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoshipy0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoshipy1]; => FAbs;
	[FI] = 2; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoshipy0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoshipy1]; => FAbs; => FQuo;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGshipstz0]; [FA1] = [VHGshipstz1]; => FSub;
	[VHGshipstz0] = [FA0]; [VHGshipstz1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfodistance0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfodistance1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG ship stz nonnegative;
	[VHGshipstz0] = [VHGinfodistance0]; [VHGshipstz1] = [VHGinfodistance1];
    "VHG ship stz nonnegative"
	[FA0] = [VHGshipstz0]; [FA1] = [VHGshipstz1];
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 0; => FCmp; A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG ship stz clamped;
	[VHGshipstz0] = 0; [VHGshipstz1] = 0;
    "VHG ship stz clamped"
	[FA0] = [VHGshipstz0]; [FA1] = [VHGshipstz1];
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 3FF40000h; => FQuo;
	[VHGshipstz0] = [FA0]; [VHGshipstz1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfodistance0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfodistance1];
	[FB0] = [VHGshipstz0]; [FB1] = [VHGshipstz1]; => FSub;
	[VHGshipden0] = [FA0]; [VHGshipden1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1; => IntToF; [FB0] = [VHGinfoeclipse0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoeclipse1]; => FSub;
	[VHGshipfactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGshipfactor1] = [FA1];

	[VHGshpcf] = 15; [VHGshpbs] = [VHTred]; [VHGshpdv] = 4; [VHGshped] = 1;
	A = [VHTred]; A + 16; [VHGshpcp] = A; [VHGshpbt] = 0;
	=> VHG ship palette component; [VHGshipr] = [VHGshprs];
	[VHGshpcf] = 7; [VHGshpbs] = [VHTgreen]; [VHGshpdv] = 2; [VHGshped] = 2;
	A = [VHTgreen]; A + 32; [VHGshpcp] = A; [VHGshpbt] = 1;
	=> VHG ship palette component; [VHGshipg] = [VHGshprs];
	[VHGshpcf] = 7; [VHGshpbs] = [VHTblue]; [VHGshpdv] = 1; [VHGshped] = 4;
	[VHGshpcp] = 63; [VHGshpbt] = 1;
	=> VHG ship palette component; [VHGshipb] = [VHGshprs];
	A = [VHGgburst]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG ship burst done; ? A >= 5 -> VHG ship burst done;
	A '* 8; C = A; A + [VHGshipr]; [VHGshipr] = A;
	A = C; A + [VHGshipg]; [VHGshipg] = A;
	A = C; A + [VHGshipb]; [VHGshipb] = A;
    "VHG ship burst done"

	A = [VHGshipr]; ? A != [VHGshipoldr] -> VHG ship palette upload;
	A = [VHGshipg]; ? A != [VHGshipoldg] -> VHG ship palette upload;
	A = [VHGshipb]; ? A = [VHGshipoldb] -> VHG ship palette update done;
    "VHG ship palette upload"
	[VHGshipoldr] = [VHGshipr]; [VHGshipoldg] = [VHGshipg]; [VHGshipoldb] = [VHGshipb];
	[PVsrc] = range8088; [PVself] = 0; [PVfirst] = 0; [PVn] = 64;
	[PVfr] = [VHGshipr]; [PVfg] = [VHGshipg]; [PVfb] = [VHGshipb]; => PAL tavola;
    "VHG ship palette update done"
	end;

"VHG ship palette component"
	( ((coefficient*ray*class_channel)/(dsd-stz))*(1-eclipse), then
	  the channel-specific lamp contribution and source clamps. )
	[PGFt] = [VHTRay]; => PGF ldf32;
	[FI] = [VHGshpcf]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[PGFt] = [VHTRay]; => PGF ldf32; => FMul;
	[VHGshprs] = 0; [VHGshipstz0] = [FA0]; [VHGshipstz1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGshpbs]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGshipstz0]; [FA1] = [VHGshipstz1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGshipden0]; [FB1] = [VHGshipden1]; => FQuo;
	[FB0] = [VHGshipfactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGshipfactor1]; => FMul;
	=> FToIntChop; A = [FI]; C = [VHGilightlevel];
	D = [VHGelight]; ? D = 0 -> VHG ship ordinary lamp;
	D = [TKtmp]; D % 30; C + 30; C - D; C / [VHGshped]; -> VHG ship lamp ready;
    "VHG ship ordinary lamp"
	C / [VHGshpdv];
    "VHG ship lamp ready"
	A + C;
	[VHGshprs] = A; ? A <= [VHGshpcp] -> VHG ship component boost;
	[VHGshprs] = [VHGshpcp];
    "VHG ship component boost"
	A = [VHGshpbt]; ? A = 0 -> VHG ship component clamp;
	A = [VHTclass]; ? A != 11 -> VHG ship component clamp;
	A = [VHTphase]; A % 360; ? A >= 90 -> VHG ship component clamp;
	A = [VHGshprs]; A '* 5; [VHGshprs] = A;
    "VHG ship component clamp"
	A = [VHGshprs]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG ship component high; [VHGshprs] = 0;
    "VHG ship component high"
	A = [VHGshprs]; ? A <= 63 -> VHG ship palette component done; [VHGshprs] = 63;
    "VHG ship palette component done"
	end;

"VHG finder render"
	( Mark the selected galactic target before calibration as well as after it.
	  This uses the same live projection as the star/globe renderers, so the
	  finder remains useful while turning and across resized presentations. )
	A = [VHGfinder]; ? A = 0 -> VHG finder render done;
	[VHTcoord] = [VHTtx]; [VHTd0] = [MgDzatX0]; [VHTd1] = [MgDzatX1];
	[VHTslot] = SFXX; => VHT relative;
	[VHTcoord] = [VHTty]; [VHTd0] = [MgDzatY0]; [VHTd1] = [MgDzatY1];
	[VHTslot] = SFYY; => VHT relative;
	[VHTcoord] = [VHTtz]; [VHTd0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [VHTd1] = [MgDzatZ1];
	[VHTslot] = SFZZ; => VHT relative;
	=> PG getcoords; A = [GCret]; ? A = 0 -> VHG finder render done;
	A = [GCx]; ? A < 4 -> VHG finder render done; ? A > 315 -> VHG finder render done;
	C = [GCy]; ? C < 6 -> VHG finder render done; ? C > 193 -> VHG finder render done;
	C '* 320; C + A; [SPoff] = C; [SPval] = 63; [SPreg] = RGADP;
	A = C; A - 2; [SPoff] = A; => SP put;
	A = C; A + 2; [SPoff] = A; => SP put;
	A = C; A - 640; [SPoff] = A; => SP put;
	A = C; A + 640; [SPoff] = A; => SP put;
    "VHG finder render done"
	end;

"VHG render"
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [VHGprofpart] = [Counts];
	( IsoKernel timer calls do not preserve the x87 stack.  Profiling was
	  inserted ahead of each render phase, so every camera began with stale
	  FPU state even though its leaves were still submitted.  Re-establish
	  the runtime ABI before the first surface/ship floating-point work. )
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG render surface;
	A = [MgStspeed]; ? A != 0 -> VHG render space fade;
    "VHG render space clear"
	=> VHG space clear;
	-> VHG render space ready;
    "VHG render space fade"
	=> VHS fade;
    "VHG render space ready"
	=> PGF constants;
	( The source renderer uses one 100-unit plane for the Stardrifter, its
	  cupolas, and every exterior view. )
	[PGFi] = FSUNEG; [PGFt] = VHGNEAR; => PGF setf32;
	[VHSdx0] = [MgDzatX0]; [VHSdx1] = [MgDzatX1];
	[VHSdy0] = [MgDzatY0]; [VHSdy1] = [MgDzatY1];
	[VHSdz0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [VHSdz1] = [MgDzatZ1];
	[VHSalpha] = [VHGalpha];
	( from_vehicle(): user_beta + navigation_beta + 180.  VHGbeta already
	  includes the port's 180-degree hull-model offset, so add only nav beta. )
	A = [VHGbeta]; A + [VHGnavbeta]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHG external beta ok; A + 360;
    "VHG external beta ok"
	[VHSbeta] = A; [VHSamp] = [VHGamp];
	[VHSsurface] = 0;
	( VHT and the local-system globe consume the shared projection matrix.
	  Their SFXX/SFYY/SFZZ inputs are already relative to the Stardrifter, so
	  install the exterior angles at a zero camera origin instead of inheriting
	  the previous frame's final interior fixture view. )
	[VHVcamxi] = 0; [VHVcamyi] = 0; [VHVcamzi] = 0;
	[VHValpha] = [VHSalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHSbeta]; => VH set view;
	[VHTdosim] = [VHGdosim]; [VHTfast] = [VHGfast];
	[VHTinterpok] = [VHGinterpok]; [VHTinterpacc] = [VHGinterpacc];
	( The source's non-spinning star classes use (clock()/360)%360 instead
	  of gl_start.  READ TIME is milliseconds, matching that visible clock. )
	=> TK read wall; A = [TKtmp]; A / 360; A % 360; [VHTclockphase] = A;
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	=> VHG visor flare mode;
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A != 0 -> VHG close star rendered;
	=> VHT render;
    "VHG close star rendered"
	=> VHG local render;
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG star palette frame done; => VHG star palette update;
    "VHG star palette frame done"
	=> VHG ship palette update;
	=> VHG rescue render;
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; A = [Counts]; A - [VHGprofpart];
	A + [VHGprofspace]; [VHGprofspace] = A;
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [VHGprofpart] = [Counts];
	=> VHG fpu clean;

	( Source vehicle() always submits both cupolas.  Their wide dome can
	  remain partly visible far outside a centre-angle reject, so clipping
	  belongs to the polygon/line paths rather than a camera shortcut. )
	( lower cupola: vehicle() camera offset z+3100,y-550 )
	[VHCroof] = 0;
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGx]; A = [VHGy]; A - 550; [VHVcamyi] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A + 3100; [VHVcamzi] = A;
	[VHValpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGbeta]; => VH set view;
	[VHCyor] = 0; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;

	( Exact vehicle() order: draw the intact upper cupola before the hull only
	  while inside.  Once y<-500 sets ontheroof, omit it here and redraw its
	  locally displaced panels after the hull. )
	A = [VHGonroof]; ? A != 0 -> VHG upper after hull;
	[VHCroof] = 0;
	[VHVcamyi] = [VHGy]; => VH set view;
	[VHCyor] = 1; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;
	-> VHG upper before hull done;
    "VHG upper after hull"
    "VHG upper before hull done"
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; A = [Counts]; A - [VHGprofpart];
	A + [VHGprofcupola]; [VHGprofcupola] = A;

	( hull at the player's clean/live camera )
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [VHGprofpart] = [Counts];
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGz];
	=> VH set view;
	=> VH draw mode2 cache;
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; A = [Counts]; A - [VHGprofpart];
	A + [VHGprofhull]; [VHGprofhull] = A;

	A = [VHGonroof]; ? A != 0 -> VHG draw roof aperture;
	-> VHG interior details;
    "VHG draw roof aperture"
	[VHCroof] = 1;
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGy];
	A = [VHGz]; A + 3100; [VHVcamzi] = A;
	[VHValpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGbeta]; => VH set view;
	[VHCyor] = 1; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;
	end;

"VHG space clear"
	( Stationary Stardrifter frames call pclear(adapted+2880,0) with the
	  live QUADWORDS=14560.  Clear that exact 58,240-pixel viewport every
	  frame so additive stars and flares cannot accumulate. )
	A = nw; A + RADPT; A + 2880; B = 7280; C = 0;
    "VHG space clear eight"
	[A] = C; A+; [A] = C; A+; [A] = C; A+; [A] = C; A+;
	[A] = C; A+; [A] = C; A+; [A] = C; A+; [A] = C; A+;
	B ^ VHG space clear eight;
	end;

    "VHG interior details"
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [VHGprofpart] = [Counts];
	=> VHG fpu clean;

	( three right-wall console faces from vehicle() )
	=> VHG screen poll;
	[VHPcamx] = [VHGx]; [VHPcamy] = [VHGy]; [VHPcamz] = [VHGz];
	[VHPalpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHPbeta] = [VHGbeta];
	[VHPactive] = [VHGscreen]; [VHPselected] = [VHGplanet]; [VHPsysphase] = [VHGframe];
	[VHPlanding] = [VHGlandingselect]; [VHPlandinglon] = [VHGlandinglon]; [VHPlandinglat] = [VHGlandinglat];
	[VHPblackout] = [VHGelight]; [VHPresetphase] = [VHGresetcount]; => VH screens;
	=> VHG onboard prepare; => VH onboard screen;

	( interior halogen fixture: vehicle() first retains the screen pass's
	  cam_z -= 2*54*15, then applies x-3395,y+480,z+200.  Relative to the
	  player's clean camera that is x-3395,y+480,z-1420. )
	A = [VHGx]; A - 3395; [VHVcamxi] = A;
	A = [VHGy]; A + 480; [VHVcamyi] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A - 1420; [VHVcamzi] = A;
	=> VH set view; [VHLpower] = [VHGilight]; [VHLemergency] = [VHGelight]; => VH alogena;
	( sky(0x405C) is the final 3-D scene pass in NOCTIS.CPP: it follows
	  vehicle() so the stellar-band neighbour test preserves the hull. )
	=> VHS stars; => VHG finder render;
	[VHPmessage] = 0;
	A = [VHGlandingselect]; ? A = 0 -> VHG source status ready;
	A = [VHGlandingview]; ? A != 0 -> VHG source status ready;
	=> VHG landing status format; [VHPmessage] = VHGlandingstatus;
    "VHG source status ready"
	[VHPfcsstatus] = [MgStatus];
	( Once local tracking has settled, the source refreshes TRACKING or
	  MOVIEMAKER every frame before the inactive approach branch. )
	A = [VHPmessage]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS status ready;
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS status ready;
	A = [MgIpreached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS status ready;
	A = [VHGsync]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS status ready;
	[VHPfcsstatus] = 13;
	A = [VHGmovie]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS status ready;
	[VHPfcsstatus] = 14;
	A = [MgApreached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS status ready;
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS status ready; [VHPfcsstatus] = 6;
    "VHG FCS status ready"
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS source HUD draw;
	A = [VHGgraphics]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS source HUD done;
    "VHG FCS source HUD draw"
	=> VH HUD FCS;
    "VHG FCS source HUD done"
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; A = [Counts]; A - [VHGprofpart];
	A + [VHGprofdetail]; [VHGprofdetail] = A;
	end;

	"VHG render surface"
		[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGx]; [VHGNDcamy] = [VHGy]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGz];
		[VHGNDplayerrefx] = [VHGsurfrefx]; [VHGNDplayerrefz] = [VHGsurfrefz];
		[VHGNDalpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHGNDbeta] = [VHGbeta]; [VHGNDdosim] = [VHGdosim];
		[VHGNDgalx0] = [MgDzatX0]; [VHGNDgalx1] = [MgDzatX1];
		[VHGNDgaly0] = [MgDzatY0]; [VHGNDgaly1] = [MgDzatY1];
		[VHGNDgalz0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [VHGNDgalz1] = [MgDzatZ1];
		[VHGNDgalamp] = [VHGamp];
		[VHGNDfast] = [VHGfast]; [VHGNDinterpok] = [VHGinterpok]; [VHGNDinterpacc] = [VHGinterpacc];
		=> VHGND render;
	A = [VHGx]; A + [VHGNDimpactx]; [VHGx] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A + [VHGNDimpactz]; [VHGz] = A;
	A = [VHGalpha]; A + [VHGNDimpacttilt]; ? A <= 44 -> VHG wave alpha high; A = 44;
    "VHG wave alpha high"
	? A >= VHGN44 -> VHG wave alpha ready; A = VHGN44;
    "VHG wave alpha ready"
	[VHGalpha] = A; => VHGND wave impact finish;
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A != 0 -> VHG render surface done;
	=> VHGND moving capsule;
    "VHG render surface done"
	end;

	"VHG input"
		( Capsule descent/return owns the camera and controls.  Do not run the
		  ship/device character pipeline against partially transitioned surface
		  state; a queued landing key could otherwise survive into that pipeline
		  and corrupt its indirect key-table return.  Escape remains immediate. )
		A = [VHGslcancelheld]; ? A = 0 -> VHG input escape ready;
		A = [KEY ESCAPE]; ? A = ON -> VHG input done; [VHGslcancelheld] = 0;
	    "VHG input escape ready"
		A = [KEY ESCAPE]; ? A = ON -> VHG input escape;
		A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG input active;
		A = [VHGlanded]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input active;
		-> VHG input done;
	    "VHG input active"
		A = [Client Owns Text Cursor]; ? A = NO -> VHG input done;
	A = [KEY RETURN]; ? A = ON -> VHG return hook armed;
	[VHGreturnhookheld] = 0;
    "VHG return hook armed"
	[VHGascii] = 0; [Console Command] = GET CONSOLE INPUT; isocall;
	? failed -> VHG console ring ready;
	[VHGascii] = [Console Input];
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 0 -> VHG console ring ready;
	( GET leaves the current character in IsoKernel's circular buffer.  Clear
	  it after copying so 60-Hz presentation cannot repeat one keypress. )
	[Console Command] = CLEAR CONSOLE BUFFER; isocall;
    "VHG console ring ready"
	=> VHG return key;
	=> VHG fps key;
	=> VHG graphics key;
	=> VHG movie key;
	=> VHG about key;
	=> VHG help key;
	=> VHG fast key;
	=> VHG checkpoint keys;
	=> VHG music key;
	=> VHG snapshot key;
	=> VHG raw snapshot key;
	=> VHG wide snapshot key;
	A = [VHGabout]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input done;
	=> VHG movie character;
	A = [VHGmovieused]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input done;
	=> VHG graphics character;
	A = [VHGgraphicsused]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input done;
	=> VHG menu mouse;
	=> VHG onboard select;
	=> VHG preference key;
	=> VHG device key;
	=> VHG FCS menu key;
	=> VHG info key;
	=> VHG screen off key;
	=> VHG accessibility release;
	A = [VHGlandingselect]; ? A != 0 -> VHG landing selector input;
	=> VHG output scroll; A = [VHGscrollused]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input done;
	=> VHG visor keys;
	=> VHG mouse look;
	A = [KEY ESCAPE]; ? A = ON -> VHG input escape;
	( Console characters are edge events, not held state.  Consume them on
	  every presented frame so the 60-Hz presenter cannot discard two of
	  every three keystrokes; only physical look/movement stays sim-paced. )
	A = [VHGdev]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input done;
	A = [VHGfcsopen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input done;
	A = [VHGprefs]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input done;
	A = [VHGinfo]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input done;
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG console input;
	=> VHG HUD brightness key;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG surface input;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 13 -> VHG activate wall console;
	? A = 71 -> VHG activate console shortcut; ? A != 103 -> VHG normal input;
    "VHG activate wall console"
	A = [VHGscreen]; ? A = 2 -> VHG request landing;
	? A != 0 -> VHG normal input;
	[VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 1; [VHGconsoleview] = 0; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG activate console shortcut"
	[VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 1; [VHGconsoleview] = 1; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG console input"
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 0 -> VHG input done;
	[VHPkey] = A; ? A != 9 -> VHG console key ready; [VHPkey] = 13;
    "VHG console key ready"
	=> VH GOES key;
	A = [VHPsubmitted]; ? A = 0 -> VHG input done;
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGunknowntext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; => VHG command;
	A = [VHGcmdsilent]; ? A != 0 -> VHG console command handled;
	A = [VHPcmdpos]; A + vhptext; A + 42; [A] = 0;
	A = vhptext; A + 42; [VHPoutptr] = A; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = [VHGnoticeptr]; => VH GOES output line;
	"VHG console command handled"
	=> VH GOES clear; [VHGconsole] = 0; [VHGconsoleview] = 0; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG normal input"
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 49 -> VHG select planet 1; ? A = 50 -> VHG select planet 2;
	? A = 51 -> VHG select planet 3; ? A = 52 -> VHG select body 4;
	? A = 91 -> VHG select previous body;
	? A = 93 -> VHG select next body; -> VHG selection done;
    "VHG select planet 1"
	[VHGplanet] = 0; => VHCAT refresh; => VHG local reset; -> VHG selection done;
    "VHG select planet 2"
	A = [nsnop]; ? A '< 2 -> VHG selection done; [VHGplanet] = 1; => VHCAT refresh; => VHG local reset; -> VHG selection done;
    "VHG select planet 3"
	A = [nsnop]; ? A '< 3 -> VHG selection done; [VHGplanet] = 2; => VHCAT refresh; => VHG local reset; -> VHG selection done;
    "VHG select body 4"
	A = [nsnob]; ? A '< 4 -> VHG selection done; [VHGplanet] = 3; => VHCAT refresh; => VHG local reset; -> VHG selection done;
    "VHG select body 5"
	A = [nsnob]; ? A '< 5 -> VHG selection done; [VHGplanet] = 4; => VHCAT refresh; => VHG local reset;
    "VHG select previous body"
	A = [nsnob]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG selection done;
	A = [VHGplanet]; A - 1; ? A >= 0 -> VHG previous body ready;
	A = [nsnob]; A - 1;
    "VHG previous body ready"
	[VHGplanet] = A; => VHCAT refresh; => VHG local reset; -> VHG selection done;
    "VHG select next body"
	A = [nsnob]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG selection done;
	A = [VHGplanet]; A + 1; ? A '< [nsnob] -> VHG next body ready; A = 0;
    "VHG next body ready"
	[VHGplanet] = A; => VHCAT refresh; => VHG local reset;
    "VHG selection done"
	( C mirrors the original emergency-console lithium collector so the action
	  remains available while the player is away from the physical console. )
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 67 -> VHG collector shortcut; ? A = 99 -> VHG collector shortcut;
	? A = 72 -> VHG help shortcut; ? A = 104 -> VHG help shortcut; -> VHG landing key;
    "VHG collector shortcut"
	=> VHG collector action; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG help shortcut"
	=> VHG help action; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG landing key"
	( L approaches, then lands on, the selected body after calibration. )
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 76 -> VHG request landing; ? A = 108 -> VHG request landing;
	-> VHG look input;
    "VHG request landing"
	A = [MgApreached]; ? A != 0 -> VHG landing star ready;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGcalibratetext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG landing star ready"
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A != 0 -> VHG landing local active;
	=> VHG local start; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG landing local active"
	A = [VHGlocaltarget]; ? A != [VHGplanet] -> VHG landing restart local;
	A = [MgIpreached]; ? A != 0 -> VHG landing local ready;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGapproachingtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG landing restart local"
	=> VHG local start; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG landing local ready"
	A = [VHGplanet]; ? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG landing unavailable;
	E = nsptype; E + A; A = [E]; ? A = 0 -> VHG landing unavailable;
	? A = 6 -> VHG landing unavailable; ? A = 9 -> VHG landing unavailable;
	? A '>= 10 -> VHG landing unavailable;
	[VHGlandinglon] = 0; [VHGlandinglat] = 60; [VHGlandingselect] = 1;
	[VHGlandingview] = 1; A = [VHGscreen]; ? A != 2 -> VHG landing view ready;
	[VHGlandingview] = 0;
    "VHG landing view ready"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGlandselecttext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	-> VHG input done;
    "VHG landing unavailable"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGlandfailtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG look input"
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG input done;
	A = [KEY LEFT]; ? A = OFF -> VHG no left; A = [VHGbeta]; A + 2; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG no left"
	A = [KEY RIGHT]; ? A = OFF -> VHG no right; A = [VHGbeta]; A - 2; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG no right"
	( The desktop port promises four-arrow look.  Preserve the source lift
	  sequence on E instead of stealing Up: Noctis IV used mouse look, so its
	  otherwise-free DOS Up scan code could start the lift without a conflict. )
	[VHGuprequest] = 0; E = KEY A; E + 4; A = [E]; ? A = OFF -> VHG lift released;
	A = [VHGupheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG lift key done;
	[VHGupheld] = 1; [VHGuprequest] = 1; -> VHG lift key done;
    "VHG lift released"
	[VHGupheld] = 0;
    "VHG lift key done"
	A = [KEY UP]; ? A = OFF -> VHG no up; A = [VHGalpha]; A - 2; [VHGalpha] = A;
    "VHG no up"
	A = [KEY DOWN]; ? A = OFF -> VHG no down; A = [VHGalpha]; A + 2; [VHGalpha] = A;
    "VHG no down"
	A = [VHGalpha]; ? A <= 44 -> VHG alpha high ok; [VHGalpha] = 44;
    "VHG alpha high ok"
	A = [VHGalpha]; ? A >= VHGN44 -> VHG alpha low ok; [VHGalpha] = VHGN44;
    "VHG alpha low ok"
	A = [VHGbeta]; ? A <= 180 -> VHG beta high ok; A = [VHGbeta]; A - 360; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG beta high ok"
	A = [VHGbeta]; ? A >= VHGN180 -> VHG beta low ok; A = [VHGbeta]; A + 360; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG beta low ok"
	( Refresh the movement basis after input changed the view angles. )
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGz];
	[VHValpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGbeta]; => VH set view;

	( The source continues sampling player movement during a lift.  The later
	  centre restraint influences the route but does not lock out controls. )
	A = [KEY W]; ? A = OFF -> VHG no W; [VHGstepv] = VHGSTEP; => VHG forward;
    "VHG no W"
	A = [KEY S]; ? A = OFF -> VHG no S; A = 0; A - VHGSTEP; [VHGstepv] = A; => VHG forward;
    "VHG no S"
	A = [KEY A]; ? A = OFF -> VHG no A; A = 0; A - VHGSTEP; [VHGstepv] = A; => VHG strafe;
    "VHG no A"
	A = [KEY D]; ? A = OFF -> VHG input done; [VHGstepv] = VHGSTEP; => VHG strafe;
	-> VHG input done;
    "VHG input escape"
	A = [VHGslactive]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input escape sl;
	A = [VHGdlactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG quit;
	=> VHG DL cancel; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG input escape sl"
	=> VHG SL cancel; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG input done"
	( The shared early-exit path is also used while a planetary capsule owns
	  the camera (mode=surface, landed=0).  Ship bounds and the Stardrifter
	  lift are invalid there: clamping a 1.6-million-unit terrain coordinate
	  to the interior corner teleports the capsule camera off its terrain tile
	  and produces an apparent fall through the ground. )
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG input cleanup done;
	=> VHG clamp position;
	=> VHG platform;
	"VHG input cleanup done"
	end;

"VHG landing selector input"
	( Original planetary-map controls: longitude wraps through 0..359 and
	  latitude clamps to 1..119.  The selector owns the arrows so looking and
	  the Stardrifter lift cannot move underneath the full-page site display. )
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 76 -> VHG landing selector confirm;
	? A = 108 -> VHG landing selector confirm; ? A = 13 -> VHG landing selector confirm;
	? A = 67 -> VHG landing selector cancel; ? A = 99 -> VHG landing selector cancel;
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG input done;
	A = [KEY LEFT]; ? A = OFF -> VHG landing selector no left;
	A = [VHGlandinglon]; A - 1; ? A >= 0 -> VHG landing selector left ready; A = 359;
    "VHG landing selector left ready"
	[VHGlandinglon] = A;
    "VHG landing selector no left"
	A = [KEY RIGHT]; ? A = OFF -> VHG landing selector no right;
	A = [VHGlandinglon]; A + 1; ? A < 360 -> VHG landing selector right ready; A = 0;
    "VHG landing selector right ready"
	[VHGlandinglon] = A;
    "VHG landing selector no right"
	A = [KEY UP]; ? A = OFF -> VHG landing selector no up;
	A = [VHGlandinglat]; A - 1; ? A >= 1 -> VHG landing selector up ready; A = 1;
    "VHG landing selector up ready"
	[VHGlandinglat] = A;
    "VHG landing selector no up"
	A = [KEY DOWN]; ? A = OFF -> VHG input done;
	A = [VHGlandinglat]; A + 1; ? A <= 119 -> VHG landing selector down ready; A = 119;
    "VHG landing selector down ready"
	[VHGlandinglat] = A; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG landing selector confirm"
	[VHGlandingselect] = 0; [VHGlandingview] = 1; [VHGlandpending] = 1;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGdescenttext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; -> VHG input done;
    "VHG landing selector cancel"
	[VHGlandingselect] = 0; [VHGlandingview] = 1; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGlandcanceltext;
	[VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG input done;

"VHG collector action"
	A = [VHGcollector]; ? A != 0 -> VHG collector switch off;
	A = [MgApreached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG collector needs calibration;
	A = [MgAptgt]; ? A != 1 -> VHG collector needs calibration;
	A = [VHTclass]; ? A = 5 -> VHG collector switch on;
	? A != 6 -> VHG collector unsuitable;
	A = [VHTRay]; ? A <= 1082130432 -> VHG collector unsuitable;
    "VHG collector switch on"
	[VHGcollector] = 1; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGcollecttext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 25; end;
    "VHG collector switch off"
	[VHGcollector] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGcollectofftext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 50; end;
    "VHG collector needs calibration"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGcalibratetext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; end;
    "VHG collector unsuitable"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGcollectbadtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 50;
	end;

"VHG screen poll"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:2718-2722 selects the three right-wall screens strictly
	  from the player's position and gaze.  Keep that geometry exact.  The
	  desktop port requires Enter before the command screen owns the keyboard
	  because WASD replaces the source game's mouse-driven walking. )
	[VHGscreen] = 0FFFFFFFFh;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG screen poll changed;
	A = [VHGonroof]; ? A != 0 -> VHG screen poll changed;
	A = [VHGelight]; ? A != 0 -> VHG screen poll changed;
	A = [VHGbeta]; ? A <= VHGN135 -> VHG screen poll changed;
	? A >= VHGN45 -> VHG screen poll changed;
	A = [VHGz]; ? A >= 0FFFFF9E8h -> VHG screen poll changed;
	? A <= 0FFFFF0A6h -> VHG screen poll changed;
	A = [VHGx]; ? A <= 2580 -> VHG screen poll changed;
	A = [VHGz]; A + 1560; C = 0; C - 810; A / C; [VHGscreen] = A;
    "VHG screen poll changed"
	A = [VHGscreen]; ? A = [VHGscreenprev] -> VHG screen poll done;
	[VHGscreenprev] = A; ? A = 0 -> VHG screen poll zero;
	? A = 1 -> VHG screen poll one; ? A = 2 -> VHG screen poll two;
	-> VHG screen poll done;
    "VHG screen poll zero"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGscreen0text; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG screen poll done;
    "VHG screen poll one"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGscreen1text; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG screen poll done;
    "VHG screen poll two"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGscreen2text; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
    "VHG screen poll done"
	end;

"VHG output scroll"
	[VHGscrollused] = 0; A = [VHGscreen]; ? A != 1 -> VHG output scroll away;
	A = [KEY HOME]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll pressed;
	A = [KEY END]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll pressed;
	A = [KEY PGUP]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll pressed;
	A = [KEY PGDN]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll pressed;
	A = [KEY UP]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll pressed;
	A = [KEY DOWN]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll pressed;
	[VHGscrollheld] = 0; end;
    "VHG output scroll away"
	[VHGscrollheld] = 0; end;
    "VHG output scroll pressed"
	[VHGscrollused] = 1; A = [VHGscrollheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG output scroll done;
	[VHGscrollheld] = 1;
	A = [KEY HOME]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll home;
	A = [KEY END]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll end;
	A = [KEY PGUP]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll page up;
	A = [KEY PGDN]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll page down;
	A = [KEY UP]; ? A = ON -> VHG output scroll up;
	A = [VHPoutview]; A + 1; -> VHG output scroll clamp;
    "VHG output scroll home"
	[VHPoutview] = 0; end;
    "VHG output scroll end"
	A = [VHPoutrows]; A - 7; ? A >= 0 -> VHG output scroll store; A = 0; -> VHG output scroll store;
    "VHG output scroll page up"
	A = [VHPoutview]; A - 7; ? A >= 0 -> VHG output scroll store; A = 0; -> VHG output scroll store;
    "VHG output scroll page down"
	A = [VHPoutview]; A + 7; -> VHG output scroll clamp;
    "VHG output scroll up"
	A = [VHPoutview]; A - 1; ? A >= 0 -> VHG output scroll store; A = 0; -> VHG output scroll store;
    "VHG output scroll clamp"
	C = [VHPoutrows]; C - 7; ? C >= 0 -> VHG output scroll max ready; C = 0;
    "VHG output scroll max ready"
	? A <= C -> VHG output scroll store; A = C;
    "VHG output scroll store"
	[VHPoutview] = A;
    "VHG output scroll done"
	end;

"VHG help action"
	( The source emergency service accepts a request only with no usable
	  power and no lithium.  NOCTIS seeds fast_random() from clock(), but its
	  following flandom() call deliberately draws from Borland random() instead.
	  Preserve that original split RNG behavior and the full delayed fly-by. )
	A = [VHGrescueactive]; ? A != 0 -> VHG help already pending;
	A = [MgPwr]; ? A > 15000 -> VHG help not required;
	A = [MgCharge]; ? A != 0 -> VHG help not required;
	=> VHG UTC timestamp;
	[FA0] = [MgDzatX0]; [FA1] = [MgDzatX1]; [FB0] = [MgDzatY0]; [FB1] = [MgDzatY1]; => FAdd;
	[FB0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [FB1] = [MgDzatZ1]; => FAdd; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; C = A; A > 31; ? A = 0 -> VHG help distance positive; A = 0; A - C;
    "VHG help distance positive"
	A '/ 10000; ? A <= 1800 -> VHG help distance ready; A = 1800;
    "VHG help distance ready"
	[VHGrescuedist] = A;
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; A = [Counts]; => SU fast srand;
	A = 32767; => BrtlRandom; A '* [VHGrescuedist]; A / 32767;
	A + 60; [VHGrescuedelay] = A;
	[VHGrescueactive] = 1; [VHGrescuetick] = 0; [VHGrescueacc] = 0; [VHGcollector] = 0; [VHGgburst] = 63;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGhelpreqtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; end;
    "VHG help already pending"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGhelppendingtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; end;
"VHG help not required"
	[VHGgburst] = 0; A = 0; A - 1; [VHGgburst] = A;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGhelpbadtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	end;

"VHG console prefill"
	=> VH GOES clear; [VHGprefilli] = 0;
    "VHG console prefill loop"
	A = [VHGprefilli]; ? A >= [VHGprefillcount] -> VHG console prefill space;
	E = [VHGprefillsrc]; E + A; C = vhptext; C + 42; C + A; A = [E]; [C] = A;
	[VHGprefilli]+; -> VHG console prefill loop;
"VHG console prefill space"
	A = [VHGprefillcount]; C = vhptext; C + 42; C + A; [C] = 32;
	A + 1; [VHPcmdpos] = A; C = vhptext; C + 42; C + A; [C] = 95;
	A + 1; C = vhptext; C + 42; C + A; [C] = 0;
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0; [VHGhelpshow] = 0;
	[VHGconsole] = 1; [VHGconsoleview] = 1; [VHGascii] = 0;
	end;

"VHG browse open"
	[VHGbrowsestep] = 1; => VHG browse seek;
	A = [VHGbrowsevalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG browse unavailable;
	[VHGdev] = 6; [VHGascii] = 0; end;
    "VHG browse unavailable"
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGtargetfailedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;

"VHG browse seek"
	[VHGbrowsevalid] = 0; A = [VHSready]; ? A = 0 -> VHG browse seek done;
	[VHGbrowsetries] = 0;
    "VHG browse seek loop"
	A = [VHGbrowsecursor]; A + [VHGbrowsestep]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG browse cursor high;
	A = [VHScount]; A - 1; -> VHG browse cursor ready;
    "VHG browse cursor high"
	? A '< [VHScount] -> VHG browse cursor ready; A = 0;
    "VHG browse cursor ready"
	[VHGbrowsecursor] = A; A '* VHSENTRY; A + vhsstarcache; [VHGbrowseptr] = A;
	A = [A plus 3]; ? A != 0 -> VHG browse found;
	[VHGbrowsetries]+; A = [VHGbrowsetries]; ? A < [VHScount] -> VHG browse seek loop;
	-> VHG browse seek done;
    "VHG browse found"
	A = [VHGbrowseptr]; [VHGbrowsex] = [A plus 0]; [VHGbrowsey] = [A plus 1];
	[VHGbrowsez] = [A plus 2]; [VHGbrowsevalid] = 1;
    "VHG browse seek done"
	end;

"VHG menu mouse"
	( The source exposes four gaze-selected command slots.  The desktop page
	  keeps those same slots but also lets the visible rows act as controls.
	  Convert the host pointer back through VHGUI's current aspect fit so the
	  hit areas remain exact after any resize. )
	[VHGmenuhover] = 0; [VHGmenuhovery] = 0; [VHGmenukey] = 0;
	A = [VHGdevaccess]; ? A = 0 -> VHG menu mouse release;
	A = [VHGdev]; ? A != 0 -> VHG menu mouse active;
	A = [VHGfcsopen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG menu mouse active;
	A = [VHGprefs]; ? A != 0 -> VHG menu mouse active; -> VHG menu mouse release;
    "VHG menu mouse active"
	A = [Client Owns Mouse Pointer]; ? A = NO -> VHG menu mouse release;
	A = [VHGUIdw]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG menu mouse release;
	A = [VHGUIdh]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG menu mouse release;
	A = [Pointer X Coordinate]; ? A < [VHGUIleft] -> VHG menu mouse release;
	C = [VHGUIleft]; C + [VHGUIdw]; ? A '>= C -> VHG menu mouse release;
	A - [VHGUIleft]; A '* 320; A '/ [VHGUIdw]; [VHGmenux] = A;
	A = [Pointer Y Coordinate]; ? A < [VHGUItop] -> VHG menu mouse release;
	C = [VHGUItop]; C + [VHGUIdh]; ? A '>= C -> VHG menu mouse release;
	A - [VHGUItop]; A '* 200; A '/ [VHGUIdh]; [VHGmenuy] = A;

	( The target browser's four actions are its last four rows; all other
	  control pages use the first four rows under their title. )
	A = [VHGdev]; ? A != 6 -> VHG menu mouse regular rows;
	A = [VHGmenuy]; ? A < 96 -> VHG menu mouse button;
	? A >= 184 -> VHG menu mouse button; A - 96; A '/ 22; C = A;
	A + 1; [VHGmenuhover] = A; A = C; A '* 22; A + 102; [VHGmenuhovery] = A;
	A = C; A + 54; [VHGmenukey] = A; -> VHG menu mouse button;
    "VHG menu mouse regular rows"
	A = [VHGmenuy]; ? A < 30 -> VHG menu mouse back row;
	? A >= 118 -> VHG menu mouse back row; A - 30; A '/ 22; C = A;
	A + 1; [VHGmenuhover] = A; A = C; A '* 22; A + 36; [VHGmenuhovery] = A;
	A = C; A + 54; [VHGmenukey] = A; -> VHG menu mouse button;
    "VHG menu mouse back row"
	A = [VHGmenuy]; ? A < 168 -> VHG menu mouse button;
	A = [VHGdev]; ? A != 0 -> VHG menu mouse device back;
	A = [VHGprefs]; ? A != 0 -> VHG menu mouse prefs back;
    "VHG menu mouse FCS back"
	[VHGmenukey] = 53;
	-> VHG menu mouse button;
    "VHG menu mouse device back"
	[VHGmenukey] = 82; -> VHG menu mouse button;
    "VHG menu mouse prefs back"
	[VHGmenukey] = 88;
    "VHG menu mouse button"
	A = [Pointer Status]; ? A - PD LEFT BUTTON DOWN -> VHG menu mouse release held;
	A = [VHGmenuheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG menu mouse done;
	[VHGmenuheld] = 1; A = [VHGmenukey]; ? A = 0 -> VHG menu mouse done;
	[VHGascii] = A; -> VHG menu mouse done;
    "VHG menu mouse release held"
	[VHGmenuheld] = 0; -> VHG menu mouse done;
    "VHG menu mouse release"
	[VHGmenuheld] = 0;
"VHG menu mouse done"
	end;

"VHG accessibility release"
	( A GAME-menu page owns physical-computer selection only while one of its
	  three accessibility mirrors is actually open. Commands that close a page
	  must not leave the world-space computer disabled. )
	A = [VHGdevaccess]; ? A = 0 -> VHG accessibility release done;
	A = [VHGdev]; A | [VHGfcsopen]; A | [VHGprefs]; ? A != 0 -> VHG accessibility release done;
	[VHGdevaccess] = 0;
    "VHG accessibility release done"
	end;

"VHG onboard select"
	( The original Select action belongs to the centre gaze, not the desktop
	  pointer position. The accessibility pages consume their own mouse rows;
	  direct R/5 leave left-click free for the physical z=0 computer. )
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG onboard select release;
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG onboard select release;
	A = [VHGdevaccess]; ? A != 0 -> VHG onboard select release;
    "VHG onboard select active"
	A = [Client Owns Mouse Pointer]; ? A = NO -> VHG onboard select release;
	A = [Pointer Status]; ? A - PD LEFT BUTTON DOWN -> VHG onboard select released;
	[VHGautoscreentick] = 100;
	A = [VHGgazecontrol]; A | [VHGgazecommand]; ? A != 0 -> VHG onboard select command;
	=> VHG navigation steering input;
    "VHG onboard select command"
	A = [VHGgazeheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG onboard select done; [VHGgazeheld] = 1;
	A = [VHGgazecontrol]; ? A = 1 -> VHG onboard select FCS;
	? A = 2 -> VHG onboard select devices; ? A = 3 -> VHG onboard select prefs;
	? A = 4 -> VHG onboard select off;
	A = [VHGgazecommand]; ? A = 0 -> VHG onboard select done;
	A + 53; [VHGascii] = A; -> VHG onboard select done;
    "VHG onboard select FCS"
	[VHGfcsopen] = 1; [VHGdev] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG onboard select done;
    "VHG onboard select devices"
	[VHGdev] = 1; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG onboard select done;
    "VHG onboard select prefs"
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 1; [VHGascii] = 0;
	-> VHG onboard select done;
    "VHG onboard select off"
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG onboard select done;
    "VHG onboard select released"
	[VHGgazeheld] = 0; -> VHG onboard select done;
    "VHG onboard select release"
	[VHGgazeheld] = 0;
    "VHG onboard select done"
	end;

"VHG navigation steering input"
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG navigation steering done;
	A = [VHGgazex]; ? A > 2500 -> VHG navigation steer right;
	? A >= 0FFFFF63Ch -> VHG navigation steering done; ( -2500 )
	A = 15; C = [VHGrevcontrols]; ? C = 0 -> VHG navigation steer add;
	A = 0; A - 15; -> VHG navigation steer add;
    "VHG navigation steer right"
	A = 0; A - 15; C = [VHGrevcontrols]; ? C = 0 -> VHG navigation steer add;
	A = 15;
    "VHG navigation steer add"
	A + [VHGnavvel]; [VHGnavvel] = A;
    "VHG navigation steering done"
	end;

"VHG systems reset action"
	[VHGresetcount] = 150; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGresetdashtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	end;

"VHG mouse look"
	( Right-drag gives the windowed port a conventional mouse-look without
	  stealing ordinary pointer motion from iGUI chrome or the GAME menu.
	  Convert host pixels through the current aspect-fit dimensions so the
	  sensitivity stays stable when the window is resized. )
	A = [Client Owns Mouse Pointer]; ? A = NO -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [VHGdev]; ? A != 0 -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [VHGinfo]; ? A != 0 -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [VHGhelpshow]; ? A != 0 -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [VHGlifter]; ? A != 0 -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [VHGUIdw]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [VHGUIdh]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [Pointer X Coordinate]; ? A < [VHGUIleft] -> VHG mouse look release;
	C = [VHGUIleft]; C + [VHGUIdw]; ? A '>= C -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [Pointer Y Coordinate]; ? A < [VHGUItop] -> VHG mouse look release;
	C = [VHGUItop]; C + [VHGUIdh]; ? A '>= C -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [Pointer Status]; ? A - PD RIGHT BUTTON DOWN -> VHG mouse look release;
	A = [VHGmouseheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG mouse look drag;
	[VHGmouseheld] = 1; [VHGmousex] = [Pointer X Coordinate];
	[VHGmousey] = [Pointer Y Coordinate]; end;
    "VHG mouse look drag"
	A = [Pointer X Coordinate]; C = A; C - [VHGmousex]; [VHGmousedx] = C; [VHGmousex] = A;
	A = [Pointer Y Coordinate]; C = A; C - [VHGmousey]; [VHGmousedy] = C; [VHGmousey] = A;
	A = [VHGmousedx]; A * 320; A / [VHGUIdw]; A / 3;
	C = [VHGbeta]; C - A; [VHGbeta] = C;
	A = [VHGmousedy]; A * 200; A / [VHGUIdh]; A / 4;
	C = [VHGalpha]; C + A; [VHGalpha] = C;
	A = [VHGalpha]; ? A <= 44 -> VHG mouse alpha high; [VHGalpha] = 44;
    "VHG mouse alpha high"
	A = [VHGalpha]; ? A >= VHGN44 -> VHG mouse alpha low; [VHGalpha] = VHGN44;
    "VHG mouse alpha low"
	A = [VHGbeta]; ? A <= 180 -> VHG mouse beta high; A - 360; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG mouse beta high"
	A = [VHGbeta]; ? A >= VHGN180 -> VHG mouse look done; A + 360; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG mouse look done"
	end;
    "VHG mouse look release"
	[VHGmouseheld] = 0;
	end;

"VHG return key"
	( The iGUI enhanced-key session forwards menu-closed Return here before
	  the client loop.  Prefer a real buffered ASCII value when present. )
	A = [VHGreturnpending]; ? A = 0 -> VHG return key done;
	[VHGreturnpending] = 0; A = [VHGascii]; ? A != 0 -> VHG return key done;
	[VHGascii] = 13; -> VHG return key done;
    "VHG return key done"
	end;

"VHG HUD brightness key"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:4472-4473.  The source adjusts surrounding()'s surlight
	  by one for each buffered plus/minus character and clamps it to 10..63.
	  This runs only in the unobstructed game view, so those characters remain
	  available to onboard pages and the GOES console when either owns input. )
	A = [VHGgraphics]; ? A != 0 -> VHG HUD brightness done;
	A = [VHGhelpshow]; ? A != 0 -> VHG HUD brightness done;
	A = [VHGmovieview]; ? A != 0 -> VHG HUD brightness done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 43 -> VHG HUD brightness raise;
	? A != 45 -> VHG HUD brightness done;
	[VHGascii] = 0; A = [VHGNDsurlight]; ? A <= 10 -> VHG HUD brightness done;
	[VHGNDsurlight]-; -> VHG HUD brightness done;
    "VHG HUD brightness raise"
	[VHGascii] = 0; A = [VHGNDsurlight]; ? A >= 63 -> VHG HUD brightness done;
	[VHGNDsurlight]+;
    "VHG HUD brightness done"
	end;

    "VHG surface input"
	( Returning to the Stardrifter is automatic, as in NOCTIS-1.CPP. R remains
	  an accessibility shortcut while the standard WASD/arrows stay active.
	  The source's digit-selected fixed advancement is additive, so it can
	  cruise while manual forward/backward input continues. )
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG input done;
	A = [VHGwidephase]; ? A != 0 -> VHG surface input done;
	=> VHG movement ascii latch;
	=> VHG surface pace key;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 82 -> VHG request return; ? A = 114 -> VHG request return;
	? A = 74 -> VHG surface jump request; ? A = 106 -> VHG surface jump request;
	? A = 32 -> VHG surface jet request;
	? A = 67 -> VHG surface jet cancel; ? A = 99 -> VHG surface jet cancel;
	? A = 76 -> VHG surface descend request; ? A = 108 -> VHG surface descend request;
	-> VHG surface look;
    "VHG request return"
	( Like the original recovery sequence, launch is available only after
	  walking back into the capsule.  The luminous beam marks it at range. )
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface look;
	A = [VHGsurfair]; ? A != 0 -> VHG return while airborne;
	A = [VHGx]; A - [VHGNDdropx]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG return absx; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG return absx"
	? A > 1600 -> VHG return too far;
	A = [VHGz]; A - [VHGNDdropz]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG return absz; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG return absz"
	? A > 1600 -> VHG return too far;
	=> VHGC start return; -> VHG surface input done;
    "VHG return too far"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGreturnfartext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG surface input done;
    "VHG return while airborne"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGreturnairtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG surface input done;
    "VHG surface jump request"
	A = [VHGsurfair]; ? A != 0 -> VHG surface look;
	A = 0; A - 500; [VHGsurfvy] = A;
	[VHGsurfair] = 1; [VHGsurfjet] = 0; [VHGsurfjumping] = 1;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGjumptext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 30;
	-> VHG surface look;
    "VHG surface jet request"
	A = [VHGsurfground]; A - 150; C = [VHGy]; ? C '<= A -> VHG surface jet arm;
	A = 0; A - 500; [VHGsurfvy] = A; [VHGsurfair] = 1; [VHGsurfjumping] = 1;
	( The source's first ground pulse launches at -500 but does not arm
	  steering thrust until the walker is at least 150 units above ground. )
	[VHGsurfjet] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGjettext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 30;
	-> VHG surface look;
    "VHG surface jet arm"
	[VHGsurfjet] = 1; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGjettext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 30;
	-> VHG surface look;
    "VHG surface jet cancel"
	[VHGsurfjet] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGjetofftext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 30;
	-> VHG surface look;
    "VHG surface descend request"
	( NIV+ L adds 400 downward velocity whenever the walker is no more than
	  ten units below the terrain plane. On the ground the following clamp
	  absorbs it; in flight it provides the original fast-descent control. )
	A = [VHGsurfground]; A + 10; C = [VHGy]; ? C '>= A -> VHG surface look;
	A = [VHGsurfvy]; A + 400; [VHGsurfvy] = A;
	-> VHG surface look;
"VHG surface look"
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface input done;
	A = [KEY LEFT]; ? A = OFF -> VHG surface no left; A = [VHGbeta]; A + 2; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG surface no left"
	A = [KEY RIGHT]; ? A = OFF -> VHG surface no right; A = [VHGbeta]; A - 2; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG surface no right"
	A = [KEY UP]; ? A = OFF -> VHG surface no up; A = [VHGalpha]; A - 2; [VHGalpha] = A;
    "VHG surface no up"
	A = [KEY DOWN]; ? A = OFF -> VHG surface no down; A = [VHGalpha]; A + 2; [VHGalpha] = A;
    "VHG surface no down"
	A = [VHGalpha]; ? A <= 44 -> VHG surface alpha high; [VHGalpha] = 44;
    "VHG surface alpha high"
	A = [VHGalpha]; ? A >= VHGN44 -> VHG surface alpha low; [VHGalpha] = VHGN44;
    "VHG surface alpha low"
	A = [VHGbeta]; ? A <= 180 -> VHG surface beta high; A - 360; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG surface beta high"
	A = [VHGbeta]; ? A >= VHGN180 -> VHG surface beta low; A + 360; [VHGbeta] = A;
    "VHG surface beta low"
	( opencapdelta freezes autonomous motion while the capsule seals. Keep
	  looking active, but do not let held walking input fight the 1/8 pull. )
	A = [VHGCstate]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface motion available;
	[VHGsurfstep] = 0; [VHGsurfshift] = 0; -> VHG surface input done;
    "VHG surface motion available"
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface input done;
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:3783-3900 accumulates input into retained forward and
	  lateral motion. Movement, friction, slope resistance, and the launch
	  heading are applied together after all input has been sampled. )
	A = [VHGsurfstep]; A + [VHGsurfacefixed]; [VHGsurfstep] = A;
	( Source backups occur after fixed cruise but before mouse/manual input.
	  An ordinary jump restores them, while an armed jetpack accepts steering. )
	[VHGsurfbkstep] = [VHGsurfstep]; [VHGsurfbkshift] = [VHGsurfshift];
    "VHG surface cruise moved"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:3798-3832 changes held-left walking by the terrain under
	  the player. Preserve its 50/75/125/150 source impulses at this port's
	  established eightfold terrain scale, and leave clicks over chrome or an
	  open overlay untouched. )
	A = [Client Owns Mouse Pointer]; ? A = NO -> VHG surface mouse moved;
	A = [VHGgraphics]; A | [VHGmovieview]; A | [VHGhelpshow];
	? A != 0 -> VHG surface mouse moved;
	A = [Pointer X Coordinate]; ? A < [VHGUIleft] -> VHG surface mouse moved;
	C = [VHGUIleft]; C + [VHGUIdw]; ? A '>= C -> VHG surface mouse moved;
	A = [Pointer Y Coordinate]; ? A < [VHGUItop] -> VHG surface mouse moved;
	C = [VHGUItop]; C + [VHGUIdh]; ? A '>= C -> VHG surface mouse moved;
	A = [Pointer Status]; ? A - PD LEFT BUTTON DOWN -> VHG surface mouse moved;
	[VHGstepv] = VHGNDSTEP;
	A = [VHGy]; ? A != 0 -> VHG surface mouse apply;
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A = 3 -> VHG surface mouse habitable;
	[VHGstepv] = 1000; -> VHG surface mouse apply;
    "VHG surface mouse habitable"
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A = 4 -> VHG surface mouse ice;
	? A = 2 -> VHG surface mouse apply;
	[VHGstepv] = 400; -> VHG surface mouse apply;
    "VHG surface mouse ice"
	[VHGstepv] = 1200;
    "VHG surface mouse apply"
	A = [VHGsurfstep]; A + [VHGstepv]; [VHGsurfstep] = A;
    "VHG surface mouse moved"
	[VHGstepv] = VHGNDSTEP;
	A = [KEY CONTROL]; ? A = OFF -> VHG surface pace ready; [VHGstepv] = VHGNDSTALK;
    "VHG surface pace ready"
	A = [KEY W]; ? A = ON -> VHG surface do W;
	A = [VHGmovew]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface no W;
    "VHG surface do W"
	A = [VHGsurfstep]; A + [VHGstepv]; [VHGsurfstep] = A;
    "VHG surface no W"
	A = [KEY S]; ? A = ON -> VHG surface do S;
	A = [VHGmoves]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface no S;
    "VHG surface do S"
	A = [VHGsurfstep]; A - [VHGstepv]; [VHGsurfstep] = A;
    "VHG surface no S"
	A = [KEY A]; ? A = ON -> VHG surface do A;
	A = [VHGmovea]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface no A;
    "VHG surface do A"
	A = [VHGsurfshift]; A - [VHGstepv]; [VHGsurfshift] = A;
    "VHG surface no A"
	A = [KEY D]; ? A = ON -> VHG surface do D;
	A = [VHGmoved]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface moved;
    "VHG surface do D"
	A = [VHGsurfshift]; A + [VHGstepv]; [VHGsurfshift] = A;
    "VHG surface moved"
	[VHGmovew] = 0; [VHGmovea] = 0; [VHGmoves] = 0; [VHGmoved] = 0;
	A = [VHGsurfair]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface movement sampled;
	A = [VHGsurfjet]; ? A != 0 -> VHG surface movement sampled;
	[VHGsurfstep] = [VHGsurfbkstep]; [VHGsurfshift] = [VHGsurfbkshift];
    "VHG surface movement sampled"
	=> VHG surface motion;
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGx]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGz]; => VHGND eye height;
	[VHGsurfground] = [VHGNDheight]; => VHG surface vertical;
	=> VHG surface capsule proximity;
    "VHG surface input done"
	end;

"VHG movement ascii latch"
	( LUCK supplies true held-key state on supported hosts.  The windowed
	  runtime also emits ordinary letters through its console ring; retain one
	  such event until the next 18.206-Hz simulation tick so a character read
	  on either intervening presentation frame cannot be lost. )
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 87 -> VHG movement ascii W; ? A = 119 -> VHG movement ascii W;
	? A = 65 -> VHG movement ascii A; ? A = 97 -> VHG movement ascii A;
	? A = 83 -> VHG movement ascii S; ? A = 115 -> VHG movement ascii S;
	? A = 68 -> VHG movement ascii D; ? A = 100 -> VHG movement ascii D; end;
    "VHG movement ascii W"
	[VHGmovew] = 1; end;
    "VHG movement ascii A"
	[VHGmovea] = 1; end;
    "VHG movement ascii S"
	[VHGmoves] = 1; end;
    "VHG movement ascii D"
	[VHGmoved] = 1;
	end;

"VHG surface pace key"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP adds digit*10 to forward motion on every surface tick and
	  pressing the selected digit again cancels it. Port world units are eight
	  times that source step, the same scale as CTRL stalking speed. )
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A < 48 -> VHG surface pace key done;
	? A > 57 -> VHG surface pace key done;
	[VHGascii] = 0; A - 48; A '* VHGNDSTALK; C = A;
	A = [VHGsurfacefixed]; ? A = C -> VHG surface pace cancel;
	[VHGsurfacefixed] = C; -> VHG surface pace key done;
    "VHG surface pace cancel"
	[VHGsurfacefixed] = 0;
    "VHG surface pace key done"
	end;

"VHG surface vertical"
	( NIV+ starts J and the first jetpack pulse at -500, applies repeated -50
	  thrust without a port-specific cap, and uses a 300-unit near-ground
	  spring before the full p_ray*2000 acceleration. )
	A = [VHGsurfair]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface vertical grounded;
	( Physical SPACE is KEY A+64, the same mapping used by ASCII debounce.
	  Once 150 units clear, holding it arms steering and supplies -50. )
	E = KEY A; E + 64; A = [E]; ? A = OFF -> VHG surface vertical move;
	A = [VHGy]; C = [VHGsurfground]; C - 150; ? A '> C -> VHG surface vertical move;
	[VHGsurfjet] = 1; [VHGsurfjumping] = 1;
	A = [VHGsurfvy]; A - 50; [VHGsurfvy] = A;
    "VHG surface vertical move"
	A = [VHGy]; A + [VHGsurfvy]; [VHGy] = A;
	A = [VHGy]; ? A '< [VHGsurfground] -> VHG surface vertical airborne;
	[VHGy] = [VHGsurfground]; [VHGsurfvy] = 0;
	[VHGsurfair] = 0; [VHGsurfjet] = 0; [VHGsurfjumping] = 0; end;
    "VHG surface vertical airborne"
	A = [VHGsurfground]; A - 300; C = [VHGy]; ? C '<= A -> VHG surface vertical full gravity;
	C - [VHGsurfground]; C '* [VHGsurfaccel]; C / 300;
	A = [VHGsurfvy]; A + C; [VHGsurfvy] = A; -> VHG surface vertical flight state;
    "VHG surface vertical full gravity"
	A = [VHGsurfvy]; A + [VHGsurfaccel]; [VHGsurfvy] = A;
    "VHG surface vertical flight state"
	A = [VHGsurfground]; A - 200; C = [VHGy]; ? C '> A -> VHG surface vertical near ground;
	[VHGsurfjumping] = 1; -> VHG surface vertical done;
    "VHG surface vertical near ground"
	[VHGsurfjumping] = 0; [VHGsurfjet] = 0; -> VHG surface vertical done;
    "VHG surface vertical grounded"
	[VHGy] = [VHGsurfground]; [VHGsurfvy] = 0; [VHGsurfjumping] = 0;
    "VHG surface vertical done"
	end;

"VHG surface capsule proximity"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:4214-4228. The first excursion outside the pod's
	  1600-unit sphere arms recovery. Re-entering that sphere then pulls the
	  player one eighth toward the pod and starts its source-rate closure. )
	A = [VHGCstate]; ? A != 0 -> VHG surface capsule proximity done;
	A = [VHGx]; A - [VHGNDdropx]; [VHGpoddx] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG surface capsule x absolute; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG surface capsule x absolute"
	? A '>= 1600 -> VHG surface capsule arm; C = A; A '* C; [VHGpodrangesq] = A;
	A = [VHGNDdropy]; A - 600; C = [VHGy]; C - A; [VHGpoddy] = C; A = C;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG surface capsule y absolute; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG surface capsule y absolute"
	? A '>= 1600 -> VHG surface capsule arm; C = A; A '* C; C = [VHGpodrangesq]; C + A; [VHGpodrangesq] = C;
	A = [VHGz]; A - [VHGNDdropz]; [VHGpoddz] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG surface capsule z absolute; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG surface capsule z absolute"
	? A '>= 1600 -> VHG surface capsule arm; C = A; A '* C; A + [VHGpodrangesq];
	? A '>= 2560000 -> VHG surface capsule arm;
	A = [VHGCrecover]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface capsule proximity done;
	[VHGcapsulestartpending] = 1; -> VHG surface capsule proximity done;
    "VHG surface capsule arm"
	[VHGCrecover] = 1;
    "VHG surface capsule proximity done"
	end;

"VHG surface motion"
	( Exact source ordering: retain the movement heading while airborne,
	  move with the old velocity, apply ground friction, then undo and replay
	  a steep uphill step with its reduced velocity. )
	A = [VHGsurfair]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface heading update;
	A = [VHGsurfjet]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface heading ready;
    "VHG surface heading update"
	[VHGsurfbeta] = [VHGbeta];
    "VHG surface heading ready"
	[VHGsurfrefx] = [VHGx]; [VHGsurfrefz] = [VHGz];
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGx]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGz]; => VHGND eye height;
	[VHGsurfoldground] = [VHGNDheight];
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGz];
	[VHValpha] = 0; [VHVbeta] = [VHGsurfbeta]; => VH set view;
	[VHGstepv] = [VHGsurfshift]; => VHG strafe;
	[VHGstepv] = [VHGsurfstep]; => VHG forward;
	A = [VHGsurfair]; ? A != 0 -> VHG surface friction done;
	( These retained velocities can be negative.  Linoleum apostrophe division
	  is unsigned, so ordinary signed division is required here and below. )
	A = [VHGsurfshift]; A '* 2; A / 3; [VHGsurfshift] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG surface shift absolute; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG surface shift absolute"
	? A >= 4 -> VHG surface shift retained; [VHGsurfshift] = 0;
    "VHG surface shift retained"
	A = [VHGsurfstep]; A '* 4; A / 5; [VHGsurfstep] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG surface step absolute friction; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG surface step absolute friction"
	? A >= 4 -> VHG surface friction done; [VHGsurfstep] = 0;
    "VHG surface friction done"
	A = [VHGsurfjumping]; ? A != 0 -> VHG surface slope done;
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGx]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGz]; => VHGND eye height;
	[VHGsurfnewground] = [VHGNDheight];
	A = [VHGsurfoldground]; A - [VHGsurfnewground];
	A '* [VHGsurfgravm]; A '* 12; A / 100; A - 24000;
	? A <= 0 -> VHG surface slope done; ? A <= 10000 -> VHG surface slope square; A = 10000;
	-> VHG surface slope factor ready;
    "VHG surface slope square"
	C = A; A '* C; A '/ 10000;
    "VHG surface slope factor ready"
	[VHGsurfuphillq] = A; C = 10000; C - A;
	A = [VHGsurfshift]; A '* C; A / 10000; [VHGsurfshift] = A;
	A = [VHGsurfstep]; A '* C; A / 10000; [VHGsurfstep] = A;
	[VHGx] = [VHGsurfrefx]; [VHGz] = [VHGsurfrefz];
	[VHGstepv] = [VHGsurfshift]; => VHG strafe;
	[VHGstepv] = [VHGsurfstep]; => VHG forward;
    "VHG surface slope done"
	=> VHG surface level pitch;
	( Q10000 tiredness uses the source's retained forward step. The remainder
	  preserves sub-unit rises after converting from the eightfold port scale. )
	A = [VHGsurftiredq]; A '* 9977; A '/ 10000; [VHGsurftiredq] = A;
	A = [VHGsurfstep]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG surface speed absolute; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG surface speed absolute"
	[VHGNDplayerstep] = A; A + [VHGsurftiredr]; C = A; A '/ 800;
	A + [VHGsurftiredq]; [VHGsurftiredq] = A;
	A = C; A '% 800; [VHGsurftiredr] = A;
	=> VHG surface clamp;
	( Restore the visible camera basis for any input work after this routine. )
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGz];
	[VHValpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGbeta]; => VH set view;
	end;

"VHG surface level pitch"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP levels user_alfa by
	      alfa /= 1 + fabs(step) * 0.000064
	  while the walker is within 1200 vertical units of the ground. Surface
	  X/Z motion is eightfold in this adapter, so the same ratio is
	  125000/(125000+abs(port_step)). Retain the fractional decrement as an
	  accumulator so the integer camera approaches level gradually instead of
	  snapping by a whole degree every source tick. )
	A = [VHGsurfground]; A - 1200; C = [VHGy]; ? C '<= A -> VHG surface level done;
	A = [VHGsurfstep]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG surface level speed; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG surface level speed"
	[VHGstepv] = A; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface level done;
	C = [VHGalpha]; ? C != 0 -> VHG surface level active;
	[VHGsurflevelacc] = 0; -> VHG surface level done;
    "VHG surface level active"
	A = C; ? A >= 0 -> VHG surface level alpha absolute; A +-;
    "VHG surface level alpha absolute"
	A '* [VHGstepv]; A + [VHGsurflevelacc]; [VHGsurflevelacc] = A;
	C = 125000; C + [VHGstepv]; A '/ C; [VHGsurfscale] = A;
	A = [VHGsurflevelacc]; A '% C; [VHGsurflevelacc] = A;
	A = [VHGsurfscale]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface level done;
	C = [VHGalpha]; ? C < 0 -> VHG surface level negative;
	C - A; ? C >= 0 -> VHG surface level store; C = 0; -> VHG surface level store;
    "VHG surface level negative"
	C + A; ? C <= 0 -> VHG surface level store; C = 0;
    "VHG surface level store"
	[VHGalpha] = C;
    "VHG surface level done"
	end;
    "VHG quit"
	=> VHG capsule checkpoint collapse; [VHGesc] = 1;
	end;

"VHG forward"
	[FI] = [VHGstepv]; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSTSB; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = FSTCA; => PGF mul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGx]; A - [FI]; [VHGx] = A;
	[PGFi] = FSTCB; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = FSTCA; => PGF mul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGz]; A + [FI]; [VHGz] = A;
	end;

"VHG strafe"
	[FI] = [VHGstepv]; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSTCB; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = FSTCA; => PGF mul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGx]; A + [FI]; [VHGx] = A;
	[PGFi] = FSTSB; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = FSTCA; => PGF mul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGz]; A + [FI]; [VHGz] = A;
	end;

"VHG clamp position"
	( vehicle main-loop interior bounds, NOCTIS.CPP:2507-2510 )
	A = [VHGx]; ? A >= 0FFFFF3E4h -> VHG x low ok; [VHGx] = 0FFFFF3E4h;
    "VHG x low ok"
	A = [VHGx]; ? A <= 3100 -> VHG x high ok; [VHGx] = 3100;
    "VHG x high ok"
	A = [VHGz]; ? A <= 0FFFFFED4h -> VHG z near ok; [VHGz] = 0FFFFFED4h;
    "VHG z near ok"
	A = [VHGz]; ? A >= 0FFFFE958h -> VHG z far ok; [VHGz] = 0FFFFE958h;
    "VHG z far ok"
	end;

"VHG platform"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:4106-4108 starts the ascent directly from the key event.
	  E deliberately substitutes for DOS Up because all four arrows look in
	  this port.  Keep the source's -100 ascent impulse.  Smooth mode presents
	  the eight authoritative lift steps across roughly 26 display frames, so
	  authentic gameplay timing no longer needs a slower surrogate.  Roof
	  return remains the source's +75. )
	A = [VHGlifter]; ? A != 0 -> VHG platform done;
	A = [VHGonroof]; ? A != 0 -> VHG platform done;
	A = [VHGuprequest]; ? A = 0 -> VHG platform done;
	A = 0; A - 100; [VHGlifter] = A; [VHGliftstep] = 0;
    "VHG platform done"
	[VHGuprequest] = 0;
	end;

"VHG lift tick"
	A = [VHGlifter]; ? A = 0 -> VHG lift hold centre;
	A = [VHGy]; A + [VHGlifter]; [VHGy] = A;
	A = [VHGlifter]; ? A > 0 -> VHG lift descending;
	[VHGlifter]+;
	A = [VHGy]; ? A <= 0FFFFFEBBh -> VHG lift rise middle; ( pos_y <= -325 )
	A = [VHGalpha]; A - 40; A * 12; A / 100;
	C = [VHGalpha]; C + A; [VHGalpha] = C;
    "VHG lift rise middle"
	( The source pushes forward by -pos_y only through the middle of the
	  ascent, after changing pitch and before the centre restraint.  This is
	  the visible approach through the opening, not ordinary player input. )
	A = [VHGy]; ? A <= 0FFFFFD35h -> VHG lift hold centre; ( pos_y <= -715 )
	? A >= 0FFFFFEBBh -> VHG lift hold centre; ( pos_y >= -325 )
	C = 0; C - A; [VHGliftstep] = C;
	-> VHG lift hold centre;
    "VHG lift descending"
	[VHGlifter]-;
	A = [VHGlifter]; ? A <= 65 -> VHG lift level look;
	A = 40; A - [VHGalpha]; A * 11; A / 100;
	C = [VHGalpha]; C + A; [VHGalpha] = C; -> VHG lift descent move;
    "VHG lift level look"
	A = [VHGalpha]; A / 4; C = [VHGalpha]; C - A; [VHGalpha] = C;
    "VHG lift descent move"
	A = [VHGlifter]; A / 2; [VHGliftstep] = A;
    "VHG lift hold centre"
	( NOCTIS.CPP clamps the vertical endpoint before its ordinary forward
	  movement and the later hold-to-middle pass.  In particular, the final
	  ascent frame has lifter=0 and must not be pulled back into the opening. )
	A = [VHGy]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG lift upper ok;
	[VHGy] = 0; [VHGlifter] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGliftdecktext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
    "VHG lift upper ok"
	A = [VHGy]; ? A >= 0FFFFFD12h -> VHG lift roof flag;
	[VHGy] = 0FFFFFD12h; [VHGlifter] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGliftrooftext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
    "VHG lift roof flag"
	[VHGonroof] = 0; A = [VHGy]; ? A >= 0FFFFFE0Ch -> VHG lift done;
	[VHGonroof] = 1;
    "VHG lift done"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:2303-2306: once on the roof and not already riding,
	  entering the spatial aperture automatically begins the +75 descent.
	  Use the retained source step, including its coast, in distance+step. )
	A = [VHGlifter]; ? A != 0 -> VHG lift motion;
	A = [VHGonroof]; ? A = 0 -> VHG lift motion;
	=> VHG lift distance;
	A = [VHGdist]; A + [VHGliftstep]; ? A >= 1100 -> VHG lift motion;
	[VHGlifter] = 75;
	-> VHG lift motion;

"VHG lift distance"
	( DfCoS = sqrt(pos_x^2 + (pos_z+3100)^2). )
	[FI] = [VHGx]; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW2; => PGF sa;
	A = [VHGz]; A + 3100; [FI] = A; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW2; => PGF add;
	=> FSqrt; => FToIntNear; [VHGdist] = [FI];
	end;

    "VHG lift motion"
	A = [VHGliftstep]; ? A = 0 -> VHG lift tick done;
	[VHGstepv] = A; => VHG lift move;
	( Source friction is step /= 1.25 after movement.  Retaining that value is
	  what carries the rider clear of the aperture on the final roof frame. )
	A = [VHGliftstep]; A * 4; A / 5; [VHGliftstep] = A;
    "VHG lift tick done"
	end;

"VHG lift postrender"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:3583-3587 restrains the player after vehicle() has rendered
	  and before keyboard input.  Keeping that order is necessary because the
	  roof aperture is spatial and follows the pre-restraint camera position. )
	( In 60-Hz presentation mode this source-loop operation still runs only
	  on an 18.206-Hz simulation tick. )
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG lift postrender done;
	A = [VHGlifter]; ? A = 0 -> VHG lift postrender done;
	A = [VHGx]; A * 3; A / 4; [VHGx] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A + 3100; A / 4; C = [VHGz]; C - A; [VHGz] = C;
    "VHG lift postrender done"
	end;

"VHG lift move"
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGz];
	( from_user() and p_Forward(step) share user_beta in NOCTIS.CPP.  Use the
	  visible camera heading here too, so the ride never moves backward under
	  a separately rotated basis. )
	[VHValpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGbeta]; => VH set view;
	=> VHG forward;
	end;

"VHG land"
	A = [VHGplanet]; ? A '< [nsnob] -> VHG land target valid;
	-> VHG land rejected;
    "VHG land target valid"
	A = nsptype; A + [VHGplanet]; [VHGptype] = [A];
	A = [VHGptype]; ? A = 0 -> VHG land rejected; ? A = 6 -> VHG land rejected;
	? A = 9 -> VHG land rejected; ? A '>= 10 -> VHG land rejected;
		[VHGshipx] = [VHGx]; [VHGshipy] = [VHGy]; [VHGshipz] = [VHGz];
		[VHGNDlon] = [VHGlandinglon]; [VHGNDlat] = [VHGlandinglat];
		=> VHG select texture buffer;
		=> VHG prepare planet; => VHG fpu clean;
		=> VHGND generate; => VHG fpu clean;
		=> VHGND sky; => VHG fpu clean; => VHG surface telemetry init;
		=> VHGC start descent; => VHG fpu clean;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGdescenttext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;
    "VHG land rejected"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGlandfailtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;

"VHG prepare planet"
	A = nsptype; A + [VHGplanet]; [VHGptype] = [A];
	[GRiptype] = [VHGptype];
	[VHGNDplanet] = [VHGplanet];
	( Keep the measured opening-system seeds exact.  Other stars use nstopo's
	  retained source geometry at the original live conversion boundary. )
	A = [VHTtx]; ? A != 3979984 -> VHG land general seed;
	A = [VHTty]; ? A != 0FFFF5671h -> VHG land general seed;
	A = [VHTtz]; ? A != 0FFFF5430h -> VHG land general seed;
	[VHGNDseed] = 149130;
	A = [VHGplanet]; ? A = 0 -> VHG land seed ready; [VHGNDseed] = 293154;
	? A = 1 -> VHG land seed ready; [VHGNDseed] = 569446;
	? A = 2 -> VHG land seed ready; [VHGNDseed] = 19668;
	? A = 3 -> VHG land seed ready; [VHGNDseed] = 31169;
	-> VHG land seed ready;
    "VHG land general seed"
	( global_surface_seed = (p_ray + p_orb_ray + p_orb_orient) * 4112. )
	A = [VHGplanet]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[FB0] = [E plus 0]; [FB1] = [E plus 1];
	E = nsporbray; E + A; [FC0] = [E plus 0]; [FC1] = [E plus 1];
	E = nspororient; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	=> GeoSurfaceSeedChop;
	[VHGNDseed] = [FI];
    "VHG land seed ready"
	[VHGNDatmosphere] = 1;
	A = [VHGptype]; ? A = 1 -> VHG land airless; ? A = 4 -> VHG land airless; ? A = 7 -> VHG land airless;
	-> VHG land atmosphere ready;
    "VHG land airless"
	[VHGNDatmosphere] = 0;
    "VHG land atmosphere ready"
	end;

"VHG select texture buffer"
	( planets() routes moons through s_background and primaries through
	  p_background.  The ground path keeps sampling the same selected base. )
	[VHGNDtexbase] = RPBG;
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGplanet]; A = [E]; ? A < 0 -> VHG texture buffer ready;
	[VHGNDtexbase] = RSBG;
    "VHG texture buffer ready"
	end;

"VHG command"
	A = [VHGslactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command ready;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGslbusytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; end;
    "VHG command ready"
	A = [VHGdlactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command parse;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGdlbusytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; end;
    "VHG command parse"
	A = [vhptext plus 42]; ? A = 83 -> VHG command starts s;
	? A = 76 -> VHG command maybe load;
	? A = 78 -> VHG command starts n;
	? A = 67 -> VHG command maybe clear;
	? A = 68 -> VHG command maybe dele;
	? A = 80 -> VHG command maybe par;
	? A = 82 -> VHG command maybe rep;
	? A = 79 -> VHG command maybe outbox;
	? A = 73 -> VHG command maybe inbox;
	? A = 87 -> VHG command maybe where;
	? A = 72 -> VHG command maybe help;
	? A = 88 -> VHG command x parse; -> VHG command done;
	"VHG command x parse"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command x ready;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command x ready; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
	"VHG command x ready"
	A = vhptext; A + 43; [VHGxmsgptr] = A; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1;
	=> VHG X queue; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe help"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 76 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 80 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command help ready;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command help ready; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command help ready"
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VHG GOES HELP; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe inbox"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A = 77 -> VHG command maybe importgd;
	? A != 78 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 66 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 79 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A != 88 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 47]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command inbox ready;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command inbox ready; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command inbox ready"
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VHG INBOX; -> VHG command done;
