    "VHG command maybe importgd"
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 80 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 79 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A != 82 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 47]; ? A != 84 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 48]; ? A != 71 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 49]; ? A != 68 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 50]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command importgd ready;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command importgd ready; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command importgd ready"
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 1; [VHPoutptr] = VHGimportold0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGimportold1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGimportold2; => VH GOES output line; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe outbox"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A != 85 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 84 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 66 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A != 79 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 47]; ? A != 88 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 48]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command outbox ready;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command outbox ready; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command outbox ready"
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VHG OUTBOX; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe dele"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A = 76 -> VHG command dl parse;
	? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 76 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command dele parse;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command dele parse; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command dele parse"
	[VHGcmdi] = 46; [VHGcatstart] = 1; [VHGcatend] = 32767;
    "VHG command dele spaces"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 32 -> VHG command dele start;
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command dele spaces;
    "VHG command dele start"
	[VHGwherequery] = A; [VHGwherelen] = 0;
    "VHG command dele length"
	? C = 0 -> VHG command dele trim; ? C = 58 -> VHG command dele range start;
	? C != 95 -> VHG command dele character;
	E = A; E + 1; E = [E]; ? E = 0 -> VHG command dele trim;
    "VHG command dele character"
	[VHGwherelen]+; [VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A];
	-> VHG command dele length;
    "VHG command dele range start"
	[VHGcmdi]+; [VHGcatvalue] = 0; [VHGcatdigits] = 0;
    "VHG command dele range first digits"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C < 48 -> VHG command dele range first done;
	? C > 57 -> VHG command dele range first done;
	A = [VHGcatdigits]; ? A >= 9 -> VHG command dele range first done;
	A = [VHGcatvalue]; A '* 10; C - 48; A + C; [VHGcatvalue] = A;
	[VHGcatdigits]+; [VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command dele range first digits;
    "VHG command dele range first done"
	[VHGcatstart] = [VHGcatvalue];
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 46 -> VHG command dele trim;
	A + 1; C = [A]; ? C != 46 -> VHG command dele trim;
	[VHGcmdi] + 2; [VHGcatvalue] = 0; [VHGcatdigits] = 0;
    "VHG command dele range last digits"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C < 48 -> VHG command dele range last done;
	? C > 57 -> VHG command dele range last done;
	A = [VHGcatdigits]; ? A >= 9 -> VHG command dele range last done;
	A = [VHGcatvalue]; A '* 10; C - 48; A + C; [VHGcatvalue] = A;
	[VHGcatdigits]+; [VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command dele range last digits;
    "VHG command dele range last done"
	[VHGcatend] = [VHGcatvalue];
    "VHG command dele trim"
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command dele ready;
	A - 1; C = [VHGwherequery]; C + A; A = [C]; ? A != 32 -> VHG command dele ready;
	[VHGwherelen]-; -> VHG command dele trim;
    "VHG command dele ready"
	[VHGwhereunderscores] = 1; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VHG DELE; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command dl parse"
	[VHGcmdi] = 44; [VHGparaction] = 2; [VHGparrange] = 100;
	-> VHG command par spaces;
    "VHG command maybe rep"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 80 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A = 65 -> VHG command maybe repair;
	? A = 0 -> VHG command rep parse;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command rep parse; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe repair"
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A != 73 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 47]; ? A != 82 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 48]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command repair ready;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command repair ready; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command repair ready"
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VHG REPAIR; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command rep parse"
	[VHGcmdi] = 45;
    "VHG command rep spaces"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 32 -> VHG command rep start;
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command rep spaces;
    "VHG command rep start"
	[VHGwherequery] = A; [VHGwherelen] = 0; [VHGreptarget] = 0; [VHGrepdigits] = 0;
	[VHGrepmsgptr] = 0; [VHGrepmsglen] = 0;
    "VHG command rep name"
	? C = 0 -> VHG command rep no object colon; ? C = 58 -> VHG command rep record start;
	? C != 95 -> VHG command rep name character;
	E = A; E + 1; E = [E]; ? E = 0 -> VHG command rep no object colon;
    "VHG command rep name character"
	[VHGwherelen]+; [VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A];
	-> VHG command rep name;
    "VHG command rep no object colon"
	[VHGwhereunderscores] = 1; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1;
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command rep usage;
	=> VHG REP object syntax; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command rep usage"
	=> VHG REP; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command rep record start"
	[VHGcmdi]+;
    "VHG command rep record digits"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C = 58 -> VHG command rep message start;
	? C < 48 -> VHG command rep no record colon; ? C > 57 -> VHG command rep no record colon;
	A = [VHGrepdigits]; ? A >= 9 -> VHG command rep no record colon;
	A = [VHGreptarget]; A '* 10; C - 48; A + C; [VHGreptarget] = A;
	[VHGrepdigits]+; [VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command rep record digits;
    "VHG command rep no record colon"
	[VHGwhereunderscores] = 1; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1;
	=> VHG REP record syntax; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command rep message start"
	[VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; [VHGrepmsgptr] = A;
    "VHG command rep message length"
	C = [A]; ? C = 0 -> VHG command rep message ready;
	? C != 95 -> VHG command rep message character;
	E = A; E + 1; E = [E]; ? E = 0 -> VHG command rep message ready;
    "VHG command rep message character"
	C = [VHGrepmsglen]; ? C >= 76 -> VHG command rep message ready;
	[VHGrepmsglen]+; [VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi];
	-> VHG command rep message length;
    "VHG command rep message ready"
	[VHGwhereunderscores] = 1; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1;
	A = [VHGrepmsglen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command rep void;
	=> VHG REP; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command rep void"
	=> VHG CAST void result; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe clear"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A = 65 -> VHG command maybe cat;
	? A != 76 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A = 69 -> VHG command maybe clean;
	? A != 82 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command clear ready;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command clear ready; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe clean"
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 65 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A != 78 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 47]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command clean ready;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command clean ready; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command clean ready"
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VHG CLEAN; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command clear ready"
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VH GOES output clear; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe cat"
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A = 83 -> VHG command maybe cast;
	? A != 84 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command cat select;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command cat select; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
	"VHG command cat select"
	[VHGcataction] = 0; -> VHG command cat parse;
    "VHG command maybe cast"
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 84 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command cast parse;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command cast parse; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command cast parse"
	[VHGcmdi] = 46;
    "VHG command cast spaces"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 32 -> VHG command cast start;
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command cast spaces;
    "VHG command cast start"
	[VHGwherequery] = A; [VHGwherelen] = 0; [VHGcastmsgptr] = 0; [VHGcastmsglen] = 0;
    "VHG command cast name"
	? C = 0 -> VHG command cast no colon; ? C = 58 -> VHG command cast message start;
	? C != 95 -> VHG command cast name character;
	E = A; E + 1; E = [E]; ? E = 0 -> VHG command cast no colon;
    "VHG command cast name character"
	[VHGwherelen]+; [VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A];
	-> VHG command cast name;
    "VHG command cast no colon"
	[VHGwhereunderscores] = 1; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1;
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command cast usage;
	=> VHG CAST syntax result; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command cast usage"
	=> VHG CAST; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command cast message start"
	[VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; [VHGcastmsgptr] = A;
    "VHG command cast message length"
	C = [A]; ? C = 0 -> VHG command cast message ready;
	? C != 95 -> VHG command cast message character;
	E = A; E + 1; E = [E]; ? E = 0 -> VHG command cast message ready;
    "VHG command cast message character"
	C = [VHGcastmsglen]; ? C >= 76 -> VHG command cast message ready;
	[VHGcastmsglen]+; [VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi];
	-> VHG command cast message length;
    "VHG command cast message ready"
	[VHGwhereunderscores] = 1; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1;
	A = [VHGcastmsglen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command cast void;
	=> VHG CAST; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command cast void"
	=> VHG CAST void result; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command cat parse"
	[VHGcmdi] = 45; [VHGcatstart] = 1; [VHGcatend] = 32767;
    "VHG command cat spaces"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 32 -> VHG command cat start;
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command cat spaces;
    "VHG command cat start"
	[VHGwherequery] = A; [VHGwherelen] = 0;
    "VHG command cat length"
	? C = 0 -> VHG command cat trim; ? C = 58 -> VHG command cat range start;
	? C != 95 -> VHG command cat character;
	E = A; E + 1; E = [E]; ? E = 0 -> VHG command cat trim;
    "VHG command cat character"
	[VHGwherelen]+; [VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A];
	-> VHG command cat length;
    "VHG command cat range start"
	[VHGcmdi]+; [VHGcatvalue] = 0; [VHGcatdigits] = 0;
    "VHG command cat range first digits"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C < 48 -> VHG command cat range first done;
	? C > 57 -> VHG command cat range first done;
	A = [VHGcatdigits]; ? A >= 9 -> VHG command cat range first done;
	A = [VHGcatvalue]; A '* 10; C - 48; A + C; [VHGcatvalue] = A;
	[VHGcatdigits]+; [VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command cat range first digits;
    "VHG command cat range first done"
	[VHGcatstart] = [VHGcatvalue];
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 46 -> VHG command cat trim;
	A + 1; C = [A]; ? C != 46 -> VHG command cat trim;
	[VHGcmdi] + 2; [VHGcatvalue] = 0; [VHGcatdigits] = 0;
    "VHG command cat range last digits"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C < 48 -> VHG command cat range last done;
	? C > 57 -> VHG command cat range last done;
	A = [VHGcatdigits]; ? A >= 9 -> VHG command cat range last done;
	A = [VHGcatvalue]; A '* 10; C - 48; A + C; [VHGcatvalue] = A;
	[VHGcatdigits]+; [VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command cat range last digits;
    "VHG command cat range last done"
	[VHGcatend] = [VHGcatvalue];
    "VHG command cat trim"
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command cat ready;
	A - 1; C = [VHGwherequery]; C + A; A = [C]; ? A != 32 -> VHG command cat ready;
	[VHGwherelen]-; -> VHG command cat trim;
    "VHG command cat ready"
	[VHGwhereunderscores] = 1; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1;
	A = [VHGcataction]; ? A != 0 -> VHG command pri ready;
	=> VHG CAT; -> VHG command done;
	"VHG command pri ready"
	=> VHG PRI; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe where"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A != 72 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 82 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 47]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command where parse;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command where parse; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command where parse"
	[VHGcmdi] = 47;
    "VHG command where spaces"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 32 -> VHG command where start;
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command where spaces;
    "VHG command where start"
	[VHGwherequery] = A; [VHGwherelen] = 0;
    "VHG command where length"
	? C = 0 -> VHG command where trim; ? C = 95 -> VHG command where trim;
	[VHGwherelen]+; [VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A];
	-> VHG command where length;
    "VHG command where trim"
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command where ready;
	A - 1; C = [VHGwherequery]; C + A; A = [C]; ? A != 32 -> VHG command where ready;
	[VHGwherelen]-; -> VHG command where trim;
    "VHG command where ready"
	[VHGwhereunderscores] = 0; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VHG WHERE; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe par"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A = 82 -> VHG command maybe pri;
	? A != 65 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 82 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command par parse;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command par parse; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
	"VHG command maybe pri"
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 73 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A = 70 -> VHG command maybe prif;
	? A = 0 -> VHG command pri parse;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command pri parse; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
	"VHG command maybe prif"
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command prif parse;
	? A = 32 -> VHG command prif parse; ? A != 95 -> VHG command done;
	"VHG command pri parse"
	[VHGcataction] = 1; -> VHG command cat parse;
	"VHG command prif parse"
	[VHGcmdi] = 46; [VHGcatstart] = 1; [VHGcatend] = 32767;
	[VHGcataction] = 2; -> VHG command cat spaces;
    "VHG command par parse"
	[VHGcmdi] = 45; [VHGparaction] = 0; [VHGparrange] = 100;
	-> VHG command par spaces;
    "VHG command st parse"
	[VHGcmdi] = 44; [VHGparaction] = 1; [VHGparrange] = 100;
    "VHG command par spaces"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 32 -> VHG command par start;
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command par spaces;
    "VHG command par start"
	[VHGwherequery] = A; [VHGwherelen] = 0;
    "VHG command par length"
	? C = 0 -> VHG command par trim; ? C = 58 -> VHG command par range start;
	? C != 95 -> VHG command par character;
	E = A; E + 1; E = [E]; ? E = 0 -> VHG command par trim;
    "VHG command par character"
	[VHGwherelen]+; [VHGcmdi]+; A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A];
	-> VHG command par length;
    "VHG command par range start"
	[VHGcmdi]+; [VHGparvalue] = 0; [VHGpardigits] = 0;
    "VHG command par range digits"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C < 48 -> VHG command par range done;
	? C > 57 -> VHG command par range done;
	A = [VHGpardigits]; ? A >= 6 -> VHG command par range default;
	A = [VHGparvalue]; A '* 10; C - 48; A + C; [VHGparvalue] = A;
	[VHGpardigits]+; [VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command par range digits;
    "VHG command par range done"
	A = [VHGpardigits]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command par range default;
	A = [VHGparvalue]; ? A <= 2 -> VHG command par range default;
	? A > 10000 -> VHG command par range default; [VHGparrange] = A;
	-> VHG command par trim;
    "VHG command par range default"
	[VHGparrange] = 100;
    "VHG command par trim"
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command par ready;
	A - 1; C = [VHGwherequery]; C + A; A = [C]; ? A != 32 -> VHG command par ready;
	[VHGwherelen]-; -> VHG command par trim;
    "VHG command par ready"
	[VHGwhereunderscores] = 1; [VHGcmdsilent] = 1;
	A = [VHGparaction]; ? A = 2 -> VHG command dl ready;
	=> VHG PAR; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command dl ready"
	=> VHG DL; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command starts s"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A = 65 -> VHG command maybe save;
	? A = 76 -> VHG command maybe sl;
	? A = 84 -> VHG command starts st; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe sl"
	[VHGcmdi] = 44;
    "VHG command sl spaces"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 32 -> VHG command sl argument;
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command sl spaces;
    "VHG command sl argument"
	? C = 0 -> VHG command sl ready; ? C = 95 -> VHG command sl ready;
	=> VHG parse coordinate; A = [VHGcmdok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command sl range tail"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C = 32 -> VHG command sl range space;
	? C = 0 -> VHG command sl range ready; ? C = 95 -> VHG command sl range ready;
	-> VHG command done;
    "VHG command sl range space"
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command sl range tail;
    "VHG command sl range ready"
	A = [VHGcmdvalue]; ? A <= 2 -> VHG command sl range default;
	? A > 10000 -> VHG command sl range default; [VHGparrange] = A;
	-> VHG command sl ranged;
    "VHG command sl range default"
	[VHGparrange] = 100;
    "VHG command sl ranged"
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VHG SL ranged start; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command sl ready"
	[VHGcmdsilent] = 1; => VHG SL; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command starts st"
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A = 65 -> VHG command maybe star;
	? A = 0 -> VHG command st parse; ? A = 32 -> VHG command st parse;
	? A = 95 -> VHG command st parse; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe save"
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 86 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	=> VHSV save; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe load"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A != 79 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 65 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 68 -> VHG command done;
	=> VHG load checkpoint; A = [VHSVok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command done;
	-> VHG command done;

    "VHG command starts n"
	A = [vhptext plus 43]; ? A = 65 -> VHG command maybe name;
	? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A = 87 -> VHG command maybe new;
	? A != 88 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 84 -> VHG command done;
	=> VHG next star; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe new"
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A > 32 -> VHG command done;
	=> VHG new game; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe name"
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 77 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 46]; ? A != 32 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 47]; ? A = 83 -> VHG command name star;
	? A = 80 -> VHG command name planet; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command name star"
	A = [vhptext plus 48]; ? A != 84 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 49]; ? A != 65 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 50]; ? A != 82 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 51]; ? A != 32 -> VHG command done;
	[VHGcmdi] = 52; => VHG capture name; A = [VHGcmdok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG name failed;
	[VHCATtype] = VHCATS; [VHCATcode] = [VHTclass];
	[VHCATid0] = [VHTid0]; [VHCATid1] = [VHTid1];
	=> VHCAT add; -> VHG name result;
    "VHG command name planet"
	A = [vhptext plus 48]; ? A != 76 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 49]; ? A != 65 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 50]; ? A != 78 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 51]; ? A != 69 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 52]; ? A != 84 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 53]; ? A != 32 -> VHG command done;
	A = [VHGplanet]; ? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG name failed;
	[VHGcmdi] = 54; => VHG capture name; A = [VHGcmdok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG name failed;
	[VHCATtype] = VHCATP; A = [VHGplanet]; A + 1; [VHCATcode] = A;
	[nsid0] = [VHTid0]; [nsid1] = [VHTid1]; [FI] = A; => NsIdentAddInt;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1];
	=> VHCAT add;
    "VHG name result"
	A = [VHCATstatus]; ? A = 1 -> VHG name added;
	? A = 2 -> VHG name charted; ? A = 3 -> VHG name used; -> VHG name failed;
    "VHG name added"
	=> VHCAT count system bodies; => VHCAT refresh;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGnameoktext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 100; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG name charted"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGnamechartedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 100; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG name used"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGnameusedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 100; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG name failed"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGnamefailtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 100; -> VHG command done;
    "VHG command maybe star"
	A = [vhptext plus 44]; ? A != 65 -> VHG command done;
	A = [vhptext plus 45]; ? A != 82 -> VHG command done;
	[VHGcmdi] = 46; => VHG parse coordinate;
	A = [VHGcmdok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG target failed; [VHGcmdx] = [VHGcmdvalue];
	=> VHG parse coordinate; A = [VHGcmdok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG target failed;
	[VHGcmdy] = [VHGcmdvalue];
	=> VHG parse coordinate; A = [VHGcmdok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG target failed;
	[VHGcmdz] = [VHGcmdvalue];
    "VHG command star tail"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C = 32 -> VHG command star tail space;
	? C = 0 -> VHG command star ready; ? C = 95 -> VHG command star ready;
	-> VHG target failed;
    "VHG command star tail space"
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG command star tail;
    "VHG command star ready"
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG target ship only;
	[VHTtx] = [VHGcmdx]; [VHTty] = [VHGcmdy]; [VHTtz] = [VHGcmdz];
	=> VHG activate target; -> VHG command done;

"VHG SL"
	( SL.CPP without its optional range lists every non-removed catalogued star
	  in file order.  Keep the leading asterisk and all twenty padded name cells
	  so the expanded physical output history is byte-for-byte scrollable. )
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG SL bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG SL bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslglobal; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGslleast] = 0; [VHGslrecno] = 0;
    "VHG SL catalogue loop"
	A = [VHGslrecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG SL catalogue done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGslptr] = A;
	C = [A]; ? C != VHCATTOMB0 -> VHG SL record live;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C = VHCATTOMB1 -> VHG SL catalogue next;
    "VHG SL record live"
	A = [VHGslptr]; A = [A plus 7]; A > 8; A & FFh;
	? A != VHCATS -> VHG SL catalogue next;
	[VHGslleast] = 1; [VHGslline plus 0] = 42; [VHGsli] = 0;
    "VHG SL label copy"
	A = [VHGsli]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGslptr]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; C = VHGslline; C + 1; C + [VHGsli]; [C] = A;
	[VHGsli]+; A = [VHGsli]; ? A < 20 -> VHG SL label copy;
	[VHGslline plus 21] = 0; [VHPoutptr] = VHGslline; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG SL catalogue next"
	[VHGslrecno]+; -> VHG SL catalogue loop;
    "VHG SL catalogue done"
	A = [VHGslleast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG SL no stars;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslend; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG SL no stars"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslnone; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG SL ranged start"
	( SL.CPP's optional range examines every non-removed catalogue star in file
	  order against a centred procedural sector cube.  The DOS module monopolised
	  the machine while doing so; retain its ordering and results but advance a
	  bounded candidate batch per host frame so resize, repaint, and Escape remain
	  responsive throughout a long scan. )
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG SL ranged bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG SL ranged bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslranged; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGslgalx0] = [MgDzatX0]; [VHGslgalx1] = [MgDzatX1];
	[VHGslgaly0] = [MgDzatY0]; [VHGslgaly1] = [MgDzatY1];
	[VHGslgalz0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [VHGslgalz1] = [MgDzatZ1];
	A = [VHGparrange]; A '* 50000; [VHGparoffset] = A;
	[VHGparaxis0] = [VHGslgalx0]; [VHGparaxis1] = [VHGslgalx1]; => VHG PAR sector base;
	[VHGparbasex] = [VHGparbaseout];
	[VHGparaxis0] = [VHGslgaly0]; [VHGparaxis1] = [VHGslgaly1]; => VHG PAR sector base;
	[VHGparbasey] = [VHGparbaseout];
	[VHGparaxis0] = [VHGslgalz0]; [VHGparaxis1] = [VHGslgalz1]; => VHG PAR sector base;
	[VHGparbasez] = [VHGparbaseout];
	[VHGslleast] = 0; [VHGslrecno] = 0; [VHGslphase] = 0; [VHGslactive] = 1;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGslbusytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;

"VHG SL advance"
	A = [VHGslactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG SL advance done;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGslbusytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 2;
	[VHGslbudget] = 65536;
    "VHG SL advance loop"
	A = [VHGslbudget]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG SL advance done;
	A = [VHGslphase]; ? A != 0 -> VHG SL scan candidate;
    "VHG SL find record"
	A = [VHGslrecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG SL ranged complete;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGslptr] = A;
	C = [A]; ? C != VHCATTOMB0 -> VHG SL ranged record live;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C = VHCATTOMB1 -> VHG SL ranged next record;
    "VHG SL ranged record live"
	A = [VHGslptr]; A = [A plus 7]; A > 8; A & FFh;
	? A != VHCATS -> VHG SL ranged next record; [VHGslleast] = 1;
	A = [VHGslptr]; [VHCATid0] = [A]; [VHCATid1] = [A plus 1];
	=> VHCAT identity valid; A = [VHCATvalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG SL ranged next record;
	A = [VHGslptr]; [FA0] = [A]; [FA1] = [A plus 1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FSub;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparlowhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparlowlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	A = [VHGslptr]; [FA0] = [A]; [FA1] = [A plus 1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FAdd;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparhighhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparhighlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[VHGparix] = 0; [VHGpariy] = 0; [VHGpariz] = 0;
	[VHGparsectx] = [VHGparbasex]; [VHGparsecty] = [VHGparbasey]; [VHGparsectz] = [VHGparbasez];
	[VHGslphase] = 1; -> VHG SL advance loop;
    "VHG SL ranged next record"
	[VHGslrecno]+; -> VHG SL advance loop;

    "VHG SL scan candidate"
	[VHGslbudget]-;
	A = [VHGparsectx]; A + [VHGparsectz]; [VHSsumxz] = A;
	A & 1FFFFh; A + [VHGparsectx]; ? A = VHSCUTOFF -> VHG SL scan next z;
	A - VHSCUTOFF; [VHStempx] = A;
	[VHSfmA] = A; [VHSfmB] = [VHSsumxz]; => VHS foldmul;
	[VHSaccum] = [VHSfmResult];
	A = [VHSsumxz]; A + [VHSaccum]; [VHSidk] = A;
	A = [VHSaccum]; A & 1FFFFh; A + [VHGparsecty];
	? A = VHSCUTOFF -> VHG SL scan next z; A - VHSCUTOFF; [VHStempy] = A;
	[VHSfmA] = A; [VHSfmB] = [VHSidk]; => VHS foldmul;
	[VHSaccum] = [VHSfmResult];
	A = [VHSaccum]; A & 1FFFFh; A + [VHGparsectz];
	? A = VHSCUTOFF -> VHG SL scan next z; A - VHSCUTOFF; [VHStempz] = A;
	[FJ0] = [VHStempx]; [FJ1] = [VHStempy]; [FJ2] = [VHStempz]; => NsIdentityD;
	[VHCATid0] = [nsid0]; [VHCATid1] = [nsid1]; => VHCAT key;
	A = [VHCATkeyhi]; B = [VHCATkeylo];
	? A '> [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG SL candidate above low;
	? A '< [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG SL scan next z;
	? B '> [VHGparlowlo] -> VHG SL candidate above low; -> VHG SL scan next z;
    "VHG SL candidate above low"
	? A '< [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG SL scan hit;
	? A '> [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG SL scan next z;
	? B '< [VHGparhighlo] -> VHG SL scan hit;
    "VHG SL scan next z"
	[VHGparsectz] + 100000; [VHGpariz]+; A = [VHGpariz];
	? A < [VHGparrange] -> VHG SL advance loop;
	[VHGpariz] = 0; [VHGparsectz] = [VHGparbasez];
	[VHGparsecty] + 100000; [VHGpariy]+; A = [VHGpariy];
	? A < [VHGparrange] -> VHG SL advance loop;
	[VHGpariy] = 0; [VHGparsecty] = [VHGparbasey];
	[VHGparsectx] + 100000; [VHGparix]+; A = [VHGparix];
	? A < [VHGparrange] -> VHG SL advance loop;
	[VHGslphase] = 0; [VHGslrecno]+; -> VHG SL advance loop;

    "VHG SL scan hit"
	=> VHG SL output hit;
	[VHGslphase] = 0; [VHGslrecno]+; -> VHG SL advance loop;
    "VHG SL ranged complete"
	[VHGslactive] = 0; [VHGslphase] = 0; [VHGslcancelheld] = 1;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGslcompletetext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	A = [VHGslleast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG SL ranged no stars;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslend; => VH GOES output line; -> VHG SL advance done;
    "VHG SL ranged no stars"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslnone; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG SL advance done"
	end;

"VHG SL cancel"
	A = [VHGslactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG SL cancel done;
	[VHGslactive] = 0; [VHGslphase] = 0; [VHGslcancelheld] = 1;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslinterrupted; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGslinterrupted; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
    "VHG SL cancel done"
	end;

"VHG SL output hit"
	[VHGslline plus 0] = 42; [VHGsli] = 0;
    "VHG SL hit label copy"
	A = [VHGsli]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGslptr]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; C = VHGslline; C + 1; C + [VHGsli]; [C] = A;
	[VHGsli]+; A = [VHGsli]; ? A < 20 -> VHG SL hit label copy;
	[VHGslline plus 21] = 0; [VHPoutptr] = VHGslline; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGparaxischar] = 88; [VHGparcoord] = [VHStempx]; => VHG SL output coordinate;
	[VHGparaxischar] = 89; A = 0; A - [VHStempy]; [VHGparcoord] = A;
	=> VHG SL output coordinate;
	[VHGparaxischar] = 90; [VHGparcoord] = [VHStempz]; => VHG SL output coordinate;
	=> VHG SL output distance;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslitembar; => VH GOES output line;
	end;

"VHG SL output coordinate"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGparline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 36; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowchar] = [VHGparaxischar]; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 61; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGparcoord]; => VHG onboard row append number;
	=> VHG onboard row finish; [VHPoutptr] = VHGparline; => VH GOES output line;
	end;

"VHG SL output distance"
	[FI] = [VHStempx]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGslgalx0]; [FA1] = [VHGslgalx1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHStempy]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGslgaly0]; [FA1] = [VHGslgaly1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FC0]; [FB1] = [FC1]; => FAdd; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHStempz]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGslgalz0]; [FA1] = [VHGslgalz1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FC0]; [FB1] = [FC1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt;
	[FB0] = 47AE147Bh; [FB1] = 3F747AE1h; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGparline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 36; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 68; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 61; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGinfofixedvalue] = [FI]; [VHGinfofixeddigits] = 2; => VHG info append fixed;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGslly; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row finish; [VHPoutptr] = VHGparline; => VH GOES output line;
	end;

"VHG DL"
	( DL.CPP resolves a charted star or planet, confirms that its parent star is
	  present in the requested procedural cube, then prints only charted planets
	  and moons in generated dependency order.  The hosted port performs the
	  range scan incrementally and restores the player's generated target after
	  temporarily preparing the queried system. )
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG DL bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG DL bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG DL heading;
	A = [MgAptgt]; ? A != 1 -> VHG DL usage;
    "VHG DL heading"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlhead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL current subject;
	[VHGwherematches] = 0; [VHGwherefound] = 0; [VHGwhererecno] = 0;
    "VHG DL catalogue loop"
	A = [VHGwhererecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG DL catalogue done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGwhererec] = A;
	=> VHG WHERE record match; A = [VHGwherematch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL catalogue next;
	[VHGwherematches]+; [VHGwherefound] = [VHGwhererec];
	A = [VHGwhereexact]; ? A != 0 -> VHG DL catalogue exact;
    "VHG DL catalogue next"
	[VHGwhererecno]+; -> VHG DL catalogue loop;
    "VHG DL catalogue exact"
	[VHGwherematches] = 1;
    "VHG DL catalogue done"
	A = [VHGwherematches]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL missing result;
	? A > 1 -> VHG DL ambiguous result;
	[VHGdlsubjectptr] = [VHGwherefound]; -> VHG DL subject ready;
    "VHG DL current subject"
	A = [MgAptgt]; ? A != 1 -> VHG DL usage;
	[VHCATid0] = [VHTid0]; [VHCATid1] = [VHTid1]; [VHCATtype] = VHCATS;
	=> VHCAT find; A = [VHCATok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL current missing;
	[VHGdlsubjectptr] = [VHCATfoundptr]; [VHGwherefound] = [VHCATfoundptr];
	-> VHG DL subject ready;
    "VHG DL usage"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage4; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlrevision; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG DL current missing"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlcurrentmissing0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlcurrentmissing1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG DL missing result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherenotfound; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG DL ambiguous result"
	=> VHG WHERE ambiguous; end;
    "VHG DL subject ready"
	A = [VHGdlsubjectptr]; C = [A plus 7]; C > 8; C & FFh; [VHGdlsubjecttype] = C;
	? C = VHCATS -> VHG DL star subject; ? C = VHCATP -> VHG DL planet subject;
	-> VHG DL missing result;
    "VHG DL star subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectstar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherefound] = [VHGdlsubjectptr]; => VHG PAR output name;
	A = [VHGdlsubjectptr]; [VHGdlstarid0] = [A]; [VHGdlstarid1] = [A plus 1];
	-> VHG DL scan prepare;
    "VHG DL planet subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectplanet; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherefound] = [VHGdlsubjectptr]; => VHG PAR output name;
	A = [VHGdlsubjectptr]; C = [A plus 7];
	A = C; A > 16; A & FFh; A - 48; A '* 10; [VHGwherecode] = A;
	A = C; A > 24; A & FFh; A - 48; A + [VHGwherecode]; [VHGwherecode] = A;
	A = [VHGdlsubjectptr]; [nsid0] = [A]; [nsid1] = [A plus 1];
	A = 0; A - [VHGwherecode]; [FI] = A; => NsIdentAddInt;
	[VHGdlstarid0] = [FA0]; [VHGdlstarid1] = [FA1];
    "VHG DL scan prepare"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[FA0] = [VHGdlstarid0]; [FA1] = [VHGdlstarid1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FSub;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparlowhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparlowlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[FA0] = [VHGdlstarid0]; [FA1] = [VHGdlstarid1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FAdd;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparhighhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparhighlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[VHGslgalx0] = [MgDzatX0]; [VHGslgalx1] = [MgDzatX1];
	[VHGslgaly0] = [MgDzatY0]; [VHGslgaly1] = [MgDzatY1];
	[VHGslgalz0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [VHGslgalz1] = [MgDzatZ1];
	A = [VHGparrange]; A '* 50000; [VHGparoffset] = A;
	[VHGparaxis0] = [VHGslgalx0]; [VHGparaxis1] = [VHGslgalx1]; => VHG PAR sector base;
	[VHGparbasex] = [VHGparbaseout];
	[VHGparaxis0] = [VHGslgaly0]; [VHGparaxis1] = [VHGslgaly1]; => VHG PAR sector base;
	[VHGparbasey] = [VHGparbaseout];
	[VHGparaxis0] = [VHGslgalz0]; [VHGparaxis1] = [VHGslgalz1]; => VHG PAR sector base;
	[VHGparbasez] = [VHGparbaseout];
	[VHGparix] = 0; [VHGpariy] = 0; [VHGpariz] = 0;
	[VHGparsectx] = [VHGparbasex]; [VHGparsecty] = [VHGparbasey]; [VHGparsectz] = [VHGparbasez];
	[VHGdlsavex] = [VHTtx]; [VHGdlsavey] = [VHTty]; [VHGdlsavez] = [VHTtz];
	[VHGdlsaveplanet] = [VHGplanet]; [VHGdlactive] = 1;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGdlbusytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; end;

"VHG DL advance"
	A = [VHGdlactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL advance done;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGdlbusytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 2; [VHGdlbudget] = 65536;
    "VHG DL advance loop"
	A = [VHGdlbudget]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG DL advance done; [VHGdlbudget]-;
	A = [VHGparsectx]; A + [VHGparsectz]; [VHSsumxz] = A;
	A & 1FFFFh; A + [VHGparsectx]; ? A = VHSCUTOFF -> VHG DL scan next z;
	A - VHSCUTOFF; [VHStempx] = A;
	[VHSfmA] = A; [VHSfmB] = [VHSsumxz]; => VHS foldmul;
	[VHSaccum] = [VHSfmResult]; A = [VHSsumxz]; A + [VHSaccum]; [VHSidk] = A;
	A = [VHSaccum]; A & 1FFFFh; A + [VHGparsecty];
	? A = VHSCUTOFF -> VHG DL scan next z; A - VHSCUTOFF; [VHStempy] = A;
	[VHSfmA] = A; [VHSfmB] = [VHSidk]; => VHS foldmul;
	[VHSaccum] = [VHSfmResult]; A = [VHSaccum]; A & 1FFFFh; A + [VHGparsectz];
	? A = VHSCUTOFF -> VHG DL scan next z; A - VHSCUTOFF; [VHStempz] = A;
	[FJ0] = [VHStempx]; [FJ1] = [VHStempy]; [FJ2] = [VHStempz]; => NsIdentityD;
	[VHCATid0] = [nsid0]; [VHCATid1] = [nsid1]; => VHCAT key;
	A = [VHCATkeyhi]; B = [VHCATkeylo];
	? A '> [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG DL candidate above low;
	? A '< [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG DL scan next z;
	? B '> [VHGparlowlo] -> VHG DL candidate above low; -> VHG DL scan next z;
    "VHG DL candidate above low"
	? A '< [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG DL scan hit;
	? A '> [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG DL scan next z;
	? B '< [VHGparhighlo] -> VHG DL scan hit;
    "VHG DL scan next z"
	[VHGparsectz] + 100000; [VHGpariz]+; A = [VHGpariz];
	? A < [VHGparrange] -> VHG DL advance loop;
	[VHGpariz] = 0; [VHGparsectz] = [VHGparbasez];
	[VHGparsecty] + 100000; [VHGpariy]+; A = [VHGpariy];
	? A < [VHGparrange] -> VHG DL advance loop;
	[VHGpariy] = 0; [VHGparsecty] = [VHGparbasey];
	[VHGparsectx] + 100000; [VHGparix]+; A = [VHGparix];
	? A < [VHGparrange] -> VHG DL advance loop;
	=> VHG DL output subject label; [VHPoutptr] = VHGdloutofrange; => VH GOES output line;
	-> VHG DL finish;
    "VHG DL scan hit"
	=> VHG DL output tree;
    "VHG DL finish"
	[VHGdlactive] = 0; [VHGslcancelheld] = 1;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGdlcompletetext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
    "VHG DL advance done"
	end;

"VHG DL cancel"
	A = [VHGdlactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL cancel done;
	[VHGdlactive] = 0; [VHGslcancelheld] = 1;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGslinterrupted; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGslinterrupted; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
    "VHG DL cancel done"
	end;

"VHG DL output subject label"
	[VHGdlline plus 0] = 42; [VHGsli] = 0;
    "VHG DL subject label copy"
	A = [VHGsli]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGdlsubjectptr]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; C = VHGdlline; C + 1; C + [VHGsli]; [C] = A;
	[VHGsli]+; A = [VHGsli]; ? A < 20 -> VHG DL subject label copy;
	[VHGdlline plus 21] = 0; [VHPoutptr] = VHGdlline; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG DL output tree"
	[VHTtx] = [VHStempx]; [VHTty] = [VHStempy]; [VHTtz] = [VHStempz];
	[VHGplanet] = 0; => VHG target world; => VHG DL output subject label;
	A = [VHGdlsubjectptr]; [VHGDBid0] = [A]; [VHGDBid1] = [A plus 1];
	[VHGdlnoteprefix] = VHGdlnote0; => VHG DL output notes;
	A = [VHGdlsubjecttype]; ? A = VHCATP -> VHG DL output subject moons;
	=> VHG DL output planets; -> VHG DL restore target;
    "VHG DL output subject moons"
	A = [VHGwherecode]; A - 1; [VHGdlparent] = A;
	=> VHG DL output moons root;
    "VHG DL restore target"
	[VHTtx] = [VHGdlsavex]; [VHTty] = [VHGdlsavey]; [VHTtz] = [VHGdlsavez];
	[VHGplanet] = 0; => VHG target world; [VHGplanet] = [VHGdlsaveplanet]; => VHCAT refresh;
	end;

"VHG DL find body"
	[nsid0] = [VHTid0]; [nsid1] = [VHTid1]; A = [VHGdlfindindex]; A + 1; [FI] = A;
	=> NsIdentAddInt; [VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; [VHCATtype] = VHCATP;
	=> VHCAT find; [VHGdlrecptr] = [VHCATfoundptr]; end;

"VHG DL output planets"
	[VHGdlbody] = 0; [VHGdlbodycount] = 0;
    "VHG DL count planet loop"
	A = [VHGdlbody]; ? A >= [nsnob] -> VHG DL count planet done;
	E = nspowner; E + A; A = [E]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG DL count planet next;
	[VHGdlfindindex] = [VHGdlbody]; => VHG DL find body; A = [VHCATok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL count planet next;
	[VHGdlbodycount]+;
    "VHG DL count planet next"
	[VHGdlbody]+; -> VHG DL count planet loop;
    "VHG DL count planet done"
	A = [VHGdlbodycount]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL no planets;
	[VHGdlbody] = 0;
    "VHG DL planet output loop"
	A = [VHGdlbody]; ? A >= [nsnob] -> VHG DL planets complete;
	E = nspowner; E + A; A = [E]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG DL planet output next;
	[VHGdlfindindex] = [VHGdlbody]; => VHG DL find body; A = [VHCATok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL planet output next;
	=> VHG DL output planet row; [VHGdlbodycount]-;
	A = [VHGdlbodycount]; [VHGdlnoteprefix] = VHGdlnote0;
	? A > 0 -> VHG DL planet note ready; [VHGdlnoteprefix] = VHGdlnote1;
    "VHG DL planet note ready"
	A = [VHGdlrecptr]; [VHGDBid0] = [A]; [VHGDBid1] = [A plus 1]; => VHG DL output notes;
	[VHGdlparent] = [VHGdlbody]; => VHG DL output moons nested;
    "VHG DL planet output next"
	[VHGdlbody]+; -> VHG DL planet output loop;
    "VHG DL planets complete"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlplanetsend; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG DL no planets"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlplanetsnone; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG DL output planet row"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGdlline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 36; A = [VHGdlbodycount]; ? A > 1 -> VHG DL planet branch ready;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 91;
    "VHG DL planet branch ready"
	=> VHG onboard row append char; A = [VHGdlbody]; A + 1; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
	A '/ 10; A + 48; [VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = [VHGonrowvalue]; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 38; => VHG onboard row append char; [VHGsli] = 0;
    "VHG DL planet name copy"
	A = [VHGsli]; ? A >= 20 -> VHG DL planet row ready;
	A = [VHGonrowpos]; ? A >= 21 -> VHG DL planet row ready;
	A = [VHGsli]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGdlrecptr]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; [VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGsli]+; -> VHG DL planet name copy;
    "VHG DL planet row ready"
	=> VHG onboard row finish; [VHPoutptr] = VHGdlline; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG DL count moons"
	[VHGdlmoon] = 0; [VHGdlmooncount] = 0;
    "VHG DL count moon loop"
	A = [VHGdlmoon]; ? A >= [nsnob] -> VHG DL count moons done;
	E = nspowner; E + A; A = [E]; ? A != [VHGdlparent] -> VHG DL count moon next;
	[VHGdlfindindex] = [VHGdlmoon]; => VHG DL find body;
	A = [VHCATok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL count moon next; [VHGdlmooncount]+;
    "VHG DL count moon next"
	[VHGdlmoon]+; -> VHG DL count moon loop;
    "VHG DL count moons done"
	end;

"VHG DL output moons nested"
	=> VHG DL count moons; A = [VHGdlmooncount]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL moons nested done;
	[VHGdlmoon] = 0;
    "VHG DL nested moon loop"
	A = [VHGdlmoon]; ? A >= [nsnob] -> VHG DL moons nested done;
	E = nspowner; E + A; A = [E]; ? A != [VHGdlparent] -> VHG DL nested moon next;
	[VHGdlfindindex] = [VHGdlmoon]; => VHG DL find body;
	A = [VHCATok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL nested moon next;
	=> VHG DL output nested moon row; [VHGdlmooncount]-;
	A = [VHGdlbodycount]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL nested note last planet;
	A = [VHGdlmooncount]; [VHGdlnoteprefix] = VHGdlnote2;
	? A > 0 -> VHG DL nested note ready; [VHGdlnoteprefix] = VHGdlnote3;
	-> VHG DL nested note ready;
    "VHG DL nested note last planet"
	A = [VHGdlmooncount]; [VHGdlnoteprefix] = VHGdlnote4;
	? A > 0 -> VHG DL nested note ready; [VHGdlnoteprefix] = VHGdlnote5;
    "VHG DL nested note ready"
	A = [VHGdlrecptr]; [VHGDBid0] = [A]; [VHGDBid1] = [A plus 1]; => VHG DL output notes;
    "VHG DL nested moon next"
	[VHGdlmoon]+; -> VHG DL nested moon loop;
    "VHG DL moons nested done"
	end;

"VHG DL output nested moon row"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGdlline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 32; A = [VHGdlbodycount]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL nested moon indent second;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 93;
    "VHG DL nested moon indent second"
	=> VHG onboard row append char; [VHGonrowchar] = 32; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 36; A = [VHGdlmooncount]; ? A > 1 -> VHG DL nested moon branch ready;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 91;
    "VHG DL nested moon branch ready"
	=> VHG onboard row append char; E = nspmoonid; E + [VHGdlmoon]; A = [E]; A + 1;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = A; A '/ 10; A + 48; [VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = [VHGonrowvalue]; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 38; => VHG onboard row append char; [VHGsli] = 0;
    "VHG DL nested moon name copy"
	A = [VHGsli]; ? A >= 20 -> VHG DL nested moon row ready;
	A = [VHGonrowpos]; ? A >= 21 -> VHG DL nested moon row ready;
	A = [VHGsli]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGdlrecptr]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; [VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGsli]+; -> VHG DL nested moon name copy;
    "VHG DL nested moon row ready"
	=> VHG onboard row finish; [VHPoutptr] = VHGdlline; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG DL output moons root"
	=> VHG DL count moons; A = [VHGdlmooncount]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL no root moons;
	[VHGdlmoon] = 0;
    "VHG DL root moon loop"
	A = [VHGdlmoon]; ? A >= [nsnob] -> VHG DL root moons complete;
	E = nspowner; E + A; A = [E]; ? A != [VHGdlparent] -> VHG DL root moon next;
	[VHGdlfindindex] = [VHGdlmoon]; => VHG DL find body;
	A = [VHCATok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL root moon next;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGdlline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 36; A = [VHGdlmooncount]; ? A > 1 -> VHG DL root moon branch ready;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 91;
    "VHG DL root moon branch ready"
	=> VHG onboard row append char; E = nspmoonid; E + [VHGdlmoon]; A = [E]; A + 1;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = A; A '/ 10; A + 48; [VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = [VHGonrowvalue]; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 38; => VHG onboard row append char;
    "VHG DL moon name copy"
	[VHGsli] = 0;
    "VHG DL moon name copy loop"
	A = [VHGsli]; ? A >= 20 -> VHG DL moon row ready;
	A = [VHGonrowpos]; ? A >= 21 -> VHG DL moon row ready;
	A = [VHGsli]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGdlrecptr]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; [VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGsli]+; -> VHG DL moon name copy loop;
    "VHG DL moon row ready"
	=> VHG onboard row finish; [VHPoutptr] = VHGdlline; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGdlmooncount]-; A = [VHGdlmooncount]; [VHGdlnoteprefix] = VHGdlnote0;
	? A > 0 -> VHG DL root note ready; [VHGdlnoteprefix] = VHGdlnote1;
    "VHG DL root note ready"
	A = [VHGdlrecptr]; [VHGDBid0] = [A]; [VHGDBid1] = [A plus 1]; => VHG DL output notes;
    "VHG DL root moon next"
	[VHGdlmoon]+; -> VHG DL root moon loop;
    "VHG DL root moons complete"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlmoonsend; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG DL no root moons"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdlmoonsnone; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG DL output notes"
	[VHGdlnotes] = 0; A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL output notes done;
	[VHCATid0] = [VHGDBid0]; [VHCATid1] = [VHGDBid1];
	[VHCATfoundptr] = [VHGDBid0]; [VHCATptr] = [VHGDBid1]; => VHCAT bounds;
	[VHGdlrecno] = 0;
    "VHG DL note loop"
	A = [VHGdlrecno]; ? A >= [VHGDBrecs] -> VHG DL note count done;
	A '* 21; A + vhguidedata; A + 1; [VHGdlrecptr] = A;
	C = [A]; ? C != VHCATTOMB0 -> VHG DL note live;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C = VHCATTOMB1 -> VHG DL note next;
    "VHG DL note live"
	[VHCATid0] = [A]; [VHCATid1] = [A plus 1]; => VHCAT identity valid;
	A = [VHCATvalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL note next; => VHCAT key;
	A = [VHCATkeyhi]; B = [VHCATkeylo];
	? A '> [VHCATlowhi] -> VHG DL note above low;
	? A '< [VHCATlowhi] -> VHG DL note next;
	? B '< [VHCATlowlo] -> VHG DL note next;
    "VHG DL note above low"
	? A '< [VHCAT highhi] -> VHG DL note hit;
	? A '> [VHCAT highhi] -> VHG DL note next;
	? B '> [VHCAT highlo] -> VHG DL note next;
    "VHG DL note hit"
	[VHGdlnotes]+;
    "VHG DL note next"
	[VHGdlrecno]+; -> VHG DL note loop;
    "VHG DL note count done"
	A = [VHGdlnotes]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DL output notes done;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGdlline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = [VHGdlnoteprefix]; => VHG onboard row append text;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGdlnotes]; => VHG onboard row append number;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGdlnotesuffix; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row finish; [VHPoutptr] = VHGdlline; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG DL output notes done"
	end;

"VHG WHERE"
	( WHERE.CPP is a bundled GOES Net module.  Search the same persistent
	  32-byte STARMAP records by their twenty-byte names, retain the source's
	  exact-match preference over ambiguous prefixes, and write its 21-column
	  response into the physical output tree. )
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG WHERE execute query;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereusage3; => VH GOES output line;
	end;
    "VHG WHERE execute query"
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG WHERE bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG WHERE bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherehead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherematches] = 0; [VHGwherefound] = 0; [VHGwhererecno] = 0;
    "VHG WHERE query loop"
	A = [VHGwhererecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG WHERE query done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGwhererec] = A;
	=> VHG WHERE record match; A = [VHGwherematch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG WHERE query next;
	[VHGwherematches]+; [VHGwherefound] = [VHGwhererec];
	A = [VHGwhereexact]; ? A != 0 -> VHG WHERE exact hit;
    "VHG WHERE query next"
	[VHGwhererecno]+; -> VHG WHERE query loop;
    "VHG WHERE exact hit"
	[VHGwherematches] = 1;
    "VHG WHERE query done"
	A = [VHGwherematches]; ? A = 0 -> VHG WHERE missing result;
	? A > 1 -> VHG WHERE ambiguous;
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound];
	A = [VHGwhererec]; C = [A plus 7]; C > 8; C & FFh; [VHGwheretype] = C;
	? C = VHCATS -> VHG WHERE star;
	? C = VHCATP -> VHG WHERE planet; -> VHG WHERE missing result;
    "VHG WHERE missing result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherenotfound; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG WHERE ambiguous"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereambiguous0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereambiguous1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherepossible; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwhererecno] = 0;
    "VHG WHERE ambiguous loop"
	A = [VHGwhererecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG WHERE ambiguous done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGwhererec] = A;
	=> VHG WHERE record match; A = [VHGwherematch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG WHERE ambiguous next;
	=> VHG WHERE output label;
    "VHG WHERE ambiguous next"
	[VHGwhererecno]+; -> VHG WHERE ambiguous loop;
    "VHG WHERE ambiguous done"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG WHERE star"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherestar0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherestar1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherestar2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherestar3; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG WHERE planet"
	=> VHG WHERE output label;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherepart0; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGwherefound]; C = [A plus 7];
	A = C; A > 16; A & FFh; A - 48; A '* 10; [VHGwherecode] = A;
	A = C; A > 24; A & FFh; A - 48; A + [VHGwherecode]; [VHGwherecode] = A;
	A = [VHGwherefound]; [nsid0] = [A]; [nsid1] = [A plus 1];
	A = 0; A - [VHGwherecode]; [FI] = A; => NsIdentAddInt;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; [VHCATtype] = VHCATS;
	=> VHCAT find; A = [VHCATok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG WHERE parent missing;
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHCATfoundptr]; => VHG WHERE output label;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherepart1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG WHERE parent missing"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereparent0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereparent1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereparent2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwhereparent3; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG WHERE record match"
	[VHGwherematch] = 0; [VHGwhereexact] = 0;
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A > 20 -> VHG WHERE record match done;
	A = [VHGwhererec]; [VHCATid0] = [A]; [VHCATid1] = [A plus 1];
	=> VHCAT identity valid; A = [VHCATvalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG WHERE record match done;
	A = [VHGwhererec]; A = [A plus 7]; A > 8; A & FFh;
	? A = VHCATS -> VHG WHERE record type ready;
	? A != VHCATP -> VHG WHERE record match done;
    "VHG WHERE record type ready"
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG WHERE record prefix"
	A = [VHGwherei]; ? A >= [VHGwherelen] -> VHG WHERE record suffix;
	B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGwhererec]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; B = A;
	A = [VHGwherequery]; A + [VHGwherei]; A = [A];
	C = [VHGwhereunderscores]; ? C = 0 -> VHG query character ready;
	? A != 95 -> VHG query character ready; A = 32;
    "VHG query character ready"
	? A != B -> VHG WHERE record match done;
	[VHGwherei]+; -> VHG WHERE record prefix;
    "VHG WHERE record suffix"
	[VHGwherematch] = 1;
	A = [VHGwherei]; ? A >= 20 -> VHG WHERE record exact;
	B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGwhererec]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; ? A != 32 -> VHG WHERE record match done;
	[VHGwherei]+; -> VHG WHERE record suffix;
    "VHG WHERE record exact"
	[VHGwhereexact] = 1;
    "VHG WHERE record match done"
	end;

"VHG WHERE output label"
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG WHERE output label loop"
	A = [VHGwherei]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGwhererec]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; C = VHGgoesline; C + [VHGwherei]; [C] = A;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 20 -> VHG WHERE output label loop;
	[VHGgoesline plus 20] = 0;
    "VHG WHERE output label trim"
	A = [VHGwherei]; ? A = 0 -> VHG WHERE output label ready;
	A - 1; [VHGwherei] = A; C = VHGgoesline; C + A; A = [C];
	? A != 32 -> VHG WHERE output label ready; [C] = 0; -> VHG WHERE output label trim;
    "VHG WHERE output label ready"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGgoesline; => VH GOES output line;
	end;

"VHG CAST"
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG CAST execute;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage4; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG CAST execute"
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG CAST bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG CAST bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcathead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherematches] = 0; [VHGwherefound] = 0; [VHGwhererecno] = 0;
    "VHG CAST catalogue loop"
	A = [VHGwhererecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG CAST catalogue done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGwhererec] = A;
	=> VHG WHERE record match; A = [VHGwherematch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CAST catalogue next;
	[VHGwherematches]+; [VHGwherefound] = [VHGwhererec];
	A = [VHGwhereexact]; ? A != 0 -> VHG CAST catalogue exact;
    "VHG CAST catalogue next"
	[VHGwhererecno]+; -> VHG CAST catalogue loop;
    "VHG CAST catalogue exact"
	[VHGwherematches] = 1;
    "VHG CAST catalogue done"
	A = [VHGwherematches]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CAST missing result;
	? A > 1 -> VHG CAST ambiguous result;
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound];
	A = [VHGwhererec]; C = [A plus 7]; C > 8; C & FFh; [VHGwheretype] = C;
	? C = VHCATS -> VHG CAST star subject;
	? C = VHCATP -> VHG CAST planet subject; -> VHG CAST missing result;
    "VHG CAST missing result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherenotfound; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG CAST ambiguous result"
	=> VHG WHERE ambiguous; end;
    "VHG CAST star subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcasttransfer; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectstar; => VH GOES output line; -> VHG CAST subject ready;
    "VHG CAST planet subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcasttransfer; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectplanet; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG CAST subject ready"
	=> VHG PAR output name;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastsent; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGwherefound]; [VHGDBid0] = [A]; [VHGDBid1] = [A plus 1];
	[VHGDBmsgptr] = [VHGcastmsgptr]; [VHGDBmsglen] = [VHGcastmsglen]; => VHGDB add;
	A = [VHGDBstatus]; ? A = 1 -> VHG CAST accepted result;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastdberror; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastrejected; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcasterror1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG CAST accepted result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastaccepted; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG CAST syntax result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastsyntax0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastsyntax1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastsyntax2; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG CAST void result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastvoid0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastvoid1; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG REP"
	( REP.CPP corrects a one-based subject record only when that record lies
	  after GUIDE.BIN's consolidated source boundary.  Resolve the catalogue
	  and binary64 tolerance exactly as CAT and CAST do. )
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG REP execute;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage4; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG REP execute"
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG REP bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG REP bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcathead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherematches] = 0; [VHGwherefound] = 0; [VHGwhererecno] = 0;
    "VHG REP catalogue loop"
	A = [VHGwhererecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG REP catalogue done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGwhererec] = A;
	=> VHG WHERE record match; A = [VHGwherematch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REP catalogue next;
	[VHGwherematches]+; [VHGwherefound] = [VHGwhererec];
	A = [VHGwhereexact]; ? A != 0 -> VHG REP catalogue exact;
    "VHG REP catalogue next"
	[VHGwhererecno]+; -> VHG REP catalogue loop;
    "VHG REP catalogue exact"
	[VHGwherematches] = 1;
    "VHG REP catalogue done"
	A = [VHGwherematches]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REP missing subject;
	? A > 1 -> VHG REP ambiguous subject;
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound];
	A = [VHGwhererec]; C = [A plus 7]; C > 8; C & FFh; [VHGwheretype] = C;
	? C = VHCATS -> VHG REP star subject;
	? C = VHCATP -> VHG REP planet subject; -> VHG REP missing subject;
    "VHG REP missing subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherenotfound; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG REP ambiguous subject"
	=> VHG WHERE ambiguous; end;
    "VHG REP star subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcasttransfer; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectstar; => VH GOES output line; -> VHG REP subject ready;
    "VHG REP planet subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcasttransfer; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectplanet; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG REP subject ready"
	=> VHG PAR output name;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepsent; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REP database error;
	A = [VHGwherefound]; [VHGpartarget0] = [A]; [VHGpartarget1] = [A plus 1];
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FSub;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparlowhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparlowlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FAdd;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparhighhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparhighlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[VHGcatrecno] = 0; [VHGcatmatchno] = 0;
    "VHG REP guide loop"
	A = [VHGcatrecno]; ? A >= [VHGDBrecs] -> VHG REP no such record;
	A '* 21; A + vhguidedata; A + 1; [VHGcatptr] = A;
	[VHCATid0] = [A]; [VHCATid1] = [A plus 1];
	=> VHCAT identity valid; A = [VHCATvalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REP guide next;
	=> VHCAT key; A = [VHCATkeyhi]; B = [VHCATkeylo];
	? A '> [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG REP guide above low;
	? A '< [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG REP guide next;
	? B '> [VHGparlowlo] -> VHG REP guide above low; -> VHG REP guide next;
    "VHG REP guide above low"
	? A '< [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG REP guide match;
	? A '> [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG REP guide next;
	? B '< [VHGparhighlo] -> VHG REP guide match; -> VHG REP guide next;
    "VHG REP guide match"
	[VHGcatmatchno]+; A = [VHGcatmatchno]; ? A != [VHGreptarget] -> VHG REP guide next;
	[VHGDBrecordno] = [VHGcatrecno]; A = [VHGwherefound]; [VHGDBid0] = [A]; [VHGDBid1] = [A plus 1];
	[VHGDBmsgptr] = [VHGrepmsgptr]; [VHGDBmsglen] = [VHGrepmsglen]; => VHGDB replace;
	A = [VHGDBstatus]; ? A = 1 -> VHG REP accepted result;
	? A = 2 -> VHG REP protected result; -> VHG REP database error;
    "VHG REP guide next"
	[VHGcatrecno]+; -> VHG REP guide loop;
    "VHG REP accepted result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepaccepted; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG REP protected result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGreprejected; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepprotected; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG REP no such record"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGreprejected; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepnosuch; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG REP database error"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastdberror; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastrejected; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcasterror1; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG REP object syntax"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastsyntax0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastsyntax1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastsyntax2; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG REP record syntax"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastsyntax0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcastsyntax1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepsyntax1; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG DELE"
	( DELE.CPP counts every matching guide record, applies its one-based range,
	  and tombstones only local contributions.  Consolidated records contribute
	  to the protected total and remain byte-for-byte unchanged. )
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG DELE execute;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdeleusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdeleusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage4; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG DELE execute"
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG DELE bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG DELE bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcathead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherematches] = 0; [VHGwherefound] = 0; [VHGwhererecno] = 0;
    "VHG DELE catalogue loop"
	A = [VHGwhererecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG DELE catalogue done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGwhererec] = A;
	=> VHG WHERE record match; A = [VHGwherematch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DELE catalogue next;
	[VHGwherematches]+; [VHGwherefound] = [VHGwhererec];
	A = [VHGwhereexact]; ? A != 0 -> VHG DELE catalogue exact;
    "VHG DELE catalogue next"
	[VHGwhererecno]+; -> VHG DELE catalogue loop;
    "VHG DELE catalogue exact"
	[VHGwherematches] = 1;
    "VHG DELE catalogue done"
	A = [VHGwherematches]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DELE missing subject;
	? A > 1 -> VHG DELE ambiguous subject;
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound];
	A = [VHGwhererec]; C = [A plus 7]; C > 8; C & FFh; [VHGwheretype] = C;
	? C = VHCATS -> VHG DELE star subject;
	? C = VHCATP -> VHG DELE planet subject; -> VHG DELE missing subject;
    "VHG DELE missing subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherenotfound; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG DELE ambiguous subject"
	=> VHG WHERE ambiguous; end;
    "VHG DELE star subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectstar; => VH GOES output line; -> VHG DELE subject ready;
    "VHG DELE planet subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectplanet; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG DELE subject ready"
	=> VHG PAR output name;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DELE database error;
	A = [VHGwherefound]; [VHGpartarget0] = [A]; [VHGpartarget1] = [A plus 1];
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FSub;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparlowhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparlowlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FAdd;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparhighhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparhighlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[VHGcatrecno] = 0; [VHGcatmatchno] = 0; [VHGdeletotal] = 0;
	[VHGdeledeleted] = 0; [VHGdelequery] = 0;
    "VHG DELE guide loop"
	A = [VHGcatrecno]; ? A >= [VHGDBrecs] -> VHG DELE guide done;
	A '* 21; A + vhguidedata; A + 1; [VHGcatptr] = A;
	[VHCATid0] = [A]; [VHCATid1] = [A plus 1];
	=> VHCAT identity valid; A = [VHCATvalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG DELE guide next;
	=> VHCAT key; A = [VHCATkeyhi]; B = [VHCATkeylo];
	? A '> [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG DELE guide above low;
	? A '< [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG DELE guide next;
	? B '> [VHGparlowlo] -> VHG DELE guide above low; -> VHG DELE guide next;
    "VHG DELE guide above low"
	? A '< [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG DELE guide match;
	? A '> [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG DELE guide next;
	? B '< [VHGparhighlo] -> VHG DELE guide match; -> VHG DELE guide next;
    "VHG DELE guide match"
	[VHGdeletotal]+; [VHGcatmatchno]+; A = [VHGcatmatchno];
	? A < [VHGcatstart] -> VHG DELE guide next;
	? A > [VHGcatend] -> VHG DELE guide next; [VHGdelequery] = 1;
	[VHGDBrecordno] = [VHGcatrecno]; => VHGDB remove;
	A = [VHGDBstatus]; ? A = 1 -> VHG DELE removed one;
	? A = 2 -> VHG DELE guide next; -> VHG DELE database error;
    "VHG DELE removed one"
	[VHGdeledeleted]+;
    "VHG DELE guide next"
	[VHGcatrecno]+; -> VHG DELE guide loop;
    "VHG DELE guide done"
	A = [VHGdelequery]; ? A != 0 -> VHG DELE summary;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdelenone0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdelenone1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG DELE summary"
	[VHGdeleprefix] = VHGdeletotalprefix; [VHGdelevalue] = [VHGdeletotal]; => VHG DELE output count;
	[VHGdeleprefix] = VHGdeledeletedprefix; [VHGdelevalue] = [VHGdeledeleted]; => VHG DELE output count;
	[VHGdeleprefix] = VHGdeleprotectedprefix; A = [VHGdeletotal]; A - [VHGdeledeleted];
	[VHGdelevalue] = A; => VHG DELE output count; end;
    "VHG DELE database error"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdeledberror; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGdeleerror4; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG DELE output count"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGcatline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = [VHGdeleprefix]; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; [VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGdelevalue];
	=> VHG onboard row append number; => VHG onboard row finish;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatline; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG INBOX"
	( INBOX.CPP folds a received OUTBOX packet into the consolidated portions
	  of STARMAP and GUIDE, then reattaches surviving player-local records.
	  Preflight the complete packet before either database is touched, and keep
	  the still-loaded original images available for rollback on a write error. )
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG INBOX bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG INBOX bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGinboxtitle; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHCATloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG INBOX starmap unavailable;
	A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG INBOX guide unavailable;
	[VHGinoldcatbytes] = [VHCATbytes]; [VHGinoldcatboundary] = [vhcatraw];
	[VHGinoldcatrecs] = [VHCATrecs]; [VHGinoldguidebytes] = [VHGDBbytes];
	[VHGinoldguideboundary] = [vhguidedata]; [VHGinoldguiderecs] = [VHGDBrecs];
	[VHGinlabels] = 0; [VHGincomments] = 0; [VHGinmutated] = 0;
	[File Name] = VHGinboxfile; [File Command] = TEST; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX missing;
	A = [File Size]; [VHGinboxsize] = A; ? A < 16 -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	? A > VHGINBOXMAX -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	[File Name] = VHGinboxfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = READ;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGinrecord; [Block Size] = 8; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 8 -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	A = [VHGinrecord plus 0]; ? A != 52415453h -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	A = [VHGinrecord plus 1]; ? A != 5F50414Dh -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	[VHGinboxpos] = 8; [VHGinlabelcount] = 0;
    "VHG INBOX preflight labels"
	A = [VHGinboxpos]; A + 8; ? A > [VHGinboxsize] -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	[File Name] = VHGinboxfile; [File Position] = [VHGinboxpos]; [File Command] = READ;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGinrecord; [Block Size] = 8; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 8 -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	A = [VHGinrecord plus 0]; ? A != 44495547h -> VHG INBOX preflight label record;
	A = [VHGinrecord plus 1]; ? A = 5F5F5F45h -> VHG INBOX preflight guide;
    "VHG INBOX preflight label record"
	A = [VHGinboxpos]; A + VHCATRECBYTES; ? A > [VHGinboxsize] -> VHG INBOX invalid;
	[VHGinboxpos] + VHCATRECBYTES; [VHGinlabelcount]+;
	-> VHG INBOX preflight labels;
    "VHG INBOX preflight guide"
	A = [VHGinboxpos]; A + 8; [VHGinboxguidepos] = A;
	A = [VHGinboxsize]; A - [VHGinboxguidepos]; C = A; A '% VHGDBREC;
	? A != 0 -> VHG INBOX invalid; C / VHGDBREC; [VHGincommentcount] = C;
	A = [VHGinoldcatboundary]; A - VHCATHDRBYTES; A / VHCATRECBYTES;
	[VHGincatsource] = A; [VHGinindex] = A; [VHGincatlive] = 0;
    "VHG INBOX count cat locals"
	A = [VHGinindex]; ? A >= [VHGinoldcatrecs] -> VHG INBOX count guide locals start;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; C = [A]; ? C != VHCATTOMB0 -> VHG INBOX cat local live;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C = VHCATTOMB1 -> VHG INBOX count cat local next;
    "VHG INBOX cat local live"
	[VHGincatlive]+;
    "VHG INBOX count cat local next"
	[VHGinindex]+; -> VHG INBOX count cat locals;
    "VHG INBOX count guide locals start"
	A = [VHGinoldguideboundary]; A - VHGDBHDR; A / VHGDBREC;
	[VHGinguidesource] = A; [VHGinindex] = A; [VHGinguidelive] = 0;
    "VHG INBOX count guide locals"
	A = [VHGinindex]; ? A >= [VHGinoldguiderecs] -> VHG INBOX capacity;
	A '* 21; A + vhguidedata; A + 1; C = [A]; ? C != VHCATTOMB0 -> VHG INBOX guide local live;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C = VHCATTOMB1 -> VHG INBOX count guide local next;
    "VHG INBOX guide local live"
	[VHGinguidelive]+;
    "VHG INBOX count guide local next"
	[VHGinindex]+; -> VHG INBOX count guide locals;
    "VHG INBOX capacity"
	A = [VHGinlabelcount]; A '* VHCATRECBYTES; A + [VHGinoldcatboundary];
	C = [VHGincatlive]; C '* VHCATRECBYTES; A + C; ? A > VHCATMAXBYTES -> VHG INBOX full;
	A = [VHGincommentcount]; A '* VHGDBREC; A + [VHGinoldguideboundary];
	C = [VHGinguidelive]; C '* VHGDBREC; A + C; ? A > VHGDBMAX -> VHG INBOX full;
	[VHGinmutated] = 1; [VHGinoutpos] = [VHGinoldcatboundary]; [VHGinindex] = 0;
    "VHG INBOX import labels"
	A = [VHGinindex]; ? A >= [VHGinlabelcount] -> VHG INBOX cat imports done;
	A '* VHCATRECBYTES; A + 8; [VHGinboxpos] = A;
	[File Name] = VHGinboxfile; [File Position] = [VHGinboxpos]; [File Command] = READ;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGinrecord; [Block Size] = VHCATRECBYTES; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX invalid after write;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHCATRECBYTES -> VHG INBOX invalid after write;
	[VHGinptr] = VHGinrecord; [VHGinscan] = 0;
    "VHG INBOX scan source label"
	A = [VHGinscan]; ? A >= [VHGincatsource] -> VHG INBOX accept label;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGincmpptr] = A; => VHG INBOX identity match;
	A = [VHGinmatch]; ? A != 0 -> VHG INBOX next import label;
	[VHGinscan]+; -> VHG INBOX scan source label;
    "VHG INBOX accept label"
	[VHGinmutated] = 1; [File Name] = vhcatfile; [File Position] = [VHGinoutpos];
	[File Command] = WRITE; [Block Pointer] = VHGinrecord; [Block Size] = VHCATRECBYTES; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHCATRECBYTES -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[VHGinoutpos] + VHCATRECBYTES; [VHGinlabels]+;
    "VHG INBOX next import label"
	[VHGinindex]+; -> VHG INBOX import labels;
    "VHG INBOX cat imports done"
	[VHGinnewboundary] = [VHGinoutpos]; [VHGinindex] = [VHGincatsource];
    "VHG INBOX append cat locals"
	A = [VHGinindex]; ? A >= [VHGinoldcatrecs] -> VHG INBOX finish cat file;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGinptr] = A;
	C = [A]; ? C != VHCATTOMB0 -> VHG INBOX scan imported labels start;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C = VHCATTOMB1 -> VHG INBOX next cat local;
    "VHG INBOX scan imported labels start"
	[VHGinscan] = [VHGinoldcatboundary];
    "VHG INBOX scan imported labels"
	A = [VHGinscan]; ? A >= [VHGinnewboundary] -> VHG INBOX retain cat local;
	[File Name] = vhcatfile; [File Position] = [VHGinscan]; [File Command] = READ;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGincompare; [Block Size] = VHCATRECBYTES; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHCATRECBYTES -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[VHGincmpptr] = VHGincompare; => VHG INBOX identity match;
	A = [VHGinmatch]; ? A != 0 -> VHG INBOX next cat local;
	[VHGinscan] + VHCATRECBYTES; -> VHG INBOX scan imported labels;
    "VHG INBOX retain cat local"
	[File Name] = vhcatfile; [File Position] = [VHGinoutpos]; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = [VHGinptr]; [Block Size] = VHCATRECBYTES; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHCATRECBYTES -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[VHGinoutpos] + VHCATRECBYTES;
    "VHG INBOX next cat local"
	[VHGinindex]+; -> VHG INBOX append cat locals;
    "VHG INBOX finish cat file"
	[VHGinheader] = [VHGinnewboundary]; [File Name] = vhcatfile; [File Position] = 0;
	[File Command] = WRITE; [Block Pointer] = VHGinheader; [Block Size] = 4; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 4 -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[File Name] = vhcatfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGinoutpos]; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[VHGinoutpos] = [VHGinoldguideboundary]; [VHGinindex] = 0;
    "VHG INBOX import comments"
	A = [VHGinindex]; ? A >= [VHGincommentcount] -> VHG INBOX guide imports done;
	A '* VHGDBREC; A + [VHGinboxguidepos]; [VHGinboxpos] = A;
	[File Name] = VHGinboxfile; [File Position] = [VHGinboxpos]; [File Command] = READ;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGinrecord; [Block Size] = VHGDBREC; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX invalid after write;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHGDBREC -> VHG INBOX invalid after write;
	[VHGinptr] = VHGinrecord; [VHGinscan] = 0;
    "VHG INBOX scan source comment"
	A = [VHGinscan]; ? A >= [VHGinguidesource] -> VHG INBOX accept comment;
	A '* 21; A + vhguidedata; A + 1; [VHGincmpptr] = A; => VHG INBOX record match;
	A = [VHGinmatch]; ? A != 0 -> VHG INBOX next import comment;
	[VHGinscan]+; -> VHG INBOX scan source comment;
    "VHG INBOX accept comment"
	[File Name] = VHGDBfile; [File Position] = [VHGinoutpos]; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGinrecord; [Block Size] = VHGDBREC; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHGDBREC -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[VHGinoutpos] + VHGDBREC; [VHGincomments]+;
    "VHG INBOX next import comment"
	[VHGinindex]+; -> VHG INBOX import comments;
    "VHG INBOX guide imports done"
	[VHGinnewboundary] = [VHGinoutpos]; [VHGinindex] = [VHGinguidesource];
    "VHG INBOX append guide locals"
	A = [VHGinindex]; ? A >= [VHGinoldguiderecs] -> VHG INBOX finish guide file;
	A '* 21; A + vhguidedata; A + 1; [VHGinptr] = A;
	C = [A]; ? C != VHCATTOMB0 -> VHG INBOX scan imported comments start;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C = VHCATTOMB1 -> VHG INBOX next guide local;
    "VHG INBOX scan imported comments start"
	[VHGinscan] = [VHGinoldguideboundary];
    "VHG INBOX scan imported comments"
	A = [VHGinscan]; ? A >= [VHGinnewboundary] -> VHG INBOX retain guide local;
	[File Name] = VHGDBfile; [File Position] = [VHGinscan]; [File Command] = READ;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGincompare; [Block Size] = VHGDBREC; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHGDBREC -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[VHGincmpptr] = VHGincompare; => VHG INBOX record match;
	A = [VHGinmatch]; ? A != 0 -> VHG INBOX next guide local;
	[VHGinscan] + VHGDBREC; -> VHG INBOX scan imported comments;
    "VHG INBOX retain guide local"
	[File Name] = VHGDBfile; [File Position] = [VHGinoutpos]; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = [VHGinptr]; [Block Size] = VHGDBREC; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHGDBREC -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[VHGinoutpos] + VHGDBREC;
    "VHG INBOX next guide local"
	[VHGinindex]+; -> VHG INBOX append guide locals;
    "VHG INBOX finish guide file"
	[VHGinheader] = [VHGinnewboundary]; [File Name] = VHGDBfile; [File Position] = 0;
	[File Command] = WRITE; [Block Pointer] = VHGinheader; [Block Size] = 4; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 4 -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[File Name] = VHGDBfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGinoutpos]; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG INBOX write error;
	[VHGinmutated] = 0; => VHCAT load; => VHGDB load;
	A = [VHCATloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG INBOX write error no rollback;
	A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG INBOX write error no rollback;
	=> VHCAT count system bodies; => VHCAT refresh;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGinlabelshead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGcleanvalue] = [VHGinlabels]; => VHG OUTBOX output count;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGincommentshead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGcleanvalue] = [VHGincomments]; => VHG OUTBOX output count;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGincomplete; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG INBOX identity match"
	[VHGinmatch] = 0; A = [VHGinptr]; [VHCATid0] = [A]; [VHCATid1] = [A plus 1];
	[VHCATfoundptr] = [VHCATid0]; [VHCATptr] = [VHCATid1]; => VHCAT bounds;
	A = [VHGincmpptr]; [VHCATid0] = [A]; [VHCATid1] = [A plus 1]; => VHCAT key;
	A = [VHCATkeyhi]; B = [VHCATkeylo];
	? A '> [VHCATlowhi] -> VHG INBOX identity above low;
	? A '< [VHCATlowhi] -> VHG INBOX identity done;
	? B '< [VHCATlowlo] -> VHG INBOX identity done;
    "VHG INBOX identity above low"
	? A '< [VHCAT highhi] -> VHG INBOX identity hit;
	? A '> [VHCAT highhi] -> VHG INBOX identity done;
	? B '> [VHCAT highlo] -> VHG INBOX identity done;
    "VHG INBOX identity hit"
	[VHGinmatch] = 1;
    "VHG INBOX identity done"
	end;
    "VHG INBOX record match"
	=> VHG INBOX identity match; A = [VHGinmatch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG INBOX record match done;
	[VHGinword] = 2;
    "VHG INBOX record word"
	A = [VHGinword]; C = [VHGinptr]; C + A; E = [VHGincmpptr]; E + A;
	A = [C]; ? A != [E] -> VHG INBOX record mismatch;
	[VHGinword]+; A = [VHGinword]; ? A < 21 -> VHG INBOX record word;
	-> VHG INBOX record match done;
    "VHG INBOX record mismatch"
	[VHGinmatch] = 0;
    "VHG INBOX record match done"
	end;
    "VHG INBOX missing"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGinmissing0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGinmissing1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG INBOX invalid after write"
	=> VHG INBOX rollback;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGininvalid0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGininvalid1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG INBOX invalid"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGininvalid0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGininvalid1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG INBOX full"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGinfull; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG INBOX starmap unavailable"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanstarmapmissing; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG INBOX guide unavailable"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanguidemissing; => VH GOES output line; end;
