    "VHG INBOX write error"
	=> VHG INBOX rollback;
    "VHG INBOX write error no rollback"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGinwriteerror; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG INBOX rollback"
	A = [VHGinmutated]; ? A = 0 -> VHG INBOX rollback done;
	[File Name] = vhcatfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = vhcatraw; [Block Size] = [VHGinoldcatbytes]; isocall;
	[File Name] = vhcatfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGinoldcatbytes]; isocall;
	[File Name] = VHGDBfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = vhguidedata; [Block Size] = [VHGinoldguidebytes]; isocall;
	[File Name] = VHGDBfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGinoldguidebytes]; isocall;
	[VHGinmutated] = 0; => VHCAT load; => VHGDB load;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGinrollback; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG INBOX rollback done"
	end;

"VHG OUTBOX"
	( OUTBOX.CPP exports only live player additions beyond each database's
	  consolidated boundary.  Preserve its packet framing exactly: STARMAP_,
	  zero or more 32-byte labels, GUIDE___, then zero or more 84-byte notes. )
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG OUTBOX bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG OUTBOX bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGoutboxtitle; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHCATloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG OUTBOX starmap unavailable;
	A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG OUTBOX guide unavailable;
	[VHGoutboxlabels] = 0; [VHGoutboxcomments] = 0; [VHGoutboxpos] = 0;
	[VHGoutboxmarker plus 0] = 52415453h; [VHGoutboxmarker plus 1] = 5F50414Dh;
	[File Name] = VHGoutboxfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGoutboxmarker; [Block Size] = 8; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG OUTBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 8 -> VHG OUTBOX write error; [VHGoutboxpos] = 8;
	A = [vhcatraw]; A - VHCATHDRBYTES; A / VHCATRECBYTES; [VHGoutboxi] = A;
    "VHG OUTBOX starmap loop"
	A = [VHGoutboxi]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG OUTBOX guide marker;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGoutboxptr] = A;
	C = [A]; ? C != VHCATTOMB0 -> VHG OUTBOX write label;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C = VHCATTOMB1 -> VHG OUTBOX next label;
    "VHG OUTBOX write label"
	[File Name] = VHGoutboxfile; [File Position] = [VHGoutboxpos]; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = [VHGoutboxptr]; [Block Size] = VHCATRECBYTES; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG OUTBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHCATRECBYTES -> VHG OUTBOX write error;
	[VHGoutboxpos] + VHCATRECBYTES; [VHGoutboxlabels]+;
    "VHG OUTBOX next label"
	[VHGoutboxi]+; -> VHG OUTBOX starmap loop;
    "VHG OUTBOX guide marker"
	[VHGoutboxmarker plus 0] = 44495547h; [VHGoutboxmarker plus 1] = 5F5F5F45h;
	[File Name] = VHGoutboxfile; [File Position] = [VHGoutboxpos]; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGoutboxmarker; [Block Size] = 8; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG OUTBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 8 -> VHG OUTBOX write error; [VHGoutboxpos] + 8;
	A = [vhguidedata]; A - VHGDBHDR; A / VHGDBREC; [VHGoutboxi] = A;
    "VHG OUTBOX guide loop"
	A = [VHGoutboxi]; ? A >= [VHGDBrecs] -> VHG OUTBOX finish file;
	A '* 21; A + vhguidedata; A + 1; [VHGoutboxptr] = A;
	C = [A]; ? C != VHCATTOMB0 -> VHG OUTBOX write comment;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C = VHCATTOMB1 -> VHG OUTBOX next comment;
    "VHG OUTBOX write comment"
	[File Name] = VHGoutboxfile; [File Position] = [VHGoutboxpos]; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = [VHGoutboxptr]; [Block Size] = VHGDBREC; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG OUTBOX write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != VHGDBREC -> VHG OUTBOX write error;
	[VHGoutboxpos] + VHGDBREC; [VHGoutboxcomments]+;
    "VHG OUTBOX next comment"
	[VHGoutboxi]+; -> VHG OUTBOX guide loop;
    "VHG OUTBOX finish file"
	[File Name] = VHGoutboxfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGoutboxpos]; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG OUTBOX write error;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGoutlabels; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGcleanvalue] = [VHGoutboxlabels]; => VHG OUTBOX output count;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGoutcomments; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGcleanvalue] = [VHGoutboxcomments]; => VHG OUTBOX output count;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGoutcreated; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGoutboxlabels]; A + [VHGoutboxcomments]; ? A = 0 -> VHG OUTBOX done;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGoutsend0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGoutsend1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGoutsend2; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG OUTBOX done"
	end;
    "VHG OUTBOX starmap unavailable"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanstarmapmissing; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG OUTBOX guide unavailable"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanguidemissing; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG OUTBOX write error"
	[File Name] = VHGoutboxfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = 0; isocall; [VHPoutptr] = VHGouterror; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG OUTBOX output count"
	A = [VHGcleanvalue]; ? A != 0 -> VHG OUTBOX output number;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGoutnone; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG OUTBOX output number"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGcatline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; => VHG onboard row append space;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; [VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGcleanvalue];
	=> VHG onboard row append number; => VHG onboard row finish;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatline; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG CLEAN"
	( CLEAN.CPP compacts both mutable databases and adjusts a consolidated
	  boundary only for tombstones that were already inside that boundary. )
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG CLEAN bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG CLEAN bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanstarmap; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHCATloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CLEAN starmap unavailable result;
	=> VHCAT clean; A = [VHCATstatus]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CLEAN write failed result;
	[VHGcleanprefix] = VHGcleanrecordsprefix; [VHGcleanvalue] = [VHCATcleantotal]; => VHG CLEAN output count;
	[VHGcleanprefix] = VHGcleanremovedprefix; [VHGcleanvalue] = [VHCATcleanremoved]; => VHG CLEAN output count;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanguide; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CLEAN guide unavailable result;
	=> VHGDB clean; A = [VHGDBstatus]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CLEAN write failed result;
	[VHGcleanprefix] = VHGcleanrecordsprefix; [VHGcleanvalue] = [VHGDBcleantotal]; => VHG CLEAN output count;
	[VHGcleanprefix] = VHGcleanremovedprefix; [VHGcleanvalue] = [VHGDBcleanremoved]; => VHG CLEAN output count;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanend; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG CLEAN starmap unavailable result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanstarmapmissing; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG CLEAN guide unavailable result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanguidemissing; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG CLEAN write failed result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanwriteerror; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG CLEAN output count"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGcatline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = [VHGcleanprefix]; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; [VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGcleanvalue];
	=> VHG onboard row append number; => VHG onboard row finish;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatline; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG REPAIR"
	( The shipped utility marks duplicates in place and deliberately asks the
	  pilot to run CLEAN as a separate compaction step. )
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG REPAIR bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG REPAIR bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairtitle; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairintro0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairintro1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairintro2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairintro3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairstarmap; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHCATloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REPAIR starmap unavailable;
	=> VHCAT repair; A = [VHCATstatus]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REPAIR starmap write failed;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairerrors; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHCATrepairerrors]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REPAIR starmap clean;
	[VHGcleanvalue] = A; => VHG REPAIR output number;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairstarmapok; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairclean; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairgarbage; => VH GOES output line;
	-> VHG REPAIR guide begin;
    "VHG REPAIR starmap clean"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairnone; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairnostarmap; => VH GOES output line;
	-> VHG REPAIR guide begin;
    "VHG REPAIR starmap unavailable"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanstarmapmissing; => VH GOES output line;
	-> VHG REPAIR guide begin;
    "VHG REPAIR starmap write failed"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanwriteerror; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG REPAIR guide begin"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairguide; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REPAIR guide unavailable;
	=> VHGDB repair; A = [VHGDBstatus]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REPAIR guide write failed;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairerrors; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGDBrepairerrors]; ? A = 0 -> VHG REPAIR guide clean;
	[VHGcleanvalue] = A; => VHG REPAIR output number;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairguideok; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairclean; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairgarbage; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG REPAIR guide clean"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairnone; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGrepairnoguide; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG REPAIR guide unavailable"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanguidemissing; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG REPAIR guide write failed"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcleanwriteerror; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG REPAIR output number"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGcatline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; => VHG onboard row append space;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; [VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGcleanvalue];
	=> VHG onboard row append number; => VHG onboard row finish;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatline; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG CAT"
	( CAT.CPP reads the original 84-byte Galactic Guide records for a named
	  catalogue identity, preserving its one-based record ranges and 21-column
	  word wrapping on the resident GOES output tree. )
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG CAT execute;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage4; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG CAT execute"
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG CAT bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG CAT bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcathead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherematches] = 0; [VHGwherefound] = 0; [VHGwhererecno] = 0;
    "VHG CAT catalogue loop"
	A = [VHGwhererecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG CAT catalogue done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGwhererec] = A;
	=> VHG WHERE record match; A = [VHGwherematch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CAT catalogue next;
	[VHGwherematches]+; [VHGwherefound] = [VHGwhererec];
	A = [VHGwhereexact]; ? A != 0 -> VHG CAT catalogue exact;
    "VHG CAT catalogue next"
	[VHGwhererecno]+; -> VHG CAT catalogue loop;
    "VHG CAT catalogue exact"
	[VHGwherematches] = 1;
    "VHG CAT catalogue done"
	A = [VHGwherematches]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CAT missing result;
	? A > 1 -> VHG CAT ambiguous result;
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound];
	A = [VHGwhererec]; C = [A plus 7]; C > 8; C & FFh; [VHGwheretype] = C;
	? C = VHCATS -> VHG CAT star subject;
	? C = VHCATP -> VHG CAT planet subject; -> VHG CAT missing result;
    "VHG CAT missing result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherenotfound; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG CAT ambiguous result"
	=> VHG WHERE ambiguous; end;
    "VHG CAT star subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectstar; => VH GOES output line; -> VHG CAT subject label;
    "VHG CAT planet subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectplanet; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG CAT subject label"
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound]; => VHG WHERE output label;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CAT database error;
	A = [VHGwherefound]; [VHGpartarget0] = [A]; [VHGpartarget1] = [A plus 1];
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FSub;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparlowhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparlowlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FAdd;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparhighhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparhighlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[VHGcatrecno] = 0; [VHGcatmatchno] = 0; [VHGcatfound] = 0;
    "VHG CAT guide loop"
	A = [VHGcatrecno]; ? A >= [VHGDBrecs] -> VHG CAT guide done;
	A '* 21; A + vhguidedata; A + 1; [VHGcatptr] = A;
	[VHCATid0] = [A]; [VHCATid1] = [A plus 1];
	=> VHCAT identity valid; A = [VHCATvalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CAT guide next;
	=> VHCAT key; A = [VHCATkeyhi]; B = [VHCATkeylo];
	? A '> [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG CAT guide above low;
	? A '< [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG CAT guide next;
	? B '> [VHGparlowlo] -> VHG CAT guide above low; -> VHG CAT guide next;
    "VHG CAT guide above low"
	? A '< [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG CAT guide match;
	? A '> [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG CAT guide next;
	? B '< [VHGparhighlo] -> VHG CAT guide match; -> VHG CAT guide next;
    "VHG CAT guide match"
	[VHGcatmatchno]+; A = [VHGcatmatchno]; ? A < [VHGcatstart] -> VHG CAT guide next;
	? A > [VHGcatend] -> VHG CAT guide done;
	[VHGcatfound] = 1; => VHG CAT output record;
    "VHG CAT guide next"
	[VHGcatrecno]+; -> VHG CAT guide loop;
    "VHG CAT guide done"
	A = [VHGcatfound]; ? A != 0 -> VHG CAT finished;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatnone0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatnone1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatnone2; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG CAT database error"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatdberror0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatdberror1; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG CAT finished"
	end;

"VHG CAT output record"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGcatline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 40; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGcatmatchno]; => VHG onboard row append number;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 41; => VHG onboard row append char;
	=> VHG onboard row finish; [VHPoutptr] = VHGcatline; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGcatmsgi] = 0; [VHGcatcol] = 0;
    "VHG CAT message loop"
	A = [VHGcatmsgi]; ? A >= 76 -> VHG CAT message done;
	[VHGcatreadi] = A; => VHG CAT read message char; A = [VHGcatchar];
	? A = 0 -> VHG CAT message done; ? A = 32 -> VHG CAT message space;
	C = VHGcatline; C + [VHGcatcol]; [C] = A; [VHGcatcol]+; [VHGcatmsgi]+;
	A = [VHGcatcol]; ? A >= 21 -> VHG CAT message full;
	-> VHG CAT message loop;
    "VHG CAT message space"
	A = [VHGcatcol]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CAT message skip space;
	A = [VHGcatmsgi]; A + 1; [VHGcatlooki] = A; [VHGcatwordlen] = 0;
    "VHG CAT message word length"
	A = [VHGcatlooki]; ? A >= 76 -> VHG CAT message word ready;
	[VHGcatreadi] = A; => VHG CAT read message char; A = [VHGcatchar];
	? A = 0 -> VHG CAT message word ready; ? A = 32 -> VHG CAT message word ready;
	[VHGcatwordlen]+; [VHGcatlooki]+; -> VHG CAT message word length;
    "VHG CAT message word ready"
	A = [VHGcatcol]; A + 1; A + [VHGcatwordlen];
	? A > 21 -> VHG CAT message break space;
	C = VHGcatline; C + [VHGcatcol]; [C] = 32; [VHGcatcol]+;
    "VHG CAT message skip space"
	[VHGcatmsgi]+; -> VHG CAT message loop;
    "VHG CAT message break space"
	=> VHG CAT message flush; [VHGcatmsgi]+; -> VHG CAT message loop;
    "VHG CAT message full"
	=> VHG CAT message flush; -> VHG CAT message loop;
    "VHG CAT message done"
	A = [VHGcatcol]; ? A = 0 -> VHG CAT output record done;
	=> VHG CAT message flush;
    "VHG CAT output record done"
	end;

"VHG CAT message flush"
	C = VHGcatline; C + [VHGcatcol]; [C] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatline; => VH GOES output line; [VHGcatcol] = 0; end;

"VHG CAT read message char"
	A = [VHGcatreadi]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGcatptr]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; [VHGcatchar] = A; end;

"VHG PRI"
	( PRI.CPP sends selected Galactic Guide text to the printer.  The resident
	  host keeps its catalogue lookup, one-based range, continuous text stream,
	  and 72-column wrapping.  PRI uses the portable GUIDE-PRINT.TXT substitute;
	  the documented PRIF variant writes the same stream to GDOUTPUT.TXT. )
	[VHGprioutput] = VHGprifile; [VHGpriexportname] = VHGpriexport1;
	[VHGpriusageptr1] = VHGpriusage1; [VHGpriusageptr2] = VHGpriusage2;
	A = [VHGcataction]; ? A != 2 -> VHG PRI mode ready;
	[VHGprioutput] = VHGpriffile; [VHGpriexportname] = VHGprifexport1;
	[VHGpriusageptr1] = VHGprifusage1; [VHGpriusageptr2] = VHGprifusage2;
	"VHG PRI mode ready"
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG PRI execute;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = [VHGpriusageptr1]; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = [VHGpriusageptr2]; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage4; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG PRI execute"
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG PRI bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG PRI bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcathead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherematches] = 0; [VHGwherefound] = 0; [VHGwhererecno] = 0;
    "VHG PRI catalogue loop"
	A = [VHGwhererecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG PRI catalogue done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGwhererec] = A;
	=> VHG WHERE record match; A = [VHGwherematch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI catalogue next;
	[VHGwherematches]+; [VHGwherefound] = [VHGwhererec];
	A = [VHGwhereexact]; ? A != 0 -> VHG PRI catalogue exact;
    "VHG PRI catalogue next"
	[VHGwhererecno]+; -> VHG PRI catalogue loop;
    "VHG PRI catalogue exact"
	[VHGwherematches] = 1;
    "VHG PRI catalogue done"
	A = [VHGwherematches]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI missing result;
	? A > 1 -> VHG PRI ambiguous result;
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound];
	A = [VHGwhererec]; C = [A plus 7]; C > 8; C & FFh; [VHGwheretype] = C;
	? C = VHCATS -> VHG PRI star subject;
	? C = VHCATP -> VHG PRI planet subject; -> VHG PRI missing result;
    "VHG PRI missing result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherenotfound; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG PRI ambiguous result"
	=> VHG WHERE ambiguous; end;
    "VHG PRI star subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectstar; => VH GOES output line; -> VHG PRI subject label;
    "VHG PRI planet subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectplanet; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG PRI subject label"
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound]; => VHG WHERE output label;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGDBloaded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI database error;
	[VHGpripos] = 0; [VHGpricol] = 0; [VHGpriok] = 1; => VHG PRI clear line;
	[VHGpristrptr] = VHGpriheader; => VHG PRI write string line;
	A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	=> VHGprioutbar; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	A = [VHGwheretype]; ? A = VHCATP -> VHG PRI file planet subject;
	[VHGpristrptr] = VHGparsubjectstar; -> VHG PRI file subject ready;
    "VHG PRI file planet subject"
	[VHGpristrptr] = VHGparsubjectplanet;
    "VHG PRI file subject ready"
	=> VHG PRI write string line; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG PRI file label loop"
	A = [VHGwherei]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGwherefound]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; [VHGprichar] = A; => VHG PRI append char;
	A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 20 -> VHG PRI file label loop;
	=> VHG PRI flush line; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	=> VHGprioutbar; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGpriexport0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = [VHGpriexportname]; => VH GOES output line;
	A = [VHGwherefound]; [VHGpartarget0] = [A]; [VHGpartarget1] = [A plus 1];
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FSub;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparlowhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparlowlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FAdd;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparhighhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparhighlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[VHGcatrecno] = 0; [VHGcatmatchno] = 0; [VHGcatfound] = 0;
    "VHG PRI guide loop"
	A = [VHGcatrecno]; ? A >= [VHGDBrecs] -> VHG PRI guide done;
	A '* 21; A + vhguidedata; A + 1; [VHGcatptr] = A;
	[VHCATid0] = [A]; [VHCATid1] = [A plus 1];
	=> VHCAT identity valid; A = [VHCATvalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI guide next;
	=> VHCAT key; A = [VHCATkeyhi]; B = [VHCATkeylo];
	? A '> [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG PRI guide above low;
	? A '< [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG PRI guide next;
	? B '> [VHGparlowlo] -> VHG PRI guide above low; -> VHG PRI guide next;
    "VHG PRI guide above low"
	? A '< [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG PRI guide match;
	? A '> [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG PRI guide next;
	? B '< [VHGparhighlo] -> VHG PRI guide match; -> VHG PRI guide next;
    "VHG PRI guide match"
	[VHGcatmatchno]+; A = [VHGcatmatchno]; ? A < [VHGcatstart] -> VHG PRI guide next;
	? A > [VHGcatend] -> VHG PRI guide done;
	A = [VHGpricol]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI output record;
	[VHGprichar] = 32; => VHG PRI append char;
	A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
    "VHG PRI output record"
	[VHGcatfound] = 1; [VHGprimsgi] = 0;
    "VHG PRI message loop"
	A = [VHGprimsgi]; ? A >= 76 -> VHG PRI guide next;
	[VHGcatreadi] = A; => VHG CAT read message char; A = [VHGcatchar];
	? A = 0 -> VHG PRI guide next;
	A = [VHGprimsgi]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI message word check;
	A = [VHGcatchar]; ? A = 32 -> VHG PRI message word check;
    "VHG PRI message emit"
	[VHGprichar] = [VHGcatchar]; => VHG PRI append char;
	A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	[VHGprimsgi]+; -> VHG PRI message loop;
    "VHG PRI message word check"
	A = [VHGprimsgi]; A + 1; [VHGprilooki] = A; [VHGpriwordlen] = 1;
    "VHG PRI message word length"
	A = [VHGprilooki]; ? A >= 76 -> VHG PRI message word ready;
	[VHGcatreadi] = A; => VHG CAT read message char; A = [VHGcatchar];
	? A = 0 -> VHG PRI message word ready; ? A = 32 -> VHG PRI message word ready;
	[VHGpriwordlen]+; [VHGprilooki]+; -> VHG PRI message word length;
    "VHG PRI message word ready"
	A = [VHGpricol]; A + [VHGpriwordlen]; ? A <= 72 -> VHG PRI message reread;
	=> VHG PRI flush line; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	A = [VHGprimsgi]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI message reread;
	[VHGprimsgi]+;
    "VHG PRI message reread"
	[VHGcatreadi] = [VHGprimsgi]; => VHG CAT read message char; A = [VHGcatchar];
	? A = 32 -> VHG PRI message leading space;
	-> VHG PRI message emit;
    "VHG PRI message leading space"
	A = [VHGpricol]; ? A != 0 -> VHG PRI message emit;
	[VHGprimsgi]+; -> VHG PRI message loop;
    "VHG PRI guide next"
	[VHGcatrecno]+; -> VHG PRI guide loop;
    "VHG PRI guide done"
	A = [VHGcatfound]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI no records;
	=> VHG PRI flush line; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	=> VHGprioutbar; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	[VHGpristrptr] = VHGpriend; => VHG PRI write string line;
	A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	=> VHG PRI finish file; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGpriready; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG PRI no records"
	=> VHG PRI finish file; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write error;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatnone0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatnone1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatnone2; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG PRI database error"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatdberror0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGcatdberror1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG PRI write error"
	[File Name] = [VHGprioutput]; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = 0; isocall; [VHPoutptr] = VHGprierror; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG PRI write string line"
	[VHGpristri] = 0;
    "VHG PRI write string loop"
	A = [VHGpristrptr]; A + [VHGpristri]; A = [A]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write string done;
	[VHGprichar] = A; => VHG PRI append char; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write string done;
	[VHGpristri]+; -> VHG PRI write string loop;
    "VHG PRI write string done"
	A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI write string end; => VHG PRI flush line;
    "VHG PRI write string end"
	end;

"VHGprioutbar"
	[VHGprilinei] = 0;
	"VHGprioutbar loop"
	[VHGprichar] = 95; => VHG PRI append char; A = [VHGpriok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGprioutbar done;
	[VHGprilinei]+; A = [VHGprilinei]; ? A < 72 -> VHGprioutbar loop;
	"VHGprioutbar done"
	end;

"VHG PRI append char"
	A = [VHGpricol]; B = A; A / 4; C = VHGpriline; C + A; E = C;
	B & 3; B '* 8; A = [VHGprichar]; A & FFh; A < B; C = [E]; C | A; [E] = C;
	[VHGpricol]+; A = [VHGpricol]; ? A < 72 -> VHG PRI append char done;
	=> VHG PRI flush line;
    "VHG PRI append char done"
	end;

"VHG PRI flush line"
	A = [VHGpricol]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PRI flush newline;
	[File Name] = [VHGprioutput]; [File Position] = [VHGpripos]; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGpriline; [Block Size] = [VHGpricol]; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG PRI flush error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != [VHGpricol] -> VHG PRI flush error;
	[VHGpripos] + [VHGpricol];
    "VHG PRI flush newline"
	[VHGpricrlf] = 00000A0Dh;
	[File Name] = [VHGprioutput]; [File Position] = [VHGpripos]; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGpricrlf; [Block Size] = 2; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG PRI flush error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 2 -> VHG PRI flush error;
	[VHGpripos] + 2; [VHGpricol] = 0; => VHG PRI clear line; end;
    "VHG PRI flush error"
	[VHGpriok] = 0; end;

"VHG PRI clear line"
	[VHGpricleari] = 0;
    "VHG PRI clear line loop"
	A = VHGpriline; A + [VHGpricleari]; [A] = 0;
	[VHGpricleari]+; A = [VHGpricleari]; ? A < 18 -> VHG PRI clear line loop; end;

"VHG PRI finish file"
	[File Name] = [VHGprioutput]; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGpripos]; isocall; ? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG PRI finish error; end;
    "VHG PRI finish error"
	[VHGpriok] = 0; end;

"VHG X queue"
	( NICE R9 X.CPP is the file bridge used by Xnice.  A message takes X.TXT
	  when that slot is free, otherwise it joins the FIFO in XBUFF.TXT.  A bare
	  X promotes the oldest buffered line after the external consumer removes
	  X.TXT.  Keep the bridge beside the running game like the other port data. )
	=> VHG X pack message;
	A = [VHGxlen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG X promote;
	[VHGxbufferexists] = 0; [VHGxbuffersize] = 0;
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Command] = TEST; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X test output;
	[VHGxbufferexists] = 1; [VHGxbuffersize] = [File Size];
    "VHG X test output"
	[VHGxoutputexists] = 0; [VHGxoutputsize] = 0;
	[File Name] = VHGxfile; [File Command] = TEST; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X choose destination;
	[VHGxoutputexists] = 1; [VHGxoutputsize] = [File Size];
    "VHG X choose destination"
	A = [VHGxoutputexists]; ? A != 0 -> VHG X append buffer;
	A = [VHGxbufferexists]; ? A != 0 -> VHG X append buffer;
    "VHG X write file"
	[File Name] = VHGxfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGxline; [Block Size] = [VHGxlen]; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != [VHGxlen] -> VHG X failure result;
	[File Name] = VHGxfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGxlen]; isocall; ? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGxwrote0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGxfile; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG X append buffer"
	A = [VHGxbuffersize]; ? A = 0 -> VHG X append message;
	[VHGpricrlf] = 00000A0Dh;
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Position] = A; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGpricrlf; [Block Size] = 2; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 2 -> VHG X failure result; [VHGxbuffersize] + 2;
    "VHG X append message"
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Position] = [VHGxbuffersize]; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGxline; [Block Size] = [VHGxlen]; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != [VHGxlen] -> VHG X failure result;
	A = [VHGxbuffersize]; A + [VHGxlen]; [VHGxbuffersize] = A;
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGxbuffersize]; isocall; ? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGxwrote1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGxbufferfile; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG X promote"
	[File Name] = VHGxfile; [File Command] = TEST; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X promote test buffer;
	A = [File Size]; ? A != 0 -> VHG X done;
    "VHG X promote test buffer"
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Command] = TEST; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X done;
	[VHGxbuffersize] = [File Size]; A = [VHGxbuffersize]; ? A = 0 -> VHG X done;
	? A > VHGXMAX -> VHG X oversize result;
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = READ;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGxbuffer; [Block Size] = [VHGxbuffersize]; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != [VHGxbuffersize] -> VHG X failure result;
	[VHGxbase] = VHGxbuffer; [VHGxreadpos] = 0;
    "VHG X promote skip empty"
	A = [VHGxreadpos]; ? A >= [VHGxbuffersize] -> VHG X promote empty;
	[VHGxsrc] = A; => VHG X get byte; A = [VHGxbyte];
	? A = 13 -> VHG X promote skip one; ? A = 10 -> VHG X promote skip one;
	[VHGxlinestart] = [VHGxreadpos]; -> VHG X promote scan;
    "VHG X promote skip one"
	[VHGxreadpos]+; -> VHG X promote skip empty;
    "VHG X promote scan"
	A = [VHGxreadpos]; ? A >= [VHGxbuffersize] -> VHG X promote line ready;
	[VHGxsrc] = A; => VHG X get byte; A = [VHGxbyte];
	? A = 13 -> VHG X promote line ready; ? A = 10 -> VHG X promote line ready;
	[VHGxreadpos]+; -> VHG X promote scan;
    "VHG X promote line ready"
	A = [VHGxreadpos]; A - [VHGxlinestart]; [VHGxlinelen] = A;
    "VHG X promote skip delimiter"
	A = [VHGxreadpos]; ? A >= [VHGxbuffersize] -> VHG X promote rest ready;
	[VHGxsrc] = A; => VHG X get byte; A = [VHGxbyte];
	? A = 13 -> VHG X promote delimiter one; ? A = 10 -> VHG X promote delimiter one;
	-> VHG X promote rest ready;
    "VHG X promote delimiter one"
	[VHGxreadpos]+; -> VHG X promote skip delimiter;
    "VHG X promote rest ready"
	[VHGxreststart] = [VHGxreadpos]; A = [VHGxbuffersize]; A - [VHGxreststart]; [VHGxrestlen] = A;
	[VHGxcopyi] = 0;
    "VHG X promote copy line"
	A = [VHGxcopyi]; ? A >= [VHGxlinelen] -> VHG X promote write line;
	A + [VHGxlinestart]; [VHGxsrc] = A; => VHG X get byte;
	[VHGxdst] = [VHGxcopyi]; => VHG X put byte; [VHGxcopyi]+; -> VHG X promote copy line;
    "VHG X promote write line"
	[File Name] = VHGxfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGxbuffer; [Block Size] = [VHGxlinelen]; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != [VHGxlinelen] -> VHG X failure result;
	[File Name] = VHGxfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGxlinelen]; isocall; ? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result;
	[VHGxcopyi] = 0;
    "VHG X promote copy rest"
	A = [VHGxcopyi]; ? A >= [VHGxrestlen] -> VHG X promote write rest;
	A + [VHGxreststart]; [VHGxsrc] = A; => VHG X get byte;
	[VHGxdst] = [VHGxcopyi]; => VHG X put byte; [VHGxcopyi]+; -> VHG X promote copy rest;
    "VHG X promote write rest"
	A = [VHGxrestlen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG X promote destroy buffer;
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGxbuffer; [Block Size] = [VHGxrestlen]; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != [VHGxrestlen] -> VHG X failure result;
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = [VHGxrestlen]; isocall; ? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG X failure result; end;
    "VHG X promote destroy buffer"
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Command] = DESTROY; isocall; end;
    "VHG X promote empty"
	[File Name] = VHGxbufferfile; [File Command] = DESTROY; isocall; end;
    "VHG X oversize result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGxtoolarge; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG X failure result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGxerror; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG X done"
	end;

"VHG X pack message"
	[VHGxsrc] = 0; [VHGxlen] = 0; [VHGxlastspace] = 0;
	[VHGxcopyi] = 0;
    "VHG X clear line"
	A = VHGxline; A + [VHGxcopyi]; [A] = 0;
	[VHGxcopyi]+; A = [VHGxcopyi]; ? A < 18 -> VHG X clear line;
	[VHGxbase] = VHGxline;
    "VHG X pack loop"
	A = [VHGxsrc]; ? A >= 68 -> VHG X pack done;
	C = [VHGxmsgptr]; C + A; A = [C]; A & FFh; ? A = 0 -> VHG X pack done;
	[VHGxsrc]+; ? A = 32 -> VHG X pack space;
	[VHGxbyte] = A; [VHGxdst] = [VHGxlen]; => VHG X put byte;
	[VHGxlen]+; [VHGxlastspace] = 0; -> VHG X pack loop;
    "VHG X pack space"
	A = [VHGxlen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG X pack loop;
	A = [VHGxlastspace]; ? A != 0 -> VHG X pack loop;
	[VHGxbyte] = 32; [VHGxdst] = [VHGxlen]; => VHG X put byte;
	[VHGxlen]+; [VHGxlastspace] = 1; -> VHG X pack loop;
    "VHG X pack done"
	A = [VHGxlastspace]; ? A = 0 -> VHG X pack end;
	A = [VHGxlen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG X pack end; [VHGxlen]-;
    "VHG X pack end"
	end;

"VHG X get byte"
	A = [VHGxsrc]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGxbase]; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; [VHGxbyte] = A; end;

"VHG X put byte"
	A = [VHGxdst]; B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGxbase]; C + A; E = C;
	B & 3; B '* 8; A = FFh; A < B; A !; C = [E]; C & A;
	A = [VHGxbyte]; A & FFh; A < B; C | A; [E] = C; end;

"VHG PAR"
	( PAR.CPP regenerates every procedural star in a centred sector cube and
	  compares the resulting binary64 identity with the selected catalogue
	  subject. Keep that original search and its X,-Y,Z coordinate convention. )
	A = [VHGwherelen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG PAR execute;
	A = [VHGparaction]; ? A = 1 -> VHG ST usage;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage4; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG PAR usage bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG PAR usage bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage5; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG ST usage"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstusage1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstusage2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparusage4; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG ST usage bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG ST usage bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstusage5; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG PAR execute"
	[VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG PAR bar fill"
	A = VHGwherebar; A + [VHGwherei]; [A] = 38;
	[VHGwherei]+; A = [VHGwherei]; ? A < 21 -> VHG PAR bar fill;
	[VHGwherebar plus 21] = 0;
	A = [VHGparaction]; ? A = 1 -> VHG ST output head;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparhead; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line; -> VHG analysis catalogue begin;
    "VHG ST output head"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstlooking; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG analysis catalogue begin"
	[VHGwherematches] = 0; [VHGwherefound] = 0; [VHGwhererecno] = 0;
    "VHG PAR catalogue loop"
	A = [VHGwhererecno]; ? A >= [VHCATrecs] -> VHG PAR catalogue done;
	A '* 8; A + vhcatraw; A + 1; [VHGwhererec] = A;
	=> VHG WHERE record match; A = [VHGwherematch]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PAR catalogue next;
	[VHGwherematches]+; [VHGwherefound] = [VHGwhererec];
	A = [VHGwhereexact]; ? A != 0 -> VHG PAR catalogue exact;
    "VHG PAR catalogue next"
	[VHGwhererecno]+; -> VHG PAR catalogue loop;
    "VHG PAR catalogue exact"
	[VHGwherematches] = 1;
    "VHG PAR catalogue done"
	A = [VHGwherematches]; ? A = 0 -> VHG PAR missing result;
	? A > 1 -> VHG PAR ambiguous result;
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound];
	A = [VHGwhererec]; C = [A plus 7]; C > 8; C & FFh; [VHGwheretype] = C;
	? C = VHCATS -> VHG PAR star subject;
	? C = VHCATP -> VHG PAR planet subject; -> VHG PAR missing result;
    "VHG PAR missing result"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherenotfound; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG PAR ambiguous result"
	=> VHG WHERE ambiguous; end;
    "VHG PAR star subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectstar; => VH GOES output line;
	=> VHG PAR output name;
	A = [VHGparaction]; ? A != 1 -> VHG PAR star target ready;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG PAR star target ready"
	A = [VHGwherefound]; [VHGpartarget0] = [A]; [VHGpartarget1] = [A plus 1];
	-> VHG PAR prepare scan;
    "VHG PAR planet subject"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparsubjectplanet; => VH GOES output line;
	=> VHG PAR output name;
	A = [VHGparaction]; ? A != 1 -> VHG PAR planet target ready;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGwherebar; => VH GOES output line;
    "VHG PAR planet target ready"
	A = [VHGwherefound]; C = [A plus 7];
	A = C; A > 16; A & FFh; A - 48; A '* 10; [VHGwherecode] = A;
	A = C; A > 24; A & FFh; A - 48; A + [VHGwherecode]; [VHGwherecode] = A;
	A = [VHGwherefound]; [nsid0] = [A]; [nsid1] = [A plus 1];
	A = 0; A - [VHGwherecode]; [FI] = A; => NsIdentAddInt;
	[VHGpartarget0] = [FA0]; [VHGpartarget1] = [FA1];
    "VHG PAR prepare scan"
	( sidlow = star_id-idscale; sidhigh = star_id+idscale.  The ordered
	  binary64 keys let the integer loop retain the source comparisons exactly. )
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FSub;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparlowhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparlowlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	[FA0] = [VHGpartarget0]; [FA1] = [VHGpartarget1];
	[FB0] = VHCATIDSC0; [FB1] = VHCATIDSC1; => FAdd;
	[VHCATid0] = [FA0]; [VHCATid1] = [FA1]; => VHCAT key;
	[VHGparhighhi] = [VHCATkeyhi]; [VHGparhighlo] = [VHCATkeylo];
	A = [VHGparrange]; A '* 50000; [VHGparoffset] = A;
	[VHGparaxis0] = [MgDzatX0]; [VHGparaxis1] = [MgDzatX1]; => VHG PAR sector base;
	[VHGparbasex] = [VHGparbaseout];
	[VHGparaxis0] = [MgDzatY0]; [VHGparaxis1] = [MgDzatY1]; => VHG PAR sector base;
	[VHGparbasey] = [VHGparbaseout];
	[VHGparaxis0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [VHGparaxis1] = [MgDzatZ1]; => VHG PAR sector base;
	[VHGparbasez] = [VHGparbaseout];
	[VHGparix] = 0; [VHGparsectx] = [VHGparbasex];
    "VHG PAR scan x"
	[VHGpariy] = 0; [VHGparsecty] = [VHGparbasey];
    "VHG PAR scan y"
	[VHGpariz] = 0; [VHGparsectz] = [VHGparbasez];
    "VHG PAR scan z"
	( PAR's extended scans are cancellable with Escape, as in the module. )
	A = [VHGparrange]; ? A <= 100 -> VHG PAR hash candidate;
	A = [KEY ESCAPE]; ? A = ON -> VHG PAR scan exhausted;
    "VHG PAR hash candidate"
	A = [VHGparsectx]; A + [VHGparsectz]; [VHSsumxz] = A;
	A & 1FFFFh; A + [VHGparsectx];
	? A = VHSCUTOFF -> VHG PAR scan next z;
	A - VHSCUTOFF; [VHStempx] = A;
	[VHSfmA] = A; [VHSfmB] = [VHSsumxz]; => VHS foldmul;
	[VHSaccum] = [VHSfmResult];
	A = [VHSsumxz]; A + [VHSaccum]; [VHSidk] = A;
	A = [VHSaccum]; A & 1FFFFh; A + [VHGparsecty];
	? A = VHSCUTOFF -> VHG PAR scan next z;
	A - VHSCUTOFF; [VHStempy] = A;
	[VHSfmA] = A; [VHSfmB] = [VHSidk]; => VHS foldmul;
	[VHSaccum] = [VHSfmResult];
	A = [VHSaccum]; A & 1FFFFh; A + [VHGparsectz];
	? A = VHSCUTOFF -> VHG PAR scan next z;
	A - VHSCUTOFF; [VHStempz] = A;
	[FJ0] = [VHStempx]; [FJ1] = [VHStempy]; [FJ2] = [VHStempz]; => NsIdentityD;
	[VHCATid0] = [nsid0]; [VHCATid1] = [nsid1]; => VHCAT key;
	A = [VHCATkeyhi]; B = [VHCATkeylo];
	? A '> [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG PAR candidate above low;
	? A '< [VHGparlowhi] -> VHG PAR scan next z;
	? B '> [VHGparlowlo] -> VHG PAR candidate above low; -> VHG PAR scan next z;
    "VHG PAR candidate above low"
	? A '< [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG PAR scan hit;
	? A '> [VHGparhighhi] -> VHG PAR scan next z;
	? B '< [VHGparhighlo] -> VHG PAR scan hit; -> VHG PAR scan next z;
    "VHG PAR scan next z"
	[VHGparsectz] + 100000; [VHGpariz]+; A = [VHGpariz];
	? A < [VHGparrange] -> VHG PAR scan z;
	[VHGparsecty] + 100000; [VHGpariy]+; A = [VHGpariy];
	? A < [VHGparrange] -> VHG PAR scan y;
	[VHGparsectx] + 100000; [VHGparix]+; A = [VHGparix];
	? A < [VHGparrange] -> VHG PAR scan x;
    "VHG PAR scan exhausted"
	[VHGwhererec] = [VHGwherefound]; => VHG WHERE output label;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGparoutofrange; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG PAR scan hit"
	A = [VHGparaction]; ? A = 1 -> VHG ST scan hit;
	[VHGparaxischar] = 88; [VHGparcoord] = [VHStempx]; => VHG PAR output coordinate;
	[VHGparaxischar] = 89; A = 0; A - [VHStempy]; [VHGparcoord] = A;
	=> VHG PAR output coordinate;
	[VHGparaxischar] = 90; [VHGparcoord] = [VHStempz]; => VHG PAR output coordinate;
	end;

"VHG ST scan hit"
	A = [VHGwheretype]; ? A = VHCATP -> VHG ST local hit;
	[VHTtx] = [VHStempx]; [VHTty] = [VHStempy]; [VHTtz] = [VHStempz];
	=> VHG activate target;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstremote0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstremote1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG ST local hit"
	A = [MgApreached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG ST local missing;
	A = [VHTtx]; ? A != [VHStempx] -> VHG ST local missing;
	A = [VHTty]; ? A != [VHStempy] -> VHG ST local missing;
	A = [VHTtz]; ? A != [VHStempz] -> VHG ST local missing;
	A = [VHGwherecode]; A - 1; ? A < 0 -> VHG ST local missing;
	? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG ST local missing; [VHGplanet] = A;
	=> VHCAT refresh; => VHG local reset; => VHG local start;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstlocal0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstlocal1; => VH GOES output line; end;
    "VHG ST local missing"
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstnotpart0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstnotpart1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstnotpart2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstnotpart3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGstnotpart4; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG PAR sector base"
	[FI] = [VHGparoffset]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGparaxis0]; [FA1] = [VHGparaxis1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [MgskA]; [FB1] = [MgskB]; => FQuo; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; A '* VHSSECTOR; [VHGparbaseout] = A; end;

"VHG PAR output name"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGparline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGparnameprefix; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; [VHGwherei] = 0;
    "VHG PAR output name loop"
	A = [VHGwherei]; ? A >= 15 -> VHG PAR output name ready;
	B = A; A / 4; C = [VHGwherefound]; C + 2; C + A; A = [C];
	B & 3; B '* 8; A > B; A & FFh; [VHGonrowchar] = A;
	=> VHG onboard row append char; [VHGwherei]+; -> VHG PAR output name loop;
    "VHG PAR output name ready"
	=> VHG onboard row finish; [VHPoutptr] = VHGparline; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG PAR output coordinate"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGparline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowchar] = [VHGparaxischar]; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 61; => VHG onboard row append char;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGparcoord]; => VHG onboard row append number;
	=> VHG onboard row finish; [VHPoutptr] = VHGparline; => VH GOES output line; end;

"VHG parse coordinate"
	( Practical NIV+ correction: the original manual Parsis editor stopped at
	  ten total characters, so a sign left room for only nine digits and made
	  the far Feltyrion region unreachable through Galactic Cartography.  The
	  hosted `STAR x y z` command accepts ten digits plus an optional sign;
	  parsed coordinates remain signed 32-bit values. )
	[VHGcmdok] = 0; [VHGcmdvalue] = 0; [VHGcmdsign] = 0; [VHGcmddigits] = 0;
    "VHG parse skip spaces"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C != 32 -> VHG parse sign;
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG parse skip spaces;
    "VHG parse sign"
	? C != 45 -> VHG parse digits; [VHGcmdsign] = 1; [VHGcmdi]+;
    "VHG parse digits"
	A = vhptext; A + [VHGcmdi]; C = [A]; ? C < 48 -> VHG parse end;
	? C > 57 -> VHG parse end;
	A = [VHGcmddigits]; ? A >= 10 -> VHG parse done;
	A = [VHGcmdvalue]; ? A < 214748364 -> VHG parse accumulate;
	? A > 214748364 -> VHG parse done;
	C - 48; A = 7; E = [VHGcmdsign]; ? E = 0 -> VHG parse last digit ready; A = 8;
    "VHG parse last digit ready"
	? C > A -> VHG parse done; C + 48;
    "VHG parse accumulate"
	A = [VHGcmdvalue]; A '* 10; C - 48; A + C; [VHGcmdvalue] = A;
	[VHGcmddigits]+; [VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG parse digits;
    "VHG parse end"
	A = [VHGcmddigits]; ? A = 0 -> VHG parse done;
	? C = 32 -> VHG parse valid; ? C = 0 -> VHG parse valid;
	? C != 95 -> VHG parse done;
    "VHG parse valid"
	A = [VHGcmdsign]; ? A = 0 -> VHG parse positive;
	A = 0; A - [VHGcmdvalue]; [VHGcmdvalue] = A;
    "VHG parse positive"
	[VHGcmdok] = 1;
    "VHG parse done"
	end;

"VHG capture name"
	[VHGcmdok] = 0; [VHCATlen] = 0; [VHGi] = 0;
    "VHG name clear"
	E = vhcatname; E + [VHGi]; [E] = 32; [VHGi]+;
	A = [VHGi]; ? A < 20 -> VHG name clear;
    "VHG name leading spaces"
	E = vhptext; E + [VHGcmdi]; A = [E]; ? A != 32 -> VHG name copy;
	[VHGcmdi]+; -> VHG name leading spaces;
    "VHG name copy"
	? A = 0 -> VHG name captured; ? A = 95 -> VHG name captured;
	C = [VHCATlen]; ? C >= 20 -> VHG name captured;
	E = vhcatname; E + C; [E] = A; [VHCATlen]+; [VHGcmdi]+;
	-> VHG name leading spaces;
    "VHG name captured"
	A = [VHCATlen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG name capture done;
    "VHG name trim"
	A = [VHCATlen]; A - 1; E = vhcatname; E + A; A = [E]; ? A != 32 -> VHG name valid;
	[VHCATlen]-; A = [VHCATlen]; ? A > 0 -> VHG name trim; -> VHG name capture done;
    "VHG name valid"
	[VHGcmdok] = 1;
    "VHG name capture done"
	end;

"VHG next star"
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG target ship only;
	A = [VHSready]; ? A = 0 -> VHG target failed;
	[VHGstartries] = 0;
    "VHG next star loop"
	[VHGstarcursor]+; A = [VHGstarcursor]; A '% [VHScount]; [VHGstarcursor] = A;
	A '* VHSENTRY; A + vhsstarcache; [VHGptr] = A;
	A = [A plus 3]; ? A = 0 -> VHG next star retry;
	A = [VHGptr]; C = [A plus 0]; ? C != [VHTtx] -> VHG next star use;
	C = [A plus 1]; ? C != [VHTty] -> VHG next star use;
	C = [A plus 2]; ? C != [VHTtz] -> VHG next star use;
    "VHG next star retry"
	[VHGstartries]+; A = [VHGstartries]; ? A < [VHScount] -> VHG next star loop;
	-> VHG target failed;
    "VHG next star use"
	A = [VHGptr]; [VHTtx] = [A plus 0]; [VHTty] = [A plus 1]; [VHTtz] = [A plus 2];
	=> VHG activate target;
	end;

"VHG activate target"
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG target ship only;
	[VHGplanet] = 0;
	=> VHG target world; => VHG flight retarget;
	[VHGlanded] = 0; [VHGCstate] = 0; [VHGCcapcount] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGtargettext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;
    "VHG target ship only"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGshiponlytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;
    "VHG target failed"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGtargetfailedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;

"VHG GOES HELP"
	( N_Help_3.asm exposes the resident GOES modules as seven 20-column rows.
	  Preserve that directory byte-for-byte; every advertised module is routed
	  by the command parser above. )
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGgoeshelp0; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGgoeshelp1; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGgoeshelp2; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGgoeshelp3; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGgoeshelp4; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGgoeshelp5; => VH GOES output line;
	[VHPoutptr] = VHGgoeshelp6; => VH GOES output line;
	end;

"VHG load checkpoint"
	=> VHSV load; A = [VHSVok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG load checkpoint done;
	=> VHG hull cache apply;
	[VHLpower] = [VHGilight]; [VHGlighttick] = 0;
	[MgAnti] = [VHGantirad]; [VHSamp] = [VHGamp]; [VHSready] = 0; [VHSdrawready] = 0;
	[VHGamptick] = 0; [VHGfindertick] = 0; [VHGsynctick] = 0;
	( Reconcile persisted user-facing settings with live timer/audio state. )
	A = [VHGfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG load original cadence;
	[VHGsimacc] = 0; [VHGsimfrac] = 0; [VHGsimcountok] = 0; -> VHG load cadence ready;
    "VHG load original cadence"
	[VHGsimacc] = 0; [VHGsimfrac] = 0; [VHGsimcountok] = 0; => TK period;
    "VHG load cadence ready"
	=> VHG timing rebase;
	=> VHA apply;
	=> VHG target world;
	A = [VHGplanet]; ? A '< [nsnob] -> VHG load target valid; [VHGplanet] = 0;
    "VHG load target valid"
	A = [VHSVlocalstored]; ? A = 0 -> VHG load legacy local;
	=> VHG restore local checkpoint; -> VHG load local ready;
    "VHG load legacy local"
	=> VHG local reset;
    "VHG load local ready"
	[VHGCstate] = 0; [VHGCcapcount] = 0; [VHGCrecover] = 0;
	[VHGcapsulestartpending] = 0;
	[VHGcapsulereturnpending] = 0;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG command load ship;
	[VHGNDlon] = [VHGlandinglon]; [VHGNDlat] = [VHGlandinglat];
	=> VHG select texture buffer;
	=> VHG prepare planet; => VHGND generate;
	( Version-11 checkpoints restore the settled capsule only after terrain
	  generation, which otherwise recreates the deterministic default site.
	  Older landed formats cannot identify a wind-displaced touchdown; anchor
	  their one-time migration at the saved walker so an RC21 save cannot leave
	  its player permanently beyond capsule range. )
	A = [VHSVdropstored]; ? A != 0 -> VHG load stored capsule;
	[VHGNDdropx] = [VHGx]; [VHGNDdropz] = [VHGz];
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGx]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGz]; => VHGND eye height;
	A = [VHGNDheight]; A + 600; [VHGNDdropy] = A;
	-> VHG load capsule ready;
    "VHG load stored capsule"
	[VHGNDdropx] = [VHSVdropx]; [VHGNDdropz] = [VHSVdropz];
	( A settled capsule rests on deterministic terrain.  Re-sample hpoint at
	  its persisted X/Z so legacy saves made while drop_y had the wrong
	  eye-height convention cannot bury or levitate the restored mesh. )
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGNDdropx]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGNDdropz]; => VHGND eye height;
	A = [VHGNDheight]; A + 600; [VHGNDdropy] = A;
    "VHG load capsule ready"
	( Capsule phase is intentionally transient and is not part of the checkpoint
	  format.  A save made during descent/recovery therefore cannot resume that
	  animation faithfully.  Recover it as a settled touchdown at the persisted
	  pod instead of leaving surface mode permanently control-locked; this also
	  repairs checkpoints written by the old unconditional ship-bound clamp. )
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A != 0 -> VHG load capsule phase ready;
	[VHGx] = [VHGNDdropx]; [VHGz] = [VHGNDdropz]; [VHGlanded] = 1;
    "VHG load capsule phase ready"
	[VHGNDcaptures] = [VHSVcaptures];
	=> VHGND restore captures;
	=> VHGND sky; => VHG fpu clean; => VHG surface telemetry init;
	=> VHG surface motion reset; -> VHG load checkpoint done;
    "VHG command load ship"
	=> VHG palette; => VHG star palette;
    "VHG load checkpoint done"
	end;

"VHG restore local checkpoint"
	( Version-14 saves carry the fine-approach integrator separately from galactic
	  dzat. Rebuild only its generated-system caches and MG boundary inputs; the
	  persisted position, distance and current coefficient remain authoritative. )
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG restore local inactive;
	A = [VHGlocaltarget]; ? A < 0 -> VHG restore local invalid;
	? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG restore local invalid;
	[VHGplanet] = A;
	[VHGlocalresident1] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGlocalresident2] = 0FFFFFFFFh;
	[VHGlocalpmapbody] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGlocalmmapbody] = 0FFFFFFFFh;
	[VHGlocalsurfaceok] = 0;
	[VHGNDplanet] = [VHGlocaltarget]; => VHGND general phase setup; => VHG fpu clean;
	A = [VHGlocaltarget]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1]; => F32Narrow; [MgPray] = [FS0];
	[MgPlX0] = 0; [MgPlX1] = 0; [MgPlY0] = 0; [MgPlY1] = 0; [MgPlZ0] = 0; [MgPlZ1] = 0;
	( A live local-system checkpoint exists only after Vimana has reached the
	  parent star.  Flight init ran before the checkpoint load, so restore the
	  outer drive state as well as the fine-approach integrator. )
	[MgApreached] = 1; [MgStspeed] = 0;
	[MgStatus] = 5; A = [MgIpreached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG restore local done; [MgStatus] = 12;
	-> VHG restore local done;
    "VHG restore local inactive"
	=> VHG local reset; -> VHG restore local done;
    "VHG restore local invalid"
	=> VHG local reset;
    "VHG restore local done"
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	end;

    "VHG command done"
	end;

"VHG new game"
	( Explicit counterpart to startup auto-resume.  Reset gameplay progress
	  and the opening flight, but retain user presentation/music preferences
	  and the persistent starmap catalogue.  Save immediately so a later crash
	  cannot resurrect the checkpoint the player deliberately replaced. )
	[VHGmode] = 0; [VHGlanded] = 0; [VHGlandpending] = 0; [VHGinterpok] = 0;
	[VHGmovie] = 0; [VHGmovieview] = 0; [VHGmoviepaused] = 0; [VHGmovieframe] = 0;
	[VHGlandingselect] = 0; [VHGlandingview] = 1; [VHGlandinglon] = 0; [VHGlandinglat] = 60;
	[VHGsurfair] = 0; [VHGsurfjet] = 0; [VHGsurfjumping] = 0; [VHGsurfvy] = 0;
	[VHGx] = 0; [VHGy] = 0; A = 0; A - 500; [VHGz] = A;
	[VHGalpha] = 0; [VHGbeta] = 180;
	[VHGshipx] = 0; [VHGshipy] = 0; [VHGshipz] = 0;
	[VHGplanet] = 0; [VHGframe] = 0; [VHGsent] = 0;
	[VHGlifter] = 0; [VHGliftstep] = 0; [VHGonroof] = 0; [VHGridearmed] = 1; [VHGuprequest] = 0;
	[VHGCstate] = 0; [VHGCcapcount] = 0; [VHGCrecover] = 0;
	[VHGrescueactive] = 0; [VHGrescuetick] = 0; [VHGrescuedelay] = 0; [VHGrescueacc] = 0; [VHGcollector] = 0;
	[VHGelight] = 0; [VHGgburst] = 0; [VHGresetcount] = 0;
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0; [VHGilight] = 1; [VHGilightlevel] = 63; [VHGlighttick] = 0; [VHLpower] = 1;
	[VHGamp] = 0; [VHGfinder] = 0; [VHGsync] = 1; [VHGantirad] = 1;
	[VHGamptick] = 0; [VHGfindertick] = 0; [VHGsynctick] = 0; [VHGtrackphase] = 0;
	[VHSamp] = 0; [VHSready] = 0; [VHSdrawready] = 0;
	[VHGsavedsecs] = 0; [VHGelapsed] = 0; [VHGofflinegain] = 0;
	[VHGNDcaptures] = 0; [VHGstarcursor] = 0;
	[VHTtx] = 3979984; A = 0; A - 43407; [VHTty] = A;
	A = 0; A - 43984; [VHTtz] = A;
	=> VHG target world; => VHG flight init;
	=> VHG palette; => VHG star palette; => VHG fps init;
	=> VHSV save;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGnewtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 100;
	end;

"VHG return ship"
	( The original surface recorder cuts off during capsule recovery so it
	  cannot follow the player indefinitely back into the Stardrifter. )
	A = [VHGmovie]; ? A = 0 -> VHG return ship movie done; => VHG movie automatic stop;
    "VHG return ship movie done"
	[VHGx] = [VHGshipx]; [VHGy] = [VHGshipy]; [VHGz] = [VHGshipz];
	[VHGalpha] = 0; [VHGbeta] = 180; [VHGmode] = 0; [VHGlanded] = 0;
	[VHGinterpok] = 0;
	[VHGsurfair] = 0; [VHGsurfjet] = 0; [VHGsurfjumping] = 0; [VHGsurfvy] = 0;
	[VHGCstate] = 0; [VHGCcapcount] = 0;
	=> VHG palette; => VHG star palette;
	end;

"VHG surface clamp"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:3903-3918 uses a circular boundary centered on the map.
	  The source landed flag remains set for the entire walking session, so
	  ordinary jumps and jetpack flight retain the full 1,500,000-unit radius. )
	A = [VHGx]; A - 1638400; [VHGdx] = A; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul; [FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHGz]; A - 1638400; [VHGdz] = A; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGdist] = [FI]; [VHGsurfradius] = 1500000;
    "VHG surface radius ready"
	A = [VHGdist]; ? A <= [VHGsurfradius] -> VHG surface clamp done;
	A - [VHGsurfradius]; C = 1000000; C - A; ? C >= 0 -> VHG surface scale ready; C = 0;
    "VHG surface scale ready"
	[VHGsurfscale] = C; [FI] = C; => IntToF; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1000000; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FQuo; [FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGdx]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [FI]; A + 1638400; [VHGx] = A;
	[FI] = [VHGdz]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [FI]; A + 1638400; [VHGz] = A;
    "VHG surface clamp done"
	end;

"VHG fps init"
	=> TK read wall;
	[VHGfpslast] = [TKtmp]; [VHGfpsframes] = 0; [VHGfps] = 0;
	[VHGfpstext plus 0] = 70; [VHGfpstext plus 1] = 80;
	[VHGfpstext plus 2] = 83; [VHGfpstext plus 3] = 32;
	[VHGfpstext plus 4] = 48; [VHGfpstext plus 5] = 48;
	[VHGfpstext plus 6] = 0;
	end;

"VHG fps tick"
	[VHGfpsframes]+; A = [VHGfpsframes]; ? A < 12 -> VHG fps tick done;
	=> TK read wall; [VHGfpsnow] = [TKtmp];
	A = [VHGfpsnow]; A - [VHGfpslast]; [VHGfpsdelta] = A;
	? A = 0 -> VHG fps tick reset;
	C = 1000; C '* [VHGfpsframes];
	A = [VHGfpsdelta]; A '/ 2; C + A; C '/ [VHGfpsdelta]; [VHGfps] = C;
    "VHG fps tick reset"
	[VHGfpslast] = [VHGfpsnow]; [VHGfpsframes] = 0;
    "VHG fps tick done"
	end;

"VHG fps overlay"
	( Reuse iGUI's already-linked font on the logical RGB game page. )
	A = [VHGfpsshow]; ? A = 0 -> VHG fps overlay done;
	A = [VHGfps]; ? A <= 99 -> VHG fps bounded; A = 99;
    "VHG fps bounded"
	C = A; A '/ 10; A + 48; [VHGfpstext plus 4] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGfpstext plus 5] = A;
	[String] = VHGfpstext; [Ink] = FFFFFFh;
	[Text X] = 12; [Text Y] = 12;
	[Text Display Width] = 320; [Text Display Height] = 200;
	[Text Region] = vector VHGUIregion;
	[Text Window] = NULL; [Text Word Wrap] = OFF; [Text Ghost Mode] = OFF;
	=> VHG text both;
"VHG fps overlay done"
	end;

"VHG energy overlay"
	( Persistent feedback for the source power bias, lithium reserve, and
	  collector state.  Keep it outside the landed landscape view. )
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHG energy overlay done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG energy overlay done;
	A = [VHGscreen]; ? A != 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG energy overlay done;
	A = [MgPwr]; A - 15000; ? A >= 0 -> VHG energy nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHG energy nonnegative"
	? A <= 99999 -> VHG energy bounded; A = 99999;
    "VHG energy bounded"
	C = A; A '/ 10000; A + 48; [VHGenergytext plus 4] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 1000; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGenergytext plus 5] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 100; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGenergytext plus 6] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGenergytext plus 7] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGenergytext plus 8] = A;
	A = [MgCharge]; ? A < 0 -> VHG energy omega;
	? A <= 999 -> VHG lithium bounded; A = 999;
    "VHG lithium bounded"
	C = A; A '/ 100; A + 48; [VHGenergytext plus 13] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGenergytext plus 14] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGenergytext plus 15] = A;
	-> VHG energy collector;
    "VHG energy omega"
	[VHGenergytext plus 13] = 79; [VHGenergytext plus 14] = 77; [VHGenergytext plus 15] = 71;
    "VHG energy collector"
	[VHGenergytext plus 17] = 79; [VHGenergytext plus 18] = 70; [VHGenergytext plus 19] = 70;
	A = [VHGcollector]; ? A = 0 -> VHG energy draw;
	[VHGenergytext plus 17] = 79; [VHGenergytext plus 18] = 78; [VHGenergytext plus 19] = 32;
    "VHG energy draw"
	( Normal play keeps these values on the explicit environment data page;
	  the source does not place a host-font resource row over the ship view. )
"VHG energy overlay done"
	end;

"VHG pod overlay"
	( Surface wayfinding: the original beacon remains in the 3D scene, while
	  this compact GUI line makes its return radius discoverable after a long
	  walk.  Range is the larger signed-axis delta in world units, matching
	  the square proximity guard used by R.  F/L/B/R points toward the pod
	  relative to the current camera yaw. )
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHG pod overlay done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG pod overlay done;
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG pod overlay done;
	A = [VHGNDdropx]; A - [VHGx]; [VHGpoddx] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG pod x positive; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG pod x positive"
	[VHGpodabsx] = A; [VHGpodrange] = A;
	A = [VHGNDdropz]; A - [VHGz]; [VHGpoddz] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHG pod z positive; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG pod z positive"
	[VHGpodabsz] = A;
	? A <= [VHGpodrange] -> VHG pod range ready; [VHGpodrange] = A;
    "VHG pod range ready"
	=> VHG pod direction;
	A = [VHGpodrange]; ? A <= 999999 -> VHG pod range bounded; A = 999999;
    "VHG pod range bounded"
	C = A; A '/ 100000; A + 48; [VHGpodtext plus 4] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10000; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGpodtext plus 5] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 1000; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGpodtext plus 6] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 100; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGpodtext plus 7] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGpodtext plus 8] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGpodtext plus 9] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcaptures]; ? A <= 9 -> VHG pod captures ready; A = 9;
    "VHG pod captures ready"
	A + 48; [VHGpodtext plus 19] = A;
	[VHGpodtext plus 37] = 0;
	( Capsule range remains available through the visible beacon, the control
	  card, and the explicit out-of-range return notice, not a permanent row. )
"VHG pod overlay done"
	end;

"VHG pod direction"
	( Quantize the target vector into eight world bearings.  Noctis yaw zero
	  faces +Z and increases toward -X; subtract the camera yaw to obtain a
	  stable camera-relative return hint without per-frame floating point. )
	[VHGpodbearing] = 0;
	A = [VHGpodabsx]; A '* 2; ? A < [VHGpodabsz] -> VHG pod bearing z;
	A = [VHGpodabsz]; A '* 2; ? A < [VHGpodabsx] -> VHG pod bearing x;
	A = [VHGpoddx]; ? A < 0 -> VHG pod diagonal west;
	A = [VHGpoddz]; ? A < 0 -> VHG pod diagonal southeast;
	[VHGpodbearing] = 315; -> VHG pod bearing ready;
    "VHG pod diagonal southeast"
	[VHGpodbearing] = 225; -> VHG pod bearing ready;
    "VHG pod diagonal west"
	A = [VHGpoddz]; ? A < 0 -> VHG pod diagonal northwest;
	[VHGpodbearing] = 45; -> VHG pod bearing ready;
    "VHG pod diagonal northwest"
	[VHGpodbearing] = 135; -> VHG pod bearing ready;
    "VHG pod bearing z"
	A = [VHGpoddz]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG pod bearing ready;
	[VHGpodbearing] = 180; -> VHG pod bearing ready;
    "VHG pod bearing x"
	A = [VHGpoddx]; ? A < 0 -> VHG pod bearing west;
	[VHGpodbearing] = 270; -> VHG pod bearing ready;
    "VHG pod bearing west"
	[VHGpodbearing] = 90;
    "VHG pod bearing ready"
	A = [VHGbeta]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHG pod view ready; A + 360;
    "VHG pod view ready"
	[VHGpodview] = A; A = [VHGpodbearing]; A - [VHGpodview]; A + 360; A '% 360;
	[VHGpoddelta] = A; [VHGpodtext plus 11] = 70;	( F )
	? A <= 45 -> VHG pod direction done; ? A >= 315 -> VHG pod direction done;
	? A < 135 -> VHG pod direction left; ? A < 225 -> VHG pod direction back;
	[VHGpodtext plus 11] = 82; -> VHG pod direction done;	( R )
    "VHG pod direction left"
	[VHGpodtext plus 11] = 76; -> VHG pod direction done;	( L )
    "VHG pod direction back"
	[VHGpodtext plus 11] = 66;	( B )
    "VHG pod direction done"
	end;

"VHG surface telemetry init"
	( surrounding() reports pp_gravity, pp_temp and pp_pressure. The sky
	  library already carries the exact source temperature/pressure outputs;
	  derive gravity from the retained p_ray at the original *38.26 scale. )
	A = [VHGplanet]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[FC0] = [E plus 0]; [FC1] = [E plus 1];
	[FI] = 38260; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGsurfgravm] = [FI];
	A = [VHGsurfgravm]; A '* 2000; A '/ 38260;
	? A > 0 -> VHG surface accel ready; A = 1;
    "VHG surface accel ready"
	[VHGsurfaccel] = A;
	[FS0] = [GRSKbasetemp]; => FLoadF32; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 10; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGsurftempt] = [FI];
	[FS0] = [GRSKbasepressure]; => FLoadF32; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1000; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGsurfpressm] = [FI];
	end;

"VHG surface motion reset"
	[VHGsurfair] = 0; [VHGsurfjet] = 0; [VHGsurfjumping] = 0; [VHGsurfvy] = 0;
	[VHGsurfstep] = 0; [VHGsurfshift] = 0; [VHGsurfbeta] = [VHGbeta];
	[VHGsurftiredr] = 0; [VHGsurflevelacc] = 0; [VHGNDplayerstep] = 0;
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGx]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGz]; => VHGND eye height;
	[VHGsurfground] = [VHGNDheight]; [VHGy] = [VHGsurfground];
	end;

"VHG surface telemetry update"
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface telemetry update done;
	A = [VHGy]; A / 4000; A + [VHGsurftempt]; [VHGsurftempnow] = A;
	( NOCTIS.CPP reseeds fast_random from secs/2 and adds two independent
	  fast_flandom draws scaled by eight to the tiredness-derived pulse. )
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A '/ 2; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU fast raw;
	A = [SUfval]; A '* 8; A '/ 32768; [VHGsurfpulsejitter] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU fast raw;
	A = [VHGsurftiredq]; A '* 118; A / 10000; A + 118;
	A + [VHGsurfpulsejitter]; C = A;
	A = [SUfval]; A '* 8; A '/ 32768; C - A; [VHGsurfpulse] = C;
	A = [VHGsurfgravm]; D = VHGsurfgravdisp; C = 4; => VHG surface smooth field;
	A = [VHGsurftempnow]; D = VHGsurftempdisp; C = 20; => VHG surface smooth field;
	A = [VHGsurfpressm]; D = VHGsurfpressdisp; C = 50; => VHG surface smooth field;
	A = [VHGsurfpulse]; D = VHGsurfpulsedisp; C = 100; => VHG surface smooth field;
	A = [VHGsurfgravdisp]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG surface gravity nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHG surface gravity nonnegative"
	? A <= 9999 -> VHG surface gravity ready; A = 9999;
    "VHG surface gravity ready"
	C = A; A '/ 1000; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 2] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 100; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 4] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 5] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 6] = A;
	A = [VHGsurftempdisp];
	[VHGsurfacetext plus 12] = 43; ? A >= 0 -> VHG surface temp positive;
	[VHGsurfacetext plus 12] = 45; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG surface temp positive"
	? A <= 9999 -> VHG surface temp ready; A = 9999;
    "VHG surface temp ready"
	C = A; A '/ 1000; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 13] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 100; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 14] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 15] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 17] = A;
	A = [VHGsurfpressdisp]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG surface pressure nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHG surface pressure nonnegative"
	? A <= 99999 -> VHG surface pressure ready; A = 99999;
    "VHG surface pressure ready"
	C = A; A '/ 10000; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 22] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 1000; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 23] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 100; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 25] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 26] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 27] = A;
	A = [VHGsurfpulsedisp];
	? A <= 999 -> VHG surface pulse ready; A = 999;
    "VHG surface pulse ready"
	C = A; A '/ 100; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 35] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 36] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGsurfacetext plus 37] = A;
	[VHGsurfacetext plus 38] = 0;
    "VHG surface telemetry update done"
	end;

"VHG surface smooth field"
	[VHGsurfsmoothden] = C;
	E = [D]; A - E; ? A = 0 -> VHG surface smooth field done;
	C = A; A / [VHGsurfsmoothden]; ? A != 0 -> VHG surface smooth apply;
	A = C; ? A > 0 -> VHG surface smooth positive; A = 0; A - 1; -> VHG surface smooth apply;
    "VHG surface smooth positive"
	A = 1;
    "VHG surface smooth apply"
	C = [D]; C + A; [D] = C;
    "VHG surface smooth field done"
	end;

"VHG surface telemetry overlay"
	( The authentic indexed surrounding() line is already in the rendered page.
	  Retain this hook so the GUI composition order and data-sheet formatter stay stable. )
	end;

"VHG FCS overlay"
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS overlay done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS overlay done;
	A = [VHGscreen]; ? A != 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG FCS overlay done;
	( MgStatus returns to zero between fine-approach batches.  Do not expose
	  that internal idle value as STANDBY while the selected body is still
	  closing: the player must wait for MgIpreached before pressing L again. )
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS star status;
	A = [MgIpreached]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS star status;
	[VHGfcsline] = VHGfcsapproach; -> VHG FCS draw;
    "VHG FCS star status"
	[VHGfcsline] = VHGfcsstandby; A = [MgStatus]; ? A = 1 -> VHG FCS pick charging;
	? A = 2 -> VHG FCS pick parking; ? A = 3 -> VHG FCS pick linking;
	? A = 4 -> VHG FCS pick driving; ? A = 5 -> VHG FCS pick ignition;
	-> VHG FCS selected;
    "VHG FCS pick charging"
	[VHGfcsline] = VHGfcscharging; -> VHG FCS selected;
    "VHG FCS pick parking"
