    "VHG FCS pick ignition"
	[VHGfcsline] = VHGfcsignition;
    "VHG FCS selected"
	A = [MgApreached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS draw;
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS draw;
	[VHGfcsline] = VHGfcscalibrated;
    "VHG FCS draw"
	( VH HUD FCS renders this state through the original 3D digit path. )
"VHG FCS overlay done"
	end;

"VHG body overlay"
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHG body overlay done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG body overlay done;
	A = [VHGscreen]; ? A != 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG body overlay done;
	( Compact one-row form: P02 T04 LAND ROCKY, or M04 T02 LAND DENSEAIR.
	  Bodies 10..79 and type 10 are real generated values, so both numeric
	  fields retain two decimal digits without overrunning the 320px view. )
	[VHGbodytext plus 0] = 80;
	[VHGbodytext plus 5] = 45; [VHGbodytext plus 6] = 45;
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkindunknown;
	A = [VHGplanet]; A + 1; C = A; A / 10; A + 48; [VHGbodytext plus 1] = A;
	C '% 10; C + 48; [VHGbodytext plus 2] = C;
	A = [VHGplanet]; ? A '< [nsnob] -> VHG body target valid;
	-> VHG body no land;
    "VHG body target valid"
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGplanet]; A = [E]; ? A < 0 -> VHG body prefix ready;
	[VHGbodytext plus 0] = 77;
    "VHG body prefix ready"
	E = nsptype; E + [VHGplanet]; A = [E]; C = A;
	A / 10; A + 48; [VHGbodytext plus 5] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGbodytext plus 6] = A;
	A = C; ? A = 0 -> VHG body kind 0; ? A = 1 -> VHG body kind 1;
	? A = 2 -> VHG body kind 2; ? A = 3 -> VHG body kind 3;
	? A = 4 -> VHG body kind 4; ? A = 5 -> VHG body kind 5;
	? A = 6 -> VHG body kind 6; ? A = 7 -> VHG body kind 7;
	? A = 8 -> VHG body kind 8; ? A = 9 -> VHG body kind 9;
	? A = 10 -> VHG body kind 10; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 0"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind0; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 1"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind1; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 2"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind2; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 3"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind3; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 4"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind4; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 5"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind5; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 6"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind6; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 7"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind7; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 8"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind8; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 9"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind9; -> VHG body kind ready;
    "VHG body kind 10"
	[VHGbodykind] = VHGkind10;
    "VHG body kind ready"
	A = C;
	? A = 0 -> VHG body no land; ? A = 6 -> VHG body no land; ? A = 9 -> VHG body no land;
	? A '>= 10 -> VHG body no land;
	[VHGbodytext plus 8] = 76; [VHGbodytext plus 9] = 65;
	[VHGbodytext plus 10] = 78; [VHGbodytext plus 11] = 68; -> VHG body draw;
    "VHG body no land"
	[VHGbodytext plus 8] = 78; [VHGbodytext plus 9] = 79;
	[VHGbodytext plus 10] = 45; [VHGbodytext plus 11] = 76;
    "VHG body draw"
	( Copy at most eight classification characters, padding shorter labels.
	  A single text transaction avoids the host service's shared-cursor wrap. )
	[VHGkindi] = 0;
    "VHG body kind copy"
	D = VHGbodytext; D + 13; D + [VHGkindi]; [D] = 32;
	E = [VHGbodykind]; E + [VHGkindi]; A = [E]; ? A = 0 -> VHG body kind next;
	[D] = A;
    "VHG body kind next"
	[VHGkindi]+; A = [VHGkindi]; ? A '< 8 -> VHG body kind copy;
	( Reassert the terminator so the mutable row cannot spill into the next
	  declaration even when the classification occupies all eight slots. )
	[VHGbodytext plus 21] = 0;
	( Body identity belongs to the physical planetary station and I data page. )
    "VHG body overlay done"
	end;

"VHG landing overlay"
	A = [VHGlandingselect]; ? A = 0 -> VHG landing overlay done;
	A = [VHGlandingview]; ? A = 0 -> VHG physical landing overlay;
	( The original selector lives on the Stardrifter planetary map.  Present
	  the same state as a stable logical page so it stays legible after resize. )
	[Rectangle Display Alignment] = 320; [Rectangle Bounds] = vector VHGUIregion;
	[Rectangle Gradients] = vector Standard Black Gradients;
	[Rectangle Effect] = service FX Raw; [Rectangle Target Layer] = VHGUIframe; => Rectangle;
	=> VHG landing map page;
	C = [VHGlandinglon]; A = C; A '/ 100; A + 48; [VHGlandingcoords plus 10] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGlandingcoords plus 11] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGlandingcoords plus 12] = A;
	C = [VHGlandinglat]; A = C; A '/ 100; A + 48; [VHGlandingcoords plus 24] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGlandingcoords plus 25] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGlandingcoords plus 26] = A;
	[Text Display Width] = 320; [Text Display Height] = 200;
	[Text Region] = vector VHGUIregion; [Text Window] = NULL;
	[Text Word Wrap] = OFF; [Text Ghost Mode] = OFF; [Text Effect] = service FX Raw;
	[Ink] = 80FF80h; [Text X] = 52; [Text Y] = 8;
	[String] = VHGlandingtitle; => VHG text both;
	[Ink] = FFFFFFh; [Text X] = 46; [Text Y] = 142;
	[String] = VHGlandingcoords; => VHG text both;
	[Text X] = 30; [Text Y] = 164; [String] = VHGlandinghint; => VHG text both;
	[Text X] = 126; [Text Y] = 182; [String] = VHGlandingcancel; => VHG text both;
	-> VHG landing overlay done;
    "VHG physical landing overlay"
	( The physical station keeps its generated map unobstructed. The original
	  status("LQ %03d:%03d") path is rendered by VH HUD FCS above. )
    "VHG landing overlay done"
	end;

"VHG landing map page"
	( Enlarged nearest-neighbour view of the same original 48x32 map. )
	[VHGlandmapy] = 0;
    "VHG landing map page row"
	[VHGlandmapx] = 0;
    "VHG landing map page pixel"
	A = [VHGlandmapy]; A + 9; A '* 256; A + [VHGlandmapx]; A + 14; A + RPSM; A + nw;
	A = [A]; A & 255; A + pal; [VHGlandmapink] = [A];
	[VHGlandmapsy] = 0;
    "VHG landing map page scale row"
	A = [VHGlandmapy]; A '* 3; A + [VHGlandmapsy]; A + 34; A '* 320;
	C = [VHGlandmapx]; C '* 3; C + 88; A + C; A + VHGUIframe; [VHGlandmapdst] = A;
	C = [VHGlandmapink]; D = [VHGlandmapdst]; [D] = C; [D plus 1] = C; [D plus 2] = C;
	[VHGlandmapsy]+; A = [VHGlandmapsy]; ? A < 3 -> VHG landing map page scale row;
	[VHGlandmapx]+; A = [VHGlandmapx]; ? A < 48 -> VHG landing map page pixel;
	[VHGlandmapy]+; A = [VHGlandmapy]; ? A < 32 -> VHG landing map page row;
	end;

"VHG landing status format"
	C = [VHGlandinglon]; A = C; A '/ 100; A + 48; [VHGlandingstatus plus 3] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGlandingstatus plus 4] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGlandingstatus plus 5] = A;
	C = [VHGlandinglat]; A = C; A '/ 100; A + 48; [VHGlandingstatus plus 7] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGlandingstatus plus 8] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGlandingstatus plus 9] = A;
	end;

"VHG output overlay"
	( The second GOES wall face draws its retained seven-row history in 3D. )
    "VHG output overlay done"
	end;

"VHG console overlay"
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A = 0 -> VHG console overlay done;
	A = [VHGconsoleview]; ? A = 0 -> VHG physical console overlay;
	( Give the keyboard editor its own stable logical page.  The earlier
	  bottom-row-only prompt could be lost when the live 3D page was republished,
	  leaving commands functional but invisible. )
	[Rectangle Display Alignment] = 320; [Rectangle Bounds] = vector VHGUIregion;
	[Rectangle Gradients] = vector Standard Black Gradients;
	[Rectangle Effect] = service FX Raw; [Rectangle Target Layer] = VHGUIframe; => Rectangle;
	[Text X] = 12; [Text Y] = 14;
	[String] = VHGconsoletitle; [Ink] = 80FF80h; [Text Effect] = service FX Raw;
	[Text Display Width] = 320; [Text Display Height] = 200;
	[Text Region] = vector VHGUIregion; [Text Window] = NULL;
	[Text Word Wrap] = OFF; [Text Ghost Mode] = OFF;
	=> VHG text both;
	=> VH GOES output window;
	[VHGconsoleouti] = 0; [VHGconsoleouty] = 30;
    "VHG console output row"
	A = [VHGconsoleouti]; A '* 22; A + vhpoutdisplay; [String] = A;
	[Text X] = 12; [Text Y] = [VHGconsoleouty]; [Ink] = 00FFFFh;
	=> VHG text both;
	[VHGconsoleouty] + 12; [VHGconsoleouti]+; A = [VHGconsoleouti];
	? A < 7 -> VHG console output row;
	[Text X] = 12; [Text Y] = 126;
	[String] = VHGgoesprompt; [Ink] = 80FF80h; [Text Effect] = service FX Raw;
	=> VHG text both;
	[Text X] = 60; [Text Y] = 126; [String] = vhptext; [String] + 42;
	=> VHG text both;
	-> VHG console overlay done;
    "VHG physical console overlay"
	( Physical focus writes directly into vhptext; VHP GOES text maps that
	  source-format buffer onto the first wall face every rendered frame. )
    "VHG console overlay done"
	end;

"VHG notice overlay"
	A = [VHGnoticeframes]; ? A = 0 -> VHG notice overlay done;
	A = [VHGlandingselect]; ? A = 0 -> VHG notice overlay draw;
	A = [VHGlandingview]; ? A = 0 -> VHG notice overlay done;
    "VHG notice overlay draw"
	[String] = [VHGnoticeptr]; [Ink] = 80FF80h;
	[Text Effect] = service FX Raw;
	[Text X] = 12; [Text Y] = 176;
	[Text Display Width] = 320; [Text Display Height] = 200;
	[Text Region] = vector VHGUIregion;
	[Text Window] = NULL; [Text Word Wrap] = OFF; [Text Ghost Mode] = OFF;
	=> VHG text both;
	[VHGnoticeframes]-;
"VHG notice overlay done"
	end;

"VHG info slide advance"
	A = [VHGinfo]; ? A = 0 -> VHG info slide advance done;
	A = [VHGinfoscroll]; A + [VHGinfodelta];
	? A <= 100 -> VHG info slide upper ready; A = 100; [VHGinfodelta] = 0;
    "VHG info slide upper ready"
	? A >= 0 -> VHG info slide lower ready;
	A = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0; [VHGinfodelta] = 0;
    "VHG info slide lower ready"
	[VHGinfoscroll] = A;
    "VHG info slide advance done"
	end;

"VHG info source fill"
	[VHGinfofilly] = [VHGinfodrawy];
    "VHG info source fill row"
	[VHGinfofillx] = [VHGinfodrawx];
    "VHG info source fill pixel"
	A = [VHGinfofilly]; A '* 320; A + [VHGinfofillx]; [SPoff] = A;
	[SPval] = [VHGinfofillcolour]; [SPreg] = RGADP; => SP put;
	[VHGinfofillx]+; A = [VHGinfofillx]; ? A '< [VHGinfofillend] -> VHG info source fill pixel;
	[VHGinfofilly]+; A = [VHGinfofilly]; ? A '< [VHGinfofillbottom] -> VHG info source fill row;
	end;

"VHG info source draw line"
	( wrouthud reveals at most c=(datasheetscroll/4)-1 characters. Copy that
	  bounded prefix, then use the same indexed 3x5 HUD glyph path. )
	[VHGinfodrawi] = 0;
    "VHG info source draw copy"
	A = [VHGinfodrawi]; ? A >= [VHGinfomax] -> VHG info source draw terminate;
	? A >= 24 -> VHG info source draw terminate;
	E = [VHGinfodrawsrc]; E + A; C = [E]; ? C = 0 -> VHG info source draw terminate;
	D = VHGinfodraw; D + A; [D] = C; [VHGinfodrawi]+;
	-> VHG info source draw copy;
    "VHG info source draw terminate"
	D = VHGinfodraw; D + [VHGinfodrawi]; [D] = 0;
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHG info source draw done;
	[VHGNDhudsource] = VHGinfodraw; [VHGNDhudx] = [VHGinfodrawx];
	[VHGNDhudy] = [VHGinfodrawy]; => VHGND HUD draw string;
    "VHG info source draw done"
	end;

"VHG source info overlay"
	A = [VHGinfo]; ? A = 0 -> VHG source info overlay done;
	A = [VHGinfoscroll]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG source info overlay done;
	A + 1; [VHGinfowidth] = A; A + 11; [VHGinfofillend] = A;
	[VHGinfodrawx] = 11; [VHGinfodrawy] = 85;
	[VHGinfofillbottom] = 94; [VHGinfofillcolour] = 72; => VHG info source fill;
	[VHGinfodrawy] = 95; [VHGinfofillbottom] = 135;
	[VHGinfofillcolour] = 112; => VHG info source fill;
	A = [VHGinfoscroll]; A '/ 4; A - 1; ? A >= 0 -> VHG source info max ready; A = 0;
    "VHG source info max ready"
	[VHGinfomax] = A; => VHG info format common;
	A = [VHGinfo]; ? A = 1 -> VHG source info remote;
	? A = 2 -> VHG source info local; -> VHG source info environment;
    "VHG source info remote"
	A = [MgAptgt]; ? A = 0 -> VHG source info no remote;
	? A != 1 -> VHG source info direct remote;
	[VHGinfonamesrc] = vhcatstarlabel; => VHG info name copy;
	=> VHG info format remote source;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfoname; [VHGinfodrawx] = 14; [VHGinfodrawy] = 87; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfomasslabel; [VHGinfodrawy] = 97; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinforemotevalues; [VHGinfodrawy] = 103; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfocentidyams; [VHGinfodrawx] = 70; [VHGinfodrawy] = 109; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfosurfacetemp; [VHGinfodrawx] = 14; [VHGinfodrawy] = 116; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfostarvalues; [VHGinfodrawy] = 122; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfomajor; [VHGinfodrawy] = 129; => VHG info source draw line;
	-> VHG source info overlay done;
    "VHG source info no remote"
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfonoremote; [VHGinfodrawx] = 14; [VHGinfodrawy] = 87;
	=> VHG info source draw line; -> VHG source info overlay done;
    "VHG source info direct remote"
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfodirect; [VHGinfodrawx] = 14; [VHGinfodrawy] = 87;
	=> VHG info source draw line;
	-> VHG source info overlay done;
    "VHG source info local"
	A = [VHGlocaltarget]; ? A = 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG source info no local;
	? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG source info no local;
	=> VHG info format local source; [VHGinfonamesrc] = vhcatbodylabel; => VHG info name copy;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfoname; [VHGinfodrawx] = 14; [VHGinfodrawy] = 87; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinforotationlabel; [VHGinfodrawy] = 97; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinforotationvalues; [VHGinfodrawy] = 103; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinforevolutionlabel; [VHGinfodrawy] = 110; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinforevolutionvalues; [VHGinfodrawy] = 116; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfolocaltypeline; [VHGinfodrawy] = 123; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfolocalradiusline; [VHGinfodrawy] = 129; => VHG info source draw line;
	-> VHG source info overlay done;
    "VHG source info no local"
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfonolocal; [VHGinfodrawx] = 14; [VHGinfodrawy] = 87;
	=> VHG info source draw line;
	-> VHG source info overlay done;
    "VHG source info environment"
	=> VHG info format environment source;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfotitle3; [VHGinfodrawx] = 14; [VHGinfodrawy] = 87; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfoenvtempk; [VHGinfodrawy] = 97; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfoenvtempc; [VHGinfodrawy] = 103; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfoenvtempf; [VHGinfodrawy] = 109; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfoenvions; [VHGinfodrawy] = 119; => VHG info source draw line;
	[VHGinfodrawsrc] = VHGinfoenvradiation; [VHGinfodrawy] = 126; => VHG info source draw line;
    "VHG source info overlay done"
	end;

"VHG info overlay"
	( Source datasheets are already composed into the indexed frame before
	  palette expansion. Keep this post-expansion hook intentionally empty. )
	end;

"VHG onboard row begin"
	[VHGonrowpos] = 0; E = [VHGonrowdst]; [E] = 0;
	end;

"VHG onboard row finish"
	E = [VHGonrowdst]; E + [VHGonrowpos]; [E] = 0;
	end;

"VHG onboard row append text"
	[VHGonrowi] = 0;
    "VHG onboard row append text loop"
	A = [VHGonrowpos]; ? A >= 108 -> VHG onboard row append text done;
	E = [VHGonrowsrc]; E + [VHGonrowi]; A = [E];
	? A = 0 -> VHG onboard row append text done;
	D = [VHGonrowdst]; D + [VHGonrowpos]; [D] = A;
	[VHGonrowpos]+; [VHGonrowi]+; -> VHG onboard row append text loop;
    "VHG onboard row append text done"
	end;

"VHG onboard row append char"
	A = [VHGonrowpos]; ? A >= 108 -> VHG onboard row append char done;
	E = [VHGonrowdst]; E + A; [E] = [VHGonrowchar]; [VHGonrowpos]+;
    "VHG onboard row append char done"
	end;

"VHG onboard row append space"
	[VHGonrowchar] = 32; => VHG onboard row append char;
	end;

"VHG onboard row append number"
	( alphavalue() emits ordinary unpadded decimal text for these integer FCS
	  fields. Keep the sign path for a depleted lithium value. )
	A = [VHGonrowvalue]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG onboard row number magnitude ready;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 45; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = 0; A - [VHGonrowvalue]; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
    "VHG onboard row number magnitude ready"
	[VHGonrowden] = 1000000000; [VHGonrowstarted] = 0;
    "VHG onboard row number loop"
	A = [VHGonrowvalue]; C = A; A '/ [VHGonrowden]; [VHGonrowdigit] = A;
	A = C; A '% [VHGonrowden]; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
	A = [VHGonrowdigit]; ? A != 0 -> VHG onboard row number emit;
	A = [VHGonrowstarted]; ? A != 0 -> VHG onboard row number emit;
	A = [VHGonrowden]; ? A = 1 -> VHG onboard row number emit;
	-> VHG onboard row number next;
    "VHG onboard row number emit"
	[VHGonrowstarted] = 1; A = [VHGonrowdigit]; A + 48;
	[VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
    "VHG onboard row number next"
	A = [VHGonrowden]; A '/ 10; [VHGonrowden] = A;
	? A > 0 -> VHG onboard row number loop;
	end;

"VHG onboard row append triad"
	( formatTriad(): always three decimal positions, including leading zeroes. )
	A = [VHGonrowvalue]; A '/ 100; A '% 10; A + 48;
	[VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = [VHGonrowvalue]; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48;
	[VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = [VHGonrowvalue]; A '% 10; A + 48;
	[VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	end;

"VHG onboard select ordinal"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGfcsunknown; A = [VHGonrowvalue];
	? A = 1 -> VHG onboard ordinal 1; ? A = 2 -> VHG onboard ordinal 2;
	? A = 3 -> VHG onboard ordinal 3; ? A = 4 -> VHG onboard ordinal 4;
	? A = 5 -> VHG onboard ordinal 5; ? A = 6 -> VHG onboard ordinal 6;
	? A = 7 -> VHG onboard ordinal 7; ? A = 8 -> VHG onboard ordinal 8;
	? A = 9 -> VHG onboard ordinal 9; ? A = 10 -> VHG onboard ordinal 10;
	? A = 11 -> VHG onboard ordinal 11; ? A = 12 -> VHG onboard ordinal 12;
	? A = 13 -> VHG onboard ordinal 13; ? A = 14 -> VHG onboard ordinal 14;
	? A = 15 -> VHG onboard ordinal 15; ? A = 16 -> VHG onboard ordinal 16;
	? A = 17 -> VHG onboard ordinal 17; ? A = 18 -> VHG onboard ordinal 18;
	? A = 19 -> VHG onboard ordinal 19; ? A = 20 -> VHG onboard ordinal 20;
	end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 1"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord1; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 2"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord2; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 3"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord3; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 4"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord4; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 5"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord5; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 6"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord6; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 7"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord7; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 8"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord8; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 9"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord9; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 10"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord10; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 11"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord11; end;
    "VHG onboard ordinal 12"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGord12; end;

    "VHG onboard ordinal 17"

    "VHG onboard ordinal 18"

    "VHG onboard ordinal 19"

    "VHG onboard ordinal 20"

"VHG onboard emergency information"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:1620-1633. Only gburst == -1 emits the source's explicit
	  no-emergency/help-not-sent report; zero is a cleared blank status. )
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGonboardrow0; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGonboardrow1; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGonboardrow2; => VHG onboard row begin;
	A = [VHGgburst]; ? A != 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG onboard emergency information done;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGonboardrow0; [VHGonrowsrc] = VHGemerquiet;
	=> VHG onboard row append text; => VHG onboard row finish;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGonboardrow1; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGemernotsent; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row finish;
    "VHG onboard emergency information done"
	end;

"VHG onboard cartography information"
	( NOCTIS.CPP devices() cartography page: EPOC/triads, rounded Parsis
	  coordinates, and navigation heading/pitch. )
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGonboardrow0; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGcartepoc; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; A = [VHGutcsecs]; A '/ 1000000000; A + 6011;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = A; => VHG onboard row append number;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; [VHGonrowsrc] = VHGcarttriads;
	=> VHG onboard row append text; => VHG onboard row append space;
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A '/ 1000000; A '% 1000; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
	=> VHG onboard row append triad; [VHGonrowchar] = 44; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A '/ 1000; A '% 1000; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
	=> VHG onboard row append triad; [VHGonrowchar] = 44; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A '% 1000; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
	=> VHG onboard row append triad; => VHG onboard row finish;

	=> VHG fpu clean;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGonboardrow1; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGcartparsis; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row append space;

"VHG onboard gaze"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:2649-2685 repeatedly advances the camera by half of |z|
	  until its centre ray reaches the z=0 computer plane. Keep that algorithm
	  and its exact control/command bounds, using integer camera coordinates. )
	[VHGgazecontrol] = 0; [VHGgazecommand] = 0; [VHGgazeinfo] = 0;
	A = [VHGdevaccess]; ? A != 0 -> VHG onboard gaze done;
    "VHG onboard gaze active"
	[VHGgazex] = [VHGx]; [VHGgazey] = [VHGy]; [VHGgazez] = [VHGz];
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGz];
	[VHValpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGbeta]; => VH set view;
	[VHGgazeiterations] = 0;
    "VHG onboard gaze loop"
	A = [VHGgazez]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG onboard gaze absz ready; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG onboard gaze absz ready"
	[VHGgazeabsz] = A;
	A = [VHGgazex]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG onboard gaze absx ready; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHG onboard gaze absx ready"
	[VHGgazeabsx] = A; A = [VHGgazeabsz]; ? A <= 25 -> VHG onboard gaze hit;
	A = [VHGgazeabsx]; ? A >= 3000 -> VHG onboard gaze done;
	A = [VHGgazeabsz]; A / 2; [VHGgazestep] = A; => VHG onboard gaze forward;
	[VHGgazeiterations]+; A = [VHGgazeiterations]; ? A < 16 -> VHG onboard gaze loop;
	-> VHG onboard gaze done;
    "VHG onboard gaze hit"
	A = [VHGgazex]; ? A >= 0FFFFFAD8h -> VHG onboard gaze command; ( -1320 )
	? A <= 0FFFFF808h -> VHG onboard gaze done; ( -2040 )
	A = [VHGgazey]; A + 25; A / 50; A + 3;
	? A >= 1 -> VHG onboard gaze control low; A = 1;
    "VHG onboard gaze control low"
	? A <= 4 -> VHG onboard gaze control ready; A = 4;
    "VHG onboard gaze control ready"
	[VHGgazecontrol] = A; -> VHG onboard gaze done;
    "VHG onboard gaze command"
	( The source boundary is cam_x >= -1320. )
	A = [VHGgazex]; ? A < 0FFFFFAD8h -> VHG onboard gaze done;
	A = [VHGgazey]; ? A >= 0FFFFFFCEh -> VHG onboard gaze information;
	A = [VHGgazex]; ? A >= 2040 -> VHG onboard gaze done;
	A + 1320; A / 810; A + 1;
	? A >= 1 -> VHG onboard gaze command low; A = 1;
    "VHG onboard gaze command low"
	? A <= 4 -> VHG onboard gaze command ready; A = 4;
    "VHG onboard gaze command ready"
	[VHGgazecommand] = A;
	-> VHG onboard gaze done;
    "VHG onboard gaze information"
	[VHGgazeinfo] = 1;
    "VHG onboard gaze done"
	end;

"VHG onboard gaze forward"

	[PGFi] = FSTSB; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = FSTCA; => PGF mul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGgazex]; A - [FI]; [VHGgazex] = A;
	[PGFi] = FSTCB; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = FSTCA; => PGF mul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGgazez]; A + [FI]; [VHGgazez] = A;
	[PGFi] = FSTSA; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF mul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGgazey]; A + [FI]; [VHGgazey] = A;
	end;

    "VHG device cartography overlay"
	[VHGinfoy] = 14; [VHGinfoline] = VHGcarttitle; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGcartstar; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGcartplanet; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGcartnext; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGcartmanual; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoy] = 176; [VHGinfoline] = VHGdevbackhint; => VHG info draw line;
	-> VHG device overlay done;
    "VHG device emergency overlay"
	[VHGinfoy] = 14; [VHGinfoline] = VHGemergencytitle; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGemergencyreset; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGemergencyhelp; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGemergencylithiumoff; A = [VHGcollector]; ? A = 0 -> VHG device emergency lithium selected;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGemergencylithiumon;
    "VHG device emergency lithium selected"
	=> VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGemergencyclear; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoy] = 176; [VHGinfoline] = VHGdevbackhint; => VHG info draw line;
	-> VHG device overlay done;
    "VHG device target browser overlay"
	=> VHG browse format rows;
	[VHGinfoy] = 14; [VHGinfoline] = VHGbrowsetitle; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGbrowserowx; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGbrowserowy; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGbrowserowz; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGbrowseprev; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGbrowsenext; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGbrowseselect; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoline] = VHGbrowseback; => VHG info draw line;
	[VHGinfoy] = 176; [VHGinfoline] = VHGdevbackhint; => VHG info draw line;
    "VHG device overlay done"
	end;

"VHG FCS menu overlay"

"VHG browse format rows"
	[VHGbrowserowaxis] = 88; [VHGbrowserowdst] = VHGbrowserowx;
	[VHGbrowserowvalue] = [VHGbrowsex]; => VHG browse format row;
	[VHGbrowserowaxis] = 89; [VHGbrowserowdst] = VHGbrowserowy;
	[VHGbrowserowvalue] = [VHGbrowsey]; => VHG browse format row;
	[VHGbrowserowaxis] = 90; [VHGbrowserowdst] = VHGbrowserowz;
	[VHGbrowserowvalue] = [VHGbrowsez]; => VHG browse format row;
	end;

"VHG browse format row"
	E = [VHGbrowserowdst]; [E] = [VHGbrowserowaxis]; [E plus 1] = 32; [E plus 2] = 43;
	A = [VHGbrowserowvalue]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG browse magnitude ready;
	E = [VHGbrowserowdst]; [E plus 2] = 45; A = 0; A - [VHGbrowserowvalue];
    "VHG browse magnitude ready"
	C = A; [VHGbrowserowi] = 11;
    "VHG browse digit loop"
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; E = [VHGbrowserowdst]; E + [VHGbrowserowi]; [E] = A;
	C '/ 10; [VHGbrowserowi]-; A = [VHGbrowserowi]; ? A >= 3 -> VHG browse digit loop;
	E = [VHGbrowserowdst]; E + 12; [E] = 0;
	end;

"VHG info append fixed"
	( Append a signed scaled integer with an exact number of decimal places to
	  the shared bounded row builder. This supplies sprintf-like source HUD
	  fields without routing indexed text through the GUI font. )
	A = [VHGinfofixedvalue]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG info fixed magnitude ready;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 45; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = 0; A - [VHGinfofixedvalue]; [VHGinfofixedvalue] = A;
    "VHG info fixed magnitude ready"
	[VHGinfofixedscale] = 1; [VHGinfocount] = [VHGinfofixeddigits];
    "VHG info fixed scale loop"
	A = [VHGinfocount]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG info fixed scale ready;
	A = [VHGinfofixedscale]; A '* 10; [VHGinfofixedscale] = A;
	[VHGinfocount]-; -> VHG info fixed scale loop;
    "VHG info fixed scale ready"
	A = [VHGinfofixedvalue]; A '/ [VHGinfofixedscale]; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
	=> VHG onboard row append number;
	A = [VHGinfofixeddigits]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG info fixed done;
	[VHGonrowchar] = 46; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = [VHGinfofixedvalue]; A '% [VHGinfofixedscale]; [VHGinfofixedfrac] = A;
	A = [VHGinfofixedscale]; A '/ 10; [VHGinfofixedden] = A;
    "VHG info fixed fraction loop"
	A = [VHGinfofixedfrac]; C = A; A '/ [VHGinfofixedden]; A + 48;
	[VHGonrowchar] = A; => VHG onboard row append char;
	A = C; A '% [VHGinfofixedden]; [VHGinfofixedfrac] = A;
	[VHGinfofixeddigits]-; A = [VHGinfofixeddigits]; ? A <= 0 -> VHG info fixed done;
	A = [VHGinfofixedden]; A '/ 10; [VHGinfofixedden] = A;
	-> VHG info fixed fraction loop;
    "VHG info fixed done"
	end;

"VHG info format remote source"
	( NOCTIS.CPP remote target data: 0.001*pi*ray^3 corrected by star
	  class, including the class-8/9 fast-random mass distortion. )
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	[FS0] = [VHTRay]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGinforay0] = [FA0]; [VHGinforay1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinforay0]; [FB1] = [VHGinforay1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGinforay0]; [FB1] = [VHGinforay1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = D2F1A9FCh; [FB1] = 3F50624Dh; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 54442D18h; [FB1] = 400921FBh; => FMul;
	A = [VHTclass]; A < 1; E = VHGstarmasscorr; E + A;
	[FB0] = [E plus 0]; [FB1] = [E plus 1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinfomass0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfomass1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHTclass]; ? A = 8 -> VHG info remote mass random;
	? A != 9 -> VHG info remote mass ready;
    "VHG info remote mass random"
	A = [VHTtx]; A % 32000; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 5; => SU fast raw; [VHGinfomasscase] = [SUfval];
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 5; [VHGinfomassop] = 1; A = [VHGinfomasscase];
	? A = 0 -> VHG info remote mass factor;
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 1; ? A = 1 -> VHG info remote mass factor;
	[VHGinfomassop] = 0; ? A = 2 -> VHG info remote mass factor;
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 20; ? A = 3 -> VHG info remote mass factor;
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 50; ? A = 4 -> VHG info remote mass factor;
	-> VHG info remote mass ready;
    "VHG info remote mass factor"
	[SUfmask] = 7FFFh; => SU fast raw; [FI] = [SUfval]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 7EDA0BE1h; [FB1] = 3F00000Eh; => FMul;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGinfomassfactor]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfomass0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfomass1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1];
	A = [VHGinfomassop]; ? A = 0 -> VHG info remote mass multiply;
	=> FQuo; -> VHG info remote mass store;
    "VHG info remote mass multiply"
	=> FMul;
    "VHG info remote mass store"
	[VHGinfomass0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfomass1] = [FA1];
    "VHG info remote mass ready"
	( Keep six decimal mass digits where they fit the source's eight-column
	  field, reducing only enough places for larger values. )
	[FA0] = [VHGinfomass0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfomass1];
	[FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 1000000; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1];
	=> FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGinfomassscaled] = [FI];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforay0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforay1];
	[FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 10000; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1];
	=> FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGinforayscaled] = [FI];
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinforemotevalues; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGinfofixedvalue] = [VHGinfomassscaled]; [VHGinfofixeddigits] = 6;
	A = [VHGinfomassscaled]; ? A < 10000000 -> VHG info remote mass text;
	A '/ 10; [VHGinfofixedvalue] = A; [VHGinfofixeddigits] = 5;
	A = [VHGinfomassscaled]; ? A < 100000000 -> VHG info remote mass text;
	A '/ 100; [VHGinfofixedvalue] = A; [VHGinfofixeddigits] = 4;
	A = [VHGinfomassscaled]; ? A < 1000000000 -> VHG info remote mass text;
	A '/ 1000; [VHGinfofixedvalue] = A; [VHGinfofixeddigits] = 3;
    "VHG info remote mass text"
	=> VHG info append fixed; [VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfobalm;
	=> VHG onboard row append text;
    "VHG info remote radius pad"
	A = [VHGonrowpos]; ? A >= 16 -> VHG info remote radius field;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; -> VHG info remote radius pad;
    "VHG info remote radius field"
	A = [VHGinforayscaled]; ? A >= 100000 -> VHG info remote radius one pad;
	=> VHG onboard row append space;
    "VHG info remote radius one pad"
	A = [VHGinforayscaled]; ? A >= 1000000 -> VHG info remote radius text;
	=> VHG onboard row append space;
    "VHG info remote radius text"
	[VHGinfofixedvalue] = [VHGinforayscaled]; [VHGinfofixeddigits] = 4;
	=> VHG info append fixed; => VHG onboard row finish;
	( Surface temperature is derived from that same corrected primary mass. )
	[FA0] = 0ED80A18h; [FA1] = 3F03EC46h;
	[FB0] = [VHGinforay0]; [FB1] = [VHGinforay1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfomass0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfomass1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FQuo;
	A = [VHTclass]; ? A != 6 -> VHG info remote temperature ready;
	[FB0] = 01A36E2Fh; [FB1] = 3F6205BCh; => FMul;
    "VHG info remote temperature ready"
	[VHGinfotemp0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfotemp1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = 66666666h; [FB1] = 40711266h; => FAdd; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGinfotempk] = [FI];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfotemp0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfotemp1]; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGinfotempc] = [FI];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfotemp0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfotemp1];
	[FB0] = CCCCCCCDh; [FB1] = 3FFCCCCCh; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 00000000h; [FB1] = 40400000h; => FAdd; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGinfotempf] = [FI];
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinfostarvalues; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGinfotempk]; => VHG onboard row append number;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfokelvin; => VHG onboard row append text;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGinfotempc]; => VHG onboard row append number;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfocelsius; => VHG onboard row append text;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGinfotempf]; => VHG onboard row append number;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfofahrenheit; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row finish; => VHG fpu clean;
	end;

"VHG info format local source"
	( Recreate the source rotation period stream for the selected local target. )
	[VHGNDplanet] = [VHGlocaltarget]; => VHGND rotation seed;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDseedval0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDseedval1];
	=> GeoAdd4112ChopLive; A = [FI]; => SU fast srand;
	C = 50; => SU rfr; A = C; A + 1; A '* 10; [VHGNDperiod] = A;
	C = 25; => SU rfr; A = C; A '* 10; A + [VHGNDperiod]; [VHGNDperiod] = A;
	C = 250; => SU rfr; A = C; A + [VHGNDperiod]; A + 41;
	[VHGNDperiod] = A; A '* 360; [VHGinfolocalperiod] = A;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinforotationvalues; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfotriads; => VHG onboard row append text;
	A = [VHGinfolocalperiod]; A '/ 1000000; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
	=> VHG onboard row append triad; [VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfocolon;
	=> VHG onboard row append text;
	A = [VHGinfolocalperiod]; A '/ 1000; A '% 1000; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
	=> VHG onboard row append triad; [VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfocolon;
	=> VHG onboard row append text;
	A = [VHGinfolocalperiod]; A '% 1000; [VHGonrowvalue] = A;
	=> VHG onboard row append triad; => VHG onboard row finish;
	( rtp(): 360 / sqrt(primary_mass / orbital_radius^2), narrowed
	  through its float return before the source EPOC/triad decomposition. )
	=> VHG fpu clean; A = [VHGlocaltarget]; A < 1; E = nsporbray; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGlocaltarget]; A = [E];
	? A >= 0 -> VHG info local revolution moon mass;
	[FS0] = [VHTRay]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGinfomass0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfomass1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfomass0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfomass1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfomass0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfomass1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 54442D18h; [FB1] = 400921FBh; => FMul;
	[FB0] = E826D695h; [FB1] = 3E112E0Bh; => FMul;
	-> VHG info local revolution mass ready;
    "VHG info local revolution moon mass"
	A < 1; E = nspray; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	[VHGinfomass0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfomass1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfomass0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfomass1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfomass0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfomass1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 54442D18h; [FB1] = 400921FBh; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 7CFA26A2h; [FB1] = 3F071194h; => FMul;
    "VHG info local revolution mass ready"
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FQuo; => FSqrt;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = 00000000h; [FA1] = 40768000h;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FQuo;
	=> F32Narrow; => FLoadF32; [VHGinforev0] = [FA0]; [VHGinforev1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = E826D695h; [FB1] = 3E112E0Bh; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	[VHGinforevepoc] = [FI];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforev0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforev1];
	[FB0] = A0B5ED8Dh; [FB1] = 3EB0C6F7h; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; A '% 1000; [VHGinforevtriad1] = A;
	[FA0] = [VHGinforev0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforev1];
	[FB0] = D2F1A9FCh; [FB1] = 3F50624Dh; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; A '% 1000; [VHGinforevtriad2] = A;
	[FA0] = [VHGinforev0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforev1]; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; A '% 1000; [VHGinforevtriad3] = A;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinforevolutionvalues; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGinforevepoc]; => VHG onboard row append number;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfoepocs; => VHG onboard row append text;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGinforevtriad1]; => VHG onboard row append triad;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfocolon; => VHG onboard row append text;
	A = [VHGinforevepoc]; ? A >= 2047 -> VHG info local revolution two unknown;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGinforevtriad2]; => VHG onboard row append triad;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfocolon; => VHG onboard row append text;
	A = [VHGinforevepoc]; ? A >= 2 -> VHG info local revolution one unknown;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGinforevtriad3]; => VHG onboard row append triad;
	-> VHG info local revolution text done;
    "VHG info local revolution two unknown"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfoqtriad; => VHG onboard row append text;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfocolon; => VHG onboard row append text;
    "VHG info local revolution one unknown"
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfoqtriad; => VHG onboard row append text;
    "VHG info local revolution text done"
	=> VHG onboard row finish;
	( Type and radius share the source's final two rows. )
	=> VHG info select local type;
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinfolocaltypeline; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinforadiuslabel; => VHG onboard row append text;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = [VHGinfolocaltypeptr]; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row finish;
	A = [VHGlocaltarget]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1]; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 10000; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGinforayscaled] = [FI]; [VHGonrowdst] = VHGinfolocalradiusline;
	=> VHG onboard row begin;
    "VHG info local radius base pad"
	A = [VHGonrowpos]; ? A >= 16 -> VHG info local radius extra pad;
	=> VHG onboard row append space; -> VHG info local radius base pad;
    "VHG info local radius extra pad"
	A = [VHGinforayscaled]; ? A >= 100000 -> VHG info local radius one pad;
	=> VHG onboard row append space;
    "VHG info local radius one pad"
	A = [VHGinforayscaled]; ? A >= 1000000 -> VHG info local radius text;
	=> VHG onboard row append space;
    "VHG info local radius text"
	[VHGinfofixedvalue] = [VHGinforayscaled]; [VHGinfofixeddigits] = 4;
	=> VHG info append fixed; => VHG onboard row finish; => VHG fpu clean;
	end;

"VHG info select local type"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype0; E = nsptype; E + [VHGlocaltarget]; A = [E];
	? A = 0 -> VHG info select local type done; ? A = 1 -> VHG info local type 1;
	? A = 2 -> VHG info local type 2; ? A = 3 -> VHG info local type 3;
	? A = 4 -> VHG info local type 4; ? A = 5 -> VHG info local type 5;
	? A = 6 -> VHG info local type 6; ? A = 7 -> VHG info local type 7;
	? A = 8 -> VHG info local type 8; ? A = 9 -> VHG info local type 9;
	? A = 10 -> VHG info local type 10; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 1"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype1; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 2"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype2; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 3"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype3; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 4"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype4; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 5"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype5; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 6"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype6; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 7"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype7; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 8"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype8; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 9"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype9; -> VHG info select local type done;
    "VHG info local type 10"
	[VHGinfolocaltypeptr] = VHGinfotype10;
    "VHG info select local type done"
	end;

"VHG info format environment source"
	( Recreate the source dsd and eclipse from the Stardrifter's actual live
	  position. Fine approach is kept in a target-relative frame in this port,
	  so the geometry helper first restores the equivalent star-relative point. )
	=> VHG info environment geometry;
	( data case 3: fast_srand(secs/2), then (16-dsd*.044)*abs(...). )
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A '/ 2; => SU fast srand;
	[FA0] = [VHGinfodistance0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfodistance1];
	[FB0] = 020C49BAh; [FB1] = 3FA6872Bh; => FMul;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 16; => IntToF; [FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfoenvironment0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoenvironment1] = [FA1];
	=> FAbs; [FB0] = [VHGinfoenvironment0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoenvironment1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinfoenvironment0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoenvironment1] = [FA1];
	( tmp_float -= (tmp_float + 273.15) * eclipse. )
	[FB0] = 66666666h; [FB1] = 40711266h; => FAdd;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoeclipse0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoeclipse1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoenvironment0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoenvironment1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfoenvironment0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoenvironment1] = [FA1];
	A = 0; A - 269; [FI] = A; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoenvironment0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoenvironment1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG info environment temperature ready;
	[SUfmask] = 7FFFh; => SU fast raw; [FI] = [SUfval]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 7EDA0BE1h; [FB1] = 3F00000Eh; => FMul;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 269; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfoenvironment0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoenvironment1] = [FA1];
    "VHG info environment temperature ready"
	( Kelvin row. )
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoenvironment0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoenvironment1];
	[FB0] = 66666666h; [FB1] = 40711266h; => FAdd;
	[FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 100; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1];
	=> FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGinfofixedvalue] = [FI];
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinfoenvtempk; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfotempprefix; => VHG onboard row append text;
	[VHGinfofixeddigits] = 2; => VHG info append fixed;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfokelvinunit; => VHG onboard row append text; => VHG onboard row finish;
	( Celsius row. )
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoenvironment0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoenvironment1];
	[FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 100; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1];
	=> FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGinfofixedvalue] = [FI];
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinfoenvtempc; => VHG onboard row begin; [VHGinfoindent] = 6;
    "VHG info environment celsius indent"
	=> VHG onboard row append space; [VHGinfoindent]-; A = [VHGinfoindent];
	? A > 0 -> VHG info environment celsius indent;
	[VHGinfofixeddigits] = 2; => VHG info append fixed;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfocelsiusunit; => VHG onboard row append text; => VHG onboard row finish;
	( Fahrenheit row. )
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoenvironment0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoenvironment1];
	[FB0] = CCCCCCCDh; [FB1] = 3FFCCCCCh; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 00000000h; [FB1] = 40400000h; => FAdd;
	[FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 100; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1];
	=> FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGinfofixedvalue] = [FI];
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinfoenvtempf; => VHG onboard row begin; [VHGinfoindent] = 6;
    "VHG info environment fahrenheit indent"
	=> VHG onboard row append space; [VHGinfoindent]-; A = [VHGinfoindent];
	? A > 0 -> VHG info environment fahrenheit indent;
	[VHGinfofixeddigits] = 2; => VHG info append fixed;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfofahrenheitunit; => VHG onboard row append text; => VHG onboard row finish;
	( Lithium ions and radiation share the source's identity-seeded Borland
	  stream; only class 5/6 consume the ion draw before radiation. )
	[FA0] = [VHTid0]; [FA1] = [VHTid1]; => FToIntChop; A = [FI]; => BrtlSrand;
	[VHGinfoions] = 0; A = [VHTclass]; ? A = 5 -> VHG info environment ion class5;
	? A = 6 -> VHG info environment ion class6; -> VHG info environment ions ready;
    "VHG info environment ion class5"
	A = 50; => BrtlRandom; [VHGinfoions] = A; [FI] = 125; -> VHG info environment ion penalty;
    "VHG info environment ion class6"
	A = 50; => BrtlRandom; [VHGinfoions] = A; [FI] = 25;
    "VHG info environment ion penalty"
	=> IntToF; [FB0] = [VHGinfodistance0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfodistance1]; => FQuo;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGinfoions]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FSub; => FToIntChop;
	[VHGinfoions] = [FI]; A = [VHTclass]; ? A != 5 -> VHG info environment ions ready;
	A = [VHGinfoions]; ? A > 0 -> VHG info environment ions ready; [VHGinfoions] = 1;
    "VHG info environment ions ready"
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinfoenvions; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfoionsprefix; => VHG onboard row append text;
	[VHGonrowvalue] = [VHGinfoions]; => VHG onboard row append number;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinfoionssuffix; => VHG onboard row append text; => VHG onboard row finish;
	A = 10; => BrtlRandom; C = A; A = 10; => BrtlRandom;
	C - A; C + 50; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[VHGinforadiation0] = [FA0]; [VHGinforadiation1] = [FA1];
	( tmp_float *= 1 - eclipse. )
	[FI] = 1; => IntToF; [FB0] = [VHGinfoeclipse0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoeclipse1]; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinforadiation0] = [FA0]; [VHGinforadiation1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 100; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfodistance0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfodistance1]; => FQuo;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinforadiation0] = [FA0]; [VHGinforadiation1] = [FA1];
	=> VHG info environment radiation class;
	( Per-second source jitter. )
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; => BrtlSrand; A = 100; => BrtlRandom; C = A;
	A = 100; => BrtlRandom; C - A; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = D2F1A9FCh; [FB1] = 3F50624Dh; => FMul;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 1; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FAdd;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1]; => FMul;
	[FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 10; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1];
	=> FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGinforadiationscaled] = [FI];
	[VHGonrowdst] = VHGinfoenvradiation; => VHG onboard row begin;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinforadiationprefix; => VHG onboard row append text;
	[VHGinfofixedvalue] = [VHGinforadiationscaled]; [VHGinfofixeddigits] = 1;
	=> VHG info append fixed;
	[VHGonrowsrc] = VHGinforadiationsuffix; => VHG onboard row append text;
	=> VHG onboard row finish; => VHG fpu clean;
	end;

"VHG info environment geometry"
	( NOCTIS.CPP:3374-3377 and 3428-3463. Ship coordinates here are relative
	  to the primary star, matching planet_xyz after its near-star translation
	  is removed. The exact vector projection is the analytic equivalent of
	  watch(star), change_angle_of_view(), and xy(body), with dpp = 200. )
	( Original eclipse and environment state advances on the 18.206-Hz source
	  tick. In optional 60-Hz presentation mode, retain that result between
	  simulation ticks instead of regenerating every orbit three times. )
	A = [VHGinfogeometryvalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG info environment geometry refresh;
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG info environment geometry done;
    "VHG info environment geometry refresh"
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG info environment galactic ship;
	A = [VHGlocaltarget]; ? A < 0 -> VHG info environment galactic ship;
	? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG info environment galactic ship;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = A; => VHGND absolute body vector;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalx0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalx1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfoshipx0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoshipx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecy1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocaly0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocaly1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfoshipy0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoshipy1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGlocalz0]; [FB1] = [VHGlocalz1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfoshipz0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoshipz1] = [FA1];
	-> VHG info environment ship ready;
    "VHG info environment galactic ship"
	[FC0] = [MgDzatX0]; [FC1] = [MgDzatX1]; [FI] = [VHTtx]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfoshipx0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoshipx1] = [FA1];
	[FC0] = [MgDzatY0]; [FC1] = [MgDzatY1]; [FI] = [VHTty]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfoshipy0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoshipy1] = [FA1];
	[FC0] = [MgDzatZ0]; [FC1] = [MgDzatZ1]; [FI] = [VHTtz]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfoshipz0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoshipz1] = [FA1];
    "VHG info environment ship ready"
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoshipx0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoshipx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipx0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipx1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoshipy0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoshipy1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipy0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipy1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoshipz0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoshipz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipz0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipz1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt;
	[VHGinfostardist0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfostardist1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1; => IntToF; [FB0] = [VHGinfostardist0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfostardist1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfodistance0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfodistance1] = [FA1];
	( ras = clamp((105 * nearstar_ray) / dsd, 1, 66). )
	[FS0] = [VHTRay]; => FLoadF32; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 105; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfodistance0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfodistance1]; => FQuo;
	[VHGinforas0] = [FA0]; [VHGinforas1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 66; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforas0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforas1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A '<= 0 -> VHG info environment ras low; [VHGinforas0] = [FB0]; [VHGinforas1] = [FB1];
    "VHG info environment ras low"
	[FI] = 1; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforas0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforas1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A '>= 0 -> VHG info environment ras ready; [VHGinforas0] = [FB0]; [VHGinforas1] = [FB1];
    "VHG info environment ras ready"
	[VHGinfoeclipse0] = 0; [VHGinfoeclipse1] = 0; [VHGinfoeclipsebody] = 0;
    "VHG info environment eclipse loop"
	A = [VHGinfoeclipsebody]; ? A '>= [nsnob] -> VHG info environment geometry done;
	E = nsptype; E + A; C = [E]; ? C = 0FFFFFFFFh -> VHG info environment eclipse next;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = A; => VHGND absolute body vector;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipx0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipx1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfobodyx0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfobodyx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecy1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipy0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipy1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfobodyy0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfobodyy1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipz0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipz1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfobodyz0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfobodyz1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfobodyx0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfobodyx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfobodyx0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfobodyx1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinfobodysq0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfobodysq1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfobodyy0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfobodyy1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfobodyy0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfobodyy1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfobodysq0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfobodysq1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfobodysq0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfobodysq1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfobodyz0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfobodyz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfobodyz0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfobodyz1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfobodysq0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfobodysq1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfobodysq0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfobodysq1] = [FA1]; => FSqrt;
	[FB0] = D2F1A9FCh; [FB1] = 3F50624Dh; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfobodydist0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfobodydist1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHGinfoeclipsebody]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	[FI] = 10; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [VHGinfobodydist0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfobodydist1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A '>= 0 -> VHG info environment eclipse next;
	( forward = dot(body-from-ship, star-from-ship) / star distance. )
	[FA0] = [VHGinfobodyx0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfobodyx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipx0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipx1]; => FMul; => FNeg;
	[VHGinfoforward0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoforward1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfobodyy0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfobodyy1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipy0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipy1]; => FMul; => FNeg;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoforward0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoforward1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGinfoforward0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoforward1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfobodyz0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfobodyz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoshipz0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoshipz1]; => FMul; => FNeg;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoforward0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoforward1]; => FAdd;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfostardist0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfostardist1]; => FQuo;
	[VHGinfoforward0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoforward1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = D2F1A9FCh; [FB1] = 3F50624Dh; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A '<= 0 -> VHG info environment eclipse next;
	( dasp = dpp * perpendicular / forward. Clamp the roundoff residue
	  before sqrt when a body lies exactly on the star axis. )
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoforward0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoforward1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoforward0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoforward1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [VHGinfobodysq0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfobodysq1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfoperp0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoperp1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 0; => FCmp; A = [FI]; ? A '>= 0 -> VHG info environment perp ready;
	[VHGinfoperp0] = 0; [VHGinfoperp1] = 0;
    "VHG info environment perp ready"
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoperp0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoperp1]; => FSqrt;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfoforward0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoforward1]; => FQuo;
	[FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 200; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FMul;
	[VHGinfodasp0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfodasp1] = [FA1];
	( rap = min((105 * body radius) / dpz, 66). )
	A = [VHGinfoeclipsebody]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	[FI] = 105; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGinfobodydist0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfobodydist1]; => FQuo;
	[VHGinforap0] = [FA0]; [VHGinforap1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 66; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforap0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforap1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A '<= 0 -> VHG info environment rap ready; [VHGinforap0] = [FB0]; [VHGinforap1] = [FB1];
    "VHG info environment rap ready"
	( eclipse = 1 - clamp((dasp + ras - rap) / (2 * ras), 0, 1). )
	[FA0] = [VHGinfodasp0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfodasp1];
	[FB0] = [VHGinforas0]; [FB1] = [VHGinforas1]; => FAdd;
	[FB0] = [VHGinforap0]; [FB1] = [VHGinforap1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfofactor0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfofactor1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforas0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforas1]; [FI] = 2; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [VHGinforas0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforas1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [VHGinfofactor0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfofactor1]; => FQuo;
	[VHGinfoeclipse0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoeclipse1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoeclipse0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoeclipse1]; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A '<= 0 -> VHG info environment eclipse low; [VHGinfoeclipse0] = [FB0]; [VHGinfoeclipse1] = [FB1];
    "VHG info environment eclipse low"
	[FA0] = [VHGinfoeclipse0]; [FA1] = [VHGinfoeclipse1];
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 0; => FCmp; A = [FI]; ? A '>= 0 -> VHG info environment eclipse clamped;
	[VHGinfoeclipse0] = 0; [VHGinfoeclipse1] = 0;
    "VHG info environment eclipse clamped"
	[FI] = 1; => IntToF; [FB0] = [VHGinfoeclipse0]; [FB1] = [VHGinfoeclipse1]; => FSub;
	[VHGinfoeclipse0] = [FA0]; [VHGinfoeclipse1] = [FA1];
    "VHG info environment eclipse next"
	[VHGinfoeclipsebody]+; -> VHG info environment eclipse loop;
    "VHG info environment geometry done"
	[VHGinfogeometryvalid] = 1;
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	end;

"VHG info environment radiation class"
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 1; A = [VHTclass];
	? A = 11 -> VHG info environment radiation class11;
	? A = 10 -> VHG info environment radiation quarter;
	? A = 9 -> VHG info environment radiation class9;
	? A = 8 -> VHG info environment radiation onehalf;
	? A = 7 -> VHG info environment radiation class7;
	? A = 6 -> VHG info environment radiation hundredth;
	? A = 5 -> VHG info environment radiation tenth;
	? A = 4 -> VHG info environment radiation class4;
	? A = 3 -> VHG info environment radiation half;
	? A = 2 -> VHG info environment radiation class2;
	? A = 1 -> VHG info environment radiation class1;
	-> VHG info environment radiation integer;
    "VHG info environment radiation class11"
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 50; A = [VHTphase]; ? A >= 90 -> VHG info environment radiation integer;
	A = 50; => BrtlRandom; A + 75; [VHGinfomassfactor] = A; -> VHG info environment radiation integer;
    "VHG info environment radiation class9"
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 3; -> VHG info environment radiation integer;
    "VHG info environment radiation class7"
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 25; -> VHG info environment radiation integer;
    "VHG info environment radiation class4"
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 10; -> VHG info environment radiation integer;
    "VHG info environment radiation class2"
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 18; -> VHG info environment radiation integer;
    "VHG info environment radiation class1"
	[VHGinfomassfactor] = 5;
    "VHG info environment radiation integer"
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1];
	[FI] = [VHGinfomassfactor]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1]; => FMul;
	-> VHG info environment radiation store;
    "VHG info environment radiation quarter"
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1];
	[FB0] = 00000000h; [FB1] = 3FD00000h; => FMul; -> VHG info environment radiation store;
    "VHG info environment radiation onehalf"
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1];
	[FB0] = 00000000h; [FB1] = 3FF80000h; => FMul; -> VHG info environment radiation store;
    "VHG info environment radiation hundredth"
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1];
	[FB0] = 47AE147Bh; [FB1] = 3F847AE1h; => FMul; -> VHG info environment radiation store;
    "VHG info environment radiation tenth"
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1];
	[FB0] = 9999999Ah; [FB1] = 3FB99999h; => FMul; -> VHG info environment radiation store;
    "VHG info environment radiation half"
	[FA0] = [VHGinforadiation0]; [FA1] = [VHGinforadiation1];
	[FB0] = 00000000h; [FB1] = 3FE00000h; => FMul;
    "VHG info environment radiation store"
	[VHGinforadiation0] = [FA0]; [VHGinforadiation1] = [FA1];
	end;

"VHG info format common"
	( Mutable datasheet rows live in dedicated workspace vectors. Refresh
	  them from immutable templates before substituting live values. )
	[VHGinfosrc] = VHGinfoclasst; [VHGinfodst] = VHGinfoclass; [VHGinfocount] = 23; => VHG info row copy;
	[VHGinfosrc] = VHGinfobodiest; [VHGinfodst] = VHGinfobodies; [VHGinfocount] = 30; => VHG info row copy;
	[VHGinfosrc] = VHGinfomajort; [VHGinfodst] = VHGinfomajor; [VHGinfocount] = 22; => VHG info row copy;
	[VHGinfosrc] = VHGinfoenergyt; [VHGinfodst] = VHGinfoenergy; [VHGinfocount] = 33; => VHG info row copy;
	[VHGinfosrc] = VHGinfostatet; [VHGinfodst] = VHGinfostate; [VHGinfocount] = 28; => VHG info row copy;
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	A = [VHTclass]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG info class nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHG info class nonnegative"
	? A <= 99 -> VHG info class ready; A = 99;
    "VHG info class ready"
	C = A; A '/ 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoclass plus 6] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoclass plus 7] = A;
	[FS0] = [VHTRay]; => FLoadF32; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1000; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG info star radius nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHG info star radius nonnegative"
	? A <= 99999 -> VHG info star radius ready; A = 99999;
    "VHG info star radius ready"
	C = A; A '/ 10000; A + 48; [VHGinfoclass plus 16] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 1000; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoclass plus 17] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 100; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoclass plus 19] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoclass plus 20] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoclass plus 21] = A;
	A = [nsnob]; ? A <= 99 -> VHG info bodies ready; A = 99;
    "VHG info bodies ready"
	C = A; A '/ 10; A + 48; [VHGinfobodies plus 7] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfobodies plus 8] = A;
	A = [nsnop]; ? A <= 99 -> VHG info major ready; A = 99;
    "VHG info major ready"
	C = A; A '/ 10; A + 48; [VHGinfomajor plus 14] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfomajor plus 15] = A;
	A = [nsnop]; ? A <= 99 -> VHG info planets ready; A = 99;
    "VHG info planets ready"
	C = A; A '/ 10; A + 48; [VHGinfobodies plus 18] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfobodies plus 19] = A;
	A = [nsnob]; A - [nsnop]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG info moons nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHG info moons nonnegative"
	? A <= 99 -> VHG info moons ready; A = 99;
    "VHG info moons ready"
	C = A; A '/ 10; A + 48; [VHGinfobodies plus 27] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfobodies plus 28] = A;
	A = [MgPwr]; A - 15000; ? A >= 0 -> VHG info power nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHG info power nonnegative"
	? A <= 99999 -> VHG info power ready; A = 99999;
    "VHG info power ready"
	C = A; A '/ 10000; A + 48; [VHGinfoenergy plus 6] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 1000; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoenergy plus 7] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 100; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoenergy plus 8] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoenergy plus 9] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoenergy plus 10] = A;
	A = [MgCharge]; ? A < 0 -> VHG info lithium omega;
	? A <= 999 -> VHG info lithium ready; A = 999;
    "VHG info lithium ready"
	C = A; A '/ 100; A + 48; [VHGinfoenergy plus 15] = A;
	A = C; A '/ 10; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoenergy plus 16] = A;
	A = C; A '% 10; A + 48; [VHGinfoenergy plus 17] = A;
	-> VHG info collector;
    "VHG info lithium omega"
	[VHGinfoenergy plus 15] = 79; [VHGinfoenergy plus 16] = 77; [VHGinfoenergy plus 17] = 71;
    "VHG info collector"
	[VHGinfoenergy plus 29] = 79; [VHGinfoenergy plus 30] = 70; [VHGinfoenergy plus 31] = 70;
	A = [VHGcollector]; ? A = 0 -> VHG info format state;
	[VHGinfoenergy plus 29] = 79; [VHGinfoenergy plus 30] = 78; [VHGinfoenergy plus 31] = 32;
    "VHG info format state"
	[VHGinfostate plus 5] = 83; [VHGinfostate plus 6] = 72; [VHGinfostate plus 7] = 73;
	[VHGinfostate plus 8] = 80; [VHGinfostate plus 9] = 32; [VHGinfostate plus 10] = 32; [VHGinfostate plus 11] = 32;
	[VHGinfostate plus 17] = 45; [VHGinfostate plus 18] = 45;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG info capture state;
	[VHGinfostate plus 5] = 83; [VHGinfostate plus 6] = 85; [VHGinfostate plus 7] = 82;
	[VHGinfostate plus 8] = 70; [VHGinfostate plus 9] = 65; [VHGinfostate plus 10] = 67; [VHGinfostate plus 11] = 69;
	[VHGinfostate plus 17] = 78; [VHGinfostate plus 18] = 79;
	A = [VHGsurfair]; ? A = 0 -> VHG info capture state;
	[VHGinfostate plus 17] = 89; [VHGinfostate plus 18] = 69;
    "VHG info capture state"
	A = [VHGNDcaptures]; ? A <= 9 -> VHG info captures ready; A = 9;
    "VHG info captures ready"
	A + 48; [VHGinfostate plus 26] = A;
	( These rows are edited in place. Keep their string boundaries explicit
	  so the text service can never continue into the next declaration. )
	[VHGinfoclass plus 22] = 0; [VHGinfobodies plus 29] = 0; [VHGinfomajor plus 21] = 0;
	[VHGinfoenergy plus 32] = 0; [VHGinfostate plus 27] = 0;
	end;

	end;

	A = [VHGhelpshow]; ? A = 0 -> VHG help overlay done;
	( Port-specific current control card: darken the completed frame, then draw
	  it in the aspect-fitted game area. The source F1 page is kept separately. )
	[Rectangle Display Alignment] = 320; [Rectangle Bounds] = vector VHGUIregion;

	end;
