	[VHGaboutsrc] = VHGaboutsurface0; [VHGaboutx] = 14; [VHGabouty] = 53; => VHG about draw string;
	[VHGaboutsrc] = VHGaboutsurface1; [VHGaboutx] = 14; [VHGabouty] = 59; => VHG about draw string;
	[VHGaboutsrc] = VHGaboutsurface2; [VHGaboutx] = 14; [VHGabouty] = 65; => VHG about draw string;
	[VHGaboutsrc] = VHGaboutsurface3; [VHGaboutx] = 14; [VHGabouty] = 71; => VHG about draw string;
	[VHGaboutsrc] = VHGaboutsurface4; [VHGaboutx] = 14; [VHGabouty] = 77; => VHG about draw string;
	[VHGaboutsrc] = VHGaboutsurface5; [VHGaboutx] = 14; [VHGabouty] = 83; => VHG about draw string;
	[VHGaboutsrc] = VHGaboutsurface6; [VHGaboutx] = 14; [VHGabouty] = 89; => VHG about draw string;
    "VHG about common lines"
	A = [MgCharge]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG about footer line;
	[VHGaboutsrc] = VHGaboutomega; [VHGaboutx] = 58; [VHGabouty] = 160; => VHG about draw string;
    "VHG about footer line"
	A = pal; A + 119; [VHGaboutink] = [A];
	[VHGaboutsrc] = VHGaboutrelease; [VHGaboutx] = 14; [VHGabouty] = 180; => VHG about draw string;
    "VHG about overlay done"
	end;

"VHG about fill"
	A = [VHGaboutrect]; [VHGaboutleft] = [A plus 0]; [VHGabouttop] = [A plus 1];
	[VHGaboutright] = [A plus 2]; [VHGaboutbottom] = [A plus 3];
	[VHGaboutfilly] = [VHGabouttop];
    "VHG about fill row"
	A = [VHGaboutfilly]; A '* 320; A + [VHGaboutleft]; A + VHGUIframe; [VHGaboutptr] = A;
	[VHGaboutfillx] = [VHGaboutleft];
    "VHG about fill pixel"
	E = [VHGaboutptr]; [E] = [VHGaboutcolour]; [VHGaboutptr]+; [VHGaboutfillx]+;
	A = [VHGaboutfillx]; ? A <= [VHGaboutright] -> VHG about fill pixel;
	[VHGaboutfilly]+; A = [VHGaboutfilly]; ? A <= [VHGaboutbottom] -> VHG about fill row;
	end;

"VHG about draw string"
	[VHGabouti] = 0;
    "VHG about draw character"
	A = [VHGaboutsrc]; A + [VHGabouti]; A = [A]; [VHGNDhudchar] = A;
	? A = 0 -> VHG about draw done;
	[VHGaboutrow] = 0;
    "VHG about draw glyph row"
	[VHGNDhudrow] = [VHGaboutrow]; => VHGND HUD row mask;
	A = [VHGabouty]; A + [VHGaboutrow]; A '* 320; A + [VHGaboutx]; A + VHGUIframe;
	[VHGaboutptr] = A; C = [VHGNDcompassmask]; C & 1; ? C = 0 -> VHG about draw pixel two;
	E = [VHGaboutptr]; [E] = [VHGaboutink];
    "VHG about draw pixel two"
	C = [VHGNDcompassmask]; C & 2; ? C = 0 -> VHG about draw pixel three;
	E = [VHGaboutptr]; E + 1; [E] = [VHGaboutink];
    "VHG about draw pixel three"
	C = [VHGNDcompassmask]; C & 4; ? C = 0 -> VHG about draw row done;
	E = [VHGaboutptr]; E + 2; [E] = [VHGaboutink];
    "VHG about draw row done"
	[VHGaboutrow]+; A = [VHGaboutrow]; ? A < 5 -> VHG about draw glyph row;
	A = [VHGaboutx]; A + 4; [VHGaboutx] = A; [VHGabouti]+;
	A = [VHGabouti]; ? A < 80 -> VHG about draw character;
    "VHG about draw done"
	end;

"VHG help draw line"
	[String] = [VHGhelpline]; [Ink] = FFFFFFh; [Text Effect] = service FX Raw;
	[Text X] = 12; [Text Y] = [VHGhelpy];
	[Text Display Width] = 320; [Text Display Height] = 200;
	[Text Region] = vector VHGUIregion; [Text Window] = NULL;
	[Text Word Wrap] = OFF; [Text Ghost Mode] = OFF;
	=> VHG text both;
	A = [VHGhelpy]; A + 14; [VHGhelpy] = A;
	end;

"VHG text both"
	( Compose HUD text once; VHGUI then expands the completed logical frame
	  into both synchronized GUI layers. )
	[Text Display Origin] = VHGUIframe; => STD Write;
	end;

"VHG checkpoint keys"
	( F6/F7 work from the ship and from a settled surface.  Mid-flight pod
	  state is deliberately not serialised, so refuse it visibly. )
	A = [KEY F6]; ? A = OFF -> VHG save key released;
	A = [VHGsaveheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG load key;
	[VHGsaveheld] = 1;
	=> VHG save checkpoint action; -> VHG load key;
    "VHG save key released"
	[VHGsaveheld] = 0;
    "VHG load key"
	A = [KEY F7]; ? A = OFF -> VHG load key released;
	A = [VHGloadheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG checkpoint keys done;
	[VHGloadheld] = 1;
	=> VHG load checkpoint action; -> VHG checkpoint keys done;
    "VHG load key released"
	[VHGloadheld] = 0; -> VHG checkpoint keys done;
    "VHG checkpoint keys done"
	end;

"VHG save checkpoint action"
	A = [VHGCstate]; ? A != 0 -> VHG save checkpoint busy;
	=> VHSV save; A = [VHSVok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG save checkpoint failed;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsavedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; end;
    "VHG save checkpoint busy"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGbusytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; end;
    "VHG save checkpoint failed"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGfailedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	end;

"VHG load checkpoint action"
	A = [VHGCstate]; ? A != 0 -> VHG load checkpoint busy;
	[VHGmovie] = 0; [VHGmovieview] = 0; [VHGmoviepaused] = 0;
	=> VHG load checkpoint; A = [VHSVok]; ? A = 0 -> VHG load checkpoint failed;
	[VHGinterpok] = 0; => VHG load success notice; end;
    "VHG load checkpoint busy"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGbusytext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; end;
    "VHG load checkpoint failed"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGfailedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	end;

"VHG load success notice"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGloadedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	A = [VHSVrecovered]; ? A = 0 -> VHG load success notice ready;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGbackuptext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 300;
    "VHG load success notice ready"
	end;

"VHG music key"
	( F8 toggles the authorized manual soundtrack once per key press. )
	A = [KEY F8]; ? A = OFF -> VHG music key released;
	A = [VHGmusicheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG music key done;
	[VHGmusicheld] = 1;
	=> VHG toggle music action; -> VHG music key done;
    "VHG music key released"
	[VHGmusicheld] = 0;
"VHG music key done"
	end;

"VHG toggle music action"
	A = [VHAavailable]; ? A = 0 -> VHG music unavailable;
	=> VHA toggle;
	A = [VHAavailable]; ? A = 0 -> VHG music unavailable;
	A = [VHAplaying]; ? A = 0 -> VHG music off notice;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGmusicontext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	end;
    "VHG music off notice"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGmusicofftext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	end;
"VHG music unavailable"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGmusicfailedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	end;

"VHG movie capture pending"
	( NIV+ records the raw adapted game page every moviefsec gameplay frames.
	  Presentation-only F5 frames do not advance this original 18.206-Hz clock. )
	A = [VHGmovie]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie capture active;
	A = [VHGmoviepaused]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie capture pending done;
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie capture pending done;
	[VHGmovieelapsed]+; -> VHG movie capture pending done;
    "VHG movie capture active"
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie capture pending done;
	[VHGmovieelapsed]+; [VHGmoviedelay]-;
	A = [VHGmoviedelay]; ? A > 0 -> VHG movie capture cutoff;
	[VHGsnapshotbase] = VHGUIframe; => VHG movie write;
	A = [VHGmovie]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie capture pending done;
	[VHGmoviedelay] = [VHGmovieinterval]; => VHG movie capture indicator;
    "VHG movie capture cutoff"
	A = [VHGCstate]; ? A != 2 -> VHG movie capture pending done;
	[VHGmoviecutoff]+; A = [VHGmoviecutoff]; ? A <= 100 -> VHG movie capture pending done;
	=> VHG movie automatic stop;
    "VHG movie capture pending done"
	end;

"VHG movie capture indicator"
	( snapshot() flashes adaptor black when requested. Its default no-flash
	  path instead marks the two bottom rows with the source tracking line. )
	A = [VHGmovieflashoff]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie tracking line;
	[Rectangle Display Alignment] = 320; [Rectangle Bounds] = vector VHGUIregion;
	[Rectangle Gradients] = vector Standard Black Gradients;
	[Rectangle Effect] = service FX Raw; [Rectangle Target Layer] = VHGUIframe; => Rectangle;
	end;
    "VHG movie tracking line"
	A = [VHGNDsurlight]; ? A > 38 -> VHG movie tracking dark;
	A = pal; A + 127; C = [A]; -> VHG movie tracking colour ready;
    "VHG movie tracking dark"
	A = pal; A + 1; C = [A];
    "VHG movie tracking colour ready"
	[VHGmovierow] = 198;
    "VHG movie tracking row"
	A = [VHGmovierow]; A '* 320; A + 1; [VHGmoviepos] = A; [VHGmoviecol] = 1;
    "VHG movie tracking pixel"
	E = VHGUIframe; E + [VHGmoviepos]; [E] = C; [VHGmoviepos]+;
	[VHGmoviecol]+; A = [VHGmoviecol]; ? A <= 319 -> VHG movie tracking pixel;
	[VHGmovierow]+; A = [VHGmovierow]; ? A <= 199 -> VHG movie tracking row;
	end;

"VHG movie automatic stop"
	[VHGmovie] = 0; [VHGmoviepaused] = 0; [VHGmovieexists] = 0;
	A = [VHGmoviedeck]; ? A >= 999 -> VHG movie automatic deck ready; [VHGmoviedeck]+;
    "VHG movie automatic deck ready"
	[VHGmovieframe] = 0; => VHG movie format deck;
	end;

"VHG movie write"
	A = [VHGmovieframe]; ? A >= 99999999 -> VHG movie write error;
	[VHGmovieframe]+; [VHGmovievalue] = [VHGmovieframe];
	[VHGmovieden] = 10000000; [VHGmoviepos] = 11;
    "VHG movie frame digit loop"
	A = [VHGmovievalue]; C = A; A '/ [VHGmovieden]; A + 48;
	E = VHGmoviefile; E + [VHGmoviepos]; [E] = A;
	A = C; A '% [VHGmovieden]; [VHGmovievalue] = A;
	A = [VHGmovieden]; A '/ 10; [VHGmovieden] = A;
	[VHGmoviepos]+; A = [VHGmoviepos]; ? A < 19 -> VHG movie frame digit loop;

	[File Name] = VHGmovieroot; [File Command] = MKDIR; isocall;
	[File Name] = VHGmoviedir; [File Command] = MKDIR; isocall;
	[VHGmovierow] = 0;
    "VHG movie assemble row"
	A = [VHGmovierow]; A '* 320; A + VHGmovieoutput; [VHGmoviedst] = A;
	A = 199; A - [VHGmovierow]; A '* 320; A + [VHGsnapshotbase]; [VHGmoviesrc] = A;
	[VHGmoviecol] = 0;
    "VHG movie assemble pixel"
	E = [VHGmoviesrc]; E + [VHGmoviecol]; D = [VHGmoviedst]; D + [VHGmoviecol];
	A = [E]; [D] = A; [VHGmoviecol]+; A = [VHGmoviecol]; ? A < 320 -> VHG movie assemble pixel;
	[VHGmovierow]+; A = [VHGmovierow]; ? A < 200 -> VHG movie assemble row;

	( Standard 320x200 uncompressed 32-bit BMP, shared with Gallery output. )
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 0] = E8364D42h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 1] = 00000003h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 2] = 00360000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 3] = 00280000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 4] = 01400000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 5] = 00C80000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 6] = 00010000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 7] = 00000020h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 8] = E8000000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 9] = 00000003h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 10] = 0; [VHGsnapshotheader plus 11] = 0;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 12] = 0; [VHGsnapshotheader plus 13] = 0;
	[File Name] = VHGmoviefile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGsnapshotheader; [Block Size] = 54; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG movie write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 54 -> VHG movie write error;
	[File Position] = 54; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGmovieoutput; [Block Size] = 256000; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG movie write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 256000 -> VHG movie write error;
	[File Name] = VHGmoviefile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = 256054; isocall; ? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG movie write error;
	[VHGmoviecaptured]+; [VHGmovieexists] = 2; end;
    "VHG movie write error"
	[VHGmovie] = 0; [VHGmovieview] = 1; [VHGmoviepaused] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGmoviefailtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 100;
	end;

"VHG snapshot key"
	( NOCTIS.CPP binds M and * to snapshot(0,1). Leave M available to the
	  GOES command line while it owns text input; everywhere else capture the
	  last fully composed logical frame, before iGUI presentation scaling. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGwidephase]; ? A != 0 -> VHG snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 77 -> VHG snapshot key pressed;
	? A = 109 -> VHG snapshot key pressed; ? A != 42 -> VHG snapshot key done;
    "VHG snapshot key pressed"
	[VHGascii] = 0; A = [VHGsnapshotready]; ? A != 0 -> VHG snapshot key ready;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsnapshotwaittext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG snapshot key done;
    "VHG snapshot key ready"
	[VHGsnapshotbase] = VHGUIframe;
	=> VHG snapshot;
    "VHG snapshot key done"
	end;

"VHG raw snapshot key"
	( NOCTIS.CPP binds B to snapshot(0,0) outside the F2 graphics menu;
	  NOCTIS-1.CPP gives settled surfaces the same action on physical Delete.
	  Queue it until VHGUI prepare has assembled the next current game frame,
	  then save before the port-only overlays are drawn. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG raw snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGwidephase]; ? A != 0 -> VHG raw snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGgraphics]; ? A != 0 -> VHG raw snapshot key done;
	A = [KEY DELETE]; ? A = OFF -> VHG raw snapshot delete released;
	A = [VHGdeleteheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG raw snapshot character;
	[VHGdeleteheld] = 1; A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG raw snapshot key pressed;
	-> VHG raw snapshot character;
    "VHG raw snapshot delete released"
	[VHGdeleteheld] = 0;
    "VHG raw snapshot character"
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 66 -> VHG raw snapshot key pressed;
	? A != 98 -> VHG raw snapshot key done;
    "VHG raw snapshot key pressed"
	[VHGascii] = 0; [VHGsnapshotrequest] = 1;
    "VHG raw snapshot key done"
	end;

"VHG raw snapshot pending"
	A = [VHGsnapshotrequest]; ? A = 0 -> VHG raw snapshot pending done;
	[VHGsnapshotrequest] = 0; [VHGsnapshotbase] = VHGUIframe;
	=> VHG snapshot;
    "VHG raw snapshot pending done"
	end;

"VHG wide snapshot key"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP binds N or slash to the annotated three-panel panorama and
	  V or period to its raw variant. This is a settled-surface feature only. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGgraphics]; ? A != 0 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGabout]; ? A != 0 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGhelpshow]; ? A != 0 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGmovie]; ? A != 0 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGmovieview]; ? A != 0 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGwidephase]; ? A != 0 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 78 -> VHG wide snapshot data;
	? A = 110 -> VHG wide snapshot data; ? A = 47 -> VHG wide snapshot data;
	? A = 86 -> VHG wide snapshot raw; ? A = 118 -> VHG wide snapshot raw;
	? A != 46 -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
    "VHG wide snapshot raw"
	[VHGwideraw] = 1; -> VHG wide snapshot start;
    "VHG wide snapshot data"
	[VHGwideraw] = 0;
    "VHG wide snapshot start"
	[VHGascii] = 0; A = [VHGsnapshotready]; ? A != 0 -> VHG wide snapshot ready;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsnapshotwaittext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG wide snapshot key done;
    "VHG wide snapshot ready"
	[VHGwideoriginalbeta] = [VHGbeta]; [VHGwidephase] = 1; [VHGwidecaptured] = 0;
	[VHGinterpok] = 0;
    "VHG wide snapshot key done"
	end;

"VHG wide raw pending"
	A = [VHGwidephase]; ? A = 0 -> VHG wide raw pending done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG wide raw cancel;
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHG wide raw cancel;
	A = [VHGwidephase]; ? A != 2 -> VHG wide raw capture;
	A = [VHGwideraw]; ? A = 0 -> VHG wide raw pending done;
    "VHG wide raw capture"
	=> VHG wide capture; -> VHG wide raw pending done;
    "VHG wide raw cancel"
	[VHGbeta] = [VHGwideoriginalbeta]; [VHGwidephase] = 0; [VHGwidecaptured] = 0;
	[VHGinterpok] = 0;
    "VHG wide raw pending done"
	end;

"VHG wide data pending"
	A = [VHGwidephase]; ? A != 2 -> VHG wide data pending done;
	A = [VHGwideraw]; ? A != 0 -> VHG wide data pending done;
	=> VHG wide capture;
    "VHG wide data pending done"
	end;

"VHG wide capture"
	A = [VHGwidephase]; A - 1; A '* 64000; A + VHGwideframes;
	[VHGwidecopybase] = A; [VHGwidecopyi] = 0;
    "VHG wide capture loop"
	E = VHGUIframe; E + [VHGwidecopyi]; D = [VHGwidecopybase]; D + [VHGwidecopyi];
	A = [E]; [D] = A; [VHGwidecopyi]+;
	A = [VHGwidecopyi]; ? A < 64000 -> VHG wide capture loop;
	[VHGwidecaptured] = 1;
	end;

"VHG wide advance"
	A = [VHGwidecaptured]; ? A = 0 -> VHG wide advance done;
	[VHGwidecaptured] = 0; A = [VHGwidephase]; ? A = 1 -> VHG wide advance left;
	? A = 2 -> VHG wide advance right;
	[VHGbeta] = [VHGwideoriginalbeta]; [VHGwidephase] = 0; [VHGinterpok] = 0;
	=> VHG wide write; -> VHG wide advance done;
    "VHG wide advance left"
	A = [VHGwideoriginalbeta]; A + 71; ? A <= 180 -> VHG wide beta left ready; A - 360;
    "VHG wide beta left ready"
	[VHGbeta] = A; [VHGwidephase] = 2; [VHGinterpok] = 0; -> VHG wide advance done;
    "VHG wide advance right"
	A = [VHGwideoriginalbeta]; A - 71; ? A >= VHGN180 -> VHG wide beta right ready; A + 360;
    "VHG wide beta right ready"
	[VHGbeta] = A; [VHGwidephase] = 3; [VHGinterpok] = 0;
    "VHG wide advance done"
	end;

"VHG snapshot"
	( snapshot() numbers files in GALLERY and writes the image bottom-up. The
	  resident renderer already owns a completed 320x200 RGB frame, so retain
	  that source behavior in a standard uncompressed 32-bit BMP without
	  quantizing the port's expanded palette and text overlays. )
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotdir; [File Command] = MKDIR; isocall;
	A = [VHGsnapshotnext]; ? A > 0 -> VHG snapshot scan;
	[VHGsnapshotnext] = 1;
    "VHG snapshot scan"
	A = [VHGsnapshotnext]; ? A > 99999999 -> VHG snapshot limit;
	[VHGsnapshotnumber] = A; [VHGsnapshotvalue] = A;
	[VHGsnapshotden] = 10000000; [VHGsnapshotpos] = 8;
    "VHG snapshot digit loop"
	A = [VHGsnapshotvalue]; C = A; A '/ [VHGsnapshotden]; A + 48;
	E = VHGsnapshotfile; E + [VHGsnapshotpos]; [E] = A;
	E = VHGsnapshotnotice; B = [VHGsnapshotpos]; B + 6; E + B; [E] = A;
	A = C; A '% [VHGsnapshotden]; [VHGsnapshotvalue] = A;
	A = [VHGsnapshotden]; A '/ 10; [VHGsnapshotden] = A;
	[VHGsnapshotpos]+; A = [VHGsnapshotpos]; ? A < 16 -> VHG snapshot digit loop;
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Command] = TEST; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG snapshot free;
	[VHGsnapshotnext]+; -> VHG snapshot scan;
    "VHG snapshot free"
	( Assemble the bottom-up pixel block in memory and submit it once. The
	  historical 200-row writer preserved the bytes but could stall the GUI
	  for several seconds through IsoKernel's file isocall boundary. )
	[VHGsnapshotrow] = 0;
    "VHG snapshot assemble row"
	A = [VHGsnapshotrow]; A '* 320; A + VHGmovieoutput; [VHGsnapshotdst] = A;
	A = 199; A - [VHGsnapshotrow]; A '* 320; A + [VHGsnapshotbase]; [VHGsnapshotsrc] = A;
	[VHGsnapshotcol] = 0;
    "VHG snapshot assemble pixel"
	E = [VHGsnapshotsrc]; E + [VHGsnapshotcol]; D = [VHGsnapshotdst]; D + [VHGsnapshotcol];
	A = [E]; [D] = A; [VHGsnapshotcol]+;
	A = [VHGsnapshotcol]; ? A < 320 -> VHG snapshot assemble pixel;
	[VHGsnapshotrow]+; A = [VHGsnapshotrow]; ? A < 200 -> VHG snapshot assemble row;
	( BITMAPFILEHEADER and BITMAPINFOHEADER, 54 bytes. Fields straddle the
	  host's 32-bit units because the file header is only 14 bytes long. )
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 0] = E8364D42h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 1] = 00000003h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 2] = 00360000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 3] = 00280000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 4] = 01400000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 5] = 00C80000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 6] = 00010000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 7] = 00000020h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 8] = E8000000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 9] = 00000003h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 10] = 0; [VHGsnapshotheader plus 11] = 0;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 12] = 0; [VHGsnapshotheader plus 13] = 0;
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGsnapshotheader; [Block Size] = 54; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG snapshot write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 54 -> VHG snapshot write error;
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Position] = 54; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGmovieoutput; [Block Size] = 256000; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG snapshot write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 256000 -> VHG snapshot write error;
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = 256054; isocall; ? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG snapshot write error;
	A = [VHGsnapshotnumber]; A + 1; [VHGsnapshotnext] = A;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsnapshotnotice; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; end;
    "VHG snapshot write error"
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Position] = 0;
	[File Command] = SET SIZE; [File Size] = 0; isocall;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsnapshoterror; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; end;
"VHG snapshot limit"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsnapshotlimittext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;

"VHG wide write"
	( The NIV+ panorama is a 916x200 composite of three 71-degree panels:
	  309 pixels from +71 degrees, 299 centered pixels, and 308 pixels from
	  -71 degrees. Write the same crop directly as a 32-bit BMP. )
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotdir; [File Command] = MKDIR; isocall;
	A = [VHGsnapshotnext]; ? A > 0 -> VHG wide scan; [VHGsnapshotnext] = 1;
    "VHG wide scan"
	A = [VHGsnapshotnext]; ? A > 99999999 -> VHG wide limit;
	[VHGsnapshotnumber] = A; [VHGsnapshotvalue] = A;
	[VHGsnapshotden] = 10000000; [VHGsnapshotpos] = 8;
    "VHG wide digit loop"
	A = [VHGsnapshotvalue]; C = A; A '/ [VHGsnapshotden]; A + 48;
	E = VHGsnapshotfile; E + [VHGsnapshotpos]; [E] = A;
	E = VHGsnapshotnotice; B = [VHGsnapshotpos]; B + 6; E + B; [E] = A;
	A = C; A '% [VHGsnapshotden]; [VHGsnapshotvalue] = A;
	A = [VHGsnapshotden]; A '/ 10; [VHGsnapshotden] = A;
	[VHGsnapshotpos]+; A = [VHGsnapshotpos]; ? A < 16 -> VHG wide digit loop;
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Command] = TEST; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG wide free;
	[VHGsnapshotnext]+; -> VHG wide scan;
    "VHG wide free"
	( Assemble bottom-up output once, then issue one contiguous pixel write.
	  This preserves the source crop without stalling the GUI on 600 file calls. )
	[VHGwiderow] = 0;
    "VHG wide assemble row"
	A = [VHGwiderow]; A '* 916; A + VHGwideoutput; [VHGwidedst] = A;
	A = 199; A - [VHGwiderow]; A '* 320; [VHGwidesrc] = A;
	[VHGwidecol] = 0;
    "VHG wide assemble left"
	E = VHGwideframes; E + 64000; E + [VHGwidesrc]; E + [VHGwidecol];
	D = [VHGwidedst]; D + [VHGwidecol]; A = [E]; [D] = A;
	[VHGwidecol]+; A = [VHGwidecol]; ? A < 309 -> VHG wide assemble left;
	[VHGwidecol] = 0;
    "VHG wide assemble center"
	E = VHGwideframes; E + [VHGwidesrc]; E + 10; E + [VHGwidecol];
	D = [VHGwidedst]; D + 309; D + [VHGwidecol]; A = [E]; [D] = A;
	[VHGwidecol]+; A = [VHGwidecol]; ? A < 299 -> VHG wide assemble center;
	[VHGwidecol] = 0;
    "VHG wide assemble right"
	E = VHGwideframes; E + 128010; E + [VHGwidesrc]; E + [VHGwidecol];
	D = [VHGwidedst]; D + 608; D + [VHGwidecol]; A = [E]; [D] = A;
	[VHGwidecol]+; A = [VHGwidecol]; ? A < 308 -> VHG wide assemble right;
	[VHGwiderow]+; A = [VHGwiderow]; ? A < 200 -> VHG wide assemble row;
	( 732854-byte BITMAPFILEHEADER/DIB: 916 * 200 * 4 pixel bytes. )
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 0] = 2EB64D42h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 1] = 0000000Bh;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 2] = 00360000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 3] = 00280000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 4] = 03940000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 5] = 00C80000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 6] = 00010000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 7] = 00000020h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 8] = 2E800000h;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 9] = 0000000Bh;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 10] = 0; [VHGsnapshotheader plus 11] = 0;
	[VHGsnapshotheader plus 12] = 0; [VHGsnapshotheader plus 13] = 0;
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGsnapshotheader; [Block Size] = 54; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG wide write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 54 -> VHG wide write error;
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Position] = 54; [File Command] = WRITE;
	[Block Pointer] = VHGwideoutput; [Block Size] = 732800; isocall;
	? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG wide write error;
	A = [Block Size]; ? A != 732800 -> VHG wide write error;
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Position] = 0; [File Command] = SET SIZE;
	[File Size] = 732854; isocall; ? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG wide write error;
	A = [VHGsnapshotnumber]; A + 1; [VHGsnapshotnext] = A;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsnapshotnotice; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; end;
    "VHG wide write error"
	[File Name] = VHGsnapshotfile; [File Position] = 0;
	[File Command] = SET SIZE; [File Size] = 0; isocall;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsnapshoterror; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75; end;
    "VHG wide limit"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGsnapshotlimittext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 75;
	end;

"VHG FCS menu key"
	( Original top-level key 5 selects fcs(); keys 6..9 dispatch its four
	  commands. The physical-key bridge has already normalized these into
	  VHGascii before this routine runs. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS menu key done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS menu key done;
	A = [VHGdev]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS menu key done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 53 -> VHG FCS menu toggle;
	A = [VHGfcsopen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS menu key done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 54 -> VHG FCS remote action;
	? A = 55 -> VHG FCS flight action; ? A = 56 -> VHG FCS local action;
	? A = 57 -> VHG FCS capsule action; -> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS menu toggle"
	A = [VHGfcsopen]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS menu open;
	[VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS menu open"
	[VHGfcsopen] = 1; [VHGdevaccess] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0; [VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0;
	-> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS remote action"
	A = [MgStspeed]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS conflict;
	[VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; => VHG browse open;
	-> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS flight action"
	A = [MgStspeed]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS flight stop;
	A = [VHGcollector]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS conflict;
	A = [MgPwr]; ? A <= 15000 -> VHG FCS no power;
	[MgStspeed] = 1; A = [MgApreached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS flight started;
	[MgApreached] = 0; => VHG local reset;
    "VHG FCS flight started"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGflightstarttext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; [VHGascii] = 0;
	-> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS flight stop"
	[MgStspeed] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGflightstoptext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	[VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS local action"
	A = [MgApreached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS needs calibration;
	A = [VHGlocalactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHG FCS local start;
	A = [MgIpreaching]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS local stop;
	A = [MgIpreached]; ? A != 0 -> VHG FCS local select;
	[MgIpreaching] = 1; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGapproachtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	[VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS local start"
	=> VHG local start; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS local stop"
	=> VHG local reset; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGlocalstoptext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	[VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS local select"
	=> VHG local reset; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGlocalselecttext;
	[VHGnoticeframes] = 75; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS capsule action"
	( Re-enter the existing source-shaped L path after closing the menu. )
	[VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGascii] = 76; -> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS conflict"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGflightconflicttext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; [VHGascii] = 0;
	-> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS no power"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGhelpbadtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; [VHGascii] = 0;
	-> VHG FCS menu key done;
    "VHG FCS needs calibration"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGcalibratetext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55; [VHGascii] = 0;
    "VHG FCS menu key done"
	end;

"VHG preference key"
	( Original pfs_commands(): physical command rows 1..4 arrive through the
	  port's shared 6..9 accessibility mapping. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG preference key done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG preference key done;
	A = [VHGprefs]; ? A = 0 -> VHG preference key done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 54 -> VHG preference auto;
	? A = 55 -> VHG preference reverse; ? A = 56 -> VHG preference menus;
	? A = 57 -> VHG preference hull; -> VHG preference key done;
    "VHG preference auto"
	A = [VHGautoscreenoff]; A '* 0FFFFFFFFh; A + 1; [VHGautoscreenoff] = A;
	[VHGautoscreentick] = 100; -> VHG preference notice;
    "VHG preference reverse"
	A = [VHGrevcontrols]; A '* 0FFFFFFFFh; A + 1; [VHGrevcontrols] = A;
	-> VHG preference notice;
    "VHG preference menus"
	A = [VHGmenusalwayson]; A '* 0FFFFFFFFh; A + 1; [VHGmenusalwayson] = A;
	-> VHG preference notice;
    "VHG preference hull"
	A = [VHGdepolarize]; A '* 0FFFFFFFFh; A + 1; [VHGdepolarize] = A;
	=> VHG hull cache apply; A = [VHGdepolarize];
    "VHG preference notice"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGprefdisabled; ? A = 0 -> VHG preference notice ready;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGprefacquired;
    "VHG preference notice ready"
	[VHGnoticeframes] = 55; [VHGascii] = 0;
    "VHG preference key done"
	end;

"VHG hull cache apply"
	( vehicle() requests randomic_mapper divisions 2 normally and 3 when the
	  depolarize preference is active. Rebuild the camera-independent cache
	  only when that mode changes. )
	[VHRcacheexpected] = 720; C = 2; A = [VHGdepolarize]; ? A = 0 -> VHG hull cache selected;
	[VHRcacheexpected] = 2880; C = 3;
    "VHG hull cache selected"
	A = [VHRcached]; ? A = 0 -> VHG hull cache rebuild;
	A = [VHRiters]; ? A = C -> VHG hull cache done;
    "VHG hull cache rebuild"
	[PVh] = 0;
	[PVxs] = VHG15F; [PVys] = VHG15F; [PVzs] = VHG15F;
	[PVxm] = 0; [PVym] = 0; [PVzm] = 0; [PVcol] = 0; [PVreq] = 0;
	[PVh] = 0; [DWmode] = 2; [DWuds] = 0;
	[DWcx] = 0; [DWcy] = 0; [DWcz] = 0;
	[DWcamx] = 0; [DWcamy] = 0; [DWcamz] = 0;
	[VHRiters] = C; => SP drawpv; => VH build mode2 cache;
    "VHG hull cache done"
	end;

"VHG device key"
	( Original keyboard access: R opens the onboard-device root; R from a
	  child returns to that root, and R at the root closes it. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG device key done;
	=> VHG device physical poll;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHG device key done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 82 -> VHG device toggle; ? A = 114 -> VHG device toggle;
	A = [VHGdev]; ? A = 0 -> VHG device key done;
	? A = 1 -> VHG device root key; ? A = 2 -> VHG device navigation key;
	? A = 3 -> VHG device miscellaneous key; ? A = 4 -> VHG device cartography key;
	? A = 5 -> VHG device emergency key; ? A = 6 -> VHG device browser key;
	-> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device root key"
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 54 -> VHG device open navigation;
	? A = 55 -> VHG device open miscellaneous; ? A = 56 -> VHG device open cartography;
	? A = 57 -> VHG device open emergency; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device open navigation"
	[VHGdev] = 2; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device open miscellaneous"
	[VHGdev] = 3; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device open cartography"
	[VHGdev] = 4; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device open emergency"
	[VHGdev] = 5; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device navigation key"
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 54 -> VHG device amplifier;
	? A = 55 -> VHG device finder; ? A = 56 -> VHG device tracking;
	? A = 57 -> VHG device radiation; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device amplifier"
	A = [VHGamp]; A '* 0FFFFFFFFh; A + 1; [VHGamp] = A;
	[VHSready] = 0; [VHSdrawready] = 0; [VHGamptick] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGampofftext; ? A = 0 -> VHG device navigation notice;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGampontext; -> VHG device navigation notice;
    "VHG device finder"
	A = [VHGfinder]; A '* 0FFFFFFFFh; A + 1; [VHGfinder] = A; [VHGfindertick] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGfinderofftext; ? A = 0 -> VHG device navigation notice;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGfinderontext; -> VHG device navigation notice;
    "VHG device tracking"
	[VHGsync]+; A = [VHGsync]; ? A <= 5 -> VHG device tracking selected; [VHGsync] = 0; A = 0;
    "VHG device tracking selected"
	[VHGsynctick] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGtrackofftext;
	? A = 0 -> VHG device navigation notice; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGtrackfixedtext;
	? A = 1 -> VHG device navigation notice; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGtrackfartext;
	? A = 2 -> VHG device navigation notice; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGtracksynctext;
	? A = 3 -> VHG device navigation notice; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGtrackorbittext;
	? A = 4 -> VHG device navigation notice; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGtrackneartext;
	-> VHG device navigation notice;
    "VHG device radiation"
	A = [VHGantirad]; A '* 0FFFFFFFFh; A + 1; [VHGantirad] = A; [MgAnti] = A;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGradofftext; ? A = 0 -> VHG device navigation notice;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGradontext;
    "VHG device navigation notice"
	[VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device miscellaneous key"
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 54 -> VHG device light;
	? A = 55 -> VHG device remote; ? A = 56 -> VHG device local;
	? A != 57 -> VHG device key done; [VHGinfo] = 3; -> VHG device data;
    "VHG device remote"
	[VHGinfo] = 1; -> VHG device data;
    "VHG device local"
	[VHGinfo] = 2;
    "VHG device data"
	[VHGinfoscroll] = 0; [VHGinfodelta] = 4;
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 0; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device light"
	A = [VHGilight]; A * 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGilight] = A; [VHLpower] = A;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGlightofftext; ? A != 1 -> VHG device light notice;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGlightontext;
    "VHG device light notice"
	[VHGnoticeframes] = 55; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device cartography key"
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 54 -> VHG device name star;
	? A = 55 -> VHG device name planet; ? A = 56 -> VHG device next target;
	? A = 57 -> VHG device manual target; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device name star"
	[VHGprefillsrc] = VHGprefillstar; [VHGprefillcount] = 9; => VHG console prefill;
	-> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device name planet"
	[VHGprefillsrc] = VHGprefillplanet; [VHGprefillcount] = 11; => VHG console prefill;
	-> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device next target"
	=> VHG browse open; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device manual target"
	[VHGprefillsrc] = VHGprefillmanual; [VHGprefillcount] = 4; => VHG console prefill;
	-> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device emergency key"
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 54 -> VHG device emergency reset;
	? A = 55 -> VHG device emergency help; ? A = 56 -> VHG device emergency collector;
	? A = 57 -> VHG device emergency clear; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device emergency reset"
	=> VHG systems reset action; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device emergency help"
	=> VHG help action; [VHGdev] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device emergency collector"
	=> VHG collector action; [VHGdev] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device emergency clear"
	[VHGgburst] = 0; [VHGnoticeptr] = VHGstatuscleartext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device browser key"
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 54 -> VHG device browser previous;
	? A = 55 -> VHG device browser next; ? A = 56 -> VHG device browser select;
	? A = 57 -> VHG device browser back; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device browser previous"
	A = 0; A - 1; [VHGbrowsestep] = A; => VHG browse seek; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device browser next"
	[VHGbrowsestep] = 1; => VHG browse seek; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device browser select"
	A = [VHGbrowsevalid]; ? A = 0 -> VHG device key done;
	[VHTtx] = [VHGbrowsex]; [VHTty] = [VHGbrowsey]; [VHTtz] = [VHGbrowsez];
	=> VHG activate target; [VHGdev] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device browser back"
	[VHGdev] = 4; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device toggle"
	A = [VHGdev]; ? A = 0 -> VHG device open; ? A = 1 -> VHG device close;
	[VHGdev] = 1; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device close"
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGascii] = 0; -> VHG device key done;
    "VHG device open"
	[VHGdev] = 1; [VHGdevaccess] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0; [VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 0;
    "VHG device key done"
	end;

"VHG device physical poll"
	( The iGUI host can delay or consume WM_CHAR while its enhanced-key table
	  still reports the physical key. Cover core character commands and body/
	  device digits with one shared held edge, so an ordinary buffered character
	  cannot be repeated on the following presentation frame. )
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 82 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 114 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 67 -> VHG device physical buffered; ? A = 99 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 71 -> VHG device physical buffered; ? A = 103 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 72 -> VHG device physical buffered; ? A = 104 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 74 -> VHG device physical buffered; ? A = 106 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 76 -> VHG device physical buffered; ? A = 108 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 49 -> VHG device physical buffered; ? A = 50 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 51 -> VHG device physical buffered; ? A = 52 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 53 -> VHG device physical buffered; ? A = 54 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 55 -> VHG device physical buffered; ? A = 56 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	? A = 57 -> VHG device physical buffered;
	A = [VHGdeviceheld]; ? A = 0 -> VHG device physical scan;
	E = KEY A; E + 2; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 6; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 7; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 9; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 11; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 17; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 27; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 28; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 29; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 30; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 31; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 32; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 33; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 34; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	E = KEY A; E + 35; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical done;
	[VHGdeviceheld] = 0; end;
    "VHG device physical scan"
	E = KEY A; E + 2; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical C;
	E = KEY A; E + 6; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical G;
	E = KEY A; E + 7; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical H;
	E = KEY A; E + 9; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical J;
	E = KEY A; E + 11; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical L;
	E = KEY A; E + 17; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical R;
	E = KEY A; E + 27; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical 1;
	E = KEY A; E + 28; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical 2;
	E = KEY A; E + 29; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical 3;
	E = KEY A; E + 30; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical 4;
	E = KEY A; E + 31; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical 5;
	E = KEY A; E + 32; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical 6;
	E = KEY A; E + 33; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical 7;
	E = KEY A; E + 34; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical 8;
	E = KEY A; E + 35; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG device physical 9;
	end;
    "VHG device physical C"
	[VHGascii] = 67; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical G"
	[VHGascii] = 71; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical H"
	[VHGascii] = 72; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical J"
	[VHGascii] = 74; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical L"
	[VHGascii] = 76; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical R"
	[VHGascii] = 82; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical 1"
	[VHGascii] = 49; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical 2"
	[VHGascii] = 50; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical 3"
	[VHGascii] = 51; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical 4"
	[VHGascii] = 52; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical 5"
	[VHGascii] = 53; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical 6"
	[VHGascii] = 54; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical 7"
	[VHGascii] = 55; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical 8"
	[VHGascii] = 56; -> VHG device physical accepted;
    "VHG device physical 9"
	[VHGascii] = 57;
    "VHG device physical accepted"
	[VHGdeviceheld] = 1; end;
    "VHG device physical buffered"
	[VHGdeviceheld] = 1;
    "VHG device physical done"
	end;

"VHG info key"
	( NIV+'s miscellaneous-device submenu exposes three independent data
	  sheets. Repeated I walks remote, local, environment, then closes. Use
	  the physical key table as well as buffered ASCII so the page remains
	  reachable when the iGUI host delays or consumes WM_CHAR. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG info key done;
	E = KEY A; E + 8; A = [E]; ? A = ON -> VHG info physical key;
	[VHGinfoheld] = 0;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 73 -> VHG info key pressed; ? A != 105 -> VHG info key done;
    "VHG info physical key"
	A = [VHGinfoheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG info key done; [VHGinfoheld] = 1;
    "VHG info key pressed"
	A = [VHGinfo]; ? A != 0 -> VHG info key cycle;
	[VHGinfoscroll] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 1; [VHGinfodelta] = 4;
	-> VHG info key selected;
    "VHG info key cycle"
	? A >= 3 -> VHG info key close; [VHGinfo]+; [VHGinfodelta] = 0;
	-> VHG info key selected;
    "VHG info key close"
	[VHGinfodelta] = 0FFFFFFFCh; ( -4, source datasheetdelta close )
    "VHG info key selected"
	[VHGinfogeometryvalid] = 0;
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 0;
"VHG info key done"
	end;

"VHG screen off key"
	( Source X\SCREEN OFF returns from the onboard control system to the
	  unobstructed Stardrifter view. Physical X covers hosts that omit WM_CHAR. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG screen off key done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 88 -> VHG screen off key pressed; ? A = 120 -> VHG screen off key pressed;
	A = [KEY X]; ? A = OFF -> VHG screen off key done;
    "VHG screen off key pressed"
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0;
	A = [VHGinfo]; ? A = 0 -> VHG screen off no data; [VHGinfodelta] = 0FFFFFFFCh;
    "VHG screen off no data"
	[VHGgraphics] = 0; [VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGabout] = 0; [VHGmovieview] = 0;
    "VHG screen off key done"
	end;

"VHG graphics key"
	A = [KEY F2]; ? A = OFF -> VHG graphics key released;
	A = [VHGgraphicsheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG graphics key done;
	[VHGgraphicsheld] = 1; => VHG toggle graphics action; -> VHG graphics key done;
    "VHG graphics key released"
	[VHGgraphicsheld] = 0;
    "VHG graphics key done"
	end;

"VHG movie key"
	( NOCTIS.CPP/NOCTIS-1.CPP bind the source moviemaker card to F3.
	  Recording continues when the card is reopened; F3 only toggles its view. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie key done;
	A = [VHGwidephase]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie key done;
	A = [KEY F3]; ? A = OFF -> VHG movie key released;
	A = [VHGmovieheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie key done; [VHGmovieheld] = 1;
	A = [VHGmovieview]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie key open;
	[VHGmovieview] = 0; [VHGmoviecrossheld] = 0; [VHGmoviehyphenheld] = 0;
	-> VHG movie key done;
    "VHG movie key open"
	[VHGmovieview] = 1; [VHGmoviecrossheld] = 0; [VHGmoviehyphenheld] = 0;
	[VHGgraphics] = 0; [VHGabout] = 0; [VHGhelpshow] = 0;
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0;
	=> VHG movie format deck; => VHG movie check deck; -> VHG movie key done;
    "VHG movie key released"
	[VHGmovieheld] = 0;
    "VHG movie key done"
	end;

"VHG movie character"
	[VHGmovieused] = 0;
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie character done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 80 -> VHG movie pause key; ? A = 112 -> VHG movie pause key;
	? A = 13 -> VHG movie enter key;
	A = [VHGmovieview]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie character done;
	A = [VHGmovie]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie character done;
	A = [KEY CROSS]; ? A = OFF -> VHG movie cross released;
	A = [VHGmoviecrossheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie consume;
	[VHGmoviecrossheld] = 1; -> VHG movie increase key;
    "VHG movie cross released"
	[VHGmoviecrossheld] = 0;
	A = [KEY HYPHEN]; ? A = OFF -> VHG movie hyphen released;
	A = [VHGmoviehyphenheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie consume;
	[VHGmoviehyphenheld] = 1; -> VHG movie decrease key;
    "VHG movie hyphen released"
	[VHGmoviehyphenheld] = 0;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 70 -> VHG movie flash key; ? A = 102 -> VHG movie flash key;
	? A = 43 -> VHG movie increase key; ? A = 45 -> VHG movie decrease key;
	-> VHG movie character done;
    "VHG movie pause key"
	A = [VHGmovie]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie pause recording;
	A = [VHGmoviepaused]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie character done;
	A = [VHGmovieview]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie character done;
	[VHGmovie] = 1; [VHGmoviepaused] = 0; [VHGmovieview] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGmovieresumedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG movie consume;
    "VHG movie pause recording"
	[VHGmovie] = 0; [VHGmoviepaused] = 1; [VHGmovieview] = 1;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGmoviepausedtext; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG movie consume;
    "VHG movie enter key"
	A = [VHGmovie]; ? A != 0 -> VHG movie stop;
	A = [VHGmovieview]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie character done;
	[VHGmovie] = 1; [VHGmoviepaused] = 0; [VHGmovieview] = 0;
	[VHGmovieframe] = 0; [VHGmoviedelay] = 0; [VHGmoviecaptured] = 0;
	[VHGmovieelapsed] = 0; [VHGmoviecutoff] = 0;
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGmovierecording; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG movie consume;
    "VHG movie stop"
	[VHGmovie] = 0; [VHGmoviepaused] = 0; [VHGmovieexists] = 0;
	A = [VHGmoviedeck]; ? A >= 999 -> VHG movie stop deck ready; [VHGmoviedeck]+;
    "VHG movie stop deck ready"
	[VHGmovieframe] = 0; => VHG movie format deck;
	A = [VHGmovieview]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie stop notice;
	=> VHG movie check deck;
    "VHG movie stop notice"
	[VHGnoticeptr] = VHGmoviestopped; [VHGnoticeframes] = 55;
	-> VHG movie consume;
    "VHG movie flash key"
	A = [VHGmovieflashoff]; ? A = 0 -> VHG movie flash disable;
	[VHGmovieflashoff] = 0; -> VHG movie consume;
    "VHG movie flash disable"
	[VHGmovieflashoff] = 1; -> VHG movie consume;
    "VHG movie increase key"
	A = [KEY CONTROL]; ? A = ON -> VHG movie deck higher;
	A = [VHGmovieinterval]; ? A >= 999 -> VHG movie consume; [VHGmovieinterval]+;
	-> VHG movie consume;
    "VHG movie decrease key"
	A = [KEY CONTROL]; ? A = ON -> VHG movie deck lower;
	A = [VHGmovieinterval]; ? A <= 1 -> VHG movie consume; [VHGmovieinterval]-;
	-> VHG movie consume;
    "VHG movie deck higher"
	A = [VHGmoviedeck]; ? A >= 999 -> VHG movie consume; [VHGmoviedeck]+;
	[VHGmovieframe] = 0; => VHG movie format deck; => VHG movie check deck;
	-> VHG movie consume;
    "VHG movie deck lower"
	A = [VHGmoviedeck]; ? A <= 1 -> VHG movie consume; [VHGmoviedeck]-;
	[VHGmovieframe] = 0; => VHG movie format deck; => VHG movie check deck;
    "VHG movie consume"
	[VHGascii] = 0; [VHGmovieused] = 1;
    "VHG movie character done"
	end;

"VHG movie format deck"
	C = [VHGmoviedeck]; A = C; A / 100; A + 48;
	[VHGmoviedeckline plus 10] = A; [VHGmoviedir plus 7] = A; [VHGmoviefile plus 7] = A;
	A = C; A / 10; A % 10; A + 48;
	[VHGmoviedeckline plus 11] = A; [VHGmoviedir plus 8] = A; [VHGmoviefile plus 8] = A;
	A = C; A % 10; A + 48;
	[VHGmoviedeckline plus 12] = A; [VHGmoviedir plus 9] = A; [VHGmoviefile plus 9] = A;
	end;

"VHG movie check deck"
	( ShowMovieSetup checks frame one regardless of the last recorded filename. )
	[VHGmoviefile plus 11] = 48; [VHGmoviefile plus 12] = 48;
	[VHGmoviefile plus 13] = 48; [VHGmoviefile plus 14] = 48;
	[VHGmoviefile plus 15] = 48; [VHGmoviefile plus 16] = 48;
	[VHGmoviefile plus 17] = 48; [VHGmoviefile plus 18] = 49;
	[File Name] = VHGmoviefile; [File Command] = TEST; isocall;
	[VHGmovieexists] = 2; ? [File Status] + ERROR -> VHG movie deck free; end;
    "VHG movie deck free"
	[VHGmovieexists] = 1;
	end;

"VHG visor keys"
	( NIV+ accepts these extended keys only when no onboard screen or overlay
	  owns input. Opening clears hud_closed immediately; closing sets it only
	  when the moving edge reaches the original 180-line endpoint. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG visor keys done;
	A = [VHGdev]; ? A != 0 -> VHG visor keys done;
	A = [VHGfcsopen]; ? A != 0 -> VHG visor keys done;
	A = [VHGinfo]; ? A != 0 -> VHG visor keys done;
	A = [VHGgraphics]; ? A != 0 -> VHG visor keys done;
	A = [VHGhelpshow]; ? A != 0 -> VHG visor keys done;
	A = [VHGlandingselect]; ? A != 0 -> VHG visor keys done;
	A = [KEY PGUP]; ? A = OFF -> VHG visor close key;
	A = 0; A - 5; [VHGhuddelta] = A; [VHGhudclosed] = 0;
    "VHG visor close key"
	A = [KEY PGDN]; ? A = OFF -> VHG visor keys done; [VHGhuddelta] = 5;
    "VHG visor keys done"
	end;

"VHG visor advance"
	A = [VHGhuddelta]; ? A = 0 -> VHG visor advance done;
	A + [VHGhudcount]; [VHGhudcount] = A;
	A = [VHGhuddelta]; ? A > 0 -> VHG visor closing;
	A = [VHGhudcount]; ? A > 0 -> VHG visor advance done;
	[VHGhudcount] = 0; [VHGhuddelta] = 0; -> VHG visor advance done;
    "VHG visor closing"
	A = [VHGhudcount]; ? A < 180 -> VHG visor advance done;
	[VHGhudcount] = 180; [VHGhuddelta] = 0; [VHGhudclosed] = 1;
    "VHG visor advance done"
	end;

"VHG visor flare mode"
	( lens_flares_for(): mode 0 reflects only from the closed visor, mode 1
	  forces reflections, and mode -1 suppresses them. )
	[VHFghost] = 0; A = [VHGlensmode]; ? A = 1 -> VHG visor flare on;
	? A != 0 -> VHG visor flare done;
	A = [VHGhudclosed]; ? A = 0 -> VHG visor flare done;
    "VHG visor flare on"
	[VHFghost] = 1;
    "VHG visor flare done"
	end;

"VHG toggle graphics action"
	A = [VHGgraphics]; ? A = 0 -> VHG toggle graphics on;
	[VHGgraphics] = 0; end;
"VHG toggle graphics on"
	[VHGgraphics] = 1; [VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGabout] = 0; [VHGmovieview] = 0;
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0;
	[VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 0;
	end;

"VHG graphics character"
	[VHGgraphicsused] = 0; A = [VHGgraphics]; ? A = 0 -> VHG graphics character done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 70 -> VHG graphics flare key; ? A = 102 -> VHG graphics flare key;
	? A = 84 -> VHG graphics hud key; ? A = 116 -> VHG graphics hud key;
	? A = 66 -> VHG graphics border key; ? A = 98 -> VHG graphics border key;
	-> VHG graphics character done;
    "VHG graphics flare key"
	[VHGgraphicsused] = 1; A = [VHGlensmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG graphics flare always;
	? A = 1 -> VHG graphics choose flare off; [VHGlensmode] = 0; -> VHG graphics character done;
    "VHG graphics flare always"
	[VHGlensmode] = 1; -> VHG graphics character done;
    "VHG graphics choose flare off"
	A = 0; A - 1; [VHGlensmode] = A; -> VHG graphics character done;
    "VHG graphics hud key"
	[VHGgraphicsused] = 1; A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHG graphics enable hud;
	[VHGdrawhud] = 0; -> VHG graphics character done;
    "VHG graphics enable hud"
	[VHGdrawhud] = 1; -> VHG graphics character done;
    "VHG graphics border key"
	[VHGgraphicsused] = 1; A = [VHGseamless]; ? A = 0 -> VHG graphics choose seamless;
	[VHGseamless] = 0; -> VHG graphics character done;
    "VHG graphics choose seamless"
	[VHGseamless] = 1;
    "VHG graphics character done"
	end;

"VHG about key"
	( NIV+ F1 toggles ShowAboutPage(). Keep it distinct from the port's F9/?
	  control card so the original screen and accurate current controls coexist. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG about key done;
	A = [KEY F1]; ? A = OFF -> VHG about key released;
	A = [VHGaboutheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG about key done; [VHGaboutheld] = 1;
	A = [VHGabout]; ? A = 0 -> VHG about key open;
	[VHGabout] = 0; -> VHG about key done;
    "VHG about key open"
	[VHGabout] = 1; [VHGhelpshow] = 0; [VHGgraphics] = 0; [VHGmovieview] = 0; [VHGdev] = 0;
	[VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0; [VHGconsole] = 0;
	-> VHG about key done;
    "VHG about key released"
	[VHGaboutheld] = 0;
    "VHG about key done"
	end;

"VHG help key"
	( F9 and ? retain the port-specific control card. F1 owns the separate
	  source-shaped Noctis IV+ About page. )
	A = [VHGconsole]; ? A != 0 -> VHG help key done;
	A = [VHGascii]; ? A = 63 -> VHG help key pressed;
	A = [KEY F9]; ? A = OFF -> VHG help key released;
    "VHG help key pressed"
	A = [VHGhelpheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG help key done;
	[VHGhelpheld] = 1; => VHG toggle help action; -> VHG help key done;
    "VHG help key released"
	[VHGhelpheld] = 0;
    "VHG help key done"
	end;

"VHG toggle help action"
	A = [VHGhelpshow]; ? A = 0 -> VHG toggle help on;
	[VHGhelpshow] = 0; end;
    "VHG toggle help on"
	[VHGdev] = 0; [VHGfcsopen] = 0; [VHGprefs] = 0; [VHGinfo] = 0;
	[VHGgraphics] = 0; [VHGabout] = 0; [VHGmovieview] = 0; [VHGhelpshow] = 1;
	end;

"VHG fps key"
	( F2-F3 belong to Noctis; F4 is unused and toggles diagnostics once/release. )
	A = [KEY F4]; ? A = OFF -> VHG fps key released;
	A = [VHGfpsheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG fps key done;
	[VHGfpsheld] = 1; => VHG toggle fps action; -> VHG fps key done;
    "VHG fps key released"
	[VHGfpsheld] = 0;
    "VHG fps key done"
	end;

"VHG toggle fps action"
	A = [VHGfpsshow]; ? A = 0 -> VHG toggle fps on;
	[VHGfpsshow] = 0; end;
    "VHG toggle fps on"
	[VHGfpsshow] = 1;
	end;

"VHG fast key"
	( F5 toggles 60 Hz presentation; gameplay continues at 18.206 ticks/s. )
	A = [KEY F5]; ? A = OFF -> VHG fast key released;
	A = [VHGfastheld]; ? A != 0 -> VHG fast key done;
	[VHGfastheld] = 1; => VHG toggle fast action; -> VHG fast key done;
    "VHG fast key released"
	[VHGfastheld] = 0;
    "VHG fast key done"
	end;

"VHG toggle fast action"
	A = [VHGfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG toggle fast on;
	[VHGfast] = 0; [VHGsimacc] = 0; [VHGsimfrac] = 0; [VHGsimcountok] = 0;
	[VHGinterpok] = 0; => TK period; => VHG timing rebase;
	end;
    "VHG toggle fast on"
	[VHGfast] = 1; [VHGsimacc] = 0; [VHGsimfrac] = 0; [VHGsimcountok] = 0;
	[VHGinterpok] = 0; => VHG timing rebase;
	end;

"VHG interpolation advance"
	( Presentation-only phase between two authoritative 18.206-Hz poses.
	  Use the scheduler's measured residual wall time.  A fixed increment per
	  presented frame cannot represent the alternating three/four-frame gaps
	  between 18.206-Hz ticks and leaves a visible catch-up step on every short
	  gap.  Airborne capsule poses participate even though VHGlanded remains
	  clear during descent and recovery. )
	A = [VHGfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation advance done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation advance eligible;
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A != 0 -> VHG interpolation advance eligible;
	A = [VHGCstate]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation advance done;
    "VHG interpolation advance eligible"
	A = [VHGsimacc]; ? A >= 0 -> VHG interpolation phase positive; A = 0;
    "VHG interpolation phase positive"
	A '* VHGSIMDEN; A / 1000000;
	? A <= VHGSIMDEN -> VHG interpolation advance store; A = VHGSIMDEN;
    "VHG interpolation advance store"
	[VHGinterpacc] = A;
    "VHG interpolation advance done"
	end;

"VHG interpolation apply"
	[VHGinterpapplied] = 0;
	A = [VHGfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation apply done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation apply eligible;
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A != 0 -> VHG interpolation apply eligible;
	A = [VHGCstate]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation apply done;
    "VHG interpolation apply eligible"
	A = [VHGinterpok]; ? A != 0 -> VHG interpolation apply ready;
	=> VHG interpolation reset; -> VHG interpolation apply done;
    "VHG interpolation apply ready"
	[VHGinterplivex] = [VHGx]; [VHGinterplivey] = [VHGy]; [VHGinterplivez] = [VHGz];
	[VHGinterplivealpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHGinterplivebeta] = [VHGbeta];
	A = [VHGx]; A - [VHGinterpx]; A '* [VHGinterpacc]; A / VHGSIMDEN;
	A + [VHGinterpx]; [VHGx] = A;
	A = [VHGy]; A - [VHGinterpy]; A '* [VHGinterpacc]; A / VHGSIMDEN;
	A + [VHGinterpy]; [VHGy] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A - [VHGinterpz]; A '* [VHGinterpacc]; A / VHGSIMDEN;
	A + [VHGinterpz]; [VHGz] = A;
	A = [VHGalpha]; A - [VHGinterpalpha]; A '* [VHGinterpacc]; A / VHGSIMDEN;
	A + [VHGinterpalpha]; [VHGalpha] = A;
	A = [VHGinterplivebeta]; A - [VHGinterpbeta]; [VHGinterpdelta] = A;
	? A <= 180 -> VHG interpolation beta low; A - 360; [VHGinterpdelta] = A;
    "VHG interpolation beta low"
	A = [VHGinterpdelta]; ? A >= VHGN180 -> VHG interpolation beta delta ready;
	A + 360; [VHGinterpdelta] = A;
    "VHG interpolation beta delta ready"
	A = [VHGinterpdelta]; A '* [VHGinterpacc]; A / VHGSIMDEN;
	A + [VHGinterpbeta]; ? A <= 180 -> VHG interpolation beta normalize low; A - 360;
    "VHG interpolation beta normalize low"
	? A >= VHGN180 -> VHG interpolation beta ready; A + 360;
    "VHG interpolation beta ready"
	[VHGbeta] = A; [VHGinterpapplied] = 1;
	[VHGinterprenderx] = [VHGx]; [VHGinterprenderz] = [VHGz];
	[VHGinterprenderalpha] = [VHGalpha];
    "VHG interpolation apply done"
	end;

"VHG interpolation restore"
	A = [VHGinterpapplied]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation restore done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation restore live;
	A = [VHGx]; A - [VHGinterprenderx]; [VHGinterpeffectx] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A - [VHGinterprenderz]; [VHGinterpeffectz] = A;
	A = [VHGalpha]; A - [VHGinterprenderalpha]; [VHGinterpeffectalpha] = A;
    "VHG interpolation restore live"
	[VHGx] = [VHGinterplivex]; [VHGy] = [VHGinterplivey]; [VHGz] = [VHGinterplivez];
	[VHGalpha] = [VHGinterplivealpha]; [VHGbeta] = [VHGinterplivebeta];
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation restore done;
	A = [VHGx]; A + [VHGinterpeffectx]; [VHGx] = A;
	A = [VHGz]; A + [VHGinterpeffectz]; [VHGz] = A;
	A = [VHGalpha]; A + [VHGinterpeffectalpha]; ? A <= 44 -> VHG interpolation restore alpha high; A = 44;
    "VHG interpolation restore alpha high"
	? A >= VHGN44 -> VHG interpolation restore alpha ready; A = VHGN44;
    "VHG interpolation restore alpha ready"
	[VHGalpha] = A;
    "VHG interpolation restore done"
	end;

"VHG interpolation snapshot"
	A = [VHGfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation snapshot done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation snapshot eligible;
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A != 0 -> VHG interpolation snapshot eligible;
	A = [VHGCstate]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation snapshot done;
    "VHG interpolation snapshot eligible"
	A = [VHGdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHG interpolation snapshot done;
	[VHGinterpx] = [VHGx]; [VHGinterpy] = [VHGy]; [VHGinterpz] = [VHGz];
	[VHGinterpalpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHGinterpbeta] = [VHGbeta];
	[VHGinterpok] = 1;
    "VHG interpolation snapshot done"
	end;

"VHG interpolation reset"
	[VHGinterpx] = [VHGx]; [VHGinterpy] = [VHGy]; [VHGinterpz] = [VHGz];
	[VHGinterpalpha] = [VHGalpha]; [VHGinterpbeta] = [VHGbeta];
	[VHGinterpacc] = 0; [VHGinterpok] = 1;
	end;

"VHG cadence"
	[VHGdosim] = 1; A = [VHGfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHG cadence done;
	[VHGdosim] = 0; [Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [TKnow] = [Counts];
	A = [VHGsimcountok]; ? A != 0 -> VHG cadence count ready;
	[VHGsimcountprev] = [TKnow]; [VHGsimcountok] = 1; [VHGdosim] = 1;
	-> VHG cadence done;
    "VHG cadence count ready"
	A = [TKnow]; A - [VHGsimcountprev]; [VHGsimcountelapsed] = A;
	[VHGsimcountprev] = [TKnow];
	( The calibrated high-resolution counter removes the former whole-ms
	  interpolation steps. A normal unsigned counter wrap subtracts to the
	  correct small positive delta; signed negatives and gaps above 250 ms are
	  still treated as focus/suspend discontinuities. )
	A = [VHGsimcountelapsed]; ? A < 0 -> VHG cadence discontinuity;
	A = [TKcpms]; ? A = 0 -> VHG cadence discontinuity;
	C = A; C '* 250; A = [VHGsimcountelapsed]; ? A > C -> VHG cadence discontinuity;
	( Convert elapsed counts to the existing million-unit 18.206-Hz
	  accumulator without overflowing: split whole milliseconds from the
	  sub-millisecond remainder, and carry the division remainder between
	  presentation frames. )
	A = [VHGsimcountelapsed]; A '/ [TKcpms]; [VHGsimwhole] = A;
	C = A; C '* [TKcpms]; A = [VHGsimcountelapsed]; A - C; [VHGsimrem] = A;
	A = [VHGsimwhole]; A '* VHGSIMADD; [VHGsiminc] = A;
	A = [VHGsimrem]; A '* VHGSIMADD; A + [VHGsimfrac]; [VHGsimrem] = A;
	A '/ [TKcpms]; C = [VHGsiminc]; C + A; [VHGsiminc] = C;
	C = A; C '* [TKcpms]; A = [VHGsimrem]; A - C; [VHGsimfrac] = A;
	A = [VHGsiminc]; A + [VHGsimacc]; [VHGsimacc] = A;
	? A '< 1000000 -> VHG cadence done;
	A - 1000000; [VHGsimacc] = A; [VHGdosim] = 1; -> VHG cadence done;
    "VHG cadence discontinuity"
	[VHGsimacc] = 0; [VHGsimfrac] = 0; [VHGdosim] = 1;
    "VHG cadence done"
	end;

"VHG timing step"
	[VHGtimingcalls]+;
	( The exact Noctis skip-to-grid timer remains authoritative in original
	  mode.  The 60-Hz presenter needs a different overrun rule: wait when
	  early, but rebase immediately when late.  TK step would skip to the
	  *next* future point after iGUI's retrace reached this one, producing
	  the observed 30-Hz double wait. )
	A = [VHGfast]; ? A != 0 -> VHG timing fast;
	=> TK step;
	-> VHG timing done;
    "VHG timing fast"
	A = [TKcpms]; A '* 17; [TKbase] = A;
	A = [TKcpms]; A '* 198864; [TKsubper] = A;
	=> TK advance;
	[Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall; [TKnow] = [Counts]; => TK is late;
	A = [TKlate]; ? A = 0 -> VHG timing fast wait;
	( Overrun: make now the new origin; do not discard another interval. )
	[TKdeadline] = [TKnow]; [TKacc] = 0; -> VHG timing done;
    "VHG timing fast wait"
	=> TK wait;
    "VHG timing done"
	end;

"VHG timing rebase"
	[TKacc] = 0; [Timer Command] = READ COUNTS; isocall;
	[TKdeadline] = [Counts];
	end;

	"VHG write sentinel"
	=> TK read wall; A = [TKtmp]; A - [VHGwallstart]; [VHGwallelapsed] = A;
	[vhgstate plus 0] = [VHGx]; [vhgstate plus 1] = [VHGy]; [vhgstate plus 2] = [VHGz];
